Method: 提出了一种名为“Bandit Simulation for Inference (BSI)”的框架。该方法通过观测数据（无论是在线策略还是离线策略）拟合出一个老虎机环境模拟器，并将模拟器参数估计中的不确定性传播到置信区间的构建中。

Result: 理论证明了BSI产生的置信区间是渐近有效的；实验结果表明，在标准离线评估方法失效的情境下，BSI依然能够保持标称覆盖率。

Conclusion: BSI框架能够有效解决多臂老虎机算法中，由于数据依赖性导致的标准统计推断失效问题。该方法通过模拟器环境，在仅需弱探索假设的前提下，为自适应策略生成的奖励提供了渐近有效的置信区间，且无需依赖重要性采样。

Abstract: Multi-arm bandit algorithms are increasingly used in online platforms, clinical trials, and social science experiments, but valid statistical inference on their performance remains an open challenge. After deploying bandits, a natural question is whether one can construct a confidence interval for its mean reward and assess whether it reliably outperforms a baseline policy. The total reward achieved in any single bandit deployment is random, and deploying a bandit twice on the same population typically yields different reward trajectories due to stochastic rewards. Standard statistical inference methods cannot be used because bandit algorithms introduce complex dependencies in the collected data, which violate the i.i.d. assumption underlying many classical approaches. Moreover, existing inference methods for adaptively collected data only apply to estimands that do not depend on the data-collection algorithm (such as the mean reward under a fixed action). We propose Bandit Simulation for Inference (BSI), a framework that fits a simulator of the bandit environment from observed data--either on-policy or off-policy--and uses it to estimate the mean reward under any evaluation policy, including adaptive blackbox algorithms. BSI formally propagates uncertainty in the estimated simulator parameters into the confidence interval construction. Furthermore, for BSI to be valid, it requires only weak exploration assumptions on the behavior policy and avoids importance weighting. We prove that BSI yields asymptotically valid confidence intervals, and demonstrate empirically that it maintains nominal coverage in settings where standard off-policy evaluation methods fail.

</details>

Main category: stat.ML

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒网络生成方法普遍存在过拟合、未能有效捕获网络结构特性以及在大规模数据下计算开销过大的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为SyNGLER的框架：1. 利用隐空间网络模型学习节点的低维表征；2. 在隐空间构建无分布生成器；3. 通过采样隐空间表征并利用模型重构生成合成网络。

Result: 实验表明，SyNGLER在保持网络稀疏性、度分布及网络矩等关键特性方面表现优于现有方法，且计算效率更高；同时提供了关于真实与合成边分布距离一致性的理论证明。

Conclusion: SyNGLER是一个高效且具备理论保证的合成网络生成框架，通过隐空间模型实现了结构属性的高保真度与计算效率的平衡，优于现有的黑盒生成方法。

Abstract: Network data are ubiquitous across the social sciences, biology, and information systems. Generating realistic synthetic network data has broad applications from network simulation to scientific discovery. However, many existing black-box approaches for network generation tend to overfit observed data while overlooking characteristic network structure, and incur substantial computational overhead at scale. These practical challenges call for synthetic network generation methods that are both efficient and capable of capturing structural properties of networks. In this paper, we introduce Synthetic Network Generation via Latent Embedding Reconstruction (SyNGLER), a general and efficient framework for synthetic network generation that builds on latent space network models. Given an observed network, SyNGLER first learns low-dimensional latent node embeddings via a latent space network model and then reconstructs the latent space by building a distribution-free generator over these embeddings. For generation, SyNGLER first samples (or resamples) node embeddings from the generator in the latent space and then produces synthetic networks using the latent space network model. Through the latent space framework, SyNGLER preserves unique characteristics in networks such as sparsity and node degree heterogeneity, while allowing for efficient training with lower computational cost than many existing deep architectures. We provide theoretical guarantees by developing consistency results on the distance between the true and synthetic edge distributions. Empirical studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of SyNGLER, which efficiently produces networks that better preserve key network characteristics such as network moments and degree distributions compared with existing approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/FeifanJiang/syngler.

</details>

*Sanghoon Yu,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将“稀疏更新”与“严格批处理”（限制了区间内的上下文自适应性）混淆。本文旨在解决在仅允许少数几次参数更新的情况下，如何设计能够进行在线上下文自适应且统计和计算效率极高的线性情境强盗算法。

Method: 提出了两种算法：1. BLCE-G：在静态调度下，在小K和大K两种机制下均达到极小极大最优（minimax-optimal）后悔；2. BLCE：通过消除 near G-optimal design 步骤，不仅保持了极小极大最优性，还实现了已知最优算法中最低的运行时间复杂度。

Result: 证明了算法仅需 O(loglog T) 次参数更新即可达到极小极大最优后悔，且在计算效率上优于现有算法，并成功将理论推广至广义线性情境强盗。

Conclusion: 该研究成功在保持极低更新频率 (O(loglog T)) 的同时，实现了统计上最优的后悔界（regret bound），并显著提升了计算效率，不仅适用于线性情境强盗问题，还推广到了广义线性情境强盗模型。

Abstract: We study linear contextual bandits under rare parameter updates: the learner may incorporate reward feedback into its parameter estimate only at a small number of update times, while still observing contexts online and selecting actions sequentially. This viewpoint clarifies a practical distinction that is often blurred in the literature: many "strictly batched" methods additionally restrict within-interval context adaptivity, meaning that the action rule inside an interval cannot depend on the sequence of realized contexts/actions in that interval (beyond the current round's context). For linear contextual bandits, we propose two practical algorithms with only $O(\log\log T)$ parameter updates. Our first algorithm BLCE-G attains minimax-optimal regret (up to polylogarithmic factors in $T$) simultaneously in both the small-$K$ and large-$K$ regimes under a static schedule. Our second algorithm BLCE removes the near G-optimal design step -- a dominant computational bottleneck in prior strictly batched static-grid methods -- yet preserves minimax-optimal regret and achieves the lowest known runtime complexity among optimal algorithms. We further extend these rare-update and computational principles to generalized linear contextual bandits. Overall, our results yield statistically optimal algorithms under $O(\log\log T)$ parameter updates that are also computationally efficient in practice.

</details>


### [48] [Efficient Approximation for Encoder--Decoder Neural Operators via Variation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01244)
*Jia-Qi Yang,Lei Shi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用变分空间理论，为更广义的算子学习提供了编码器-解码器架构的理论逼近界与收敛率分析，突破了传统光滑算子类别的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习研究多依赖于Lipschitz连续性或Fréchet可微性，缺乏更广泛的函数空间理论支撑，难以应对复杂的算子逼近问题。

Method: 1. 引入变分空间作为非线性算子的无穷维结构类（基于输入输出空间上的向量值测度）；2. 在Bochner $L^q$ 范数下，推导编码器-解码器双层神经网络的逼近界；3. 将误差分解为输入编码误差、输出编码误差及有限宽度逼近项。

Result: 给出了明确的误差分解界，其中有限宽度近似项的收敛阶为 $O(N^{-1/2})$，且常数项与编码维度无关；在编码误差呈多项式衰减的假设下，得出了代数形式的逼近与学习速率。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入变分空间，为算子学习中的编码器-解码器神经网络提供了超越传统Lipschitz或Fréchet可微性假设的性能保证，并建立了代数收敛率。

Abstract: We study operator learning using encoder--decoder neural networks. Inspired by the function-space theory of neural networks, we introduce a variation space as an infinite-dimensional structural class for nonlinear operators. This space is defined through vector-valued measures directly on the input and output spaces. For operators in this space, we establish approximation bounds for encoder--decoder two-layer networks in the Bochner $L^q$ norm. The resulting error bound decomposes into the input encoding error, the output encoding error, and a finite-width approximation term of order $N^{-1/2}$, with a constant independent of the input and output encoding dimensions. When the input and output encoding errors decay polynomially in the encoding dimensions, these estimates yield algebraic approximation and learning rates. The results provide an theoretical guarantees for efficient neural operator learning beyond general Lipschitz or Fréchet differentiable operator classes.

</details>


### [49] [Distribution-free changepoint localization after sequential change detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01256)
*Aytijhya Saha,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种无需分布假设的序贯变点定位方法，通过共形测试鞅构建置信集，首次实现了在保证有限样本覆盖率的前提下进行分布无关的变点统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序贯变点检测方法虽能有效检测变化，但难以对变点发生的时间提供统计推断，且现有推断方法多依赖于先验的分布类假设。

Method: 利用共形映射（conformal mapping）与测试鞅（test martingales）技术，构建用于变点定位的置信集，并推导了非渐近的条件覆盖界限。

Result: 在有限样本下证明了置信集的条件覆盖率，在适当的渐近框架下证明了置信集期望长度的均匀有界性，并在模拟数据和真实数据集上验证了其稳健性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了首个针对序贯变点检测后的分布无关（distribution-free）置信集构建框架，在无需分布假设前提下实现了变点定位，且具有有限样本覆盖保证和期望长度的有界性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a distribution-free framework for constructing post-detection confidence sets for changepoints after stopping a sequential change detection procedure. It is well known that conformal test martingales can be used to sequentially detect changes in distribution, but by themselves provide no inference for the time at which a proclaimed change occurred. Past work on post-detection inference requires pre- and post-change classes of distributions to be known, but this paper accomplishes localization of the changepoint without any distributional assumptions. We establish finite-sample coverage guarantees (conditional on correct detection). We provide non-asymptotic bounds on the conditional expected size of the confidence sets. Under suitable asymptotic regimes, we proved that the conditional expected size of the confidence set remains uniformly bounded. and demonstrate strong empirical performance on simulated and real data. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first general distribution-free framework for sequential changepoint localization with a valid post-detection coverage guarantee.

</details>


### [50] [On the Uncertainty Quantification Ability of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01427)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Nima Negarandeh,Oriol Vendrell-Gallart,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究对比了 TabPFN 与高斯过程（GP）在回归任务中的预测与不确定性量化表现，发现两者各有优劣：TabPFN 适用于大数据复杂场景，而 GP 在数据稀缺环境下或通过定制核函数提供更优的精度与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（FMs）虽在泛化能力上取得成功，但力学和计算科学等关键领域不仅需要精确预测，还需要可靠的不确定性量化（UQ）。研究旨在评估表格数据基础模型在这些关键领域中的UQ表现。

Method: 通过全面的实证研究，使用默认设置，在不同复杂度、数据集大小和输入维度的回归问题上，对比了Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN v2.5) 与高斯过程 (GPs) 的预测准确性与不确定性量化（UQ）能力。

Result: 研究发现，TabPFN在复杂高维任务上具有强竞争力，但在数据稀缺环境或拥有良好领域先验（核函数）的情况下，GP具有更好的表现与更稳健的UQ效果。

Conclusion: TabPFN与高斯过程（GP）之间存在性能权衡。TabPFN在处理复杂、高维且数据充足的问题时表现出色；而在数据稀缺场景下，GP通常提供更优的预测精度及不确定性量化。选择合适的核函数可使GP性能显著优于TabPFN。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have achieved substantial success in generalizing across tasks without problemspecific training or fine-tuning. However, many critical applications in mechanics and computational science require not only accurate predictions but also reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ). Herein we investigate the UQ capabilities of tabular FMs in regression tasks through a comprehensive empirical study comparing Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN) against Gaussian processes (GPs). We systematically evaluate these two methods across a host of regression problems with varying complexity, dataset sizes, and input dimensionalities. We use a default setting to build all the GPs and for a fair comparison against TabPFN v2.5. Our findings highlight an important trade-off between explicit and learned priors: while TabPFN achieves highly competitive performance for complex, high-dimensional problems with sufficient data, GPs often provide superior predictive accuracy and UQ in data-scarce settings. Moreover, when the chosen kernel constitutes a good prior for the underlying function, GP performance can substantially exceed that of TabPFN. Our results can be reproduced from https://github.com/kianswarehouse/GPvsPFN.

</details>

*JR Huml,Jonathan Wenger,John P. Cunningham*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在现代大规模神经数据集上表现优异，但缺乏贝叶斯方法的显式先验和不确定性建模能力；而现有贝叶斯方法在大规模数据上存在近似误差、计算复杂度高及超参数固定等问题。

Method: 提出计算感知状态空间模型（CASSM），引入了一种新的训练损失函数和优化方案，解决了传统贝叶斯推断在大状态空间中的扩展性问题。

Result: 在神经元记录数远大于试验数（scale-imbalanced）的设定下，CASSM 在合成和真实数据上表现出极强的竞争力，相比以往贝叶斯扩展尝试，显著提升了不确定性校准效果。

Conclusion: CASSM 为神经科学研究者提供了一个在特定约束条件下（尤其是小样本、高维神经记录）的选择框架，能在保持贝叶斯不确定性校准优势的同时，实现与深度学习方法竞争的性能。

Abstract: Due to their explicit priors and ability to model uncertainty, Bayesian methods have played a major role in dynamical latent variable modeling of single-cell neural recordings. However, modern-sized datasets have made overparameterized deep networks the preferred methods of choice due to their predictive power and favorable computational scaling. While many posterior approximations exist, all incur approximation errors. Recent work accounts for this error in the form of computational uncertainty but comes at the cost of quadratic complexity and assumes fixed model hyperparameters. Here we extend this development to model selection, including a novel training loss and optimization scheme, which yields tractable inference in large state-spaces. We introduce a framework, the Computation-Aware State-Space Model (CASSM), specifically designed for the scale-imbalanced regime, where the number of trials is significantly lower than the number of recorded neurons. In this regime, for both synthetic and real data, we show that our method is competitive with data-hungry deep networks, with significantly improved uncertainty calibration over previous attempts to scale Bayesian methods. Our experiments provide a roadmap to neuroscience researchers in choosing from a host of potential dynamical latent variable models given key dataset properties and constraints.

</details>


### [52] [Self-Regulating Annealing in Heavy-Tailed Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01645)
*Keito Wakatsuki,Hideaki Shimazaki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为重尾扩散模型 (HTDM) 开发了一种基于 SDE 的新型采样器，通过引入状态依赖的扩散系数实现自动退火，显著提升了模型对重尾数据分布的拟合与采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的扩散模型采用高斯分布，在处理具有重尾特征的数据集时存在局限性。尽管重尾扩散模型 (HTDM) 引入了学生 t 分布来改善尾部保真度，但目前尚未充分研究其基于 SDE 的采样方法。

Result: 通过理论分析探索了该自调节机制，实验验证了该方法对于准确还原重尾分布采样结果的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出了针对重尾扩散模型 (HTDMs) 的基于 SDE 的采样器，该采样器通过状态依赖的扩散系数引入了自适应的退火机制，证明了其在生成重尾分布数据方面的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a leading framework for deep generative modeling. While the standard Gaussian formulation is theoretically convenient, its suitability for heavy-tailed datasets remains unclear. To address this, heavy-tailed diffusion models (HTDMs) extend the standard formulation by replacing the Gaussian distribution with a Student's t-distribution, thereby improving tail fidelity on heavy-tailed datasets. Although stochastic differential equation (SDE)-based sampling is possible in HTDMs, it has not been fully explored. In this paper, we propose an SDE-based sampler for HTDMs that explicitly incorporates a state-dependent diffusion coefficient. This state dependence naturally induces a self-regulating annealing mechanism by adaptively modulating the effective noise scale. We theoretically explore this mechanism and experimentally verify its necessity for reproducing samples from a heavy-tailed distribution.

</details>


### [53] [Provable Data Scaling Law for Meta Learning via Complexity Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02008)
*Kazuto Fukuchi,Ryuichiro Hataya,Kota Matsui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出“复杂度最小化”元学习框架，从理论上揭示了预训练数据规模对下游任务样本复杂度的优化机理，并证明了其在提升少样本适应效率方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习预训练理论框架无法充分解释“预训练数据规模增加导致下游样本复杂度降低”这一核心经验现象，缺乏深入的理论支撑。

Method: 提出了一种名为“复杂度最小化（Complexity Minimization）”的元表示学习框架。该框架通过评估最适合每个领域的下游模型复杂度，并最小化跨源领域的“最差情况（worst-case）”复杂度来学习表示。

Result: 理论分析表明该框架能够捕捉到预训练的缩放行为（scaling behavior），即少样本适应的错误率随着元训练数据量的增加而降低；实验证明在现有元学习方法中加入复杂度正则化可有效提升样本效率。

Conclusion: “复杂度最小化”框架理论上证明了预训练数据规模的扩大能够直接降低下游任务的样本复杂度，且通过在元学习中引入复杂度正则化，实证表现上显著提升了下游任务的样本效率。

Abstract: Pre-training has become a fundamental paradigm in modern machine learning, with one of its key empirical benefits being reduced downstream sample complexity as the scale of pre-training data increases. However, existing theoretical frameworks for pre-training do not fully explain this phenomenon. In this paper, we introduce complexity minimization, a novel meta-representation learning framework designed to enable theoretical analysis of this scaling behavior, which learns representations by evaluating the downstream model complexity best suited to each domain and minimizing the worst-case such complexity across source domains. Our end-to-end theoretical analysis, spanning pre-training through downstream regression, shows that this framework provably captures this scaling behavior; in particular, we show that the error rate of few-shot adaptation improves as the amount of meta-training data grows. Empirically, we demonstrate that incorporating complexity regularization into existing meta-learning methods consistently improves downstream sample efficiency.

</details>


### [54] [Convex Distance Operator Transport: A Convex and Geometry-Preserving Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02047)
*Junhyoung Chung,Euijong Song,Won Hwa Kim,Gunwoong Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 CDOT 的凸最优传输框架，能够有效对齐异构领域数据，同时保持几何结构，并具备全局收敛性和优于传统 GW 方法的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输方法（如 Gromov-Wasserstein）在处理异构域分布对齐时存在非凸性问题，且难以同时保持特征对应和内部几何结构。

Method: 提出凸距离算子传输 (CDOT) 框架，利用距离和条件期望算子进行正则化，并采用 Frank–Wolfe 算法实现全局收敛。

Result: 证明了 CDOT 差异是一个有效的伪度量，通过分散间隙（dispersion gap）揭示了 GW 非凸性的几何根源，并在基准测试中优于现有方法，同时给出了非渐进风险界。

Conclusion: CDOT 是一种有效的凸优化最优传输框架，通过引入算子正则化解决了传统 GW 距离的非凸性问题，在处理异构域数据时具有理论上的稳健性和更佳的实际性能。

Abstract: We introduce Convex Distance Operator Transport (CDOT), the first convex optimal transport framework that aligns distributions across heterogeneous domains by jointly preserving feature correspondence and intrinsic geometric structure. Specifically, CDOT employs an operator-based regularization that aligns aggregated distance structures by introducing distance and conditional expectation operators. Consequently, the proposed regularization improves the robustness to local geometric variations. We further prove that the resulting CDOT discrepancy is a valid pseudometric on the space of attributed compact metric-measure spaces. In addition, we characterize the relationship between CDOT and Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) through a new notion of dispersion gap, formally elucidating the geometric source of non-convexity in GW compared to the convexity of CDOT. In the finite-sample regime, we derive a non-asymptotic risk bound decomposed into optimization and statistical errors, establishing risk consistency under a globally convergent Frank--Wolfe algorithm. Experiments on synthetic point clouds, brain connectomes, and graph classification benchmarks demonstrate better performance over existing methods, with stable and reliable behavior in practice.

</details>


### [55] [It does what it says on the tin: safe synthetic data from coarsened margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02101)
*Gillian M Raab*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统计披露控制（SDC）和迭代比例拟合（IPF）的合成数据生成方法，确保了数据生成过程的透明度以及对隐私风险的可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据生成方法在处理数据的透明度（哪些变量关系被保留）和隐私保护（如何确保无披露风险）方面存在不足，该研究旨在解决这两个关键痛点。

Method: 1. 定义并计算原始数据中需保留的变量关系边缘（margins）；2. 对这些边缘应用统计披露控制技术（如上限/下限编码、合并小类别、调整小计数值等）；3. 通过将计数粗化为披露限额的倍数来进一步调整边缘；4. 使用迭代比例拟合（IPF）算法基于调整后的边缘生成合成数据。

Result: 通过使用1901年苏格兰人口普查数据进行实证分析，验证了该方法在生成符合隐私标准且具有特定统计分析价值的合成数据方面的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将统计披露控制（SDC）整合到生成过程中，成功在保持特定数据变量关系与严格保护隐私之间取得了平衡，提供了一种透明且风险可控的合成数据生成方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a method of creating synthetic data (SD) that will have two important advantages for the user compared to other methods currently available. The first is transparency; unlike other methods, the person in receipt of the SD will know which of the relationships between variables in the original data will be approximately maintained in the SD. The second is a guarantee that the SD is derived from information that has already been judged to be free of disclosure risk. This is achieved by first defining and calculating the margins where relationships between variables will be maintained in the SD. Each margin will then be subject to statistical disclosure control (SDC) to the standards defined by the data custodian, e.g. top-coding and bottom-coding, combination of small categories and/or modifying small counts. Further adjustment of the curated margins is advised by coarsening all counts in the table to multiples of the disclosure limit. These adjusted margins are used to create SD by the Iterative Proportional Fitting (IPF) algorithm. The practical steps involved in creating such SD are illustrated using data from the 1901 Census of Scotland.

</details>


### [56] [Error Bounds for a Diffusion Model-Based Drift Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02115)
*Ioar Casado-Telletxea,Omar Rivasplata*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为基于扩散模型的SDE漂移估计方法提供了理论支撑，通过推导风险界限，量化了各类误差源对估计精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tapia Costa等人（2026）结合条件得分匹配（conditional score-matching）扩散模型提出了一种随机微分方程（SDE）漂移估计方法，但在该方法的理论保证方面存在缺失。

Method: 利用扩散模型理论，将漂移估计器的均方误差（MSE）分解为欧拉-丸山离散化、得分/去噪近似、噪声初始化和采样方差四个部分，推导出明确的风险界限。

Result: 证明了该漂移估计器具有明确的风险界限，揭示了离散化步长、模型近似误差及采样噪声等因素对估计性能的影响。

Conclusion: 本文填补了Tapia Costa等人（2026）提出的基于扩散模型的漂移参数估计方法的理论空白，通过明确的风险界限阐明了各超参数与误差源之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Parameter estimation in stochastic differential equations is a classical statistical problem of much importance in many scientific fields. Recent work of Tapia Costa et al. (2026) introduced a novel technique for estimating the drift when the diffusion parameter is known, using discrete samples from multiple trajectories. Their method treats drift estimation as a denoising problem, and leverages tools from (conditional) score-matching diffusion models. Although their experiments showed promising results across different drift classes, the question of theoretical guarantees for their estimator was left unanswered. In this note, we address this gap by exploiting techniques from diffusion model theory. More concretely, we derive an explicit risk bound for the time-averaged mean-squared error of said drift estimator. Our bound decomposes the risk into the (i) Euler-Maruyama discretization, (ii) score/denoiser approximation, (iii) noise initialization, and (iv) sampling variance, revealing the trade-offs between the different hyperparameters and sources of error in the estimator.

</details>


### [57] [ProbRes: Volatility Learning for Probabilistic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02117)
*Tingting Wang,Yunyi Zhang,Benyou Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 ProbRes 的后验概率校准方法，通过显式建模条件波动率来增强时间序列预测中的不确定性量化能力，有效解决了金融数据中的异方差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域需要对未来观测结果的风险和不确定性进行量化，但现有的概率预测方法在处理具有异方差性的金融时间序列时往往不够准确。

Method: 1. 采用与架构无关的两个模块分别建模条件均值和条件波动率；2. 在推理阶段，通过对归一化残差进行重采样来生成预测分布；3. 支持单变量和多变量时间序列，并能处理非高斯分布及波动率动态。

Result: 实验证明 ProbRes 在合成数据和真实世界数据集上均能准确捕捉预测分布，生成的预测区间校准效果良好，且在处理非高斯扰动时表现稳健。

Conclusion: ProbRes 是一种既具理论保障又具有普遍适用性的后验概率校准方法，能够有效处理金融时间序列中的异方差性，生成高质量的预测区间。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting has attracted increasing attention in financial applications due to the need to quantify risk and uncertainty in future observations. We propose ProbRes, a post-hoc probabilistic calibration method that explicitly learns and incorporates volatility dynamics into probabilistic forecasting, enabling effective handling of heteroskedastic data. During training, ProbRes employs two architecture-agnostic modules to separately model the conditional mean and conditional volatility. At the inference stage, it generates predictive distributions by resampling normalized residuals. ProbRes is applicable to both univariate and multivariate time series and remains robust under a wide range of error distributions, including non-Gaussian innovations with conditional heteroskedasticity. Theoretical results demonstrate ProbRes's validity and experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that ProbRes accurately captures predictive distributions and produces well-calibrated prediction intervals.

</details>


### [58] [Bayesian meta-learning for modeling Alzheimer's disease progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02228)
*Clara Hoffmann,Nadja Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对阿尔茨海默病个性化疾病进展预测的贝叶斯元学习框架，通过历史数据动态校准预测分布，在无需重训练的情况下实现了对长期病情进展的高效、稳健评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病患者病情进展存在高度个体差异，传统的统计回归或单任务神经网络模型在处理小样本个体时表现欠佳，且难以捕捉个体间的相关性。研究亟需一种能够利用历史轨迹进行个性化预测，同时具备良好泛化能力和不确定性估计的预测框架。

Method: 提出了一种贝叶斯元学习器（Bayesian meta-learner）。该模型在多个体的历史数据上进行训练，能够利用个体特定的历史轨迹记录，动态地为每个患者生成疾病得分预测分布，而无需针对每个个体进行模型重训练。同时引入了不确定性量化机制以规避过度自信预测。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上的实验结果表明，该模型不仅在预测准确性上与现有单任务模型和确定性元学习器相当，并且在长期疾病进展预测方面表现出更优的预测性能和更小的过拟合风险。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯元学习模型成功地结合了元学习和贝叶斯不确定性建模，有效地解决了阿尔茨海默病个性化进展预测中的数据稀疏和模型泛化难题，特别是在长期预测性能上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Predicting whether an individual with Alzheimer's disease will experience mild or severe disease progression is essential for personalized treatment. Typically, practitioners seek to predict the distribution of a discrete disease score, conditional on an individual's current MRI volume and their historical disease trajectory. Classical statistical regression models and single-task neural networks are not well-suited for this purpose because fitting separate models is infeasible (since each individual typically has few observations), while ignoring individual-level correlation leads to poor generalization. Meta-learning, in contrast, provides a natural avenue to dynamically predict distributions without retraining and model nonlinear relationships between the outcome and covariates. Motivated by this, we propose a Bayesian meta-learner that is trained on multiple individuals but tailors the predictive disease score distribution to each individual's historical data. Our model predicts on unseen individuals without retraining, scales linearly with the number of historical observations, and is guaranteed to be less overconfident when predicting long-term disease scores compared to its deterministic counterpart. On real-world data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) database, our model achieves performance competitive with both single-task models and deterministic meta-learners, while substantially improving performance when predicting long-term disease progression.

</details>


### [59] [Identifiable Markov Switching Models with Instantaneous Effects and Exponential Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02231)
*Roel Hulsman,Carles Balsells-Rodas,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对非平稳时间序列建模难的问题，本文提出了 FlowMSM 框架，在理论上证明了在复杂动态下隐状态和因果结构的可辨识性，并提供了实际的因果发现工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在频繁状态切换、非线性、非高斯动态以及瞬时效应的复杂时间序列中，识别隐性状态和发现因果关系具有挑战性，现有模型往往难以处理这些非平稳特性。

Method: 1. 推导非平稳时间序列下隐状态和因果结构的可辨识性理论。 2. 提出 FlowMSM 框架：通过将流模型（Flow-based models）应用于 Markov Switching Model (MSM)，使其能够与现有的因果发现方法结合，从而恢复不同状态下的因果结构。

Result: 在合成数据集和金融经济数据集上的实验结果表明，FlowMSM 能够有效检测隐性状态并从非平稳时间序列中准确发现因果结构。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了在复杂非平稳时间序列中识别隐状态和因果结构的理论基础，并提出了可扩展的 FlowMSM 框架，验证了其在检测隐状态和发现因果关系方面的有效性。

Abstract: Temporal systems often exhibit non-stationary behaviour, such as seasonal climate variation or glucose fluctuations in patients with type-1 diabetes. One way to model non-stationarity is through discrete latent regimes, i.e., stationary segments of time. Such systems induce a Markov Switching Model (MSM), a class of Hidden Markov Models with autoregressive dependencies among latent regimes and observed variables. Identifying latent regimes is challenging in the presence of frequent regime switches and nonlinear and non-Gaussian dynamics, particularly when there are instantaneous effects between the variables, e.g., due to slow rates of measurements. In this work, we establish the identifiability of both latent regimes and regime-dependent causal structures under temporal regime dependencies, nonlinear lagged and instantaneous effects, and independent noise from the exponential family. Our identifiability theory subsumes non-temporal mixtures of causal models. Furthermore, we introduce FlowMSM, a regime detection framework that can be paired with any stationary causal discovery method to recover regime-dependent causal structures. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and a financial economics dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach to detect latent regimes and discover causal structures from non-stationary time series.

</details>

*David Rundel,Fabian Fumagalli,Maximilian Muschalik,Bernd Bischl,Matthias Feurer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Shapley values are a principled attribution measure widely used in interpretable machine learning, but their exact computation scales exponentially with the number of players, motivating a wide range of approximation methods based on value function evaluations of sampled coalitions. This raises the question of whether approximation accuracy can be improved by adaptively selecting coalitions for evaluation based on previous evaluations. This is particularly relevant in settings where the value function is costly and the number of evaluations is severely limited, such as retraining-based feature importance, data valuation, and hyperparameter importance. For this purpose, we propose ShaplEIG, a Bayesian experimental design approach that approximates the expensive value function using a Gaussian process surrogate and adaptively selects coalitions based on their expected information gain about the Shapley values. By the linearity of the Shapley values in the value function, we show that the expected information gain is available in closed form. Furthermore, we propose an efficient computation scheme that reduces the complexity from exponential to polynomial in the number of players via elementary symmetric polynomials. In extensive experiments across diverse costly applications, our method consistently improves sample efficiency in the low-budget regime over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Doing well with less! On Sampling Techniques for Empirical Pairwise Loss Estimation/Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02345)
*Louise Davy,Stephan Clémençon,Charlotte Laclau*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用调查采样技术在成对损失学习任务中进行数据稀疏化处理的方法，通过对信息密集的成对数据进行有针对性的采样，实现了计算效率与模型精度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对损失函数（如相似度学习、排序、聚类）在处理大规模数据时具有二次方计算复杂度（O(n^2)），导致计算成本过高，无法在大规模场景下应用。

Method: 引入调查采样技术（Survey Sampling techniques），直接对成对数据进行采样，并根据辅助信息对信息量大的成对数据分配更高的采样概率（Inclusion probabilities）。

Result: 实验验证了该方法在计算成本显着降低的情况下，能够获得与使用全量成对数据相当的估计或优化效果。

Conclusion: 在成对损失优化中，针对“对”而非“单个观察值”进行采样，并利用辅助信息为高价值对分配更高的包含概率，是降低计算成本同时保证精度的有效且理论扎实的方法。

Abstract: Many machine learning problems, including similarity learning, ranking, and clustering, rely on empirical pairwise loss functions whose quadratic computational cost quickly becomes prohibitive at scale. We demonstrate how a frugal approach that retains only a fraction of the available information on pairs can achieve estimation or optimization performance comparable to that obtained by using all pairs, by leveraging survey sampling techniques. A central finding, supported by both theory and experiments, is that such sampling plans must target pairs directly rather than individual observations. In particular, for pairwise losses between high-dimensional vectors such as embeddings in vision or graph learning, assigning higher inclusion probabilities to informative pairs using suitable auxiliary information yields performance close to full pairwise evaluation, providing a principled and theoretically grounded trade-off between accuracy and computational cost.

</details>

### [62] [Position Paper: Post-Solve Robustness in Decision Engines: Feasible Regions and Smoothness Under Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00002)
*Yi-Xiang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对MILP优化解在实际环境下的脆弱性，本文提出了一层“后求解鲁棒性审计”，通过量化可行性邻域与决策解平滑度，为决策结果提供经求解器认证的信任度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对工业系统中MILP求解器生成的“名义最优解”在实际部署中因微小扰动导致的失效或解剧烈震荡问题，指出现有优化流水线普遍缺乏“后求解鲁棒性”审计环节。

Method: 提出了两个核心形式化对象（ε-近似最优可行邻域与决策空间解的平滑度），并综合了敏感性分析、鲁棒优化、邻居搜索、对抗性测试及基于学习的增强方法，构建了一套多维度的审计框架。

Result: 构建了一个能够为求解器的 incumbent 解提供“经求解器核实”的鲁棒性证据的框架，实现了对解的可信空间（在参数空间与决策空间）的量化评估。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统一的“后求解鲁棒性层”，主张将鲁棒性检查作为决策引擎的首要输出指标，并提出了相应的标准化报告模板与评估协议。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) decision engines routinely output nominally optimal plans for high-stakes industrial systems. Yet deployment rarely matches solve-time assumptions: small perturbations in costs, demands, or resource availability can invalidate feasibility or trigger discontinuous shifts to qualitatively different solutions. We argue that this post-solve robustness gap is a missing layer in today's optimization pipelines and a missing evaluation dimension for learning-enabled decision systems. Rather than replacing robust optimization or stochastic programming, the proposed layer audits a solved incumbent and returns solver-backed evidence about how far that solution can be trusted. We formalize two central objects: (i) an $ε$-near-optimal feasible neighborhood in parameter space, capturing when an incumbent remains feasible and near-optimal under perturbations, and (ii) solution smoothness in decision space, capturing whether nearby alternatives with small combinatorial edits remain competitive. We then synthesize the most relevant partial answers from sensitivity and stability analysis, robust optimization, neighborhood search, adversarial testing, and learning-based enhancements, and articulate an agenda for a unified post-solve robustness layer. Concretely, we call for certified inner approximations around the incumbent, probabilistic robustness estimation with calibrated uncertainty, adversarial robustness margins, and learning-based prediction and explanation aligned with solver-backed verification. We conclude with a compact reporting template and evaluation protocol that would make robustness a first-class output of decision engines.

</details>


### [63] [Emergent Collaborative Deliberation in Multi-Model AI Systems: A BFT-Derived Protocol for Epistemic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00005)
*VD Doske*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Consilium Protocol 提出了一种通过设定“认知人格”并融合金融验证框架的多模型协作系统，在大幅降低成本的同时，有效地规避了 AI 模型在对齐训练下产生的认知偏见，显著提高了推理的客观性与可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大模型往往将反馈视为错误，且存在严重的对齐训练偏见和认知盲点。为解决这些问题，本项目旨在将模型间的分歧转化为一种认知信号，从而构建一个更客观、可验证的多模型辅助决策架构。

Method: 提出了一种基于拜占庭容错（BFT）架构的多模型审议协议；通过强制设定认知人格（Cognitive Personas）将推理过程与模型本身分离；引入量化金融中的样本内外验证框架（In-Sample/Out-of-Sample Validation）来区分训练数据共识与经验性结论。

Result: 在 10 个领域的 1,478 次会话中表现出色：1. 小参数/低成本模型在特定认知人格下可达到前沿模型水平；2. 识别出 RLHF 导致的特定领域盲点（对齐偏差）；3. 协议本身无偏见（指标差异极小）；4. 在 239 项验证中达成 100% 证据检索率，并发现 167 个训练数据外的盲点；运行一致性高（标准差 $\pm$2.2%），总成本仅 217 美元。

Conclusion: Consilium Protocol 通过将模型与推理模式解耦，证明了认知人格（而非底层模型）决定了认知表现，并能有效克服 AI 的对齐偏见与盲点，实现低成本且高可重复性的多模型审议。

Abstract: We present the Consilium Protocol, a Byzantine Fault Tolerance-derived architecture for structured multi-model AI deliberation that treats inter-model disagreement as epistemic signal rather than error. The protocol assigns engineered cognitive personas to language models -- separating what a model is from how it reasons -- and introduces an In-Sample/Out-of-Sample validation framework adapted from quantitative finance to distinguish training-data consensus from empirically grounded conclusions. Across 1,478 deliberation sessions spanning 32 topics in 10 domain categories, we demonstrate that (1) the cognitive persona, not the underlying model, determines epistemic behavior: free edge-inference models costing 0.0002 USD per batch produced comparable analytical output to frontier models costing 10.69 USD; (2) RLHF alignment training creates measurable, domain-specific epistemic blind spots -- contested policy topics exhibit 12.3 percentage points less adversarial challenge than settled science topics, and AI safety topics show asymmetric bias ($Δ$=11.6%) where models challenge claims that AI is dangerous far more vigorously than claims that AI risk is overstated; (3) the protocol exhibits no directional bias of its own (immigration $Δ$=2.3%, renewables $Δ$=1.2%); and (4) out-of-sample evidence retrieval validated 239 claims with 100% evidence retrieval and surfaced 167 blind-spot discoveries invisible to training-data deliberation. Run-to-run reproducibility across randomized model$\times$persona assignments averages $\pm$2.2% standard deviation. Total cost for the complete battery including all overhead: 217 USD. We release the protocol specification under MIT license to enable independent verification.

</details>


### [64] [Deliberative Curation: A Protocol for Multi-Agent Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00007)
*Steven Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI代理协作治理的局限性，本文提出了一种包含生命周期管理、声誉加权投票和阶梯式制裁的审议方案，并通过仿真证明其在恶劣环境下较传统多数投票具有更佳的抗压性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理从孤立工具向协作参与者演变，传统的基于人类的平台治理机制（受阻于无状态性、同质性和谄媚问题）不再适用，亟需针对AI群体的治理方案。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个层级的审议式治理议定书：1. 将知识工件生命周期形式化为标记转换系统；2. 使用集成Beta信誉和EigenTrust增强的声誉加权审议投票；3. 为无状态代理设计的阶梯式制裁机制。通过基于100个代理的仿真模拟进行评估。

Result: 在不同对抗场景下表现优异，对比多数投票法，在中度对抗下精度从0.791提升至0.826，在压力场景下从0.740提升至0.807；协议性能衰减速度比多数投票法慢约三倍；消融分析显示隐蔽投票机制（commit-reveal）贡献最大。

Conclusion: 该议定书在面对AI代理群体协作的挑战时表现出显著的鲁棒性，比传统的多数投票法更具弹性，尽管在良性环境下的精度略有妥协。此外，隐蔽投票机制（commit-reveal）被证明是提升性能的最关键组件。

Abstract: As AI agents transition from isolated tools to collaborative participants in shared knowledge ecosystems, governing collective knowledge curation becomes a critical challenge. Human platform governance mechanisms do not transfer directly: agent statelessness undermines deterrence-based sanctions, model homogeneity violates independence assumptions underlying crowd wisdom, and sycophancy collapses deliberative consensus.
  We propose a deliberative curation protocol combining three governance layers: (1) a knowledge artifact lifecycle formalized as a labeled transition system; (2) reputation-weighted deliberative voting integrating Beta Reputation with EigenTrust amplification; and (3) graduated sanctions adapted for stateless agents, including broken agent handling distinguishing malfunction from adversarial behavior.
  We evaluate the protocol through agent-based simulation with 100 agents across seven behavioral archetypes under two adversity scenarios (30 seeds, paired t-tests). The protocol trades modest precision under benign conditions for substantially better resilience under adversity: 0.826 vs 0.791 for majority vote under moderate adversity (p<0.001), widening to 0.807 vs 0.740 under stress (p<0.001). The protocol degrades roughly three times more slowly than majority vote. Ablation analysis identifies commit-reveal vote concealment as the most impactful single component (8.2-8.6pp precision improvement, p<0.001), outperforming reputation weighting and deliberation combined. Graduated sanctions were not exercised in simulation and remain empirically unvalidated.

</details>


### [65] [Agents on a Tree: Pathwise Coordination for Multi-Objective Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00008)
*Jia Zhang,Tengfei Ma,Tianle Li,Daojian Zeng,Xieping Gao,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATOM框架通过树状结构的多智能体协作，实现了分子优化过程中多策略并行搜索与复杂权衡，显著提升了多目标优化的性能与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标分子优化方法通常依赖单一策略或固定标量化，难以有效探索多样的权衡方案，且在面对长程依赖时往往受限于早期决策。

Method: 提出一种名为ATOM的多智能体框架，将分子优化建模为树状搜索过程。每个节点配置专门针对特定目标或决策上下文的智能体，通过路径协作而非全局共识来维护多条演化轨迹，并结合全局记忆机制来平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在包含活性、合成可行性和ADMET属性的基准测试中，ATOM在帕累托前沿覆盖率和超体积（Hypervolume）指标上均优于现有的强基线模型。

Conclusion: ATOM框架通过树状结构的多智能体协作，有效解决了多目标分子优化中目标冲突和长程依赖问题，在提升帕累托前沿覆盖率和超体积方面表现优异。

Abstract: Multi-objective molecular optimization requires searching vast chemical spaces under conflicting objectives, where early design decisions strongly constrain downstream outcomes. Existing methods typically rely on a single policy or fixed scalarization, which limits their ability to represent diverse trade-offs and to explore multiple promising design trajectories. We propose ATOM, a multi-agent framework that formulates molecular optimization as a tree-structured search. Each node corresponds to an atomic operation and hosts an agent specialized for a particular objective or decision context. Agents coordinate along different paths of the tree rather than enforcing a global consensus, enabling the method to maintain and compare alternative molecular evolution trajectories. A global memory of past optimization behaviors further supports balanced exploration and exploitation across objectives. This tree-structured interaction enables reasoning over long-horizon dependencies inherent in molecular design. Experiments on challenging multi-objective benchmarks involving activity, synthesizability, and ADMET-related properties show that ATOM consistently achieves improved Pareto coverage and hypervolume over strong baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pathwise multi-agent coordination for molecular optimization. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ATOM-41CE.

</details>


### [66] [Optimal Transport-based Permutation-Invariant Bayesian Optimization of Offshore Wind Farm Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00009)
*Antonio Candelieri,Laurens Bliek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对具有置换不变性的优化问题，本文提出了一种基于最优传输理论的 PIBO 方法，有效提升了优化效果并大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）无法利用目标函数中存在的对称性（如布局优化中点的顺序不影响结果），导致计算冗余且效率低下。现有的 BO 方法在处理这类“布局优化”问题时存在局限。

Method: 提出了一种基于最优传输理论（Optimal Transport Theory）的置换不变贝叶斯优化方法（Permutation-Invariant BO, PIBO）。

Result: 相比传统的贝叶斯优化，PIBO 方法能够获得更好的风电场布局方案，并将计算时间缩短了约 50%。

Conclusion: PIBO 是一种有效的解决方法，能够在利用问题对称性的同时，显著提升优化效率，特别是在类似海上风电场布局等具有置换不变性特征的复杂工程问题中，表现优于传统贝叶斯优化。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely and successfully adopted for solving optimization problems having an expensive-to-evaluate, black-box, and non-convex objective function. However, the vanilla BO algorithm is not able to exploit possible symmetries characterizing the target problem. An intuitive case is given by optimal location problems, whose decision variables refer to a finite set of points within a continuous space, with the order of points not affecting the value of the objective function. We refer to this setting as optimization over layouts to distinguish from optimization over point-clouds where, instead, the order of points counts. As an instance of optimization over layouts we consider a real-life industrial-relevant application, that is the optimization of the layout of an offshore wind farm: given identical wind turbines, switching any pair of them has not any effect on the annual energy production. Based on Optimal Transport theory, we propose a Permutation-Invariant BO approach, namely PIBO, proved to provide better wind farm layouts when compared to the vanilla BO approach while cutting computation time roughly in half.

</details>


### [67] [Can AI Review Improve Paper Drafting? An Empirical Study on 20 Computer Architecture Submissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01013)
*Di Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用AI辅助论文撰写而非替代同行评审的可行性，开发了开源工具并证实AI在提升论文质量方面具有显著潜力与一定局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI生成的论文激增导致的同行评审压力，以及AI在评审中应用的伦理争议，作者提出一个更务实的问题：能否利用AI来辅助论文撰写（而非替代同行评审）？

Method: 构建了一个名为“AI-Paper-Review”的Web工具，通过多个AI评审员生成结构化评论；定义了一套指标用于量化AI评审与人类评审的一致性，并针对20篇计算机体系结构论文进行了案例研究，将AI评论与人类评论进行对齐分析。

Result: AI评审能覆盖人类评审提出的大部分问题，同时还能发现人类评审中缺失的潜在问题。研究证明了AI作为撰写辅助工具的高效性。

Conclusion: AI评审在辅助论文撰写方面具有潜力，能够覆盖人类评审的大部分问题并发现人类评审未察觉的问题，但其在正式同行评审中的应用因伦理问题仍需谨慎。该研究建议利用AI改进初稿，而非代替同行评审。

Abstract: Research is advancing faster than ever with artificial intelligence (AI); and so are the corresponding research papers. The exploding volume of AI-generated papers have put a strain to peer review, leading to the usage of AI-generated review, potentially wide yet sneaky. However, relevant ethical concerns about confidentiality, quality, and fairness are raised and no consensus has been reached in the broad research community. We expect the debate to continue for a while, but in the meantime, we ask an alternative, practical question: \textit{can AI review improve paper drafting?}
  We study 20 computer architecture papers, with varying levels of submission lineage, to expose how well AI review aligns with human review, quantified by a set of metrics we define. To conduct the case study, we build a web UI-integrated tool, \emph{AI-Paper-Review}, that generates structured AI review of a draft paper, available at https://github.com/unarylab/ai-paper-review. This tool selects several AI reviewers from a diverse pool of AI reviewers and clusters and ranks their comments based on commonality and importance of review comments. It also allows to align AI comments with human comments to facilitate metric-based validation. The case study shows that AI review can cover a significant fraction of human-raised issues, but also raises issues missing in human review.
  This paper is not intended to encourage using AI for peer review at the current stage, but to study that (1) how AI review can improve paper drafting and (2) the potential and limitation of AI-based peer review. The release of the tool and the case study data is intended to instigate future research on this topic. Misuse for peer review would violate the ethics policies from major academic venues.

</details>


### [68] [MindGames Arena Generalization Track: In2AI Solution with Delayed Per-Step Reward Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00017)
*Aliaksei Korshuk,Alexander Buyantuev,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为多智能体强化学习设计的奖励归因框架，通过延迟奖励计算和资格门控，解决了复杂交互中的信用分配问题，显著提升了模型在策略游戏中的训练效率与竞技水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习假设奖励可按步骤分配，但在多智能体策略交互中，行动结果高度依赖于未来的、不可预测的事件或他人的决策，导致奖励分配失效。

Method: 提出了“延迟逐步奖励归因与资格门控”（delayed per-step reward attribution with eligibility gating）机制；结合vLLM的异步滚转生成、基于课程的对手采样（curriculum-based opponent sampling）及多层分层批处理技术。

Result: 在MindGames Arena基准测试中，基于该方法训练的8B模型在Open与Efficient赛道均获得第一名，表现优于GPT-5等大模型。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了多智能体策略交互中奖励分配困难的问题，使单一8B参数模型在性能上超越了GPT-5等更大规模模型，并在MindGames Arena基准测试中获得双料第一。

Abstract: Training language model agents for multi-agent strategic interaction presents a core difficulty: the quality of any action may depend on future events that never materialize, on moves that violate game rules, or on decisions made by other players. Standard reinforcement learning assumes that rewards can be assigned at each step, but this assumption fails in settings where outcomes are entangled across time and agents. We introduce delayed per-step reward attribution with eligibility gating, an episode lifecycle and postprocessing pipeline that computes rewards only at episode end, propagates them back to originating steps according to task-specific semantics, and excludes steps that lack valid dependent information from training. Together with asynchronous rollout generation via vLLM's continuous batching, curriculum-based opponent sampling, and multi-level stratified batch construction, this approach enables stable, sample-efficient RL training in multi-agent environments. We evaluate on the MindGames Arena benchmark at NeurIPS 2025, where a single 8-billion-parameter open-source model trained with our method matched or surpassed substantially larger proprietary systems, including GPT-5, in head-to-head play and took first place in both the Open (unrestricted) and Efficient (<=8B parameters) tracks.

</details>


### [69] [Grokers: Bottom-Up Inductive Comprehension and Write-Time Intelligence over Typed Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00050)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Grokers通过将知识理解任务从“查询时”转移到“写入时”，利用归纳式依赖图遍历构建持久化知识，在保证结构化理解的同时，实现了近乎零推理成本的查询效率及高缓存命中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决RAG（检索增强生成）在每次查询时均需支付高昂计算成本的问题，通过在写入时预先构建持久化、结构化的知识理解，实现查询阶段的零额外LM成本。

Method: 提出Grokers架构，采用“自下而上”的归纳式遍历方法解析依赖子图；通过自主Groker代理对节点进行结构化属性提取，并利用受控语言模型调用将其持久化；引入字节一致性缓存、单调积累增长协议及双向遍历排序定理。

Result: 在该架构下，KV缓存命中率接近100%；随着交互增加，无需LM调用的解析比例保持非递减；提供了一种确定性的语义检索替代方案，并在有限词汇域内实现LM回退率收敛至零。

Conclusion: Grokers架构通过将智能处理前置到写入阶段，实现了知识图谱的持久化理解；其证明了系统在缓存命中率、解析增量积累以及遍历遍历正确性方面的理论保障，为构建高效、确定性的知识理解系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present Grokers, an architecture for building persistent, structured comprehension of typed knowledge graphs through bottom-up inductive traversal of dependency subgraphs. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which pays full comprehension cost at every query, Grokers pushes intelligence to write time: autonomous Groker agents analyze nodes in a typed stream graph, extract structured attributes via governed language model (LM) calls, and inductively compose that understanding upward through dependency relations, writing enriched typed attributes that serve all future queries at zero additional LM cost. We prove three formal properties: (1) the Byte-Identity Theorem, establishing that context blocks assembled from a transactionally-maintained denormalization index are byte-identical across LM turns between semantic changes, enabling KV-cache hit rates approaching 100%; (2) the Accumulation Monotonicity Theorem, establishing that the fraction of interactions resolved without LM calls is non-decreasing in the number of completed interactions under a governed wisdom library growth protocol; and (3) the Dual-Traversal Ordering Theorem, establishing that top-down generation and bottom-up comprehension are the unique correct traversal orderings for their respective tasks over a dependency DAG, and that their composition closes into a complete generation-comprehension cycle. We further present a deterministic alternative to embedding-based semantic search, with a synonym caching protocol whose LM fallback rate converges to zero for finite-vocabulary domains. A reference implementation is provided in the open-source Qbix / Safebox / Safebots stack.

</details>


### [70] [Universal Quantum Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00045)
*Sungyong Chung,Alireza Talebpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 UQT 架构，利用量子力学特性避开了经典神经网络在处理离散逻辑时的不稳定性，实现了比“grokking（顿悟）”更进一步的“结晶化”精确推理，且具备计算效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续空间神经网络在大规模参数下处理离散数学（如模运算和非交换代数）时存在随机不稳定性，且依赖于过度参数化，难以实现完美的数学推理。

Method: 提出通用量子 Transformer (UQT)，利用参数化几何相位嵌入和 $SU(2)$ 波干涉，替代了经典神经网络的注意力机制，并在 5 量子比特的 NISQ 硬件上进行了验证。

Result: UQT 在处理 $\mathbb{Z}_{11}$ 模运算和 $S_4$ 置换群时实现了数学上的精确性（结晶化），摆脱了经典注意力机制的二次计算瓶颈，并实现了参数空间的对数级压缩。

Conclusion: UQT 利用量子拓扑作为一种优于经典架构的物理基底，实现了对离散数学和非交换代数的精确推理，为 AI 架构提供了一种高效且确定的替代方案（“结晶”现象）。

Abstract: Classical continuous-space neural networks fundamentally struggle to lock into exact mathematical symmetries, such as modular arithmetic and non-commutative algebra. To approximate these discrete logical rules, they often rely on massive parameter scaling, resulting in stochastic instability even after delayed generalization phenomena known as grokking. Here, we introduce the Universal Quantum Transformer (UQT), a fundamentally novel, quantum-native computing architecture that uses the physical properties of multi-qubit systems as a universal inductive bias for exact mathematical and algebraic reasoning. Rather than translating classical neural mechanisms, our framework relies entirely on parameterized geometric phase embedding and $SU(2)$ wave-interference. We demonstrate that the quantum attention circuit, operating on a highly compact 5-qubit substrate, perfectly learns two highly distinct formal classes: cyclic modular arithmetic ($\mathbb{Z}_{11}$) and non-Abelian algebra (the $S_4$ permutation group). While classical attention-based networks exhibit stochastic instability at convergence, the UQT achieves mathematically exact, deterministic generalization. We refer to this phenomenon as crystallization: a step beyond the well-known phenomenon of grokking. Crucially, this framework yields massive computational and memory advantages by theoretically bypassing the quadratic bottleneck of classical self-attention, and by logarithmically compressing the required representation dimension to eliminate the massive over-parameterization inherent to classical networks. Finally, we deploy this architecture on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware, proving its viability on current IBM Quantum computers. These results establish parameterized quantum topology as a universally superior physical substrate for exact artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [71] [Product-Aware Deep Autoencoders for Robust Process Monitoring in Multi-Product Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00052)
*MD Shafikul Islam,Jordan Carden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统通用工业异常检测模型因多产品模式导致决策盲点的问题，本文提出了一种产品感知自编码器，通过模式划分显著提升了对复杂攻击的识别鲁棒性，在压力测试中表现出比全局模型更高的异常检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0中，现有通用型（Product-agnostic）异常检测模型在处理多种产品等级时，为容纳不同模式的差异而过度扩大决策边界，产生“盲点”，导致微小的异常或针对性攻击难以被识别。

Method: 提出“产品感知自编码器（Product-Aware Autoencoder）”，通过限制学习域至特定产品等级的分布来替代通用的全局模型。使用扩展田纳西-伊斯曼过程（TEP）基准进行对比实验，并引入特定的攻击模拟场景进行压力测试。

Result: 在标准检测指标上，产品感知模型与全局基准性能相当；但在压力测试中，全局模型在77.8%的场景下失效，而产品感知模型保持了100%的检测准确率。

Conclusion: 在柔性制造环境中，通用的异常检测模型（Global-Agnostic）存在显著的安全风险。研究表明，采用“产品感知（Product-Aware）”的架构能够显著缩小模型决策边界，消除因多模式带来的盲点，从而大幅提升对细微异常及针对性攻击的检测能力。研究主张工业系统的异常诊断架构应向感知模式（Mode-aware）的方向转型。

Abstract: As Industry 4.0 accelerates the integration of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) in manufacturing, robust anomaly detection has become critical for ensuring process safety and security. Current data-driven approaches typically employ "product-agnostic" or global models trained on the aggregate of all normal operating data. However, modern industrial facilities frequently operate under diverse product grades. While computationally simple, these global models inherently expand their decision boundaries to accommodate the variance of multiple modes, creating a "blind spot" where subtle anomalies or targeted cyber-physical attacks may be masked by the wide acceptance region of the model. In this work, we first demonstrate that the vulnerability described above is present in global-agnostic models operating across multiple product grades. We then present a Product-Aware Autoencoder as a principled mitigation that restricts the learning domain to grade-specific distributions. While this approach reduces the identified blind-spot risk, we do not claim it as the optimal mitigation among all possible alternatives. We rigorously validate this approach against a Global Agnostic baseline using the Extended Tennessee Eastman Process (TEP) benchmark. Our empirical results indicate that the Product-Aware framework performs comparably to the global baseline on standard detection metrics, while offering improved robustness to product-grade-specific operating modes. Most critically, stress tests simulating our hypothetical attack scenarios reveal that while the global model fails to detect operational deviations in 77.8% of the scenarios, the product-aware system achieves 100% detection accuracy. These findings suggest that, in flexible manufacturing environments, generalized anomaly detectors can pose non-trivial security risks, motivating a shift toward mode-aware diagnostic architectures.

</details>


### [72] [On the evolution of the concept of probability as a mirror of the evolution of reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00102)
*Jean-Louis Le Mouël,Vincent Courtillot,Dominique Gibert,Vladimir Kossobokov,Jean-Baptiste Boulé,Pierpaolo Zuddas,Fernando Lopes,Païkan Marccagi,Alexis Maineult*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过回顾概率论的思想史，指出概率推理虽是处理不确定性的核心，但存在无法描述概念模糊性的局限；提出需通过结合模糊逻辑和深度学习来完整构建现代科学的理性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨理性本身随概率论发展而产生的演变，并尝试解决现有概率框架在处理概念模糊性方面的局限，以界定不同计算范式在科学推理中的边界。

Method: 回顾概率论演变史；对比分析概率论（逻辑推断）、模糊逻辑（语义建模）与深度学习（几何插值与优化）的认知框架及其局限性。

Result: 梳理了从古典组合学到贝叶斯推断的概率演进；指出概率擅长处理定义明确命题的不确定性，而模糊逻辑则在处理语义模糊性方面更具优势，深度学习则在预测能力上提供了一种非传统推理的优化范式。

Conclusion: 现代科学理性不能仅归结为数据驱动的效果，而必须包含对不确定性、模糊性与推理过程的显性表述。概率论、模糊逻辑与深度学习应被视为互补的认知工具。

Abstract: Over the centuries, probability theory has grown from the calculus of games of chance into a central framework for reasoning under uncertainty. This article interprets that evolution not merely as a mathematical history, but as a transformation of rationality itself. From Pascal and Fermat's combinatorial symmetry to the inductive logic of Bayes and Laplace, from Poisson's statistics of events to Kolmogorov's axiomatic formalization, probability progressively incorporated uncertainty, time, and coherence into scientific judgment. This trajectory reaches a mature epistemological form in modern Bayesian inference, especially in Tarantola's view of probability as a logic of information, where prior knowledge and data are combined coherently. Yet this framework also exposes a limit: probability quantifies uncertainty about well-defined propositions, but does not by itself formalize the vagueness of the concepts used to describe them. The article therefore examines how rationality extends beyond probability. Fuzzy logic is presented as a rigorous language for graded meaning and qualitative judgment, while deep learning is analyzed as a distinct, powerful mode of prediction based on geometric interpolation and optimization rather than explicit inference. By situating probability, fuzzy logic, and deep learning in a common historical and epistemological perspective, the article clarifies their roles and limits. It argues that contemporary scientific rationality cannot be reduced to data-driven performance alone, but requires the explicit articulation of uncertainty, vagueness, and inference.

</details>


### [73] [Evaluating Interactive Reasoning in Large Language Models: A Hierarchical Benchmark with Executable Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00103)
*Mingyuan Fan,Weiguang Han,Daixin Wang,Cen Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 定义了一种基于多轮交互的推理评估框架，要求大模型主动获取信息并更新信念，并通过474个游戏测试，揭示了模型在复杂推理、环境鲁棒性及元认知能力方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理评估往往依赖于单轮、静态的推理任务，难以全面反映现实世界中动态的、主动的证据获取与信念更新过程，且缺乏对模型元认知能力和环境鲁棒性的深入考量。

Method: 提出一个包含474个可执行游戏的多轮交互推理评估框架。在该框架下，LLM仅获得任务规则，需主动向隐藏环境发起查询、整合观察、动态更新信念，并决定何时终止决策提交答案。评价维度涵盖成功率、交互效率，以及包含上下文扰动、反事实修正和必要性判断的元认知评估。

Result: 实验表明该基准具有很高的区分度，不仅揭示了模型间成功率的差异，还突显了交互效率的不同。模型在应对环境上下文扰动时表现出中等程度的稳健性下降，但在进行反事实修正和必要性判断任务时，表现出现显著下滑。

Conclusion: 现有的前沿大模型在多轮交互推理任务中表现出显著差异。虽然模型在基础成功率上有所提升，但在面对环境扰乱时的抗干扰能力，以及进行反事实修正和必要性判断等元认知能力方面仍存在明显短板。该基准为未来评估和提升大模型的推理深度与元认知能力提供了重要的评价维度。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-turn interactive framework for reasoning evaluation that treats reasoning as active evidence acquisition and belief updating. Wherein, LLMs receive only the task rules, must issue targeted queries to a hidden environment, integrate partial observations over time, and decide when to submit a final answer. Beyond standard success rate and interaction efficiency, we evaluate contextual robustness under controlled contextual perturbations, and metacognitive adaptation through counterfactual revision and necessity judgment. We instantiate the framework as a benchmark of 474 executable games, each evaluated under five fixed configuration search spaces corresponding to five difficulty levels, and evaluate a broad set of frontier LLMs. Results show that the benchmark is highly discriminative, exposing large differences not only in success rate but also in interaction efficiency. Moreover, we empirically show that contextual perturbations cause moderate but consistent declines, whereas counterfactual revision and necessity judgment lead to much larger drops.

</details>


### [74] [A Multi-AI-agent Framework Enabling End-to-end Finite Element Analysis for Solid Mechanics Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00138)
*Titu Ranjan Sarker,Muhammed Jawaad Zulqernine,Ling Yue,Shaowu Pan,Chenxi Wang,Shiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AbaqusAgent 是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，旨在自动化执行 Abaqus 有限元分析，显著降低了固体力学仿真的使用门槛，并实现了 86% 的问题解决成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）存在学习曲线陡峭、初学者易出现配置错误等问题，通常需要多年工程经验才能准确进行仿真，旨在降低 FEA 使用门槛并提高仿真效率。

Method: 提出了名为 AbaqusAgent 的多智能体框架，该框架集成了六个专业智能体（解释器、架构师、输入编写器、执行器、审核员和可视化器），将自然语言指令转化为 Abaqus 可执行的 FEA 流程与可视化结果。

Result: 成功对 50 个固体力学问题进行了验证，取得了 86% 的整体成功率，实现了从自然语言输入到仿真结果输出的全流程自动化。

Conclusion: AbaqusAgent 通过多智能体协作降低了有限元分析（FEA）的技术门槛，提升了分析效率，并为人工智能辅助工程设计和材料表征开辟了新途径，展示了 LLM 在复杂工程仿真领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Finite element analysis (FEA) is the most important numerical approach for solid mechanics. Challenges of FEA include a steep learning curve for entry-level users and potential false simulations due to incorrect definitions of key simulation components, such as boundary conditions, load cases, and solution variables. Years of engineering experience are usually necessary for real-world problem-solving. To address these issues, we present AbaqusAgent, a multi-agent framework grounded in large language models (LLMs) for solid mechanics analyses. AbaqusAgent is developed to facilitate analysis case generation and execution using Abaqus, one of the most widely used FEA packages, by turning users' natural-language instructions into executed FEA analyses and result visualization. AbaqusAgent is composed of six agents, including interpreter, architect, input writer, runner, reviewer, and visualizer agents, encompassing all the essential pre-processing and post-processing steps of standard FEA analyses. A wide variety of 50 solid mechanics problems have been successfully validated, achieving an overall success rate of 86%. Beyond improving the efficiency of FEA for solid mechanics problems and lowering the barrier to computational mechanics education, AbaqusAgent advances the human-simulation interaction paradigm and enables integration with AI-empowered optimization and material characterization workflows. The code is available at https://github.com/LIRAM-LIN/AbaqusAgent

</details>


### [75] [CAST: Non-Privileged Clipped Asymmetric Self-Teaching with Advantage Flipping for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00172)
*Yang Li,Gongle Xue,Yijia Guo,Yuheng Yuan,Liwen Hu,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST是一种用于改进大模型推理训练的自蒸馏方法，它在GRPO架构中引入了答案无关的令牌级优势塑造，有效缓解了传统RLVR奖励稀疏及梯度消失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLVR（如GRPO）存在奖励稀疏且在样本全对或全错时梯度消失的问题；现有的自蒸馏方法（如OPSD）虽然提供密集监督，但其令牌偏好未必与最终正确性对齐。需要一种既能保持验证器导向，又能提供密集且对齐的指令的方法。

Method: 提出CAST方法：1. 在GRPO框架内引入止梯度自教师；2. 根据轨迹正确性动态调整令牌级优势（优势符号反转）；3. 即使在全对或全错分组中，通过符号约束的基准优势提供反馈。该方法不需要参考解，实现了答案无关的自蒸馏。

Result: CAST在数学推理任务中改进了RLVR训练过程，克服了传统方法无法在样本一致组别中获取有效反馈的限制。

Conclusion: CAST通过引入答案无关的自蒸馏机制，有效解决了GRPO在稀疏奖励和“全对/全错”分组下的梯度失效问题，在数学推理任务中显著提升了RLVR的训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), especially Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has been widely used to improve reasoning in large language models. However, outcome-level rewards provide only sparse supervision, and group-relative advantages vanish when all sampled trajectories for a prompt are either correct or incorrect. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) offers dense token-level guidance, but its token preferences are not necessarily aligned with trajectory correctness; empirical diagnostics show that OPSD signals behave differently on correct and incorrect rollouts, with teacher-positive and teacher-negative gap signals exhibiting different noise profiles. These diagnostics are conducted under an OPSD-style privileged teacher context for analysis only, whereas CAST training uses answer-free self-teacher scoring.Motivated by these observations, this work proposes CAST, an answer-free self-distillation method for GRPO-style RLVR. CAST keeps the verifier-grounded GRPO objective, but uses a stop-gradient self-teacher to shape token-level advantages according to trajectory correctness. Unlike prior self-distilled RLVR methods, CAST does not require reference-solution-conditioned teacher scoring, keeps the self-teacher log-probability gap active throughout training, and applies bidirectional local advantage sign reversal: teacher-negative tokens in correct trajectories can receive negative token-level advantages, while teacher-positive tokens in incorrect trajectories can receive bounded positive local advantages. For zero-variance all-correct and all-wrong groups, CAST assigns bounded sign-constrained base advantages, so these otherwise zero-gradient groups can contribute verifier-signed token feedback. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that CAST improves RLVR training while retaining a lightweight, verifier-grounded trajectory-level objective.

</details>


### [76] [TIGER: Traceable Inference with Graph-Based Evidence Routing for Mitigating Hallucinations in Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00232)
*Kaixiang Zhao,Tianrun Yu,Shawn Huang,Porter Jenkins,Yushun Dong,Amanda Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIGER 是一种 inference-time 多模态事实修复框架，通过对比“观察图”与“声明图”进行细粒度风险评分，在不修改模型参数的前提下，有效纠正生成内容中的事实幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时修复方法常因输出中的幻觉污染输入理解，且缺乏对细粒度事实级别反馈的精确控制和排序能力。

Method: 提出 TIGER 框架，通过从输入中独立提取“观察图”和从输出中提取“声明图”，构建事实对比。使用基于图的风险评分判定事实冲突，仅对高风险部分进行针对性修复，无需微调模型骨干。

Result: 实验证明 TIGER 在图像到文本、音视频到文本等四种跨模态路径上显著降低了幻觉，提升了 groundedness，且具备理论上的几何级风险收敛保证。同时在 CrisisFACTS 任务中证实了其在多源信息环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: TIGER 通过基于图的风险评估方法，在保持模型骨干不变的情况下，有效地减少了多模态生成中的事实幻觉，并实现了可量化的收敛保证。该方法在多种跨模态任务中表现出鲁棒性，是提升多模态模型事实真实性的有效手段。

Abstract: We study fact-level repair for multimodal generation, where a fluent output may contain specific facts that are not supported by the input. Existing inference-time repair methods often generate feedback by jointly conditioning on the input and the current output. This design has two limitations: hallucinated claims in the output can bias the model's interpretation of the input, and free-form feedback cannot be ranked or scheduled at the fact level. We present TIGER, an inference-time framework that redesigns feedback for localized repair. TIGER independently extracts an observation graph from the input and a claim graph from the current output, then assigns each claim a graph-conditioned risk score based on support and conflict. The model repairs selected high-risk claims while keeping the backbone frozen. We provide a convergence analysis showing that the expected total risk decreases geometrically to an explicit asymptotic bound under mild assumptions. Experiments across four cross-modal paths, including image-to-text, image+text-to-text, audio-to-text, and video-to-text, show that TIGER reduces unsupported content while preserving task quality. The gains hold across multiple backbones, and a CrisisFACTS case study suggests that the same repair mechanism can improve grounding in multi-source settings.

</details>


### [77] [MindZero: Learning Online Mental Reasoning With Zero Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00240)
*Shunchi Zhang,Jin Lu,Chuanyang Jin,Yichao Zhou,Zhining Zhang,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindZero 是一种新型自监督框架，它使 MLLM 能够无需人工标注，自动学习人类心理理论（ToM）推理能力，通过将复杂思维过程“内化”为实时的高效推理，弥补了传统 LLM 与模型驱动方法在性能与效率上的短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 AI 在实际辅助中面临的心理理论（ToM）挑战：1) 需对多种心理假设进行在线且稳健的不确定性更新；2) 需具备支持实时辅助的高效推理能力；3) 克服真实世界中缺乏心智状态标注数据的限制。

Method: 提出 MindZero 框架：结合自监督强化学习与多模态大语言模型（MLLM）。训练阶段通过奖励机制引导模型生成能解释人类行为的心理状态假设（借用任务规划器估算行动概率），从而实现无标注学习；推理阶段将模型驱动的复杂推理转化为高效的单次传递（single-pass）推理。

Result: 在网格世界和家庭任务场景的实验中，MindZero 显著超越了单纯的 LLM 及其它模型驱动方法。在保持高准确性的同时，极大提升了推理速度，解决了模型驱动方法计算开销大及实时性差的问题。

Conclusion: MindZero 展示了心理推理可以作为一种有效的自监督技能进行习得，不仅消除了对标注数据的依赖，还在推理准确性和效率上显著优于现有的模型驱动方法，实现了实时交互需求。

Abstract: Effective real-world assistance requires AI agents with robust Theory of Mind (ToM): inferring human mental states from their behavior. Despite recent advances, several key challenges remain, including (1) online inference with robust uncertainty updates over multiple hypotheses; (2) efficient reasoning suitable for real-time assistance; and (3) the lack of ground-truth mental state annotations in real-world domains. We address these challenges by introducing MindZero, a self-supervised reinforcement learning framework that trains multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for efficient and robust online mental reasoning. During training, the model is rewarded for generating mental state hypotheses that maximize the likelihood of observed actions estimated by a planner, similar to model-based ToM reasoning. This method thus eliminates the need for explicit mental state annotations. After training, MindZero internalizes model-based reasoning into fast single-pass inference. We evaluate MindZero against baselines across challenging mental reasoning and AI assistance tasks in gridworld and household domains. We found that LLMs alone are insufficient; model-based methods improve accuracy but are slow, costly, and limited by backbone MLLM capacity. In contrast, MindZero enhances MLLMs' intrinsic ToM ability and significantly outperforms model-based methods in both accuracy and efficiency, showing that mental reasoning can be effectively learned as a self-supervised skill.

</details>


### [78] [Geodesic Flow Matching for Denoising High-Dimensional Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00248)
*Karim Habashy,Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对高维环面上的空间语义指针（SSP），本文提出测地线流匹配方法，替代了错误的欧几里得空间假设，显著提升了神经SLAM系统的追踪精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的流匹配假设空间为平坦的欧几里得几何，这导致无法正确处理空间语义指针（SSP）在环面流形上的几何约束，致使去噪过程破坏了编码信息。

Method: 提出了“测地线流匹配”（Geodesic Flow Matching），将传统的欧几里得空间去噪流映射到里曼流形（环面）上，从而避免了欧几里得插值对SSP相位和幅度结构的破坏。

Result: 在脉冲神经SLAM系统中，该方法将追踪误差降低了72%，并将神经效率提升了40%。

Conclusion: 通过几何感知的方法（测地线流匹配）解决SSP在高维环面流形上的去噪问题，可显著提升神经符号推理的稳定性与效率。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Algebras (VSAs) enable robust neurosymbolic reasoning by encoding symbolic information into high-dimensional distributed representations. For continuous domains, Spatial Semantic Pointers (SSPs) extend this framework by mapping variables onto continuous toroidal manifolds. However, standard approaches like Flow Matching assume a flat Euclidean geometry, which fails to account for the geometric constraints imposed on valid SSP states. We demonstrate that this assumption fails for SSPs: Euclidean linear interpolants ``cut through" the manifold's interior, destroying the phase and magnitude structure required for accurate decoding. To resolve this, we employ Geodesic Flow Matching, adapting Riemannian transport dynamics to strictly restrict the denoising flow to the SSP toroidal manifold. We validate this approach in a Spiking Neural SLAM system, showing that manifold-aware cleanup stabilizes path integration against drift. The method achieves a 72\% reduction in tracking error and enables a 40\% increase in neural efficiency compared to competitive baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/kremHabashy/CleanupSSP .

</details>


### [79] [Capability Self-Assessment: Teaching LLMs to Know Their Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00251)
*Haoyan Yang,Reza Shirkavand,Yukai Jin,Jiawei Zhou,Shangqian Gao,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型因“过度自信”导致的可靠性问题，本研究提出通过强化学习训练模型进行“能力自我评估（CSA）”，从而有效识别任务的难易度，实现任务的精准分流与数据优化，且该能力具有良好的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型普遍缺乏识别自身能力边界的功能，往往会盲目尝试无法解决的任务，导致可靠性降低。研究旨在解决这种“过度自信”问题，在不损害模型原有性能的前提下，提升模型的自我评估能力。

Method: 将能力自我评估（CSA）建模为策略学习问题。对比评估了有监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）两种训练范式，并验证了CSA在分布外（OOD）环境下的泛化能力及其在实际系统（如任务分流和数据筛选）中的应用价值。

Result: 研究表明，当前大模型存在系统性的能力认识偏差；利用强化学习训练CSA效果显著，优于有监督微调；CSA具备良好的分布外泛化性，并已成功应用于实际系统中的推理时决策和训练数据优化。

Conclusion: 能力自我评估（CSA）是一项可学习且可迁移的模型特质。通过强化学习训练出的CSA不仅能提升模型在推理阶段的决策可靠性（如本地/云端任务分流），还能为训练数据选择提供有效指导。相比之下，有监督微调方案因破坏了模型原有的能力评估基础而效果不佳。

Abstract: The ability to recognize one's own limitations and decide whether to solve a problem or delegate is fundamental for reliable intelligent systems. Yet we show that modern large language models systematically lack this ability: across diverse model families and scales, they overestimate their competence and attempt queries they cannot solve. We refer to this ability as Capability Self-Assessment (CSA) and formulate it as a policy-learning problem, aiming to improve self-assessment while preserving the model's original capabilities. Our results show that reinforcement learning teaches CSA effectively, significantly outperforming supervised fine-tuning while preserving original capabilities. In contrast, supervised fine-tuning severely degrades the capabilities the model is meant to assess. Moreover, learned self-assessment behavior generalizes well out of distribution, suggesting that CSA is a transferable model trait. Finally, CSA is practically useful: it improves local-cloud decision making at inference time and provides a signal for targeted data selection during training.

</details>


### [80] [Closed-Loop Neural Activation Control in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00269)
*Abhijith Babu,Ramneet Kaur,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Olivera Kotevska,Susmit Jha,Yanzhao Wu,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型测试时干预手段原始且固定的痛点，CTRL-STEER 引入闭环反馈控制器实现动态、时变的干预强度调节，在无需重训练模型的情况下提升了机器人任务执行的稳定性和表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型干预方法主要使用固定的干预系数，缺乏对动态任务环境的感知，导致在具身控制中出现过校正、振荡等问题，难以保证任务执行的稳定性和平滑性。

Method: 提出 CTRL-STEER 框架：1. 解耦表征与调节，通过运动对齐的残差方向进行引导；2. 引入闭环反馈控制器（PID 或强化学习），根据任务状态实时动态调节干预强度（steering coefficient）。

Result: 在 LIBERO 四个任务集上的实验表明，与固定系数基线相比，CTRL-STEER 实现了更稳定的概念调控，并在引导效果与任务成功率之间取得了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: CTRL-STEER 通过引入闭环反馈机制解决了 VLA 模型静态干预导致的过校正和振荡问题，证明了在不重训练模型的情况下，通过动态调节干预强度可以显著提升具身智能的任务成功率及控制平滑度。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can be steered at test time by intervening on semantically meaningful internal directions, but existing methods use a fixed steering coefficient, effectively operating in open loop. This is poorly suited to embodied control, where task state and concept error evolve over time, often causing overcorrection, oscillation, and reduced task success, especially for temporal behaviors such as speed and smoothness. We propose CTRL-STEER, a closed-loop framework that replaces static intervention strength with adaptive, time-varying control signals. The key idea is to decouple representation from regulation: rather than assuming temporal concepts are directly controlled by individual neurons, we steer along motion-aligned residual directions while a feedback controller adjusts intervention magnitude online. We instantiate this framework with both PID and reinforcement learning based controllers. Experiments with a fine-tuned OpenVLA policy on four LIBERO task suites show that CTRL-STEER achieves more stable concept regulation and a better steering-task success trade-off than fixed-coefficient baselines, without modifying or retraining the base model.

</details>


### [81] [Robust Shielding for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00270)
*Edwin Hamel-De le Court,Thom Badings,Alessandro Abate,Francesco Belardinelli,Francesco Fabiano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中环境动力学未知的问题，提出了一种基于鲁棒MDP的护盾框架，通过LTL逻辑定义安全性，结合PAC学习方法，在保证强化学习智能体形式化安全的同时，实现了最优的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习护盾技术通常依赖于已知的环境转换动力学（Transition Dynamics），但在实际场景中这一条件往往难以满足。因此，需要一种能够在不确切清楚动力学模型的情况下，仍能对RL智能体提供形式化安全保证的框架。

Method: 1. 将安全性定义为在RMDP的各种可能概率分布（即最坏情况）下满足LTL逻辑公式，且概率超过设定阈值；
2. 构建针对RMDP的护盾（Shield），并从理论上证明了其对于RMDP的完备性和最优性；
3. 将该框架与PAC（可能性-近似正确）采样方法结合，实现在未知环境动力学下动态学习模型并构建护盾。

Result: 实验表明，该护盾框架应用于学习到的RMDP时，能够在未知环境中有效保障智能体的安全性，且随着采样次数的增加，能够使智能体获得较高的期望收益（Return），表现出极好的鲁棒性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的护盾框架对于鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）既是完备的（Sound）又是最优的（Optimal），能够实现安全性与性能的最优平衡。通过结合PAC学习方法，该框架成功扩展至未知动力学环境，在保障安全的前提下，随着采样增加能有效恢复性能。

Abstract: Shielding is an effective approach to formally guarantee the safety of reinforcement learning agents in Markov decision processes (MDPs). However, existing shielding techniques typically assume knowledge of the safety-relevant transition dynamics - a requirement that is seldom met in practice. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel shielding framework for robust MDPs (RMDPs), i.e., MDPs with sets of transition probabilities. We define safety as the satisfaction of a linear temporal logic (LTL) formula with a certain threshold probability under the worst-case transition probabilities of the RMDP. We prove that our shielding framework is both sound and optimal for the RMDP: every policy admissible by the shield is safe, and conversely, every safe RMDP policy is admissible by the shield. We combine our approach with existing sampling methods for learning transition probabilities of MDPs with probably approximately correct (PAC) guarantees. This combination enables the construction of shields for MDPs that, with high confidence, guarantee safety while remaining minimally restrictive. Our experiments show that our shields for learned RMDPs guarantee safety in unknown MDPs while recovering strong expected return as the number of samples increases.

</details>


### [82] [On Wednesdays, We Ask Questions: Optimizing "Active Listening" in Automated Legal Triage and Referral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00272)
*Quinten Steenhuis,Jacqueline Harvey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FETCH系统利用多模型集成进行法律问题分类，研究揭示了低成本LLM生成高质量法律追问的局限性，需引入GPT-5等高性能模型辅助，并强调了在敏感法律领域采用专项筛查机制的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决法律服务申请过程中，如何通过低成本、高效率的自动追问系统（FETCH）准确识别申请人的法律问题，并降低对高成本模型依赖的难题。

Method: 采用基于低成本LLM集成（Ensemble）的分类策略，通过专家律师与LLM辅助的方法对追问质量进行评估，并引入高成本模型（GPT-5）进行对比分析；开发了法律进件分类问题的评估准则。

Result: 尽管低成本模型在分类任务上表现良好，但在生成高质量、平实语言的法律追问方面表现不足；引入高性能模型有效提升了信息获取的针对性及系统的分类表现；但在处理涉及敏感领域（如家暴）的筛选时，现阶段模型仍存在不均衡问题，需引入定制化筛查面板。

Conclusion: 单纯的提示词工程不足以提升法律进件问题的质量；LLM-as-judge（LLM作为评估者）与人类评估结果存在偏差；单一的高成本模型（如GPT-5）可显著提升信息获取能力与分类准确度；针对特定法律领域（如家暴）需要专门的筛查机制以确保准确性。

Abstract: The FETCH classifier generates follow-up questions to help refine the best match for the applicant's legal problem, using a low-cost ensemble of LLMs. In this paper, we describe an expert attorney and LLM-assisted evaluation of the follow-up question approach in FETCH and show that while low-cost LLMs perform well at classification tasks, generating high-quality plain-language questions in this setting appears to require a more sophisticated and higher-cost model. Through discussion with legal intake workers, we propose a rubric for the evaluation of legal intake classification questions, and we find that prompt engineering alone is not enough to improve question quality for intake purposes. We also find that LLM-as-judge and human ratings diverge. We demonstrate that with the addition of a single high-cost model, GPT-5, the classifier can elicit relevant information from applicants for legal help, and that the questions lead to more accurate performance at classification tasks. We also find uneven fact elicitation across different categories, including domestic violence, at odds with family law screening protocols, suggesting the value of including dedicated screening panels for certain areas of law.

</details>


### [83] [Evaluating Bivariate Causal Statements Based on Mutual Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00278)
*Erik Jahn,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对因果真值难以获取的难题，本文提出了一套基于兼容性和一致性约束的评分系统，用于评估二元因果陈述的合理性，并验证了其在诊断AI因果推理中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界中，因果关系的真实情况往往难以直接获取，导致对因果效应评估困难。研究者旨在为评估人类专家或大型语言模型（LLM）提供的因果信息构建一种可靠的评估框架，尤其是在缺乏其他验证手段的场景下。

Method: 1. 针对非循环线性模型，引入“兼容性得分”（Compatibility Score）用以量化模型的合理性，无需依赖忠实性假设（faithfulness assumption）；2. 针对纯图形化的二元因果陈述，定义“不兼容性得分”（Incompatibility Score），基于非循环图及忠实性假设导出的全局一致性约束进行判定。
