Result: 理论与实验结果均证明，所提出的兼容性得分与不兼容性得分能够有效区分正确与错误的因果陈述。该方法已成功应用于评估大型语言模型所做出的因果主张。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一套用于评估因果陈述合理性的得分机制，能够有效识别因果陈述的正确性，为在缺乏客观标准的情况下验证专家或AI生成的因果关系提供了可靠的数学基础。

Abstract: For many real-world systems, causal ground truth is difficult to obtain, making claims about causal effects hard to assess. We develop methods for evaluating collections of $\binom{n}{2}$ bivariate causal statements over a set of $n$ variables. In the setting of acyclic linear statements, any such collection can be extended to a unique multivariate causal model, but we argue that this induced model is implausible if it imposes substantial additional confounding to explain observed correlations. We introduce a compatibility score that quantifies this notion of plausibility, notably without relying on the faithfulness assumption. Additionally, we define an incompatibility score for purely graphical bivariate causal statements, based on global consistency constraints that are derived from acyclicity and faithfulness assumptions. We give theoretical and empirical evidence that both scores can successfully distinguish correct from incorrect causal statements in generic settings. Moreover, we demonstrate the practical applicability of our methods by analyzing causal claims made by large language models. Our work aims to provide a foundation for assessing the reliability of causal information derived from human experts or artificial intelligence in settings where alternative forms of validation are unavailable.

</details>


### [84] [Model-Native Computing Architecture: Envisioning Future System Architecture Through the Lens of Computer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00288)
*Hai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将大模型系统类比为计算机体系结构，提出了统一的“智能计算架构模型”（ICAM）六层框架及三大核心设计定律，旨在为模型原生计算系统的构建提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型从单一技术演进为系统技术，现有的智能体框架、内存管理及调度方案呈现零散化，迫切需要一种类似计算机操作系统架构的统一理论框架来指导模型原生系统的开发与治理。

Method: 通过类比计算机体系结构，构建了六层“智能计算架构模型”（ICAM）框架，并提出了三个核心设计定律（语义局部性定律、上下文预算定律、智能体加速定律）来量化分析模型原生系统的性能与效率。

Result: 定义了 ICAM 六层架构及确定性/概率双平面设计，通过现有文献数据验证了三大设计定律的有效性，为理解模型能力与系统控制提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“模型原生计算”（model-native computing）的愿景，指出当前大模型系统研究缺乏统一范式，并给出了相应的研究路线图。同时强调了该领域与传统计算机科学类比的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models are undergoing a transition from model technology to system technology. As developers use Codex, Claude Code, AutoGPT, and related agents to write code, manage projects, and execute multi-step tasks, recurring engineering problems such as cache reuse, context management, agent scheduling, and permission control increasingly resemble classical computer systems problems. This paper develops that analogy as a visionary survey. We map concepts from computer architecture to the emerging model-native stack and review work on LLM-as-OS, memory management, agent frameworks, tool protocols, multi-agent coordination, cognitive architectures, and safety governance. We argue that these strands address different layers of the same system but lack a unified model.
  To fill this gap, we propose the Intelligent Computing Architecture Model (ICAM), a six-layer framework for model-native computing with explicit interface contracts and design axioms. ICAM resolves the apparent tension over whether an LLM is more like a CPU or an operating system through a dual-plane view: a probabilistic execution plane concerned with what can be computed, and a deterministic control plane concerned with what should be computed. We further introduce three design laws: the Semantic Locality Law for KV-cache reuse and inference speedup, the Context Budget Law for effective working sets under finite windows and attention decay, and the Agent Speedup Law for diminishing returns in multi-agent collaboration. We validate these laws against published system-level data and relate them to recent evidence on agentic software practices. We conclude by identifying where the analogy breaks down and outlining a research roadmap for model-native computing. This is a conceptual and survey contribution; it does not report new experiments.

</details>


### [85] [Coupling Language Models with Physics-based Simulation for Synthesis of Inorganic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00315)
*Edward W. Staley,Tom Arbaugh,Michael Pekala,Alexander New,Christopher D. Stiles,Nam Q. Le,Gregory Bassen,Wyatt Bunstine,Tyrel McQueen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对无机材料合成规划难题，本文提出了一种融合热力学与动力学模型的混合评估框架，证明了LLMs通过隐式先验知识在生成可行合成路径方面优于传统算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式ML模型已能预测具有目标特性的新型无机晶体材料，但由于物理过程复杂且缺乏有效的计算工具，这些材料的合成规划仍面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出了一个混合评估框架，将热力学数据库与简化的动力学模型相结合，用于评估LLMs在无机合成规划中的表现，并以铌-氧系统作为研究案例，对比了LLM生成方案与传统路径规划算法。

Result: 研究表明，LLMs能够利用其内置的隐式先验知识生成更可行的合成方案。在所设定的评估场景中，传统搜索方法在复杂问题面前显得局限，进一步凸显了LLMs在复杂策略规划中的优势。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LLMs）所具备的隐式先验知识在无机材料合成路径规划中展现出独特价值，能够生成比传统路径规划算法更具可行性的合成策略，是解决复杂材料合成规划问题的有力工具。

Abstract: Modern generative machine learning (ML) models can propose novel inorganic crystalline materials with targeted properties; however, synthesis planning of these materials remains difficult due to the complexity of the associated physical processes and limited availability of computational tools. We introduce a novel hybrid framework to evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) in inorganic synthesis planning by combining thermodynamic databases with simplified kinetics models to approximate realistic synthesis conditions. As a case study, we focus on the niobium-oxygen system, which features multiple industrially relevant oxide phases with well-characterized data. In computational simulations, we compare LLM-generated synthesis routes with classical path-planning algorithms, showing that the implicit priors in LLMs can yield more viable strategies. In our evaluation setting, classical search methods serve primarily as a foil rather than a direct competitor. This illustrates the relative complexity of the problem and highlights where the LLM's implicit priors add value.

</details>


### [86] [From Noise to Control: Parameterized Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00336)
*Renhao Zhang,Haotian Fu,Mingxi Jia,George Konidaris,Yilun Du,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PDP框架，通过将扩散模型锚定在语义行为流形上，实现了无需微调权重即可精确控制和插值生成多模态机器人行为的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散策略主要侧重于生成的多样性，难以在复杂多模态任务中精确控制和组合策略，且针对新约束的适应能力受限。

Method: 提出参数化扩散策略（Parameterised Diffusion Policy, PDP），通过将扩散策略条件化于低维连续的行为流形空间，并设计该流形以确保潜在空间的距离反映轨迹的语义相似性，从而实现对生成行为的精确引导。

Result: PDP在模拟及真实机器人实验的复杂多模态基准测试中，表现出比标准扩散策略更优的适应能力，尤其是在需要合成新颖行为的场景中。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散模型与学习到的行为流形相结合，PDP框架成功实现了行为的可控性与泛化性，证明了在无需更新策略权重的前提下，可通过参数化手段实现对复杂行为的精确调节和迁移。

Abstract: We propose Parameterized Diffusion Policy (PDP), a framework for learning diffusion policies conditioned on low-dimensional, continuous parameters embedded in a learned behavior manifold. By constructing this manifold so that distances between latent representations reflect the semantic similarity between physical trajectories, we transform diffusion from a mechanism for stochastic diversity into a precise and optimizable tool for behavior steering. Our approach enables smooth interpolation between known strategies and efficient adaptation to novel constraints without updating policy weights. We demonstrate that PDP significantly improves adaptation performance on complex multimodal benchmarks in both simulated and real-robot experiments compared to standard diffusion policies, particularly in scenarios requiring the synthesis of novel behaviors.

</details>


### [87] [From "Weak" Signals to Strong Models: Preference Delta Aggregation with LoRA Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00357)
*Qi Sun,Siyue Zhang,Yulin Chen,Yuxiang Xue,Ru Peng,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过聚合多个弱模型对的偏好差异（PDA）来训练强LLM的方法，并引入几何对齐（GAM）以优化LoRA适配器的合并，有效利用碎片化的“弱”信号实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量监督数据稀缺，虽然弱模型产生的响应质量有限，但其提供的“弱”信号（相对质量差）仍具有优化潜力。旨在探讨如何通过聚合多个“弱”信号来提升强语言模型的性能。

Method: 1. 偏好差聚合（PDA）：从多个弱-弱模型对中提取偏好差，并将其实例化为通过偏好优化学习到的LoRA适配器。
2. 几何对齐聚合（GAM）：一种几何感知合并方法，在聚合前对齐适配器子空间，以减轻LoRA合并过程中的方向干扰。

Result: 在知识推理和智能体搜索任务中，PDA结合GAM显著提升了强模型性能（相比基线分别提高6.8和7.3分），且性能随聚合信号数量增加而提升；其效果优于所有单差和多差基线。

Conclusion: 研究证明通过PDA框架聚合多个“弱”信号（来自弱-弱模型对的偏好差）能产生比单一信号更强的模型优化效果，且具有协同效应。提出的GAM方法通过对齐适配器子空间解决了聚合时的方向干扰问题。

Abstract: Training strong large language models (LLMs) requires high-quality supervision, which is often scarce. Recent work shows that paired preference data from weak-weaker model pairs (e.g., Qwen3 4B over 1.7B), despite the limited quality of individual responses, can provide an effective supervision signal through relative quality deltas, which we term a "weak" signal. This motivates a key research question: can multiple "weak" signals be constructively aggregated for improving strong models (e.g., Qwen3 8B)? To this end, we propose Preference Delta Aggregation (PDA), the first framework that derives a preference delta from each weak-weaker model pair, instantiates it as a LoRA adapter learned through preference optimization, and aggregates the resulting deltas via LoRA merging. To further mitigate directional interference during LoRA merging, we introduce Geometric Alignment Merging (GAM), a geometry-aware merging method that aligns adapter subspaces before aggregation, enabling more robust composition of diverse deltas. Evaluations on knowledge reasoning and agentic search benchmarks show that aggregating multiple "weak" signals pushes performance beyond any single signal, with further gains as additional signals are incorporated. Correspondingly, PDA with GAM improves the strong model by 6.8 and 7.3 points on average for knowledge reasoning and agentic search, respectively. It outperforms all single-delta and multi-delta baselines, exceeding the best single-delta baseline by 2.1 and 4.3 points. Further analysis attributes these gains to the effective composition of complementary capabilities encoded across distinct preference deltas.

</details>


### [88] [The Deterministic Horizon: When Extended Reasoning Fails and Tool Delegation Becomes Necessary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00376)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究证明了大型语言模型在处理长链及确定性状态跟踪任务时存在由注意力机制决定的架构性瓶颈，单纯延长思维链会导致性能崩溃，必须引入工具集成推理才能解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现扩展思维链（Extended CoT）在确定性状态跟踪任务中反而会导致性能下降，旨在探究其根源是模型偏好问题，还是解码器注意力机制的信息论容量限制。

Method: 1. 提出注意力瓶颈定理（Attention Bottleneck Theorem）以建模状态跟踪能力；2. 构建依赖上下文的错误模型来分析准确率衰减；3. 引入状态空间杰卡德度量（State-Space Jaccard metric）区分能力失效与偏好失效；4. 跨12个模型及8个领域进行对比实验（神经思维链 vs. 工具集成推理）。

Result: 1. 建立了理论容量界限 $O(H \cdot \log(L/H) \cdot \sqrt{d_h})$；2. 揭示了超指数级的准确率衰减现象；3. 在12个模型上验证了工具集成推理优于单纯神经思维链（准确率对比：86-94% vs. 24-42%）；4. 细调模型无法突破架构上限（改进幅度 <5%），证明了故障的架构本质。

Conclusion: 纯神经网络的思维链（CoT）存在架构性的性能上限，对于确定性状态跟踪任务，系统应在达到确定性视界（Deterministic Horizon, $d^* \in [19, 31]$）后转向工具集成推理以保证任务成功率。

Abstract: Extended chain-of-thought reasoning can degrade performance on deterministic state-tracking tasks, not due to preference biases, but limits rooted in the information-theoretic capacity of decoder-only attention. We establish: (1) an Attention Bottleneck Theorem with a complementary achievability construction, bounding state-tracking capacity as $O(H \cdot \log(L/H) \cdot \sqrt{d_h})$; (2) a context-dependent error model yielding super-exponential accuracy decay; (3) the State-Space Jaccard metric distinguishing capability from preference failures; (4) a Deterministic Horizon $d^* \in [19, 31]$ beyond which tool delegation becomes necessary. Across 12 models and 8 task domains (including SWE-Bench, WebArena, and SQL-Multi), tool-integrated reasoning consistently outperforms neural chain-of-thought; on the primary model suite it reaches 86-94% accuracy versus 24-42% for neural chain-of-thought. Fine-tuning on optimal-length traces yields $<$5% improvement, confirming an architectural ceiling, and high cross-model correlation ($r = 0.81$-$0.91$) indicates these failures are architectural rather than training-specific. Our results provide principled guidance for when pure neural reasoning should yield to hybrid approaches in agentic systems.

</details>


### [89] [Don't Ask the LLM to Track Freshness: A Deterministic Recipe for Memory Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01435)
*Vikas Reddy,Sumanth Challaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现LLM记忆系统处理事实冲突的瓶颈在于错误的“组装”策略（依赖LLM判断），而非存储。通过引入基于版本感知的确定性聚合算子（如max(serial)），在FactConsolidation任务上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM记忆系统在处理随时间演变的事实冲突时存在性能瓶颈，现有的依赖LLM在检索或生成阶段处理冲突的方法效果不佳（即“MemoryAgentBench”测试中表现惨淡）。作者旨在探索更优的冲突解决机制，以提升大语言模型对动态记忆事实的时效性与准确性。

Method: 1. 指出当前基准（如HippoRAG-v2, LLM判别）在冲突解决上的低效；2. 提出基于版本感知的确定性聚合方法（candidate-extraction + Python max(serial)）；3. 在FactConsolidation任务中，通过每跳（per-hop）确定性扩展Self-Ask机制，将冲突解决逻辑从LLM推断转向规则化处理；4. 实现与HippoRAG-v2等现有系统的同构对比测试。

Result: 该方法在FC-SH任务上达到了78.0%（gpt-4o-mini）和94.8%（gpt-4o）的准确率，且在FC-MH（多跳）任务上显著超越现有基准（如FC-MH比现有最佳结果高出20个百分点，FC-SH比HippoRAG-v2高出28个百分点）。确定性聚合策略在处理“当前值冲突”时表现远优于LLM判别。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM记忆系统在事实冲突解决上的瓶颈在于“组装环节”（后检索聚合策略），而非存储本身。确定性聚合（如按序号取最大值）是处理当前值冲突的有效基元，但需根据问题类型结合多种逻辑策略。

Abstract: LLM-based memory systems increasingly maintain facts that evolve over time, where a recurring failure is conflict resolution: when a fact has multiple contradictory values, which should the agent return? MemoryAgentBench (MAB; Hu et al., 2026) makes this explicit in its FactConsolidation task: facts are numbered, the counterfactual has the higher serial, and agents are told newer facts have larger serials. Yet every published system underperforms: HippoRAG-v2 reaches 54% on single-hop (FC-SH), BM25 48%, Mem0 18%, and the temporal KG Zep/Graphiti just 7%. Multi-hop is near-unsolved (at most 7% across 22 systems).
  We argue the bottleneck is the assembly step: baselines leave conflict resolution to LLM-mediated retrieval or generation rather than version-aware aggregation. A matched-setup comparison (same backbone, retrieval, chunking, TOP_K) shows that replacing the LLM-judgment answer pipeline with candidate-extraction plus Python max(serial) yields +10.8 points on FC-SH (gpt-4o-mini), widening from +8 at 6K to +21 at 262K. This is a whole-pipeline effect (resolver, prompt, format, and temperature vary jointly); isolating the resolver is future work. The recipe reaches 78.0% on FC-SH (gpt-4o-mini), 94.8% (gpt-4o), and 30.2% on FC-MH (gpt-4o-mini, rising to 51.5% with gpt-4o) via a per-hop deterministic extension of Self-Ask. At matched-262K, it beats HippoRAG-v2 by +28 points and the best published FC-MH result by +20.
  The implication is corrective for the subfield: the bottleneck on conflict resolution is assembly (post-retrieval aggregation), not storage. A LongMemEval knowledge-update check shows the mechanism ports from max(serial) to max(timestamp) but only ties LLM judgment (57.8% vs 64.4%, n=45): deterministic aggregation is the right primitive for current-value conflicts and must be composed with question-type-aware handling for broader memory QA.

</details>


### [90] [VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00384)
*William Rudman,Abhishek Divekar,Kanishk Jain,Sebastian Joseph,Stella S. R. Offner,Matthew Lease,Kyle Mahowald,Greg Durrett,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决自动化科学建模中的痛点，VESTA 框架引入了动态工具生成与探索机制，配合新基准测试 DAWN，显著提升了模型在复杂数据拟合与诊断任务中的准确度与专业性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大模型（LLM/VLM）的自动化数据建模系统在处理复杂任务时表现不佳，缺乏深度的自动数据探索能力，仅依赖于迭代反馈（critique）而忽略了 proactive 的工具使用。

Method: 提出了 VESTA 框架，该框架赋予视觉语言模型（VLM）动态增长的探索工具包；引入了 DAWN 基准测试集，包含从分布拟合到引力波信号建模等不同难度的任务。

Result: VESTA 在 DAWN 基准测试中优于现有的代理流水线，特别是在复杂任务上表现突出；生成的工具比现有系统更复杂，能够跨多种诊断类别，并优先提供便于模型分析的视觉输出。

Conclusion: 动态工具创建机制显著提升了代理在复杂及领域特定数据建模任务中的表现。VESTA 通过动态生成的诊断工具，不仅增强了模型对数据的理解，还提升了统计模型拟合的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fitting quantitative models to data is a central step in scientific workflows, yet it remains one of the least automated. Recent agent-based systems leverage language and vision-language models (VLMs) to iteratively propose and refine statistical models, but these systems struggle on more challenging modeling tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents, a framework that equips VLMs with a dynamically growing exploration toolkit to guide model refinement through data transformations, hypothesis-driven visualizations, and robust statistical tests. Unlike prior systems that rely on iterative critique alone, VESTA actively explores data before and during refinement by selecting or creating diagnostic tools, which accumulate in the model's context and can be reused later. We evaluate VESTA against established baselines in three toolkit configurations: no tools, static expert-written tools, and dynamic model-written tools. To support this evaluation, we introduce DAWN (Dataset for Automated Workflows and Numerical Modeling), a benchmark targeting distribution fitting and time series modeling with varying difficulty tiers, and culminating in real-world astronomy tasks including modeling initial mass functions and gravitational-wave chirp signals. We find that VESTA's dynamic tool creation outperforms prior agentic pipelines, with the largest gains on complex and domain-specific tasks. We further show that dynamically generated tools are substantially more sophisticated than those produced by existing visual tool-creation systems, covering more diagnostic categories per function and strongly preferring visual outputs that the VLM critic can reason over directly.

</details>


### [91] [Weak Critics Make Strong Learners: On-Policy Critique Distillation for Scalable Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00424)
*Can Jin,Jiakang Li,Rui Wu,Eddy Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型难以被弱模型监督的问题，该论文提出“弱批评者”机制，通过让弱模型提供改进建议而非直接评估，配合OPCD蒸馏方法，有效提升了强模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型能力增强，弱监督者难以直接为复杂输出提供可靠标签或判断；为了克服“弱到强”泛化及可扩展监督的限制，研究尝试利用弱模型提供修正导向而非直接解决问题。

Method: 提出“弱批评者强监督（weak-critic strong oversight）”框架，通过赋予弱模型评估与修正建议（而非直接生成答案）的角色，并引入“渐进策略批评蒸馏（OPCD）”方法，通过过滤高质量评论并利用自适应自教师信号对强模型进行微调。

Result: 实验表明，弱批评在推理阶段即可提升强模型性能，且批评质量至关重要；OPCD 方法在推理和对齐任务基准上显著提升了强模型的表现，证明了该方法在可扩展监督方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种利用弱模型作为“批评者（critic）”辅助强模型的可扩展监督范式，证明了利用弱批评进行策略蒸馏（OPCD）可有效提升强模型性能，为解决弱监督下的模型对齐与可扩展监督问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: As large language models become stronger, weak supervisors may fail to provide reliable labels, preferences, or final judgments for complex outputs, limiting both weak-to-strong generalization and scalable oversight. We study a more tractable form of weak supervision: using a weak model as a critic rather than as a labeler or judge. Instead of solving the task or selecting the correct answer, the weak critic only needs to provide a non-misleading revision direction that helps the strong model better use its own knowledge. We call this setting *weak-critic strong oversight*. We first show that weak critiques can improve frozen strong models at inference time, and that critique quality is key to this improvement. We then propose progressive on-policy critique distillation (**OPCD**), which filters high-quality critiques and distills critic-guided behavior into the strong model through adaptive self-teacher signals. Experiments on reasoning and alignment benchmarks show that our method improves strong models over training epochs, suggesting an effective path for scalable oversight with weak supervision.

</details>


### [92] [Harness-1: Reinforcement Learning for Search Agents with State-Externalizing Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02373)
*Pengcheng Jiang,Zhiyi Shi,Kelly Hong,Xueqiang Xu,Jiashuo Sun,Jimeng Sun,Hammad Bashir,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过引入 Harness-1 架构，将状态管理从搜索模型剥离至环境侧，实现了更高效的任务决策，显著提升了检索质量与跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的搜索代理通常直接在不断增长的上下文（transcripts）上运行，导致模型需要同时处理核心搜索决策和繁琐的状态维护（ bookkeeping），这增加了强化学习的难度并降低了效率。

Method: 提出 Harness-1 架构，将搜索过程中的“状态管理”与“逻辑决策”解耦：由名为 Harness 的环境侧模块负责处理工作记忆、证据链存储、记录验证等琐碎事务，而模型策略仅专注于语义搜索决策（如搜索什么、保留/验证哪些内容）。

Result: 在 8 个涵盖网络、金融、专利和多跳问答的检索基准测试中，Harness-1 平均整理召回率（curated recall）达到 0.730，较现有的开源代理提升了 11.4 个百分点，且在迁移测试中表现出优秀的通用性。

Conclusion: 将搜索任务的状态管理从模型策略中分离到环境侧（Stateful Search Harness），能显著提升强化学习代理的检索性能和泛化能力。Harness-1 证明了这种架构在多领域复杂检索任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Search agents are often trained as policies over growing transcripts: the model must decide how to search while also remembering what it has seen, which evidence is useful, which constraints remain open, and which claims have actually been checked. We argue that this formulation puts too much routine state management inside the policy: reinforcement learning is forced to optimize both semantic search decisions and recoverable bookkeeping that the environment can maintain more reliably. We introduce Harness-1, a 20B search agent (retrieval subagent) trained with reinforcement learning inside a stateful search harness. The harness maintains environment-side working memory, including a candidate pool, an importance-tagged curated set, compact evidence links, verification records, compressed and deduplicated observations, and budget-aware context rendering. The policy retains the semantic decisions: what to search, which documents to keep or discard, what to verify, and when to stop. Across eight retrieval benchmarks spanning web, finance, patents, and multi-hop QA, Harness-1 achieves 0.730 average curated recall, outperforming the next strongest open search subagent by +11.4 points and remaining competitive with much larger frontier-model searchers. Its gains are especially strong on held-out transfer benchmarks, suggesting that reinforcement learning over explicit search state can produce retrieval behaviors that generalize beyond the training domains. Our code is available at https://github.com/pat-jj/harness-1.

</details>


### [93] [SDR: Set-Distance Rewards for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00440)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Max Van Puyvelde,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对胸部X光报告无序的特点，提出了基于“集合到集合距离”的奖励机制，显著提升了VLM在放射科报告生成中的性能，并支持更高效的推理阶段缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光报告由无序且正交的医学发现组成，缺乏标准的因果推理链，导致传统的精确匹配（exact-match）和步骤级强化学习奖励难以有效衡量报告质量，因此需要一种更契合医学报告特性的评估与优化方式。

Method: 1. 集合化处理：将报告拆分为句子并通过冻结的句子Transformer编码，形成无序嵌入集； 2. 奖励建模：利用集合到集合（set-to-set）的距离作为连续且置换不变（permutation-invariant）的奖励信号； 3. 模型优化：结合GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行模型后训练，并在推理阶段应用该距离进行最佳候选生成（best-of-N selection）及中间生成剪枝。

Result: 在三个VLM（Qwen3-VL-2B/4B, Gemma3-4B）上，相比监督微调和精确匹配GRPO，该方法在BERTScore、RadGraph F1和CheXbert F1指标上平均分别提升了6.80%、7.82%和4.45%；在测试时，该方法提升了最佳候选选择效果，且通过阶段性剪枝可节省超50%的生成token。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于集合距离的奖励机制为胸部X光报告生成任务提供了一个统一的信号，成功解决了传统序列级奖励在处理无序、正交医学发现时的局限性，适用于强化学习后的训练及测试时推理扩展（test-time scaling）。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has rapidly advanced reasoning in vision--language models. However, for chest X-ray report generation, the standard rewards (i.e. exact-match accuracy and step-level processes) are incompatible because the reports consist of unordered and orthogonal findings, rather than a causal reasoning chain. We address this gap with a set-based view: each report is split into sentences and embedded by a frozen sentence transformer, yielding unordered embedding sets. We propose the use of set-to-set distances between generated and reference embeddings as continuous, permutation-invariant rewards. Across two datasets and three vision--language models (Qwen3-VL-2B/4B, Gemma3-4B), post-training with set-to-set distance based rewards via GRPO consistently outperforms supervised fine-tuning and exact-match GRPO on all headline metrics (BERTScore, RadGraph F1 and CheXbert F1 by average \%6.80, \%7.82 and \%4.45 relative improvements respectively). The same set distances also enable test-time best-of-$N$ selection: scoring candidates by their distance to training-report embeddings outperforms random selection on our trained models as well as three closed-source LLMs (Mistral-Small, Gemini-2.5 Flash-Lite, GPT-4o-mini) with on average \%16.4 relative improvement on BERTScore. Used as a streaming signal, they support a more efficient form of test-time scaling: pruning low-scoring candidates mid-generation reduces generated tokens by over 50\% while preserving the Findings quality of full best-of-$N$ selection. Together these results establish set-distance rewards as a unified signal for both post-training and test-time scaling in chest X-ray report generation. Our code is publicly \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Set-Distance-Rewards-CXR-BFDA}{available}.

</details>


### [94] [GuidaPA: Privacy-Preserving Chatbot for Public Administration via Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01386)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Albenzio Cirillo,Raffaele Nicolussi,Alessio Beltrame,Andrea Vitaletti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuidaPA 是一项利用联邦学习微调大模型实现意大利公共行政领域问答机器人的研究，在保障数据隐私的同时取得了媲美集中式微调的高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对意大利公共行政（PA）部门文档（如手动工具、工单、数据库提取物）因法规和组织约束无法进行集中化汇集的问题，旨在开发一种保护隐私的聊天机器人解决方案。

Method: 构建了名为 GuidaPA 的隐私保护聊天机器人，采用联邦微调（Federated Fine-tuning）大语言模型（LLM）方法；具体技术包括：使用 QLoRA（4-bit）进行参数高效微调，实施基于角色的访问控制，进行安全的客户端预处理，以及处理非独立同分布（non-IID）数据影响。

Result: GuidaPA 在联邦环境下取得了优异表现（ROUGE-1: 61.10, BLEU-4: 45.02, METEOR: 63.94），且表现接近私有集中式微调，显著优于通用基准模型，证明了联邦学习在该领域应用的有效性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习（FL）能够实现在不进行集中式数据共享的前提下，为公共行政部门提供高质量的对话式AI服务，验证了该方案在处理受限数据时的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: We present GuidaPA, a privacy-preserving chatbot for the Italian Public Administration (PA) trained via Federated Learning (FL) on documentation from two national PA platforms, SIGESON and SIDFORS. Our corpus includes approximately 8 pages of SIGESON manuals and 31 pages of SIDFORS manuals/FAQs; while this study uses public documentation as a safe proxy, the intended deployment extends to restricted internal sources (e.g., tickets, officer manuals, database extracts) that can not be centrally pooled due to regulatory and organizational constraints. GuidaPA integrates role-based access control, secure client-side preprocessing, explicit monitoring of non-IID effects, and parameter-efficient federated fine-tuning of large language models. Using QLoRA (4-bit) over 15 federated rounds with an 80/20 train-test split per client, we evaluate answer quality with ROUGE, BLEU-4, and METEOR. The best federated model achieves ROUGE-1/2/L of 61.10/55.77/59.44, BLEU-4 of 45.02, and METEOR of 63.94-close to private centralized fine-tuning while keeping data on-site. Compared to the general-purpose baseline, domain fine-tuning improves ROUGE-1 from 41.45 to 62.18 and BLEU-4 from 26.97 to 50.90. Overall, the results indicate that FL can deliver high-quality conversational AI for public services without centralized data sharing

</details>


### [95] [Doing What They Say, Not What They Reason: Locating the Faithfulness Gap in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00476)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过德州扑克模拟器量化了LLM智能体的“过程保真度”，发现模型在推理逻辑与最终决策之间存在一致性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在社会模拟中的应用日益广泛，但其公开展示的推理过程是否真正驱动了最终的行为（即过程保真度）尚不明确，且在缺乏真实标准答案的开放场景下难以衡量。

Method: 在德州扑克模拟器这一受控环境中，将智能体的决策过程解构为“推理-结论”与“结论-行动”两个阶段，通过对比模型输出的逻辑推理与实际执行的动作，量化评估过程保真度（Process Fidelity）。

Result: 研究发现“推理-结论”阶段与“结论-行动”阶段存在相反的表现，揭示了LLM并非总是严格按照其陈述的推理逻辑来执行动作。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在“推理-结论”与“结论-行动”两个环节表现出截然不同的保真度特性；该研究为评估LLM在缺乏标准答案场景下的行为一致性提供了量化框架。

Abstract: Do LLM agents act on the reasoning they state? This question of process fidelity is central to using LLMs in social simulation, yet it is hard to measure where no reference for correct behavior exists. We study it in acontrolled setting, a Texas Poker simulator with a verifiable reference action for every decision by decomposing the faithfulness gap into two steps: reasoning-conclusion and conclusion-action. The two steps behave oppositely.

</details>


### [96] [TAPS: Target-Aware Prefix Tree Selection for Diffusion-Drafted Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00487)
*Zhuoyu Wang,Junnan Huang,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散模型辅助推理（Speculative Decoding）中草稿树验证效率低的问题，提出了 TAPS 方法。通过将扩散边缘概率转换为路径条件接受度，实现了更高效的子树选择，在大幅提升推理速度的同时保持了模型输出的无损性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型草稿方案（如 DFlash, DDTree）在构建草稿树时未使用前缀条件下的真实接受概率进行排序，导致验证了大量不可达或低价值节点，造成计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TAPS（Target-Aware Prefix Selection）的算法，将扩散模型的边缘概率转化为路径条件的接受度估计，并能在固定验证预算下选择最优的前缀闭合子树，从而平衡接受长度与验证延迟。

Result: 在多项任务上实现了最高 7.9 倍的无损加速，显著超越了当前最优的 DFlash (1.36 倍) 和 DDTree (1.74 倍)。

Conclusion: TAPS 通过引入目标感知的路径条件接受度估计，有效优化了扩散模型推理的草稿树选择，在保持无损生成质量的前提下显著提升了端到端推理速度。

Abstract: Using a diffusion model for parallel drafting is a promising approach for speculative decoding. By predicting tokens at multiple future positions in a single forward pass, diffusion drafters substantially reduce drafting latency. However, this shifts the bottleneck to verification: verifying a single sequence limits acceptance length, while verifying large draft trees incurs excessive target-model latency. We identify a key mismatch in existing draft-tree methods: existing diffusion-tree methods rank nodes by the marginal probability, ignoring that verification is prefix-conditioned. As a result, they may verify unreachable descendants of rejected prefixes, increasing latency with limited acceptance gains. To address this, we propose TAPS, a target-aware prefix selection method that turns diffusion marginals into path-conditioned acceptance estimates. TAPS then selects a compact prefix-closed subtree under a fixed verification budget, improving the acceptance-cost tradeoff rather than simply expanding the draft tree. Experiments across diverse datasets and model families demonstrate that TAPS achieves up to 7.9x lossless end-to-end speedup over vanilla autoregressive decoding, outperforming state-of-the-art DFlash and DDTree by 1.36x and 1.74x respectively. Our work is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TAPS-EMNLP2026-53DD

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Maximizing Compute Capacity in AI Data Centers through Cooling, Energy Storage, and Computing Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00457)
*Shaolei Ren,Mohammad A. Islam,Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对数据中心受限功率下的算力与冷却需求冲突，提出的 ComputeAmp 框架通过协同电池储能、冷却调控与计算适应，优化了算力容量利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心计算容量受制于站点总功率限制，而冷却系统的功耗随环境温度波动极大。传统的保守方案导致资源利用率低，而激进方案在高温天气下面临电力超载风险。

Method: 提出了 ComputeAmp 框架，通过联合协同调度冷却系统、电池储能系统以及计算任务自适应调整（Compute-based adaptation），实现在受限功率预算下的动态计算容量最大化。

Result: 该框架能够缓解冷却功耗波动带来的容量限制问题，在本地电力和水资源约束条件下提升了数据中心可用的计算容量，并指出了相关算法与运营挑战。

Conclusion: ComputeAmp 框架通过协同冷却、电池储能和计算任务自适应，能够有效突破电网容量限制，在保障供电安全前提下显著提升数据中心的实际计算效能。

Abstract: The deployment of artificial intelligence is increasingly constrained by limited site-level power capacity, which must support both compute systems and non-compute systems (primarily cooling) at all times. Cooling power demand, especially in non-evaporative cooling systems, can increase substantially with ambient temperature in the summer, producing recurring periods of elevated cooling power that often lasts for multiple hours per day. Therefore, maximizing compute capacity under a limited site-level power budget is an important planning and operational challenge. Sizing the compute system conservatively based on peak cooling power can leave part of the site-level power capacity underutilized when the cooling power is below its peak, particularly in cooler months. On the other hand, sizing the compute system aggressively based on low cooling power can cause the total site-level power demand to exceed the site-level power capacity during hot days in the summer. This paper proposes ComputeAmp (Compute Amplifier), a framework that maximizes the compute capacity by jointly and dynamically leveraging cooling, battery energy storage, and computing-based adaptation. We discuss the opportunities and limitations of ComputeAmp and illustrate its potential to significantly expand usable compute capacity within local power and water resource limits. We also present a problem formulation for ComputeAmp and highlight a few algorithmic and operational challenges.

</details>


### [98] [SparseX: Efficient Segment-Level KV Cache Sharing for Interleaved LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01751)
*Quqing Zhang,Kai Chen,Ning Liao,Zehao Lin,Bo Tang,Feiyu Xiong,Xiaoxing Wang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: SparseX 是一种在 LLM 推理服务中实现段级 KV Cache 共享的技术，通过稀疏 KV 重计算和混合注意力机制，高效解决了非前缀重复内容的缓存重用问题，显著加速了长上下文任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 vLLM 的 Prefix Cache 仅支持前缀重用，难以应对实际生产中常见的非前缀、跨请求、跨轮次及跨 Agent 的重复内容请求，导致长上下文推理代价过高。

Method: 1. 提出以连续 Token 段为单位的细粒度 KV Cache 共享机制；2. 利用 Sparse-Q 索引识别并定位需要修正的关键 Token；3. 实施单次前向传播中的 Sparse-KV 重计算，以恢复跨段上下文交互；4. 采用层级混合注意力机制（浅层全注意力，深层稀疏重计算）。

Result: 在 vLLM 上实现了 SparseX-vLLM，在处理多轮对话、RAG 和 Agent 工作流等复杂场景时，能够在保持上下文交互质量的同时，显著降低预填充阶段的计算开销。

Conclusion: SparseX 是一种通用的、无需训练且兼容 Prefix Cache 的段级 KV Cache 共享方案，能有效提升包括 RAG、Agent 和多轮对话在内的复杂长上下文服务场景的性能。

Abstract: In long-context LLM serving, the prefill stage often dominates time-to-first-token and computational cost. Although Prefix Cache in vLLM/PagedAttention has been widely used to reuse identical prompt prefixes, repeated content in practical applications frequently appears as non-prefix, cross-request, cross-turn, and cross-agent segments, which makes conventional cache mechanisms insufficient. This paper presents SparseX, a segment-level KV Cache sharing method for common serving scenarios. SparseX uses contiguous token segments as reuse units and exploits Sparse-Q indices that naturally arise in KV Cache reuse workloads to estimate the key tokens that require correction. Based on this estimate, SparseX performs Sparse-KV Recomputation within a single forward pass, thereby restoring cross-segment contextual interactions under complex interleaved reuse patterns while avoiding additional models or separate preprocessing stages for token selection. SparseX further implements a full+sparse hybrid attention mode based on a layer-specific threshold: early layers retain full attention to obtain a more stable token-importance signal, and later layers switch to sparse recomputation to improve reuse quality on complex long-context tasks. We implement SparseX-vLLM on top of vLLM, integrating segment-level cache lookup, PagedAttention management, RoPE alignment, Sparse-Q token selection, and FlashAttention backends into a unified execution path. SparseX is model-agnostic, training-free, and compatible with Prefix Cache, and it provides unified support for common online serving scenarios including multi-round chat, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and agent workflows.

</details>


### [99] [$γ$-CounterBoost: Optimizing response time tails using job type information only](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02311)
*Nils Charlet,Benny Van Houdt*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出$\gamma$-CounterBoost调度策略，证明了在仅具备作业类型信息的M/G/1队列中，无需利用到达时间即可实现响应时间尾部分布的最优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有$\gamma$-Boost策略虽然可以优化响应时间尾部，但在部分信息场景下依赖于“作业到达时间”信息；本文探讨在不使用到达时间信息、仅利用作业类型信息的情况下，如何实现类似的最优调度。

Method: 引入了$\gamma$-CounterBoost调度策略，该策略不依赖到达时间，仅基于作业类型进行决策；通过数学证明将其与Contextual CounterBoost策略类进行对比，验证了其尾部优化性能。

Result: 成功定义了$\gamma$-CounterBoost策略，且证明对于$d \ge 2$种作业类型，该策略在Contextual CounterBoost类中实现了尾部最优；同时证实了当$d=2$时，该策略等价于已知的Nudge-M策略。

Conclusion: 提出并证明了$\gamma$-CounterBoost策略在部分作业大小信息（利用作业类型，但不利用到达时间）的M/G/1队列中，能够在大范围的Contextual CounterBoost策略类中最小化响应时间的尾部分布；该策略是针对两类作业场景下Nudge-M策略的通用化推广。

Abstract: In a recent paper the $γ$-Boost scheduling policy was shown to minimize the tail of the response time distribution in a light-tailed M/G/1-queue. This policy schedules jobs using a boosted arrival time, defined as the arrival time of a job minus its boost, where the boost of a job depends on its exact job size. The $γ$-Boost policy can also be used when only partial job size information is available, such as the type of an incoming job. In such case the boost $b_i$ of a job depends solely on its type $i$ and $γ$-Boost was shown to optimize the tail among all boost policies, where a boost policy is fully determined by the $b_i$ values. In the partial information setting $γ$-Boost relies on two types of information: job types and arrival times.
  This paper focuses on the problem of minimizing the tail in a light-tailed M/G/1-queue in the partial job size information setting when the scheduler only makes use of the job types and {\it does not exploit arrival times}. Prior work showed that in case of $2$ job types the so-called Nudge-$M$ policy minimizes the tail in a large class of scheduling policies. In this paper we introduce the $γ$-CounterBoost policy in the partial information setting with $d \geq 2$ job types and prove that it minimizes the tail in an even broader class of scheduling policies called Contextual CounterBoost policies. The $γ$-CounterBoost policy reduces to the Nudge-$M$ policy in case of $d=2$ job types.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Beyond Text and Tables: Vision-Language Model Integration in ComProScanner for Extracting Materials Data from Scientific Figures with High Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00065)
*Aritra Roy,Enrico Grisan,Chiara Gattinoni,John Buckeridge*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一款集成了 VLM 的全自动多模态材料数据挖掘工具，能够从科学文献的图表中高效、精准且低成本地提取性能数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料信息提取框架多局限于文本和表格，忽略了科学文献中大量仅以图表形式存在的定量性能数据。

Method: 扩展现有的 ComProScanner 框架，新增 FigureExtractor 工具进行图表筛选，并引入 GraphExtractorTool 使用 VLM 处理图表以恢复数据；评估了四种高性价比的 VLM，并引入了基于范围的数值误差评价指标。

Result: 通过在 50 篇压电陶瓷论文上的基准测试，Gemini-1.5-Flash-Preview 表现最优，组分准确率和归一化 F1 分数均达到 0.97，实现了成本效益与性能的平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了集成视觉-语言模型（VLM）的 ComProScanner 框架，实现了从科学文献（文本、表格及图表）中全自动提取材料组分-性能数据的能力，是该领域首个多模态挖掘平台，且通过引入基于范围的误差阈值显著提升了评估的物理意义。

Abstract: Automated extraction of materials composition-property data from scientific literature has advanced considerably with the development of large language model-based pipelines; however, existing frameworks remain limited to textual and tabular content, overlooking the substantial proportion of quantitative property data reported exclusively in scientific figures. Here, we extend ComProScanner, a fully end-to-end multi-agent framework for automated composition-property database construction, with a native vision-language model (VLM) based figure extraction capability. The extension introduces a FigureExtractor utility for caption-keyword-based figure filtering across all supported publishers, and a GraphExtractorTool agent that passes extracted figures to a configurable VLM to recover composition-property pairs from scientific charts and plots. Four VLMs are selected for evaluation on the basis of the LMArena Diagram leaderboard with an input cost criterion of less than \$1.50 per million tokens. Benchmarking on 50 piezoelectric ceramic articles from the established $d_{33}$ test corpus demonstrates that Gemini-3-Flash-Preview achieves the highest performance with a composition accuracy of 0.97 and a normalised F1 score of 0.97, whilst remaining the most cost-effective model among the four evaluated. We additionally introduce a range-based value error threshold parameter into the evaluation framework, providing a more physically meaningful assessment of numeric property values extracted from figures than exact value matching. These contributions establish VLM-integrated ComProScanner as the first materials-specific, fully automated, multimodal literature mining platform capable of extracting structured composition-property data from text, tables, and figures within a single unified pipeline.

</details>


### [101] [SentimentLens: Reconciling Sentiment and Ratings via Dual-Modality in the Hospitality Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00084)
*Dineth Jayakody,Pasindu Thenahandi,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SentimentLens 是一个基于 ABSA 的可扩展分析系统，能自动从酒店评论中提取结构化洞察，并结合多维数据分析识别运营痛点和改进机会，助力旅游业数据驱动决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线旅游平台产生海量反馈，但如何将非结构化评论转化为结构化、可操作的业务洞察仍具挑战。

Method: 提出 SentimentLens 系统，基于方面级情感分析（ABSA），集成方面项提取、情感分类、语义类别分配及多层级分析模块（区域、酒店、类别）；结合文本情感与数值评分进行跨模态对齐，应用重要性-表现分析（IPA）及基于熵的分析方法。

Result: 通过 10,000 条酒店评论的实证分析，揭示了不同地区、类别和酒店类型的服务质量差异，识别出潜在的运营冲突、服务一致性问题及改进机会。

Conclusion: SentimentLens 有效地将大规模非结构化评论转化为可操作的洞察，为酒店管理和旅游政策制定提供了数据驱动的决策支持，且具有跨领域通用性。

Abstract: Online travel platforms generate vast volumes of user-generated hotel reviews, offering rich opportunities to understand traveler experiences at scale. However, transforming unstructured textual feedback into structured, actionable insights remains a challenging task. This paper presents SentimentLens, a scalable analysis system based on Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis that performs knowledge extraction from unstructured hotel reviews and organizes them into interpretable service categories. SentimentLens integrates aspect term extraction, aspect sentiment classification, semantic category assignment, and multi-level analytical modules to support region-level, hotel-level, and category-level evaluation. The system is designed to operate across different geographic contexts and hospitality settings. To demonstrate its practical utility, we apply SentimentLens to a large real-world dataset of over 10,000 publicly available hotel reviews. Through extensive analysis, the framework reveals how traveler sentiment varies across regions, service categories, and hotel archetypes. We further implement a cross-modal reconciliation of textual sentiment and numerical ratings to identify latent operational conflicts, structural inconsistencies in service quality, and high-impact improvement opportunities using importance--performance and entropy-based analyses. The results show that SentimentLens effectively transforms large-scale unstructured reviews into actionable intelligence, supporting data-driven decision-making for hospitality management and tourism policy. While demonstrated using a national case study, the proposed system is generalizable to other destinations and review-driven service domains.

</details>


### [102] [Multimodal Music Recommendation System using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00125)
*Srikar Prabhas Kandagatla,Sreehitha R. Narayana,Chandana Magapu,Swetha Mohan,Shamanth Kuthpadi,Hongjie Chen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Nesreen Ahmed*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合音频、歌词、元数据及用户交互的多模态音乐推荐框架，通过扩展LLM基座，证明内容特征显著优于传统的ID-only方法，并发布了多模态基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音乐推荐系统多将歌曲视为不透明的ID令牌，缺乏对歌曲音频及语义内容的深度理解。作者旨在解决多模态信号（语义、声学、交互）未能在一个统一的LLM框架下得到综合建模的问题，以实现更具内容导向性的推荐。

Method: 1. 数据增强：利用预训练模型提取音频和歌词嵌入、通过LLM生成语义元数据、融入听完率（Completion Ratios）；2. 框架创新：基于E4SRec框架，扩展支持多模态特征，并结合SASRec、BERT4Rec、GRU4Rec作为编码器；3. 测试规模：在LLM后端引入LLaMa-2(13B)、Qwen2.5(7B)和LLaMa-3(70B)，并对比了零样本（zero-shot）与微调（fine-tuned）设置的表现。

Result: 整合内容特征后，模型准确率大幅提升，Recall指标提升最高达95%，NDCG指标提升最高达79%。实验发现“简单多模态融合”方法并不总能带来性能增益，揭示了跨模态信息整合的复杂性和挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了多模态内容特征（音频、歌词、元数据）对提升音乐推荐性能具有显著作用，尤其是结合听觉和语义信息能极大弥补仅依赖交互历史的局限性。同时，研究指出多模态融合并非简单的叠加，其在复杂任务中的有效性仍存在挑战，并提出了一个大规模多模态音乐推荐基准供学术界使用。

Abstract: Music recommendation systems typically treat songs as opaque tokens, relying on collaborative interaction histories which overlooks semantic or acoustic content. Prior work has explored LLM-augmented, multimodal, and text-enhanced approaches to sequential recommendation, and while some methods partially combine semantic, acoustic, or engagement signals, none jointly model all three within a unified LLM-based sequential reasoning framework that grounds recommendations in actual song content. In this work, we propose a multimodal framework for session-based music recommendation that enriches the LastFM-1K dataset with three complementary signals: (1) audio and lyric embeddings extracted using pretrained music and text representation models, (2) LLM-generated semantic metadata using the MGPHot annotation schema, and (3) listening completion ratios. We adopt the E4SRec framework by extending it with multimodal features and different item ID encoder backbones, including SASRec, BERT4Rec, and GRU4Rec. We further extend the LLM backbone option with LLaMa-2-13B, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and LLaMa-3-70B in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Our experiments show that integrating content-based features improves over ID-only baselines up to 95% in terms of Recall and 79% in terms of NDCG. Moreover, our experiments show that naive multimodal fusion does not always yield additive improvements, highlighting challenges in cross-modal integration. We release a large-scale multimodal benchmark for music recommendation.

</details>


### [103] [Synthetic Data from Cross-Domain Events for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00282)
*Xiangyu Wang,Yawen He,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Han Huang,Mengtong Hu,Sharath Ciddu,Yi-Hsuan Hsieh,Erik Groving,Yi Ding,Jieming Di,Tony Wang,Min Yun,Xiaoyu Chen,Ling Leng,Rob Malkin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统数据稀疏和噪声问题，本文提出了SCALR框架，旨在通过生成跨域合成交互事件来增广训练数据，显著提升了推荐系统的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模推荐系统中常见的数据稀疏性（data sparsity）和噪声隐式反馈（noisy implicit feedback）问题，克服传统特定模型知识蒸馏在跨域迁移中的局限性。

Method: 提出SCALR框架，分为两个阶段：第一阶段，将事件生成建模为在已知源域交互条件下，估计用户与目标域物品交互的可能性；第二阶段，利用生成的合成事件作为训练目标，对下游推荐模型进行模型无关的增广训练。

Result: 在工业级推荐平台进行的在线A/B测试中，该方法取得了具有统计学意义的性能提升。

Conclusion: SCALR框架证明了通过生成合成交互数据来辅助跨域推荐的可行性与有效性，并首次明确将跨域事件迁移表述为推荐系统中的合成数据生成任务。

Abstract: Large-scale recommendation systems operate across diverse domains, yet they face the challenges of data sparsity and noisy implicit feedback. Traditional approaches mitigate this via model-specific knowledge distillation from source domains to a target domain. Inspired by the transformative success of synthetic data generation in large language models (LLMs), we introduce Synthetic Cross-domain Augmentation and Learning for Recommendation (SCALR), a framework that generates synthetic user-item interaction events for a target recommendation domain by leveraging observed events from a source domain. SCALR decomposes cross-domain learning into two modular stages. First, it translates observed user events in source domains by framing event generation as estimating the likelihood that a user would interact with a target-domain item, conditioned on their observed interactions in a source domain. Second, downstream models train on these synthetic events as cross-domain learning objectives, where the synthetic events augment the target domain's training data in a model-agnostic manner. Our approach yields statistically significant improvements in online A/B tests on an industrial recommendation platform. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first works to explicitly frame cross-domain event transfer as synthetic data generation for recommendation systems.

</details>


### [104] [LLMs Need Encoders for Semantic IDs Too](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00324)
*Xiangyi Chen,Zelun Wang,Xinyi Li,Yi-Ping Hsu,Jaewon Yang,Jiajing Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中语义ID（SID）上下文感知不足的问题，提出了一种名为PrefixMem的轻量级前缀记忆编码器，能显著提升推荐任务中SID的预测准确率和检索召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态LLM通过专门的编码器处理非语言模态数据，但生成式推荐中的语义ID（SID）由于其含义高度依赖于上下文，目前仅通过简单的token嵌入训练，难以捕获其结构特性。

Method: 提出了一种名为PrefixMem的轻量级SID编码器，该编码器基于前缀n-gram内存表。它能在SID token位置向LLM提供结构化的、受前缀调节的表示，并支持像视觉编码器一样进行预训练后适配各种LLM。

Result: 在Pinterest大规模数据集上的实验表明，PrefixMem在保持相同计算成本的前提下，最深层SID准确率提升最高达46%，全SID检索召回率提升最高达22%；在贪婪解码失效的困难样本上，准确率提升高达77%。

Conclusion: 研究证实，语义ID（SID）作为一种特定模态，受益于专门的编码器，类似于多模态大型语言模型（LLM）中的视觉编码器。PrefixMem架构通过引入结构化的前缀条件表示，有效解决了SID依赖于上下文的本质问题，在生成式推荐任务中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs use dedicated encoders to bridge non-language modalities (vision encoders for images, depth models for audio codec tokens) because raw token embeddings alone cannot capture modality-specific structure. We argue that Semantic IDs (SIDs), the hierarchical codes used in generative recommendation, constitute another such modality: a SID level token's meaning depends on its prefix context, yet current systems simply add SID tokens to the vocabulary and rely on training to learn these context-dependent meanings from scratch. We propose PrefixMem, a lightweight SID encoder based on prefix n-gram memory tables that provides the LLM with structured, prefix-conditioned representations at SID token positions. Like vision encoders in multimodal LLMs, PrefixMem can be pre-trained independently and then attached to any LLM for joint training. We evaluate on large-scale data from Pinterest across multiple LLM families and show that PrefixMem improves deepest-level SID accuracy by up to 46% relative and full-SID retrieval recall by up to 22% relative at matched training compute. The encoder's benefit concentrates on hard examples where greedy decoding fails, with up to 77% relative accuracy gains, confirming that SID tokens benefit from a dedicated encoder just as other non-language modalities do.

</details>


### [105] [UniPinRec: Unifying Generative Retrieval and Ranking at Pinterest Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00422)
*Hanyu Li,Yi-Ping Hsu,Aditya Mantha,Prabhat Agarwal,Laksh Bhasin,Jialu Wang,Hongtao Lin,Bella Huang,Yaxin Li,Xinyi Li,Chuxi Wang,Kousik Rajesh,Hooshmand Shokri Razaghi,Shunyao Li,Zongyue Qin,Jaewon Yang,James Li,Dhruvil Deven Badani,Jiajing Xu,Charles Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Pinterest 推出了 UniPinRec，通过共享底层 Transformer 模型及跨阶段缓存优化，实现了推荐系统中检索与排序任务的输入格式、训练流程及推理基础设施的全栈统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统将检索和排序视为独立模型，导致计算资源浪费、参数冗余以及部署成本高昂。现有研究虽尝试算法统一，但未解决输入、训练及底层基础设施的碎片化问题。

Method: 1. 架构统一：使用单一Transformer编码用户行为序列。2. 屏蔽动作建模 (MAM)：在无需增加上下文长度的前提下实现参数共享。3. 混合训练示例：结合行动序列与展示列表，联合优化检索与排序目标。4. 跨阶段KV缓存共享：在推理阶段重复利用检索计算结果，优化排序性能。

Result: 在 Pinterest 生产环境部署后，在线用户互动率提升约 1%，端到端推理延迟降低 11.1%，每秒查询率 (QPS) 提升 63.6%。

Conclusion: UniPinRec 是首个在工业界大规模生产环境中实现的检索与排序全栈统一方案，通过共享变换器架构和创新的训练、推理优化，在显著提升在线指标的同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems predominantly train retrieval and ranking as separate models despite both increasingly relying on large transformers encoding the same user behavior data, duplicating parameters, compute, and serving cost. Prior work unifies the model architecture but not the full pipeline: input formats, training procedures, and serving stacks remain fragmented across stages. We present UniPinRec, which achieves full-stack unification of retrieval and ranking at Pinterest: one input format, one model, one training stage, deployed within existing serving infrastructure. A shared transformer encodes the user action sequence into candidate-independent representations that branch into retrieval (ANN dot-product) and ranking (cross-attention) via task-specific heads. Three ideas make this work: (1) Masked Action Modeling (MAM) eliminates interleaving, enabling weight sharing without doubling context length; (2) Blended training examples pair action sequences with feedview impression slates to satisfy both objectives jointly; (3) Cross-stage KV cache sharing reuses user-history computation from retrieval for ranking, reducing total FLOPs versus serving two independent models. Deployed in the Pinterest core surfaces, UniPinRec delivers approximately +1% online engagement lift while cutting end-to-end serving latency by 11.1% and lifting QPS by 63.6%. To our knowledge, this is the first full-stack unification of retrieval and ranking, covering inputs, model, training and serving, deployed in a production recommendation system.

</details>


### [106] [Trustworthy Recommendation in the Era of Large Language Models: Opportunities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00540)
*Bohao Wang,Yu Cui,Zhenxiang Xu,Jujia Zhao,Chenxiao Fan,Jizhi Zhang,Weiqin Yang,Shengjia Zhang,Sirui Chen,Yang Zhang,Xiaoyan Zhao,Wenjie Wang,Chongming Gao,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文系统综述了LLM在可信推荐领域的应用，构建了新的分类框架，揭示了LLM在提升推荐可信度方面的机遇与引入偏见与幻觉的新挑战，并指明了后续研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统正经历“从单纯精度追求向全面可信度（鲁棒性、公平性、隐私等）转型”以及“向大语言模型技术范式迁移”的双重变革，亟需探索两者融合对可信推荐的影响。

Method: 通过对200多篇近期文献进行系统性梳理，构建了涵盖六大可信维度（鲁棒性、公平性等）的新分类框架，并分析了常用的数据集与评估指标。

Result: 识别了LLM赋能推荐系统中的13个机遇与18个挑战，揭示了LLM对可信推荐的“双刃剑”效应，并为评估此类系统提供了评价体系支撑。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LLMs）既为构建可信推荐系统提供了强大工具，也引入了偏见和幻觉等新风险，研究指出未来需在增强模型可信度与应对新兴技术挑战之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The field of recommender systems (RS) is currently undergoing two profound paradigm shifts. From the perspective of objectives, the goal has shifted beyond mere recommendation accuracy to comprehensive trustworthiness, encompassing multiple dimensions such as robustness, fairness, and privacy preservation. From a technical perspective, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been extensively integrated into RS, reshaping the foundations of recommendation through richer semantic understanding, stronger intent reasoning, and more flexible user interactions. The convergence of these two shifts prompts a timely and pivotal question: how does the integration of LLMs reshape the landscape of trustworthy recommendation? In this work, we present a systematic review of trustworthy LLM-empowered recommendation. By comprehensively analyzing over 200 recent studies, we reveal that the introduction of LLMs acts as a double-edged sword. While their advanced mechanisms and user-friendly interfaces offer unprecedented opportunities to enhance trustworthiness, they simultaneously introduce new risks, such as novel forms of bias and hallucination-induced issues. To characterize this dual impact, we systematically identify 13 opportunities and 18 challenges across six fundamental dimensions of trustworthiness, and accordingly organize the existing literature into a novel taxonomy. We also provide a comprehensive review of commonly used datasets and evaluation metrics to facilitate empirical validation. Finally, we identify critical open challenges and outline future directions, hoping to inspire future research on this emerging topic.

</details>


### [107] [Critic-R: Improving Agentic Search using Instruction-tuned Retrievers with Natural Language Introspective Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00590)
*Md Zarif Ul Alam,Alireza Salemi,Hamed Zamani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Critic-R 是一种无需人工标注的代理搜索系统优化框架，通过引入“评论员”模型闭环评估推理轨迹，并利用推理轨迹自动优化检索模型，显著改善了复杂查询任务的检索与回答效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理搜索系统优化往往依赖繁重的协同训练或金标准标注，限制了其在实际应用场景中的推广和适用性。

Method: 提出 Critic-R 框架，包含两个核心机制：1. Critic-R-Zero：推理时反馈循环，用于迭代重写查询和检索指令；2. Critic-Embed：一种利用成功/失败推理轨迹进行自动监督的检索模型优化方法。

Result: 在 HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA、MuSiQue 和 Bamboogle 数据集上的实验结果表明，Critic-R 有效提升了检索质量和下游回答的准确性。

Conclusion: Critic-R 框架通过闭环反馈机制，无需人工标注即可优化代理搜索系统，同时提升了检索质量与回答准确度。

Abstract: Agentic search systems iteratively interact with retrieval models to answer complex queries. Despite substantial progress, optimizing retrievers for agentic search remains challenging, often requiring heavy co-training or gold-standard annotations that limit real-world applicability. We propose Critic-R, a framework that explicitly closes the feedback loop between the reasoning agent and the retrieval model during both inference and training. Critic-R introduces a critic model that evaluates the agent's introspective reasoning trace after consuming retrieved evidence to determine whether the retrieved context sufficiently supports the next reasoning step. Critic-R has two complementary mechanisms: Critic-R-Zero, an inference-time query refinement loop that iteratively rewrites queries and retrieval instructions, and Critic-Embed, an optimization approach for retrieval models that leverages successful and failed refinement trajectories as automatic supervision without requiring manual relevance annotation. We evaluate Critic-R on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, MuSiQue, and Bamboogle. Results show that Critic-R significantly improves both retrieval quality and downstream answer accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [MemGraphRAG: Memory-based Multi-Agent System for Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00610)
*Chuanjie Wu,Zhishang Xiang,Yunbo Tang,Zerui Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemGraphRAG框架，通过引入全局共享记忆的多智能体协作来构建高质量、逻辑一致的知识图谱，并配合特定的分层检索算法，有效解决了传统GraphRAG在处理大规模复杂数据时的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG在处理大规模非结构化语料时存在局限性，而现有基于片段的GraphRAG方法缺乏全局视角，导致知识图谱存在逻辑冲突、主题不一致及结构碎片化问题，严重影响检索准确性。

Method: 提出MemGraphRAG框架：1. 利用基于共享记忆的多智能体系统进行全局一致的知识图谱构建，支持智能体间的协作与冲突解决；2. 设计了适配该图谱结构的记忆感知分层检索算法（memory-aware hierarchical retrieval algorithm）。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，MemGraphRAG在具备同等计算效率的前提下，检索与生成表现优于现有的前沿（SOTA）模型。

Conclusion: MemGraphRAG通过引入基于共享记忆的多智能体协作模式，成功解决了传统GraphRAG在全局上下文缺失、逻辑冲突及结构碎片化方面的问题，在确保高效率的同时显著提升了复杂信息检索的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become an essential method for mitigating hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) by leveraging external knowledge. Although effective for simple queries, traditional RAG struggles with large-scale, unstructured corpora where information is highly fragmented. Graph-based RAG (GraphRAG) incorporates knowledge graphs to capture structural relationships, enabling more comprehensive retrieval for complex reasoning. However, existing GraphRAG methods rely on isolated, fragment-level extraction for graph construction, lacking a global perspective on the whole corpus. As a result, these methods frequently lead to thematically inconsistent, logically conflicting, and structurally fragmented graphs that degrade retrieval performance. In this paper, we propose MemGraphRAG, a novel framework that introduces a memory-based multi-agent system to ensure high-quality graph construction. Specifically, MemGraphRAG employs a collaborative society of agents supported by shared memory, which provides a unified global context throughout the extraction process. This mechanism allows agents to dynamically resolve logical conflicts and maintain structural connectivity throughout the corpus. Furthermore, we propose a memory-aware hierarchical retrieval algorithm tailored for the constructed graph. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that MemGraphRAG outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline models with comparable efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/MemGraphRAG.

</details>


### [109] [SpikeHash: Learning Binary Codes with Spiking Neural Networks for Cross-Modal Hashing Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00740)
*Yukuan Zhang,Jiarui Zhao,Shangqing Nie,Shengsheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SpikeHash 是一种新型跨模态哈希框架，通过脉冲神经网络模拟哈希状态演化与竞争性脉冲输出，在保持高效检索性能的同时降低了模型计算能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨模态哈希方法主要在连续特征空间学习语义，最后通过符号函数进行离散化，导致离散化过程与训练优化之间存在弱耦合问题，且计算成本较高。

Method: 提出了一个统一的脉冲哈希框架，核心包括：1. 将跨模态特征转换为多时间步脉冲序列；2. 设计共同驱动的哈希状态演化机制；3. 实施定向脉冲调制以实现跨模态交互；4. 引入正负脉冲竞争机制直接输出哈希位。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上取得了具有竞争力的检索准确率，同时在参数量、运算次数及估计能效方面表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: SpikeHash 证明了基于脉冲神经网络的哈希框架是一种高效且轻量级的替代方案，能够有效解决传统连续空间哈希学习中离散化过程导致的语义损失问题，同时显著降低计算能耗。

Abstract: Cross-modal hashing retrieval encodes heterogeneous data into compact binary codes for efficient Hamming-space search. Existing methods usually learn cross-modal semantics in continuous feature spaces and generate binary codes through a final sign operation, which weakly couples training optimization with discrete hash retrieval. We propose SpikeHash, a unified spiking framework that formulates cross-modal hashing as spike-state evolution, directional spike interaction, and competitive spike readout. Specifically, SpikeHash converts image and text features into multi-timestep spike sequences. In a shared Hamming space, the two spike sequences jointly drive the temporal evolution of a shared hash state. Cross-modal interaction is further performed through directional spike modulation, enabling each modality to influence the firing dynamics of the other. Crucially, SpikeHash replaces the conventional continuous hash head with a positive-negative spiking hash readout, where each hash bit is produced by temporal competition between paired spike channels. Experimental results show that SpikeHash achieves competitive retrieval accuracy on three benchmark datasets while reducing the parameter size, operation count, and estimated energy of the hash learning stage, suggesting a compact spiking alternative to conventional continuous hash mapping. The project page is available at https://shuqiao-111.github.io/.

</details>


### [110] [SkillPager: Query-Adaptive Intra-Skill Navigation via Semantic Node Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00822)
*Zicai Cui,Zihan Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SkillPager 通过将技能文档解析为语义节点并进行精准检索，在大幅降低 Token 消耗的同时，实现了与全文档提示相媲美的技能执行上下文充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决技能类 LLM 代理在使用长程序文档时，全文档提示带来的 Token 浪费及信息稀释问题。

Method: 提出了 SkillPager 两阶段框架：1. 离线阶段将 Markdown 文档解析为 typed semantic nodes；2. 在线阶段利用最大边缘相关性 (MMR) 进行全局、查询条件的节点筛选。

Result: 在包含395个技能和1975个查询的基准测试中，SkillPager 在保持 78.89% 的上下文充分性前提下，将 Prompt Token 减少了 47.04%，相较于现有的图基准表现提升了 12.16%。

Conclusion: 针对技能型LLM代理的文档检索问题，利用基于类型的语义节点（typed semantic nodes）比简单的原始固定长度分块更具效率优势，证实了 typed intra-document retrieval 是优化长程序文档处理的关键路径。

Abstract: Skill-based LLM agents increasingly rely on long procedural documents, but full-document prompting wastes tokens and dilutes information critical to execution. We study this setting as intra-skill retrieval, where the goal is to select a minimal, execution-sufficient context from a known skill document given a query. We present SkillPager, a two-stage framework that parses each Markdown skill into typed semantic nodes offline and leverages Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to perform global, query-conditioned node selection online. On a benchmark of 395 skills and 1,975 queries, SkillPager achieves 78.89% LLM-judged context sufficiency, compared to 82.23% for the exhaustive full-document baseline, while reducing prompt tokens by 47.04%. A granularity ablation shows that applying the same retrieval algorithm to raw fixed-length chunks reaches a comparable 81.77% sufficiency but increases token cost by 28.81%, demonstrating that efficiency gains are driven by typed semantic granularity rather than the retrieval algorithm alone. Among graph-based baselines, SkillPager outperforms the strongest baseline by a margin of 12.16%. Further ablations show that supporting content is most effective when retained in the candidate pool and selected adaptively rather than removed by static heuristics. These results identify typed intra-document retrieval as a distinct access problem for skill-based agents.

</details>


### [111] [Test-Time Training for Zero-Resource Dense Retrieval Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01070)
*Shiyan Liu,Yichen Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DART是一种无需额外监督训练、推理时自适应的重排序方法，通过对密集检索的初始结果进行快速微调，以极低的延迟实现了零样本场景下检索质量的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集检索器重排序面临“两难困境”：交叉编码器（Cross-encoder）虽然精度高但训练昂贵且延迟高；无监督的BM25重排序在多数数据集上表现不佳。

Method: 提出DART框架：1.利用当前查询的检索结果构建伪正负样本；2.通过少量梯度更新动态适配双线性评分矩阵 $W$；3.引入置信度加权边缘损失（confidence-weighted margin loss）和跨查询动量缓冲池以提升适配效果。

Result: 在6个BEIR基准测试上，相对NDCG@10平均提升2.1%，且单次查询额外延迟低于10ms。

Conclusion: DART通过在推理阶段进行自适应调整，有效地解决了零资源设置下密集检索器的重排序瓶颈，在低延迟的前提下显著提升了零样本检索性能。

Abstract: Dense retrievers excel at first-stage candidate generation but lack effective reranking in zero-resource settings. Existing approaches face a fundamental dilemma: cross-encoders deliver strong reranking quality but require costly supervised training and incur high latency, while unsupervised BM25 reranking consistently degrades dense retrieval performance on most of BEIR benchmarks. We propose DART (Dense Adaptive Reranking at Test-time), which resolves this dilemma by adapting the scoring function at inference time. For each query, the top-ranked documents serve as pseudo-positive examples and the bottom-ranked as pseudo-negative examples, providing noisy but readily available supervision to adapt a bilinear scoring matrix $W$ via a small number of gradient updates. We further introduce a confidence-weighted margin loss and a cross-query momentum buffer that warm-starts adaptation across queries. On six BEIR benchmarks, DART achieves a mean per-dataset relative NDCG@10 gain of +2.1% over the dense retrieval baseline with under 10ms additional latency per query, demonstrating a powerful capability for zero-shot performance enhancement and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [112] [Quantizing Intent: Cross-Domain Semantic IDs from Organic Activity for Industrial Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01396)
*Julie Choi,Haoran Ye,Zhiwei Ding,Bo Long,Benjamin Zelditch,Arpita Vats*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于跨域语义ID（SIDs）和RQ-FSQ量化技术的用户行为迁移方案，成功解决了广告领域用户行为稀疏的难题，在大幅压缩存储空间的同时显著提升了CTR预测性能和冷启动效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告点击率（CTR）预测面临严重的稀疏监督问题，即大部分用户在广告场景的交互极少。由于领域不匹配、服务成本和工程复杂性，有效利用外部的有机行为数据来提升广告推荐效果是一个技术难点。

Method: 1. 引入跨域用户语义ID（SIDs），从有机信息流行为数据中提取用户特征；2. 提出RQ-FSQ（残差有限标量量化）方法，对预训练嵌入进行离散化压缩；3. 设计层次化离散嵌入模块，通过前缀n-gram稀疏表进行端到端训练。

Result: 1. 直接使用Feed行为嵌入的SIDs带来了+0.213%的AUC提升；2. RQ-FSQ方法在实现~30倍存储压缩的同时，提升了各类嵌入的表现（最高达+0.351% AUC）；3. 在工业级系统中，冷启动用户的表现提升高达+1.522%。

Conclusion: 本文证明了跨域语义ID（SIDs）能够有效解决广告点击率预测中的稀疏监督问题，特别是对于冷启动用户。RQ-FSQ技术在保持甚至提升原始稠密嵌入性能的同时，通过显著压缩存储空间，为工业级大规模推荐系统提供了高效的落地方案。

Abstract: Ads click-through rate (CTR) prediction is constrained by sparse user supervision: most users engage with ads infrequently while generating dense behavioral evidence in organic surfaces such as feed. Transferring these cross-domain signals into ads ranking is difficult due to domain mismatch, serving cost, and production complexity. We introduce cross-domain user Semantic IDs (SIDs) derived from organic feed activity and show that behavioral activity richness governs cross-domain transfer quality: SIDs from user profile text yield +0.036% AUC, SIDs from an activity-tuned LLaMA-based user embedding model yield +0.107%, and SIDs from direct feed activity behavioral embeddings yield +0.213%. We further propose RQ-FSQ, a residual finite scalar quantization method that discretizes pre-trained embeddings while matching dense-embedding AUC at substantially smaller storage. Across two heterogeneous sources, RQ-FSQ matches or slightly exceeds dense source embeddings, achieving +0.351% AUC for Feed Activity at about 30x smaller storage and +0.265% AUC for Activity-Tuned LLaMA at about 280x smaller storage. We also introduce a Hierarchical Discrete Embedding module that encodes multi-level SIDs through prefix n-gram sparse embedding tables trained end-to-end under the CTR objective. In a large-scale industrial ads ranking system, cold-start segment analysis shows gains up to +1.522% for users with near-zero ad interaction history, validating cross-domain behavioral transfer as an effective bridge for sparse-history ranking.

</details>


### [113] [Semantic Retrieval for Product Search in E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01504)
*Nikhil Kothari,Saksham Samdani,Ritam Mallick,Praveen Gupta,Ankit Vijay,Surender Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段Siamese LLM训练方法，通过对比学习引入伪负样本过滤，并结合创新的ROAR偏好优化目标，显著提升了电商场景下处理短文本检索及细粒度产品排名的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商检索面临三大痛点：1. 查询词短且具有噪声、口语化；2. 超大规模产品目录；3. 产品间细粒度的属性差异难以区分。现有模型难以在精准匹配与相关替代品推荐之间取得平衡。

Method: 1. 架构：Siamese LLM双编码器。2. 两阶段训练：(1) 带伪负样本（false-negative）边际掩码的对比学习，处理近似重复品；(2) Relative Odds Alignment for Retrieval (ROAR)，一种基于Bradley-Terry模型的偏好优化目标，处理多维度分级相关性。3. 数据：利用替代品查询-产品对进行粗粒度监督，利用分级相关性标注实现细粒度排序。

Result: 该系统在不同查询频率分布和业务品类下均表现出稳定的性能提升，不仅能精准检索完全匹配产品，还能正确排序替代品和互补品，并已通过大规模线上A/B测试验证其统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Siamese LLM的双编码器架构，结合由粗到细的两阶段训练策略（对比学习+ROAR优化），有效解决了电商场景下短文本、噪声、细粒度属性挑战，并在大规模上线测试中验证了显著的检索性能提升。

Abstract: Semantic retrieval in e-commerce must handle short, noisy, and colloquial queries over large product catalogs with fine-grained attribute distinctions. We present a Siamese LLM dual-encoder trained through a two-stage pipeline: contrastive learning with a false-negative margin mask to prevent penalization of near-duplicate products, followed by Relative Odds Alignment for Retrieval (ROAR), a preference optimization objective that extends Bradley-Terry to variable-sized graded relevance groups via consecutive odds-ratio margins. The training corpus mirrors this progression - substitute query-product pairs provide coarse semantic supervision in Stage 1 and graded relevance annotations drive fine-grained ranking in Stage 2. The resulting system accurately retrieves exact matches while correctly ordering substitutes and complementary products, with gains confirmed across query-frequency strata and business verticals, and statistical significance validated through live A/B deployment at scale.

</details>


### [114] [TechGraphRAG: An Agentic Graph-Augmented RAG Framework for Technical Literature Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01613)
*Kanwar Bharat Singh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对特定技术领域（如车辆工程）的Agentic RAG框架，通过引入13步自主处理流程、多维度打分机制、外部数据库迭代循环及知识图谱，实现了高证据支撑度和高可靠性的学术技术推理支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用型RAG系统无法满足特定技术领域对严谨推理、证据溯源和深层上下文建模的高需求，旨在解决传统单通道检索在复杂技术问题上可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 提出了一个包含13个步骤的自主智能体管道：包括意图分类、多维度证据充分性评分（基于权重与衰减）、支持重试的查询重构（防漂移机制）、集成外部学术数据库（Crossref等）的迭代搜索循环、Neo4j知识图谱关联检索、以及包含引用检查与自动生成的后置质量控制环节。

Result: 构建了一个包含约2100篇学术文献的特定领域知识库，实现了具备多维度打分机制、知识图谱关联和自我纠正能力的智能推理系统，在保证引用完整性和内容质量方面表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种基于Agent的RAG框架，能够有效支持特定领域（智能轮胎、车辆动力学与控制）的深度技术推理，证明了通过自我纠正和多源检索循环，可以显著提升领域文献导航和技术推理的准确性和证据支撑度。

Abstract: This paper presents an agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for domain-specific technical reasoning support, instantiated over a curated corpus of approximately 2,100 academic papers in intelligent tires, vehicle dynamics, and vehicle control. Unlike conventional single-pass RAG systems, the proposed architecture employs a 13-step autonomous pipeline that classifies queries by intent, scores evidence sufficiency against a multi-dimensional rubric, performs agentic retry with drift-guarded query reformulation, searches external academic databases (Crossref, OpenAlex, Semantic Scholar) through iterative optimize--search--vet loops, traverses a Neo4j knowledge graph for relational context, verifies citation integrity, and applies post-generation quality checks with automatic regeneration. Key contributions include a 100-point evidence sufficiency scoring framework across five dimensions with relevance damping and hybrid rule-based/LLM review; a route-dependent external search architecture with iterative agentic loops; a knowledge graph constructed via LLM-based entity extraction and OpenAlex author validation with intra-corpus citation resolution; and a self-correcting generation loop with citation verification and quality assessment. The framework is presented as a practical, implemented case study illustrating how agentic, evidence-grounded RAG can support literature navigation and technical reasoning over large, domain-specific corpora.

</details>


### [115] [Time-Aware Diffusion based on Preference Disentanglement for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01670)
*Bangguo Zhu,Peng Huo,Yuanbo Zhao,Zhicheng Du,Jun Yin,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TDPM 提出了一种时间感知的扩散推荐框架，通过将用户偏好拆解为周期性和瞬时性特征，克服了现有扩散模型在捕捉动态用户兴趣方面的不足，显著提升了推荐准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型推荐系统（GRs）未能处理用户交互历史中随时间变化的动态性，仅对所有历史项进行均匀的扩散处理，忽略了用户偏好的非平稳分布特征。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TDPM (Time-aware Diffusion Prototypical Model) 的框架，旨在基于语义索引（SID）实现时间感知的扩散。该方法将用户偏好解耦为两部分：1. 长期保持一致的“周期偏好”（period preference）；2. 由近期事件触发的“瞬时偏好”（point preference），并将此时间演变特征显式融合进扩散生成过程中。

Result: TDPM 在三个公开真实数据集上的表现显著优于当前最先进的模型，在 HR@20 和 NDCG@20 指标上分别取得了最高 29.21% 和 25.45% 的提升。

Conclusion: TDPM 通过引入时间感知扩散机制，成功解决了现有生成式推荐模型在处理用户动态兴趣时的局限性。实验证明，显式地对长期偏移（period preference）和短期焦点（point preference）进行解耦建模，对于提升扩散模型在推荐系统中的性能至关重要。

Abstract: Recently, Generative Recommenders (GRs) have emerged as a transformative recommendation paradigm by replacing traditional item IDs with semantic indices (SIDs). Owing to the exceptional generative capabilities of diffusion models, a few pioneering works explore developing GRs with diffusion architectures as the backbone. However, a fatal limitation of existing diffusion-based GRs is that the diffusion process applies uniformly to all items within the historical interactions. In contrast, the user preference is shaped by multifaceted time-evolving factors and thus exhibits a non-stationary distribution in the temporal aspect. To bridge this gap, this study proposes a novel GR framework, named TDPM, by designing the time-aware diffusion on SID tokens. Specifically, TDPM explicitly integrates the impact of time-evolving user preferences into the diffusion process. In detail, the user preference is disentangled into (i) the period preference, which remains consistent over a long time-span, and (ii) the point preference, which is triggered by recent focal events. Extensive experiments on three public real-world datasets demonstrate the significant superiority of TDPM over the state-of-the-art baselines. TDPM achieves average improvements of up to 29.21% and 25.45% in terms of HR@20 and NDCG@20, respectively. The ablation study further underscores the necessity of time-aware token diffusion in diffusion-based GRs.

</details>


### [116] [Whole-Pool Setwise Reranking with Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01782)
*Hang Li,Chuting Yu,Teerapong Leelanupab,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对传统 LLM 重排序开销大的问题，提出了一种名为 DualEnd 的长上下文重排序方法，通过在单次调用中同时识别最相关和最不相关的段落，显著提升了排序效率与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于 LLM 的重排序方法因语境约束需要进行大量依赖性的模型调用，导致计算昂贵且推理缓慢。作者旨在利用长上下文 LLM 的能力，将重排序任务从“局部比较”转变为“全池化”处理，以优化效率。

Method: 提出了 Whole-Pool Setwise 重排序方法，并引入了 DualEnd 策略：在单次模型调用中同时识别出相关性最高和最低的段落，通过两端同时填充候选池来实现排序。

Result: 在九种开源 LLM 和两个重排序基准测试中验证，DualEnd 仅需 50 次串行调用即可完成 100 个候选段落的排序（相比传统方法的 99 次调用），有效降低了推理延迟、Token 消耗和调用次数，同时保持了输出的可靠性与效果。

Conclusion: 长上下文 LLM 不应仅被视为更大的提示空间，更应被利用来提升段落重排序任务的效率与效果。DualEnd 方法证明了通过双向填充策略，无需牺牲准确性即可显著降低推理开销。

Abstract: Previous LLM-based passage re-rankers are often expensive and slow because the input context constraints require the LLM to make many dependent model calls. We study how recent long-context LLMs change this problem: when the full set of retrieved candidate passages can be shown to the model at once, ranking no longer has to be reconstructed from many overlapping local comparisons. We propose Whole-Pool Setwise re-ranking, where each call considers all currently unranked candidate passages, and introduce DualEnd, which identifies both the most and least relevant passages in one call. By filling the ranking from both ends, DualEnd ranks 100 candidates with 50 serial LLM calls, compared with 99 calls for comparable one-passage-at-a-time whole-pool methods. Experiments with nine open-weight LLMs on two passage re-ranking benchmarks, measuring effectiveness, call count, token use, runtime, and output reliability shows that long context is not merely more prompt space, but an opportunity to make LLM re-rankers both effective and efficient.

</details>


### [117] [Breaking the Information Silo: Semantic Personas for Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01783)
*Jonathan Mayo,Moshe Unger,Konstantin Bauman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对传统跨域推荐依赖重合数据的问题，SPHERE 利用大模型提取语义用户画像，在无重叠用户/物品的互斥平台间实现了成功的知识迁移，显著提升了推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的许多跨域推荐系统依赖于共享用户、共享物品或相似的交互图结构，这在现实世界的独立平台间往往难以满足（即信息孤岛问题）。研究旨在解决如何在没有重叠数据的前提下，实现跨域知识的高效迁移。

Method: 提出了一种名为SPHERE的架构：1. 利用大语言模型（LLM）将用户行为转化为共享语义词汇并构建结构化语义“用户画像（Personas）”；2. 通过检索行为相似的源域社群构建“社群源画像”；3. 设计双塔架构与动态融合门控机制，将这些语义信号与传统的协同过滤信号相融合，以此增强现有的推荐系统骨干网络。该方法无需共享用户或物品，适用于严格互斥的跨域场景。

Result: 在 Amazon Books、Goodreads 和 Steam 数据集上的实验表明，SPHERE 在全排序评估中持续优于 NCF、SVD++ 和 LightGCN 等基准模型。验证了模型在处理严格互斥域时的有效性及其对推荐性能的提升作用。

Conclusion: 本研究通过将跨域推荐重构为基于行为的语义对齐，提出了一种实用的跨域知识迁移机制。研究表明，迁移效果不仅取决于域间的语义亲近度，还高度依赖于目标域的结构密度和预测潜力。SPHERE框架在保证可解释性和模块化的同时，有效打破了信息孤岛。

Abstract: Digital platforms increasingly operate as isolated information silos, limiting their ability to construct comprehensive user representations across domains. Cross-domain recommender systems seek to overcome this limitation by transferring knowledge from a source domain to a target domain, yet most existing approaches depend on shared users, shared items, or structurally similar interaction graphs. These assumptions are often unrealistic across independent platforms. We propose SPHERE (Semantic Personas for Heterogeneous cross-domain Recommendation), a design artifact that enables recommendation knowledge transfer across strictly disjoint domains with no shared users or items. Rather than aligning domains through identity or graph structure, SPHERE uses large language models to induce a shared behavioral vocabulary, generate structured semantic personas for users, and retrieve behaviorally similar source-domain communities that form a Community Source Persona. This semantic signal is integrated with collaborative signals through a dual-tower architecture and dynamic fusion gate, allowing SPHERE to augment standard recommender backbones. Empirical evaluation across Amazon Books, Goodreads, and Steam demonstrates consistent improvements over NCF, SVD++, and LightGCN baselines under full-ranking evaluation. The results show that cross-domain transfer effectiveness is not determined solely by semantic proximity between domains; rather, it depends critically on the structural density and native predictive strength of the target domain. The study contributes to information systems research by reframing cross-domain personalization as behavior-based semantic alignment, offering a practical mechanism for overcoming information silos while preserving interpretability and modularity.

</details>


### [118] [Decoupled Residual Quantization for Robust Semantic IDs in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01844)
*Xuesi Wang,Junjie Wang,Ziliang Wang,Weijie Bian,Guanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种诊断语义ID（Semantic IDs）失效的量化框架，并通过解耦残差量化（DRQ）优化了重构与量化过程，提升了工业推荐任务中的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义ID技术缺乏有效的诊断工具，难以分析分词器（tokenizer）失效的具体原因（如码本利用率不足、边界不稳定或几何失真），制约了推荐和检索系统的性能调优。

Method: 1. 提出定量诊断框架：利用“期望码字重叠”评估检索扰动下的码字混淆，利用“有效码本容量”量化可用码字数量。2. 引入解耦残差量化（DRQ）：将连续几何重构与离散分布匹配过程解耦，以缓解性能瓶颈。

Result: 在工业级数据集上的实验表明，该框架能准确识别语义边界混淆的成因（即码字分布不均与欧几里得几何限制），且DRQ方法在多个指标上均优于基准模型。

Conclusion: 语义ID的质量是一个多目标优化问题，需平衡符号鲁棒性、重构保真度及行为感知软匹配；提出的DRQ方法通过解耦重构与量化，有效改善了语义ID的表征质量。

Abstract: Semantic IDs represent items as shared discrete token sequences and have become a practical tool for recommendation and retrieval. Yet it remains difficult to tell why a tokenizer fails: poor quality may come from codebook underutilization, unstable decision boundaries, or geometric distortion of the embedding space. This paper develops a quantitative framework for diagnosing these failures through expected codeword overlap and effective codebook capacity. The former measures expected codeword confusion under retrieval-time perturbation, while the latter converts that confusion into an effective number of usable, well-separated codes. The framework links semantic boundary confusion to both code usage imbalance and Euclidean geometric constraints. As a proof of concept, we present Decoupled Residual Quantization (DRQ), which separates continuous geometry reconstruction from discrete distribution matching. Experiments on a large-scale industrial dataset show that Semantic ID quality is multi-objective: symbolic robustness, reconstruction fidelity, and behavior-aware soft matching each stress different aspects of a tokenizer.
