These downstream observations are based on one proprietary industrial dataset, so they should be read as a case study rather than a universal benchmark claim.

</details>


### [119] [Rank-Constrained Deep Matrix Completion for Group Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01948)
*Mubaraka Sani Ibrahim,Lehel Csató,Isah Charles Saidu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Group RC-DMC 框架，通过引入 Set-Transformer 和低秩矩阵补全技术，解决了群组推荐中因数据稀疏导致的影响，实现了高效且精准的个人与群组级推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群组推荐系统在处理现实中常见的高维、高度稀疏评分数据时表现不足，难以同时有效建模个人偏好聚合与组特征学习。

Method: 提出 Group RC-DMC 框架，结合了：1. 低秩矩阵补全（通过核范数近端算子和奇异值阈值处理）；2. 基于 Set-Transformer 的组聚合器（进行非线性组特征学习）；3. 线性编码器-解码器架构（低秩分解以提升推断效率）。

Result: 在 MovieLens 和 Goodbooks 数据集上实验表明，Group RC-DMC 显著降低了组 RMSE，且在 Precision、Recall 和 F1-score 等指标上表现出色，同时在不同规模的群组中均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Group RC-DMC 成功整合了显式低秩正则化与基于注意力的非线性组表达学习，有效解决了高维稀疏数据下的群组推荐难题，在重构精度和计算效率上均优于现有的 WBF 和 AF 基准方法。

Abstract: The growing popularity of group activities has increased the need for methods that provide recommendations to groups of users given their individual preferences. Many existing group recommender systems rely on aggregating individual user preferences, but they often struggle with high-dimensional and highly sparse rating data commonly found in real-world scenarios. We propose Group Rank-Constrained Deep Matrix Completion (Group RC-DMC), a novel framework that extends RC-DMC by integrating group-level representation learning via a Set-Transformer aggregator, jointly leveraging low-rank structure and attention-based nonlinear modeling. Unlike most existing group recommender systems, Group RC-DMC unifies explicit low-rank regularization, linear encoder-decoder architectures, and attention-based nonlinear group modeling within a single framework, yielding accurate predictions at both the individual and group levels. Group RC-DMC addresses data sparsity through low-rank matrix completion, computing per-user latent representations from observed ratings only, and enforcing a rank constraint on the latent space using a nuclear-norm proximal step based on periodic singular value thresholding. The decoder is parametrized as a low-rank factorization, enabling efficient inference. Experimental results on the MovieLens and Goodbooks datasets demonstrate that Group RC-DMC achieves superior reconstruction accuracy, measured by lower group RMSE, while remaining computationally efficient and competitive in group-level performance in terms of precision, recall, and F1 score compared with weighted-before-factorization (WBF) and after-factorization (AF) baselines. The results highlight the model's ability to recover the underlying low-rank structure of user-item interactions and provide robust group recommendations across small, medium, and large user groups.

</details>


### [120] [Dynamic Spectral Denoising with Global-Context Attention for Multi-Behavior Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02417)
*Miaomiao Cai,Yunshan Ma,Fangqi Zhu,Junfeng Fang,Zhijie Zhang,Zhiyong Cheng,Xiang Wang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多行为推荐中的噪声污染与行为间不一致性问题，本文提出了 SpectraMB 模型，通过特征频域动态滤波和全局上下文注意力融合，有效净化了用户表示并实现了可靠的跨行为信息聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多行为推荐时面临表征层面的瓶颈：一是行为内部的表示纠缠，由于多跳传播导致噪声与偏好混合，难以在去噪时保留细微信号；二是行为间的可靠性异质性，导致不可靠的辅助行为干扰目标行为预测。

Method: 提出 SpectraMB 模型，包含两个核心组件：1. 动态特征级谱滤波 (Dynamic Feature-Level Spectral Filtering)：将嵌入表示转换到特征频域，并在目标行为监督下进行自适应谱调制，实现细粒度表征净化；2. 全局上下文注意力融合 (Global-Context Attention Fusion)：以净化后的全局表示为锚点，评估不同行为的可靠性并进行加权聚合。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SpectraMB 在多数评估指标上达到了最优性能，且在存在噪声交互的情况下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SpectraMB 通过在特征频域进行动态滤波实现表征去噪，并利用全局上下文注意力机制缓解行为间的不一致性，显著提升了多行为推荐的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-behavior recommendation improves target-behavior prediction by exploiting heterogeneous auxiliary feedback (e.g., view, collect, and cart), yet its robustness is undermined by behavior-dependent noise and inconsistency. We argue that the key bottleneck is a representation-level failure caused by two coupled heterogeneities. First, intra-behavior representation entanglement arises when multi-hop propagation blends incidental signals with true preferences in the embedding space, making coarse spatial denoising unable to suppress noise without sacrificing informative niche signals. Second, inter-behavior reliability heterogeneity complicates cross-behavior fusion because the predictive value of auxiliary behaviors varies across users and contexts. Without reliability calibration, frequent yet unreliable signals may dominate aggregation and cause target-intent drift.
  To address this bottleneck, we propose Dynamic Spectral Denoising with Global-Context Attention for Multi-Behavior Recommendation (SpectraMB), a target-oriented model that performs representation purification before reliability-aware fusion. SpectraMB introduces Dynamic Feature-Level Spectral Filtering, which re-parameterizes embeddings along the feature dimension into a feature-frequency space and learns view-adaptive spectral modulation under target supervision, enabling component-wise purification without hand-crafted frequency assumptions. It further proposes Global-Context Attention Fusion, which uses a purified global representation as a context anchor to assess view compatibility and perform reliability-aware aggregation, while a residual global backbone preserves collaborative structure. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets show that SpectraMB achieves the best results in most evaluation settings and exhibits improved robustness under noisy interactions.

</details>


### [121] [ODTQA-FoRe: An Open-Domain Tabular Question Answering Dataset for Future Data Forecasting and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02433)
*Zhensheng Wang,Xiaole Liu,Wenmian Yang,Kun Zhou,Yiquan Zhang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 难以进行未来数值预测的问题，本文提出了 TimeFore 框架，通过检索、预测、分析三阶段协作，实现了对表格数据的精准预测与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 表格问答系统缺乏对“面向未来的数值预测”能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，并建立首个覆盖时间序列预测和基于预测推理的房地产领域数据集。

Method: 提出了 TimeFore 框架，该框架将任务分解为三个协作角色：1. Retriever（检索器）：生成 SQL 检索历史数据；2. Forecaster（预测器）：调用外部时间序列模型以提高预测准确性；3. Analyzer（分析器）：综合预测结果与数据构建最终回答。

Result: 通过实验验证，TimeFore 框架能够有效克服 LLM 在数据检索的精确性、预测能力的局限性以及响应标准化方面的挑战。

Conclusion: TimeFore 作为一个基于 LLM 智能体的框架，通过角色协作成功解决了开放领域表格数据预测与推理的问题，实验证明了其有效性。

Abstract: The rapid development of LLMs has significantly advanced tabular question answering, but most systems cannot perform future-oriented numerical prediction. To address this gap, we introduce a novel task, Open-Domain Tabular Question Answering for Future Data Forecasting and Reasoning, and propose the first dataset to cover time-series forecasting and forecast-based reasoning scenarios using real estate data. This task poses challenges in retrieving precise historical data, overcoming the forecasting limitations of LLMs, and standardizing responses for diverse queries. To solve the above challenges, we propose TimeFore, an LLM agent-based framework that decomposes the problem into three collaborative roles: a Retriever autonomously generates SQL to fetch data, a Forecaster invokes external time-series models for higher accuracy, and an Analyzer synthesizes the results to construct a precise and consistent final answer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our TimeFore.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Easy, robust approximate message passing for planted spike models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00500)
*Misha Ivkov,Tselil Schramm*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文为受局部对抗性噪声干扰的尖峰矩阵模型提供了一种鲁棒的AMP算法，通过谱预处理和鲁棒初始化，实现了对原始AMP结果的高效逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在“尖峰矩阵模型（spiked matrix setting）”中，当矩阵受到局部对抗性噪声（即支持在小主子式上的矩阵扰动）干扰时，现有的AMP算法（如稀疏PCA、非负PCA等）面临的稳健性挑战。

Method: 提出并分析了一种包含“谱预处理（spectral pre-processing）”和“鲁棒谱初始化（robust spectral initialization）”的算法框架，并证明了在此基础上的AMP算法能够输出接近未受损矩阵的计算结果。

Result: 对于受扰动矩阵 $Y=X+E$（$E$ 为局部小主子式上的对抗噪声），算法输出的向量 $v_{\mathrm{ALG}}(Y)$ 与在未受损矩阵 $X$ 上运行的 $v_{\mathrm{AMP}}(X)$ 在 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ 的误差范围内保持一致。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了即便在矩阵受到局部对抗性损坏的情况下，通过特定的预处理与鲁棒谱初始化，现有的近似消息传递（AMP）算法仍然能够保持稳健性，且无需修改核心迭代步骤。

Abstract: We present a simple and efficient algorithm for robust approximate message passing (AMP) in the spiked matrix setting. In particular, let $\varepsilon$ be a sufficiently small constant, and suppose that $X \in \mathbb R^{n \times n}$ is a Gaussian matrix with a planted rank-$1$ spike, and $E \in \mathbb R^{n \times n}$ is an adversarially chosen matrix supported on an $\varepsilon n \times \varepsilon n$ principal minor. Let $v_{\mathrm{AMP}}(X)$ be the output of an AMP iteration on the uncorrupted matrix $X$. We give a procedure that, given access only to the corrupted matrix $Y = X + E$, computes a vector $v_{\mathrm{ALG}}(Y)$ which is $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$-close to $v_{\mathrm{AMP}}(X)$, for any of a class of AMP iterations which includes sparse Principal Component Analysis (PCA), non-negative PCA, and $\mathbb Z_2$ synchronization. Our algorithm consists of a spectral pre-processing step combined with a robust spectral initialization procedure; given these inputs, we prove that (perhaps surprisingly) AMP is robust out-of-the-box.

</details>


### [123] [Eulerian-spanning set and coboundary operator: An investigation of maxcut beyond planar graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00725)
*Qiming Fang,Sihong Shao,Yuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将平面图Max-Cut问题的Hadlock转换推广至一般图，提出了一种针对交叉数为k的图的FPT算法，时间复杂度达到现有最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Max-Cut问题的转换算法主要局限于平面图，需要将此方法推广至更广泛的图类，以探索更具普适性的求解路径。

Method: 结合Eulerian生成集（Eulerian-spanning set）概念与上边缘算子（coboundary operator），将Hadlock针对平面图的Max-Cut转换方法泛化至一般图，并利用该转换推导算法。

Result: 提出了一种针对交叉数为k的图形的算法，其时间复杂度为$O(2^k(n+k)^{3/2}\log (n+k))$，在非负权值限制下达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 通过引入Eulerian生成集和上边缘算子，成功将平面图Max-Cut问题的处理方法推广至含非负权值的通用图，并为$k$-压缩顶点图（k-contraction apex graphs）及交叉数（crossing number）为k的图提供了高效的固定参数可处理（FPT）算法。

Abstract: Using the concepts of Eulerian-spanning set and coboundary operator, we generalize Hadlock's conversion of the maxcut problem on planar graphs to one on general graphs with non-negative weights. Using our conversion, we can explore algorithms for maxcut beyond the class of planar graphs. We obtain a Fixed-Parameter Tractable algorithm for $k$-contraction apex graphs. Specifically, our algorithm can be applied to graphs with crossing number $k$, giving an $O(2^k(n+k)^{3/2}\log (n+k))$-time algorithm that matches the best known results when restricted to non-negative weights.

</details>


### [124] [Constant-Stretch Rounding on the Hypersimplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00996)
*Nima Anari,Alireza Haqi,Eric Ma*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种针对超单纯形（hypersimplex）的常数拉伸相关舍入方案，通过基于最大熵分布的公共随机排序和阈值技术，将输出差异控制在输入差异的 6 倍以内，打破了既往 $O(\log k)$ 的理论限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超单纯形上进行相关舍入是组合优化中的一个基本问题。之前的相关舍入方案在拉伸因子上存在 $O(\log k)$ 的限制，研究者旨在寻找一种能够实现常数拉伸（constant-stretch）的方案，以优化在该领域中对相关采样的准确性要求。

Method: 该方法利用了最大熵 $k$-子集分布及其固有的边际分布特性，通过采用“公共随机排序”和“公共均匀阈值”的机制来实现相关舍入。该技术有效地将输入点的变化耦合到输出 $k$-子集的变化中，从而在保证边际分布一致性的同时最小化输出的差异。

Result: 给出了一种常数因子为 6 的相关舍入方案。在该方案下，对于任意输入 $x,y$，输出集合的对称差的期望值严格受限于输入向量 $L_1$ 范数差值的 6 倍。该结论显著优于之前的理论界，同时推广至“至多 $k$”多面体时同样取得了优良的常数界。值得注意的是，该论文的证明部分由 AI（GPT 5.5 Pro Extended）协助生成，且代码辅助工具 Codex 参与了手稿整合。

Conclusion: 提出了一种用于超单纯形（hypersimplex）的常数拉伸相关舍入方案。证明了对于任意输入 $x,y \in [0,1]^n$（满足 $\sum x_i = \sum y_i = k$），该方案满足 $\mathbb{E}[|A(x)\triangle A(y)|]\le 6\|x-y\|_1$。该结果改进了之前的 $O(\log k)$ 界，并回答了相关领域的一个公开问题。此外，该方案通过增加虚拟坐标可扩展至“至多 $k$”多面体（at-most-k polytope），拉伸因子不超过 12。

Abstract: We study correlated rounding on the hypersimplex, the base polytope of the uniform matroid. For each point $x$ in the hypersimplex, the goal is to sample a $k$-subset $A(x)$ with marginals $x$, while coupling the samples for all choices of $x$ so that nearby inputs produce nearby sets. We give a constant-stretch scheme. Our scheme samples the maximum-entropy $k$-subset distribution with prescribed marginals using a common random ordering and common uniform thresholds. For every $x,y\in[0,1]^n$ with $\sum_i x_i=\sum_i y_i=k$, it satisfies $\mathbb{E}[|A(x)\triangle A(y)|]\le 6\|x-y\|_1$. This improves the previous $O(\log k)$ bound for hypersimplex correlated rounding and answers an open question raised by Naor, Raju, Shetty, Srinivasan, Valieva, and Wajc. By adding dummy coordinates, the same result gives stretch at most $12$ for the at-most-$k$ polytope. The proof was found in a GPT 5.5 Pro Extended conversation prompted by the authors, and Codex was used to help assemble the manuscript under the authors' supervision.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamic Breadth First Search with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01187)
*Shahbaz Khan,Shubham Kumar Verma,Utkarsh Lohiya*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于预测模型的动态图 BFS 树维护算法，通过量化预测误差（$η$），在增量、减量及全动态环境下，实现了与预测准确度挂钩的高效更新，突破了传统动态 BFS 算法在最坏情况下的复杂度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 BFS 树维护算法在最坏情况下的复杂度受限（$O(mn)$ 总时间），且受限于组合 BMM 猜想，难以取得突破。该研究旨在通过引入“预测模型”，利用预测的更新信息来优化动态图 BFS 树的实时更新效率，打破经典算法的性能限制。

Method: 1. 基于经典 ES Tree (Even-Shiloach Trees) 的扩展，针对批量更新和全动态环境进行优化。
2. 引入预测误差度量框架（$η_v, η^*_v, η_e$），根据预测偏差的程度量化算法性能。
3. 结合空间优化技术和误差校正机制，在保持经典预处理开销的前提下，降低更新时间复杂度。

Result: 1. 增量环境：最坏情况更新时间 $O(η_v + η_e)$；
2. 减量环境：最坏情况更新时间 $O(\min\{m, η^*_v + η_e\})$；
3. 全动态环境：最坏情况更新时间 $O(\min\{m, η^*_v + η_e\})$。
4. 总更新时间在相关设置下可达 $O(η^*_v + η_e)$。

Conclusion: 通过利用预测模型，该研究在动态图（增量、减量及全动态）BFS树维护问题上提出了首个受预测误差性能界定的算法。尽管经典算法在最坏情况下性能受限，但当预测准确时，新算法能显著降低更新开销，在动态图算法领域提供了一种有效利用预测信息以规避经典复杂度瓶颈的新范式。

Abstract: Given a graph $G(V,E)$ having $n$ vertices and $m$ edges, we maintain its Breadth-First Search (BFS) tree from source $s$ under an online sequence of edge updates in the prediction model. Our approach leverages a predicted update sequence aiding online processing. We present algorithms for incremental (insertions-only), decremental (deletions-only), and fully dynamic (insertions and deletions) settings that maintain a BFS tree (parent and level information). Classically, the incremental and decremental BFS tree requires total $O(mn)$ time [JACM81], with amortized $O(n)$ and worst-case $O(m)$ update time. The combinatorial BMM conjecture restricts any polynomial improvement [FOCS14] even when the updates are known in advance [STOC15]. For fully dynamic BFS trees, only the trivial $O(m)$ time recomputation is known.
  Our complexity bounds are expressed in prediction error measures, where error vertices are those having incorrectly predicted distances, with the corresponding difference as their error. The vertex prediction error $η_{v}$ is the sum of degrees of error vertices, weighted vertex prediction error $η^*_{v}$ is error-weighted sum of degrees of error vertices, and $η_e$ counts the incorrectly predicted updates.
  For incremental and decremental BFS, our algorithm requires respectively $O(η_v + η_e)$ and $O(\min\{m,η^*_v + η_e\})$ worst case update time using $O(mn)$ preprocessing time and space, and total update time of $O(η^*_v + η_e)$. For fully-dynamic updates, our algorithm requires $O(\min\{m,η^*_v+η_e\})$ worst case update time. At its core, we extend the classical ES Trees [JACM81] for batch updates and fully dynamic updates. This simple extension is sufficient to give a competitive prediction algorithm, which may be generalized to other graph problems. We also consider space optimizations and error correction to improve our results.

</details>


### [126] [The anti-lexicographic SUS-anchor: a near-optimal k=1 sampling scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01190)
*Groot Koerkamp,Ragnar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 SUS-anchor 的序列采样方案，通过优先选择窗口内的独特后缀进行采样，在小 $k$ 值条件下显著降低了采样密度，克服了传统 minimizer 和现有通用 sampling 方案的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 minimizer 方案（如 mod-minimizer 和 greedy minimizer）在处理较小的 $k$ 值时表现不佳，而现有的通用采样方案（如 bd-anchors）在小 $k$ 值下的采样密度距离理论下界仍有较大差距，需要一种更优化且通用的采样机制。

Method: 提出 SUS-anchor 采样方案：在给定窗口内，选取所有仅在窗口内出现一次的后缀中，通过特定的“反字典序”（最小化首字符，最大化剩余字符）确定的最小后缀作为采样锚点；并给出了一个线性时间和 $O(w)$ 空间的流式算法。

Result: 在 $\sigma=4, k=1$ 时，SUS-anchors 的采样密度比理论下界高不到 $1\%$（优于 bd-anchors 的 $>15\%$）；在 $\sigma=2$ 时，密度比理论下界高不超过 $10\%$（优于 bd-anchors 的 $>50\%$）。

Conclusion: SUS-anchors 提供了一种高效的序列采样方法，在小 $k$ 值（特别是 $k=1$）和不同字母表大小下，其采样密度显著优于现有的 bd-anchors，且非常接近理论下界。

Abstract: In recent years, there has been a renewed interest in the search for low density minimizer schemes. These schemes take a window of $w$ consecutive $k$-mers, and sample one of them: the smallest under some specific order. Schemes such as the mod-minimizer provide a low density (fraction of sampled $k$-mers) when $k \gg w$, while schemes such as the greedy minimizer work well for explicit small parameters roughly in the regime $k \leq 2w$, for $k$ and $w$ up to $15$ or so.
  When $k < \log_σw$ is very small, minimizer schemes cannot do well, and more general sampling schemes are needed that can be richer than just comparing $k$-mers. Bidirectional-string anchors (bd-anchors) form one such scheme.
  Inspired by bd-anchors, we introduce the smallest unique substring or SUS-anchor: Given a window, this considers all suffixes that do not occur as a substring elsewhere in the window. It then samples the start position of the smallest suffix according to the new anti-lexicographic order that minimizes the first character and maximizes the remaining characters. We give a linear-time and $O(w)$ space streaming algorithm to compute all SUS-anchors of a string.
  For alphabet size $σ=4$ and $k=1$, the anti-lexicographic SUS-anchor empirically has density $<1\%$ away from the density lower bound, significantly improving over bd-anchors that are often $>15\%$ above it. For alphabet size $σ=2$, the density is at most $10\%$ above the lower bound, which again improves over the $>50\%$ overhead of bd-anchors.

</details>


### [127] [Multiagent Matroid Upgrading: Greedy is Fair and Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01309)
*Qingwen Ma,Chao Peng,Changfeng Xu,Chenyang Xu,Ruilong Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了“多智能体拟阵升级”问题模型，通过为不同智能体分配资源并进行有限的成本“升级”，利用拟阵理论优化非减凸函数，从而在多智能体系统中实现高效与公平的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决实际多智能体系统中资源分配的复杂性，通过引入“升级”机制（降低成本）并在多个智能体之间平衡资源，以同时满足效率（最小化总成本）和公平性（通过凸函数平衡不同智能体利益）的需求。

Method: 建立了多智能体拟阵升级问题的数学模型，将问题定义为在选定至多 k 个元素升级的前提下，最小化各智能体最小基成本的非减凸函数。通过拟阵理论与凸优化方法的结合，设计了相应的求解算法。

Result: 提出了一个通用框架来建模并求解此类资源分配任务，证明了该方法能够有效处理多智能体环境下同时兼顾效率与公平性的资源配置挑战。

Conclusion: 该论文确立了多智能体拟阵升级问题的计算复杂度和算法框架。证明了在该问题中，通过优化升级策略，即使在复杂的公平性和效率权衡目标下，也能够有效地求解多智能体资源分配问题。

Abstract: This paper introduces a general multiagent matroid upgrading problem that models a broad class of real-world resource allocation tasks. In this setting, there are multiple agents and a ground set of elements, where each element is assigned to a specific agent and has two associated costs: a default cost and a reduced (upgraded) cost. Upgrading an element lowers its cost to the upgraded value, while non-upgraded elements retain their default costs. Each agent is associated with its own matroid, with the goal of finding a minimum-cost basis. The central task is to select at most k elements to upgrade so as to minimize a non-decreasing convex function over the agents' minimum basis costs, capturing both efficiency and fairness objectives in multiagent systems.

</details>


### [128] [On Thin Perfect Matchings up to Polylogarithmic Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01330)
*Alireza Haqi,Shayan Oveis Gharan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对“稀疏匹配问题”取得了突破，证明了对于任何分数完美匹配，总存在一个 polylog(n)-thin 的完美匹配，并给出了相应的算法分析及应用讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 Anari 等人 [ACR23] 提出的关于完美匹配是否能作为分数完美匹配的“稀疏化”近似（即 thin matching）的猜想。

Method: 作者分析了匹配是否必须位于分数完美匹配支撑集内的两种情况。针对支撑集受限情况，设计了 O(n log n)-thin 匹配算法；针对非受限情况，通过去限制证明了存在 polylog(n)-thin 的完美匹配。结果适用于二部图和非二部图。

Result: 1. 若匹配限制在分数匹配的支撑集中，则 α 至少为 Ω(n)，并设计了 O(n log n) 的算法；2. 若取消该限制，成功证明了存在 polylog(n)-thin 的完美匹配，实现了对 ACR23 猜想的近乎最优解答。

Conclusion: 本文解决了“稀疏匹配问题”（Thin Matching Problem），证明了对于任意分数完美匹配，均存在 polylog(n)-thin 的完美匹配，修正了此前关于匹配需在支撑集内的限制观点。

Abstract: We resolve the thin matching problem proposed by Anari, Charikar and Ramakrishnan [ACR23] up to polylogarithmic factors. Given a fractional perfect matching $x$, we say a perfect matching $M$ is $α$-thin w.r.t. $x$ if for any cut $(S,\overline{S})$, we have $$ |M \cap E(S,\overline{S})| \leq α\cdot x(S,\overline{S}).$$ [ACR23] conjectured that for any fractional perfect matching $x$, there exists a perfect matching $M$ which is $O(1)$-thin w.r.t. $x$.
  First, we show that if $M$ is restricted to be in the support of $x$, then $α\geq Ω(n)$ and we complement this by designing an efficient algorithm that outputs an $O(n\log n)$-thin perfect matching where $n$ is the number of vertices.
  Then, we relax this constraint and show that for any fractional perfect matching $x$, there is a perfect matching $M$ (which is not necessarily in the support of $x$) such that $M$ is $\text{polylog}(n)$-thin w.r.t. $x$. All results work for both bipartite and non-bipartite graphs. We also discuss applications to the metric distortion problem.

</details>


### [129] [Adversarial Configurations for the ReCom Transition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01333)
*Micah Gold*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了计算重划选区中常用的ReCom算法在最坏情况下不具备多项式时间收敛性，即不是快速混合的，且在特定图结构下其收敛所需的步数为指数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReCom作为计算重划选区中采样平衡图划分的主流MCMC算法，尽管在实践中表现高效，但其理论上的多项式时间保证（是否属于多项式时间算法）此前尚不明确。此研究旨在从理论上明确其计算复杂性。

Method: 构造了一类特定的平面方格图上的3划分家族，并对ReCom算法在该结构下的分割过程进行分析，评估其重划分（re-split）的期望步数。

Result: 在该特定的图形家族中，ReCom算法在重划分时需要指数级的预期步数，证明了该算法在最坏情况下不具备多项式时间收敛性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了ReCom算法在最坏情况下无法在多项式时间内完成采样（即不满足多项式时间复杂性约束），并且不是快速混合的（rapidly mixing），这意味着从受限的初始状态出发，其达到平稳分布需要指数级的时间。

Abstract: ReCom is a leading Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithm for sampling balanced graph partitions in computational redistricting. At each step, its transition function proposes a new partition by merging two adjacent districts and if possible re-splitting the conjoined region. The transition function is efficient in practice, however, it is unknown whether it is guaranteed to run in polynomial time. In this report we exhibit an explicit family of 3-partitions on planar square grid graphs from which ReCom requires an exponentially large expected number of steps to re-split the graph (even if we admit approximately balanced splits), showing that in the worst case ReCom does not run in polynomial time. Notably, this result implies that ReCom is not technically rapidly mixing (if started from an adversarial configuration, ReCom requires exponential many steps to reach the stationary distribution).

</details>


### [130] [Towards Optimal Robustness in Learning-Augmented Paging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01342)
*Peng Chen,Hailiang Zhao,Xueyan Tang,Yixuan Wang,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了学习增强分页算法中鲁棒性竞争比的理论缺口，通过引入“相对预测预算”框架，实现了最优的 H_k + O(1) 鲁棒边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习增强分页算法在随机化设置下的鲁棒性边界为 2H_k + O(1)，未能达到理论最优值 H_k，旨在缩小这一理论性能差距并提升实际性能。

Method: 1. 分析现有 H_k-竞争算法的特性；2. 提出“相对预测预算”概念，揭示现有算法在预测利用上的局限性；3. 构建一个新的算法框架，以优化预测的使用效率。

Result: 新框架在理论上实现了最优的鲁棒性边界 H_k + O(1)，实验表明该算法在实际应用中表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过引入“相对预测预算（relative prediction budget）”统一框架，本文成功将学习增强分页算法的鲁棒性竞争比提升至最优的 H_k + O(1)，消除了理论上的性能差距。

Abstract: Learning-augmented paging has been extensively studied in recent years. A key advantage over naive ML-based approaches is \emph{bounded robustness}, which guarantees worst-case performance even when predictions are inaccurate, making these algorithms valuable for real-world systems. Prior work achieves robustness bounds of $2H_k + O(1)$ in the randomized setting, leaving a gap to the optimal competitive ratio $H_k$.
  In this paper, we study how to close this gap. We begin by reviewing online optimality and proving a new property of the latest $H_k$-competitive algorithm, which facilitates our analysis in the learning-augmented setting. Then, we review existing learning-augmented paging algorithms and introduce a unifying primitive, the \emph{relative prediction budget}, which captures the essence of establishing robustness and reveals that prior algorithms either overuse or underutilize predictions. Guided by the above analysis, we develop a new framework that achieves the best-possible robustness up to an additive constant for learning-augmented paging: $H_k + O(1)$. Experiments further demonstrate strong practical performance.

</details>


### [131] [A Near-Optimal Offline Algorithm for Dynamic All-Pairs Shortest Paths in Planar Digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01809)
*Debarati Das,Maximilian Probst Gutenberg,Christian Wulff-Nilsen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对平面图全源最短路径（APSP）问题，提出了离线设置下接近理论最优的 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ 时间复杂度算法，并改进了在线增量APSP问题的性能，为解决动态APSP问题提供了重要进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平面动态全源最短路径（APSP）问题中，现有最优算法的时间复杂度为 $\tilde{O}(n^{2/3})$，而条件性下界暗示存在 $n^{0.5-\delta}$ 的性能瓶颈。作者旨在打破现有界限，寻求更接近理论最优值的 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ 算法。

Method: 1. 在离线场景下，通过提出首个能比从头计算更快的稠密距离图（DDG）维护算法，实现了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ 的更新与查询时间。 2. 在在线场景下，设计了增量APSP算法，并将在线动态APSP问题归约为在线减量APSP问题。

Result: 1. 实现了首个在离线设置下，更新和查询时间均为 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ 的算法。 2. 实现了在线增量APSP问题的 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ 最坏情况时间复杂度算法。

Conclusion: 该研究为平面图全源最短路径（APSP）问题提供了新的算法突破，成功在离线场景下达到近最优的 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ 更新与查询时间，并为在线增量APSP问题提供了相同复杂度的算法，为解决在线动态APSP这一难题迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: In the planar, dynamic All-Pairs Shortest Paths (APSP) problem, a planar, weighted digraph $G$ undergoes a sequence of edge weight updates and the goal is to maintain a data structure on $G$, that can quickly answer distance queries between any two vertices $x,y \in V(G)$.
  The currently best algorithms for this problem require $\tilde{O}(n^{2/3})$ worst-case update and query time, while conditional lower bounds show that either update or query time $n^{0.5-δ}$ is needed for any constant $δ> 0$.
  In this article, we present the first algorithm with near-optimal $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ worst-case update and query time for the offline setting, where the update sequence is given initially. This result is obtained by giving the first offline dynamic algorithm for maintaining dense distance graphs (DDGs) faster than recomputing from scratch after each update.
  Further, we also present an \emph{online} algorithm for the incremental APSP problem with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ worst-case update/ query time. This allows us to reduce the online dynamic APSP problem to the online decremental APSP problem, which constitutes partial progress even for the online version of this notorious problem.

</details>


### [132] [Efficiently Listing Projected Trees, and Equivalence of Listing and Enumeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02183)
*Karl Bringmann,Nick Fischer,Yanheng Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了带投影树枚举的首个多项式预处理算法，并通过建立通用的枚举-列表归约，证明了列表与枚举在计算复杂性上的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决子图同构及其推广问题（如带投影的合取查询）中，枚举和列表算法在处理大规模数据时尚存在较高复杂度的问题。

Method: 1. 利用快速矩形矩阵乘法和输出敏感矩阵乘法技术；2. 建立通用的“从枚举到列表”的归约方法。

Result: 1. 实现了带投影树枚举的首个多项式预处理时间（$\widetilde{O}(n^{17.42})$）与多对数延迟算法；2. 提出了通用归约方法，证明了子图同构枚举与列表任务在复杂度和效率上的等价性；3. 通过细粒度复杂性分析支撑了结果的理论地位。

Conclusion: 本文证明了投影树枚举可实现多项式预处理时间和多对数延迟，并确立了列表算法与枚举算法在自然假设下的等价性。此外，研究指出突破现有时间复杂度界限必然依赖于快速矩阵乘法。

Abstract: The subgraph isomorphism problem and its generalizations such as conjunctive queries, where some nodes are projected, are among the most fundamental problems in graph algorithms and database theory. In this paper, we study the listing and enumeration variants of these problems and present two main results.
  (1) We present the first algorithms for enumerating projected trees with polynomial preprocessing time ($\widetilde{O}(n^{17.42})$) and polylogarithmic delay ($\mathrm{polylog}(n)$). Prior to this work, all algorithms in the literature required time $Ω(n^{Ω(k)} + t)$ or $t \cdot n^{Ω(1)}$ to list all copies of a $k$-node tree with projections, where $t$ is the number of solutions. Our result generalizes to arbitrary projected hypergraphs, achieving enumeration in preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}(m^{17.42 \cdot \mathrm{subw}(H)})$ and polylogarithmic delay, where $\mathrm{subw}(H)$ is the submodular width of the pattern hypergraph $H$. We heavily rely on fast (rectangular and output-sensitive) matrix multiplication, which we complement by fine-grained lower bounds indicating that any algorithm beating time $Ω(n^{Ω(k)} + t)$ must rely on fast matrix multiplication.
  (2) As our second main result, we present a generic enumeration-to-listing reduction, establishing that listing and enumeration are equivalent under natural assumptions. For (colored) subgraph isomorphism, our reduction transforms any listing algorithm running in time $O(f(n,m) + t \cdot g(n,m))$ into an enumeration algorithm with preprocessing time $O((f(n,m)+g(n,m)+m) \log^2 n)$ and delay $O(g(n,m))$. We utilize this equivalence as a tool for proving our first main result, and we expect that our generic reduction will find many future applications.

</details>


### [133] [The Completion-Threshold Framework for Obligatory-Test Scheduling on Multiple Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02029)
*Kao-Chuan Liang,Ya-Chun Liang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对带必选测试的多机在线调度问题，本文利用“完成阈值”分析法，首次确立了该问题的多机下界（渐近3/2），并给出了一个2-竞争列表调度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机在线调度且必须执行必选测试（obligatory testing）的场景下，现有研究缺乏多机环境的确定性下界，且如何平衡“测试以获取信息”与“处理已知作业”之间的权衡是该领域亟待解决的核心算法挑战。

Method: 1. 框架构建：引入并利用完成阈值（Completion-threshold quantities, $T_X$）作为分析指标。2. 下界推导：通过对阈值的界定，系统推导出多机环境下的确定性下界（包括1.4811的三类型界和渐近3/2的动态构造）。3. 算法设计：设计了一种适用于任意测试时间的确定性2-竞争列表调度算法。

Result: 1. 建立了多机调度问题中总完成时间目标函数的最强确定性下界，包括精确值1.4811以及渐近下界1.5。2. 提出了一种确定性2-竞争列表调度算法。

Conclusion: 本文引入了“完成阈值”($T_X$)作为分析在线调度问题的核心指标，建立了多机环境下总完成时间问题的确定性下界（渐近趋近于1.5），并提出了一种测试时间任意的2-竞争列表调度算法，弥补了该领域多机设置下的理论空白。

Abstract: We study online scheduling with obligatory testing on $m$ identical machines with the objective of minimizing the sum of completion times. In this model, every job must undergo a test before its actual processing time is revealed. Consequently, the central algorithmic challenge is no longer whether to acquire information, but how to optimally balance machine capacity between revealing unknown jobs and processing currently known ones. While this tradeoff becomes structurally richer in the multiple-machine setting, the only prior explicit deterministic lower bound for this objective was $\sqrt{2}$, established strictly for a single machine in 2024 by Dogeas et al. [ESA 2024: 48:1-48:14]. Our core conceptual contribution is demonstrating that completion-threshold quantities, denoted $T_X$, serve as the fundamental analytical metric for this setting. Because every completed job must first pass through the testing phase, delayed revelation inherently forces delayed completion. By bounding these $T_X$ thresholds, we systematically derive strong lower bounds on the total completion time. Utilizing this framework, we establish the first substantial deterministic lower bounds for multiple machines, including a three-type bound of $1.4811$ and a multi-type dyadic construction that asymptotically approaches $3/2$. Finally, we complement these theoretical limits with a deterministic $2$-competitive list-scheduling algorithm for arbitrary test times.

</details>


### [134] [Exact Sampling of Permutations with a Fixed Longest Increasing Subsequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02263)
*Peter Clifford,Raphaël Clifford*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种针对具有特定 LIS 长度 k 的排列的精确均匀采样算法：针对 k ∈ Θ(n) 的高效拒绝采样算法，以及适用于任意 k 的基于 Robinson–Schensted 对应和行列式优化的采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确生成具有给定最长递增子序列（LIS）长度的排列在组合数学和概率计算中具有重要意义，但由于其约束条件复杂，存在计算效率低下或难以直接采样的问题，需要更有效的算法。

Method: 1. 对于 k ∈ Θ(n)，提出了一种直接拒绝采样法，通过扩展提案空间并利用左侧 LIS 的性质进行筛选。
2. 对于任意 1 ≤ k ≤ n，基于 Robinson–Schensted 对应关系，通过行列式恒等式计算计数，并结合 Hankel 矩矩阵优化行列式预言机，实现采样。

Result: 1. k ∈ Θ(n) 时的拒绝采样算法预期运行时间为 O(n log log n)（word-RAM 模型）。
2. 基于 Robinson–Schensted 的通用算法预期运行时间从 O(n^4 k^5) 优化至 O(n^3 k^4)（基于 Hankel 矩阵）。

Conclusion: 本文为生成给定最长递增子序列（LIS）长度 k 的长度为 n 的排列提供了两种高效的精确均匀采样算法，解决了在该约束条件下进行采样的高计算复杂度问题。

Abstract: We study exact uniform sampling of permutations of length $n$ whose longest increasing subsequence (LIS) has prescribed length $k$. For $k \in Θ(n)$, we give a direct rejection sampler whose expected running time is $O(n\log\log n)$ in the word-RAM model. The sampler uses an expanded proposal space consisting of permutations together with a specified increasing subsequence, and accepts exactly those proposals whose specified subsequence is the leftmost LIS. For arbitrary $1\le k\le n$, we give an exact sampler based on the Robinson--Schensted correspondence. The algorithm samples the corresponding Plancherel-conditioned shape by computing exact completion counts via determinant identities, and then samples two uniform tableaux of that shape. The direct implementation runs in $\tilde O(n^4k^5)$ expected time. We then show that the same sampler can be implemented in expected $\tilde O(n^3k^4)$ time by evaluating a determinant oracle through Hankel moment matrices.

</details>


### [135] [Terminal Steiner tree problem : Complexity and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02325)
*Jyothish S,Sadagopan Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了终端斯坦纳树问题的复杂性，给出了其在不同图类中的表现，并提出了一种针对终端数量的FPT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯坦纳树（ST）问题是经典的NP完全问题，而终端斯坦纳树（所有终端节点必须为叶子节点）作为一种变体，其复杂性、在不同图类中的表现及定参算法尚需深入研究。

Method: 通过理论分析探讨不同图类下的复杂性，利用黑盒归约方法研究其应用，并设计了关于终端数量的参数化算法（FPT算法）。

Result: 确立了终端斯坦纳树在多种图类下的复杂性结果，并提出了一个以终端数量为参数的最佳定参可解（FPT）算法。

Conclusion: 本文通过研究终端斯坦纳树的性质，从复杂性、应用及算法设计（FPT）等多个维度完善了该问题的理论框架。

Abstract: Given a connected graph $G$ and a terminal set $R \subseteq V(G)$, the Steiner tree problem (ST) asks for a tree that spans all of $R$ with at most $r$ vertices from $V(G)\backslash R$, for some integer $r\geq 0$. It is known from (Garey et al.,1977 ) that ST is NP-complete. A Steiner tree in which all terminal vertices are constrained to be leaves is called a terminal Steiner tree. Our study addresses the existence of a terminal Steiner tree, its complexity across various graph classes, black-box applications of the ST, and a fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) algorithm with respect to the number of terminals.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [EMA: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with General Attribute Filtering and Dynamic Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00734)
*Mocheng Li,Baotong Lu,James Cheng,Chenhao Ma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EMA 是一种支持混合属性多谓词查询的过滤 ANN 算法，通过引入“Marker”边摘要技术，在保证过滤精度的同时显著提升了搜索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过滤近似最近邻（FANN）搜索方法主要限制于简单的范围或标签过滤，存在索引构建耗时长、内存开销大以及无法有效处理复杂谓词选择性等问题。

Method: 提出了 EMA 算法：1. 引入“Marker”（标记）作为附加在图边上的紧凑摘要，提供谓词和几何感知的引导；2. 采用由 Marker 增强的联合搜索策略，结合边界恢复机制，在确保零假阴性的前提下保持图的可导航性。

Result: 在多种负载下，EMA 相比当前最先进的通用 FANN 方法实现了 1.68 倍至 12.25 倍的速度提升，并能有效处理数值与分类属性混合的多谓词查询及动态更新。

Conclusion: EMA 证明了通过 Marker 机制和基于图的边界恢复策略，可以在支持复杂混合属性谓词查询的同时，显著提升过滤近似最近邻搜索的效率，是目前处理此类任务的先进方案。

Abstract: Filtering Approximate Nearest Neighbor (FANN) search is a critical and emerging task for strengthening the query capability of vector databases, supporting applications such as recommendation systems, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and agent memory. However, most existing methods are limited to range or label filtering, often incurring unacceptable index construction time and memory overhead. Predicate-agnostic approaches further struggle to handle a wide range of predicate selectivities effectively. In this paper, we propose EMA, a filtering ANN algorithm that supports multi-predicate queries over mixed numerical and categorical attributes, and efficient dynamic updates. EMA introduces Markers as compact summaries attached to graph edges, providing conservative predicate- and geometric-aware guidance with zero false negatives at the Marker level. During query processing, EMA performs Marker-augmented joint search with a bounded edge recovery mechanism, enabling efficient filtering while preserving graph navigability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMA achieves 1.68x--12.25x speedup over state-of-the-art general filtering ANN methods across diverse workloads.

</details>


### [137] [SCOPE: Cost-Efficient Model Selection for Compound AI Systems under Quality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00774)
*Yiqian Huang,Shiqi Zhang,Tianyuan Jin,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对复合 AI 系统中大模型选型成本过高的问题，本文提出的 SCOPE 算法通过利用 per-query 评估和置信区间搜索，实现了在满足质量约束的前提下，大幅降低系统运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合 AI 系统使用单一高性能大模型导致部署成本过高，特别是在大规模数据处理任务中；现有方法多依赖于昂贵的全数据集评估，效率低下。

Method: 提出 SCOPE 优化算法：利用每条查询（per-query）的执行结果快速评估系统成本和质量，通过构造置信上界（confidence bounds）来指导搜索策略。

Result: 在三个数据处理任务上显著优于 7 个对比基准；搜索过程中成本降低达 20 倍，最终解的成本降低达 6 倍。

Conclusion: SCOPE 能够以更低的搜索搜索成本高效地在复合 AI 系统中选择最优的大模型组合，并提供理论保证，显著优于现有基准方法。

Abstract: A compound AI system consists of multiple LLM modules, together handling complex and multi-step tasks that exceed the capabilities of a single model. Existing systems often use a single expensive LLM across all modules to improve the result quality of the whole system. However, this configuration incurs prohibitive costs, particularly for data management and analytics tasks at scale, such as data manipulation. To this end, we formalize the problem of constrained LLM selection for compound AI systems, leveraging the diverse pricing and capabilities of different LLMs to achieve competitive quality at lower cost. Given a query dataset and a user-specified quality threshold, we aim to select an LLM for each module to minimize the system's average cost while ensuring that overall quality meets the required threshold. To solve this problem, we propose SCOPE, a cost-efficient optimization algorithm. Unlike existing approaches that rely on expensive dataset-level evaluations, SCOPE exploits per-query results to rapidly estimate the system's cost and quality, and constructs confidence bounds to guide the search for promising LLM combinations. Furthermore, SCOPE provides theoretical guarantees for meeting the quality threshold and achieving near-optimal average cost. We evaluate SCOPE against 7 baselines on three data processing tasks, demonstrating that it outperforms all baselines. Under the same search budget and quality constraint, it finds solutions with up to $20\times$ lower cost than the best competitor during the search and achieves up to $6\times$ lower final cost in the returned solution.

</details>


### [138] [Can we trust LLM Self-Explanations for Entity Resolution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01210)
*Tommaso Teofili,Donatella Firmani,Nick Koudas,Paolo Merialdo,Divesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了LLM在实体解析任务中自我解释的不可靠性，并提出了 Uncerta 混合框架，通过以自我解释引导后验解释，在保证解释质量的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在实体解析（ER）中生成的自我解释虽然计算成本极低，但其真实可靠性及忠实度尚未得到验证。作者旨在揭示这些解释在实用中的局限性，并寻求一种兼顾准确性与效率的解释方案。

Method: 1. 在10个数据集上对三种主流LLM的自我解释（特征归因、反事实解释）进行大规模系统评估；2. 提出 Uncerta 混合解释框架，利用自我解释作为先验引导后验解释过程。

Result: 研究发现LLM自我解释存在不稳定性、忠实度低、与反事实证据匹配度差的问题，与真正的因果关联存在鸿沟。Uncerta 框架实现了与昂贵的后验解释方法相当的质量，同时将计算成本降低了一个数量级。

Conclusion: LLM的自我解释虽然高效，但在实体解析任务中可靠性较差且缺乏因果关联。提出的 Uncerta 框架通过将自我解释作为先验，有效平衡了后验解释的可靠性与计算成本。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong performance on Entity Resolution (ER). Additionally, akin to their prowess in providing accurate predictions, these models often generate self-explanations alongside their predictions through prompting. While such self-explanations are appealing due to their negligible computational cost, their actual reliability remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we present the first large-scale systematic evaluation of LLM self-explanations for ER, focusing on feature attribution and counterfactual explanations at both the attribute and token levels. Across three LLMs, ten datasets, and multiple prompting strategies, we show that self-explanations are often unstable, weakly faithful, and poorly aligned with counterfactual evidence, revealing a substantial gap between plausibility and causal relevance.
  We further demonstrate that established post-hoc explanation methods provide significantly higher trustworthiness, but at a prohibitive computational cost when applied to LLMs. To bridge this gap, we introduce \uncerta{}, a hybrid explanation framework that leverages self-explanations as priors to guide post-hoc exploration. \uncerta{} achieves explanation quality comparable to post-hoc methods while reducing cost by up to an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [139] [Real-world and simulated thermal data from 960 residential multi-zone buildings in Central Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01994)
*Fabian Raisch,Matthias Kersken,Markus Male,Benjamin Tischler*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ThermBuild是一个包含真实测量与近千个仿真模型的热动力学数据集，旨在提升建筑节能控制及故障诊断算法的泛化性与可复制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑热动力学研究缺乏高质量且多样化的数据集，限制了数据驱动控制、故障诊断以及跨建筑模型泛化能力的发展。

Method: 采用了结合实际测量（两栋独栋住宅）与大规模仿真（958个TRNSYS模型）的混合方法。涵盖了热泵系统、建筑物理属性、多种欧洲气候及通风配置等多种变量，提供15分钟时间间隔的详细运行与环境数据。

Result: 发布了ThermBuild数据集，包含15个月的实际运行数据和3年的仿真数据，涵盖了建筑热物理、HVAC系统及室内气候等多维变量，具备极高的模型训练与基准测试价值。

Conclusion: ThermBuild数据集为建筑热动力学建模、能源效率控制及故障诊断提供了高质量、多维度的基准，特别适用于支持迁移学习和模拟到现实的跨域迁移研究。

Abstract: This paper presents the ThermBuild dataset, which comprises real-world measurements from two single-family homes and simulations of 958 TRNSYS building models. The buildings cover diverse combinations of air-source heat pump systems, numbers of thermal zones, occupancy profiles, building ages, thermal masses, sizes, orientations, window glazings, five European climates, and ventilation configurations. The dataset contains 15-minute-resolution operational data spanning 15 months for the real-world buildings and 3 years for the simulated buildings. Each building time series includes detailed measurements of heat pump operation, the heating distribution system, the domestic hot water system, weather conditions, and zone-level indoor climate variables. The ThermBuild dataset is designed for data-driven thermal dynamics modeling, thereby supporting the deployment of energy-efficient control, as well as fault detection and diagnosis in buildings. It is particularly suited for transfer learning, generalization modeling, benchmarking, simulation-to-reality transfer, and reproducible thermal modeling research.

</details>


### [140] [Less Is More? When Dataset Context Hurts LLM-Generated Dataset Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02334)
*Lisa-Yao Gan,Arunav Das,Johanna Walker,Klaus Diepold,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了如何利用LLM提升数据集描述质量，发现单纯增加数据集Schema信息可能适得其反，需平衡数据上下文与叙述质量，并揭示了不同LLM在生成描述时的稳定性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集的发现与重用受限于元数据的质量（尤其是自然语言描述的稀疏或不一致），且目前缺乏关于LLM生成高质量数据集描述所需上下文信息的实证指南。

Method: 基于文献构建了数据集描述质量框架，并对来自data.europa.eu的252个数据集（1,336个CSV文件）进行了大规模消融研究。通过LLM-as-a-judge框架评估了三种场景：(1)仅由标题生成；(2)由标题+模式生成；(3)由标题+模式+代表性数据生成。

Result: 发现存在“模式惩罚”（schema penalty）效应：仅提供Schema会降低叙述质量；增加代表性数据能部分恢复内容扎实性，但未能显著提升整体质量。同时指出不同LLM具备稳定的描述风格。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅提供表结构信息往往会降低描述质量，而引入样本数据虽能提升内容的扎实度，但对整体人类可读性改善有限。此外，不同LLM表现出稳定的描述偏好（Persona），这些发现为基于LLM的数据发布工作流提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Dataset search and reuse are strongly constrained by the quality of metadata such as natural language descriptions, which are often sparse or inconsistent. Although large language models (LLMs) can generate such descriptions automatically, little empirical guidance exists on what makes a good dataset description and what dataset context LLMs actually need. We study these questions through a literature-grounded framework of dataset description quality and a large-scale ablation study using 252 datasets (1,336 CSV files) from the European data portal data.europa.eu. We generate descriptions with LLMs in a baseline scenario and two ablation scenarios: (1) using only dataset titles, (2) titles and schema, and (3) titles, schema and representative data, and evaluate them with an LLM-as-a- judge framework and a semantic descriptive attribute analysis grounded in our quality dimensions. Our results reveal a consis- tent schema penalty: table-schemas alone often degrade narrative quality, while representative data partially restores grounding without improving overall human-facing quality. We further show that different LLMs exhibit stable descriptive personas. These findings provide practical guidance for LLM-supported data publishing workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [HeLoCo: Efficient asynchronous low-communication training under data and device heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00271)
*Abdullah Al Asif,Patrick Diem,Juan Pablo Muñoz,Felix Wolf,Ali Jannesari,Arya Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HeLoCo 是一种针对异步低通信分布式训练的方向感知校正方法，通过利用外部动量修复因滞后梯度导致的偏差，显著提升了异构环境与非 IID 数据下的收敛效率与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步分布式低通信训练（如 DiLoCo）虽然提高了硬件利用率，但由于异步产生的滞后（staleness）导致伪梯度与当前全局模型状态不匹配，特别是在非 IID 数据和异构计算环境下性能下降显著。

Method: 提出 HeLoCo 方法，利用外部优化器动量作为参考轨迹，对异步通信接收到的伪梯度进行方向一致性检测。对于与全局优化方向冲突的梯度分量进行动态校正，对一致的梯度保持更新。

Result: 在多语言大模型训练任务中，HeLoCo 表现优异：在固定 token 下性能比异步 DiLoCo 基线提升约 7.5%，在固定时钟时间下比异步动量超前（look-ahead）提升 3.3%，在极度系统异构下比同步基线提升高达 22.1%。

Conclusion: HeLoCo 通过方向感知的校正机制，有效解决了异步低通信训练中陈旧伪梯度带来的偏差问题。实验证明该方法在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据和异构环境下具备显著的性能优势与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distributed Low-Communication (DiLoCo) training reduces communication overhead by allowing workers to perform multiple local optimization steps before sending pseudo-gradients to a global outer update. Its asynchronous variant further improves hardware utilization by removing synchronization barriers, but at the cost of stale pseudo-gradients computed from outdated model states. As a result, these updates can become misaligned with the current global optimization direction, particularly in heterogeneous systems. This issue becomes even more pronounced when data are non-IID, a setting that has not been well studied in asynchronous low-communication training. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{HeLoCo}, a direction-aware correction method for asynchronous low-communication training that uses outer momentum as a reference for the current optimization trajectory and selectively adjusts incoming pseudo-gradients before the outer update. Updates that remain aligned are preserved, while directionally conflicting components are corrected. On multilingual language-model training with heterogeneous workers and non-IID data, HeLoCo consistently improves validation loss. It outperforms existing asynchronous DiLoCo-based baselines by up to 7.5\% at a fixed token budget, exceeds asynchronous momentum look-ahead by up to 3.3\% at a fixed wall-clock budget, and surpasses the synchronous baseline by up to 22.1\% under severe system heterogeneity. Our analysis further shows how staleness, worker speed, and data heterogeneity shape update quality and convergence in highly decentralized and heterogeneous training setups.

</details>


### [142] [Augur: Pre-Execution Energy Prediction for Workflow Tasks in Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00348)
*Kathleen West,Vasilis Bountris,Philipp Thamm,Ulf Leser,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Augur，一种无需大量历史数据即可准确预测科学工作流能耗与运行时间的新方法，旨在解决现有能源感知调度在处理新工作流时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流处理大规模数据导致高能源消耗和碳排放；现有的能源感知调度方法严重依赖以往的执行数据，难以处理全新的、修改后的工作流或在不同基础设施上运行的工作流。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Augur 的预测方法，结合了对集群基础设施和特定工作流的有效分析（Profiling），在不需要大量先前执行数据的情况下实现了对任务运行时间和总能耗的预测。

Result: 在公共和私有云基础设施上的实验表明，Augur 的预测性能优于现有方法；相比 Ichnos 和 Intel RAPL，Augur 在能耗预测方面表现出更低的误差，且在任务运行时间和总工作流能量预测上超过了现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: Augur 是一种高效的能源预测方法，仅需极少量的历史执行数据即可实现准确的科学工作流能源与运行时间预测，为低碳和节能的科学计算提供了可靠的决策基础。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are widely used to process large quantities of data, leading to significant energy consumption and carbon emissions. To reduce this environmental impact, energy and carbon-aware scheduling approaches could be employed. However, such methods require runtime and energy predictions, which are typically only available for workflows that have been executed previously. Meanwhile, scientists may execute new or modified workflows, use workflows with different input data, or run them on alternative infrastructure. To address this critical gap, we propose Augur, a novel method to predict the energy consumption of scientific workflow tasks prior to execution. By efficiently profiling both the available cluster infrastructure and the workflow at hand, Augur is capable of predicting the overall energy consumption of the workflow with a median prediction error of $16.3\pm15.3\%$ compared to Ichnos, an energy estimation method that uses fitted power models, and $18.2\pm14.7\%$ compared to Intel RAPL, as observed in our experimental evaluation on public and private cloud infrastructure. Relying on only minimal historical execution data, Augur outperforms two state-of-the-art methods in predicting both task runtime and total workflow energy, providing a robust foundation for energy-efficient and carbon-aware scientific data analysis.

</details>


### [143] [The Cartan-Topos Protocol: A Unified Geometric and Categorical Framework for Resilient Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00714)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文通过细胞层理论（Cellular Sheaves）和几何范畴论，统一了多智能体系统中的连续几何协同与离散逻辑推理，解决了一致性与鲁棒性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体协同中连续欧几里得共识（无法处理非可积约束）与离散符号逻辑（无法处理开放世界假设）之间的根本对立。

Method: 1. 利用黎曼重心流（Riemannian center-of-mass flows）在齐次流形上实现共识；2. 采用Clifford代数（转子、电机）进行SE(3)位姿同步；3. 构建细胞层（Cellular Sheaves）与层拉普拉斯算子（Sheaf Laplacian）替代传统加权网络；4. 引入Cartan联络实现逻辑和乐（holonomy）；5. 使用Grothendieck拓扑（Grothendieck topos）进行时序规划与推理。

Result: 证明了在异步非线性层扩散条件下，系统能够保证以线性收敛速度达到狄利克雷能量最小化，并在离散与连续约束下展现出强的鲁棒性与一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的几何与范畴统一框架，通过层论（Sheaf Theory）连接了流形几何与符号逻辑，证明了该框架能够作为处理物理、认知及时间多智能体系统协同问题的通用基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent coordination faces a fundamental divide between continuous Euclidean consensus, which fails under non-integrable constraints, and discrete symbolic logic, which collapses under open-world assumptions. This report presents a unified geometric and categorical framework bridging these paradigms. Agent states are modeled on homogeneous manifolds (Lie groups, Grassmannians) with consensus achieved via Riemannian center-of-mass flows. Clifford-algebraic representations (rotors, motors) enable singularity-free SE(3) pose synchronization. Network interactions are formalized as cellular sheaves, where heterogeneous stalks connected by linear restriction maps replace uniform weights; the sheaf Laplacian drives diffusion toward globally consistent sections. The Cartan connection encodes logical holonomy directly into restriction maps. Asynchronous nonlinear sheaf diffusion guarantees linear convergence to Dirichlet energy minimizers under bounded delays. Sheaf-Theoretic Planning (STP) models time as a Grothendieck topos, using intuitionistic logic and abductive repair for resilient temporal reasoning. Applications include discourse sheaves for opinion dynamics and knowledge sheaves for graph embedding. This synthesis establishes geometric consensus as a universal foundation for resilient multi-agent systems across physical, epistemic, and temporal domains.

</details>


### [144] [ViBE: Co-Optimizing Workload Skew and Hardware Variability for MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00735)
*Seokjin Go,Marko Scrbak,Ephrem Wu,Srilatha Manne,Divya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ViBE 是一种硬件感知型 MoE 专家分配框架，通过将高负荷任务分配给更快的 GPU，克服了硬件异构性导致的推理木桶效应，显著改善了分布式大模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决分布式 MoE 推理中由于 GPU 硬件性能的不一致性（如制造偏差、功耗限制、热波动）与专家路由负载不均叠加导致的严重的同步推理延迟（计算滞后）问题。

Method: 提出了 Variability-Informed Binning of Experts (ViBE) 框架，该框架结合了 GPU 性能建模与专家激活分析（expert activation profiling），将高负载专家分配给高性能 GPU，低负载专家分配给较慢的 GPU，并支持针对性能偏移的轻量级重校准。

Result: ViBE 使 SLO（服务水平目标）达成率提高了 14%，P90 TTFT（首字延迟）最高降低了 45%。

Conclusion: ViBE 通过感知底层 GPU 的硬件异构性，通过智能分配 MoE 专家模型实现了更平衡的任务负载，有效缓解了分布式推理中的“长尾效应”（stragglers），并提升了系统整体性能。

Abstract: In distributed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference, input-dependent token routing interacts with GPU performance variability to create persistent stragglers under synchronized execution, where the slowest GPU determines layer latency. This performance variability is inherent to modern accelerators: manufacturing variation, power limits, and thermal conditions introduce measurable execution-time differences across nominally identical GPUs. The core challenge is that MoE execution-time imbalance arises from the interaction of workload skew and hardware asymmetry. Token routing produces uneven and layer-varying expert loads, while GPU throughput depends on device-specific operating characteristics and workload intensity. Prior work mitigates routing skew but assumes homogeneous hardware, optimizing token balance rather than execution latency. As a result, even balanced token assignments can leave hardware-induced stragglers unaddressed. Thus, we propose Variability-Informed Binning of Experts (ViBE), a hardware-aware expert placement framework that minimizes execution-time imbalance across GPUs. ViBE combines per-GPU performance modeling with expert activation profiling to assign high-load experts to faster devices and low-load experts to slower ones, reducing layer-level stragglers without modifying model semantics or hardware. Because both workload characteristics and effective GPU throughput can shift across serving conditions, ViBE supports lightweight recalibration under workload/performance drift to refresh its routing and performance estimates when needed. Results show that ViBE consistently reduces execution-time imbalance and improves SLO attainment by 14%, while lowering P90 TTFT by up to 45%. We further show that the impact of hardware variability increases at scale, making variability-aware placement important for efficient, high-utilization LLM serving.

</details>


### [145] [Lodestar: An Online-Learning LLM Inference Router](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00946)
*Gangmuk Lim,Wanyu Zhao,Brighten Godfrey,Jiaxin Shan,Le Xu,Liguang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lodestar 是一款针对 LLM 分布式推理的自适应请求路由系统，通过在线奖励学习显著降低了首字延迟（TTFT），并能无缝集成至现有推理框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理具有高输入依赖性、请求间的强耦合性（KV cache 重用）以及非线性的延迟响应特征，传统的负载均衡算法及现有启发式策略在分布式 GPU 集群中难以实现最优性能。

Method: 提出了 Lodestar 系统，采用在线强化学习方法：实时采集集群状态（实例负载、请求特征、性能数据），训练在线奖励预测器（Reward Predictor），从而动态地将推理请求路由至预期收益最大的 GPU 实例。

Result: 在公有云 GPU 集群测试中，相比现有最先进的启发式策略，Lodestar 将平均 TTFT 和 P99 TTFT 分别降低了 1.41 倍和 1.47 倍（在异构集群中最高可分别优化至 4.38 倍和 4.42 倍），且仅需约 5 分钟即可完成路由策略的在线学习与收敛。

Conclusion: Lodestar 通过基于强化学习的请求调度，有效应对了 LLM 推理任务的高度动态性和复杂耦合性，在提高 GPU 利用率的同时显著优化了首字延迟（TTFT），且具备良好的环境适应性和兼容性。

Abstract: Efficiently serving large language model (LLM) inference tasks is crucial both for user-perceived latency such as time-to-first-token (TTFT) and for GPU utilization. However, LLM request routing, that is, assigning each inference request to a GPU instance, is particularly challenging: execution is highly input-dependent; batching and KV-cache reuse create strong cross-request coupling; and latency responds nonlinearly to context length, model/engine settings, and heterogeneous accelerators. As a result, simple traditional load balancing algorithms, and even heuristics tailored for LLM inference, fail to achieve good performance. We present Lodestar, a novel learning-based request routing system for distributed GPU clusters. Lodestar continuously collects a snapshot of the cluster at per-request level, including real-time instance state, request characteristics, and observed performance, and trains an online reward predictor that it uses to route inference requests to the instance that will maximize given reward (e.g., minimizing TTFT). Lodestar is cloud-native and works seamlessly with existing serving stacks (vLLM). With continuous online adaptation to changing workloads and infrastructure conditions, Lodestar achieves 1.41x lower average TTFT and 1.47x lower P99 TTFT on average (up to 2.15x/1.86x on homogeneous and 4.38x/4.42x on heterogeneous clusters) compared to a state-of-the-art prefix cache and load-aware heuristic, and learns these efficient routing strategies within about 5 minutes, based on experiments in a public cloud GPU cluster.

</details>


### [146] [The World's Fastest Matching Engine Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01183)
*Jake Yoon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对电子交易中订单簿匹配延迟问题，提出了无需比较的PIN数据结构和利用上下文信息的邻居感知树更新算法，极大地减少了搜索开销，实现了超高性能与极低尾延迟的订单匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子交易所使用的订单簿实现（基于平衡树和链表）存在指针追踪和根到叶遍历的性能瓶颈。在微突发流量下，这些开销导致尾延迟激增，影响市场流动性。

Method: 1. 提出了“优先级指示节点”（PIN）结构：利用固定容量、连续寻址的插槽和全局优先级指示器，实现O(1)复杂度的插入与更新，无需比较操作。
2. 提出了“邻居感知”（Neighbor-aware）数据结构操作：针对有序事件流场景，利用已知的邻居信息进行插入与删除，将树结构的调整复杂度降低为常数级引用更新加单路径重平衡。

Result: 在单核上实现每秒3200万条订单消息的处理能力，尾延迟小于微秒级；相比现有最佳开源引擎性能提升5-11倍。在96核实例上可扩展至每秒6.4亿条消息。

Conclusion: 通过引入PIN数据结构和邻居感知（Neighbor-aware）树操作，有效消除了传统订单簿实现中的指针追踪和冗余遍历开销，显著提升了电子交易系统的吞吐量，并实现了极低的尾延迟。

Abstract: Every electronic exchange relies on an order book whose storage layer determines matching latency. The dominant implementation -- linked lists chained through a balanced tree -- imposes two costs on every operation: pointer-chased traversal to reach the insertion point, and root-to-leaf search to locate the target price level. Under micro-burst conditions these costs produce tail-latency spikes that degrade market quality when liquidity is most needed. We present two data-structure contributions that eliminate these costs. The first is the Priority-Indicated Node (PIN), a priority queue in which entries occupy fixed-capacity, contiguously addressable slots, each carrying a per-slot indicator encoding the entry's global priority. Unlike heaps, which require O(log n) comparisons per operation, the PIN resolves insertion position directly from the indicators without comparing entries; indicator updates are O(1), independent of queue size. The second addresses a broader inefficiency: balanced search trees search root-to-leaf on every insertion and deletion, even when the caller already knows the key's in-order neighbors -- as in ordered event streams, incremental index already knows the key's in-order neighbors -- as in ordered event streams, incremental index maintenance, and electronic trading. Neighbor-aware insertion and deletion exploit known neighbor references to attach or remove a node with O(1) reference writes, followed by single-path rebalancing, uniformly across red-black, AVL, and B/B+-tree variants. A single CPU core sustains 32 million order messages per second with sub-microsecond tail latency under multi-million message-per-second micro-bursts, and is 5-11x faster than the best available open-source matching engines on the same hardware. Scaled to a single 96-core instance, the engine sustains 640 million messages per second across 10,000 symbols.

</details>


### [147] [Leyline: KV Cache Directives for Agentic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01065)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Koestler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 Agentic LLM 频繁编辑上下文导致的重计算成本高昂问题，Leyline 提供了一套声明式的 KV 缓存编辑原语，支持在不丢失后续缓存的前提下进行高效的插入、删除和替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KV 缓存管理机制均基于“静态聊天机器人”模式，无法应对 Agentic LLM 常见的动态编辑需求（如撤销轨迹、修改上下文等），导致系统不得不频繁执行开销巨大的完全重计算（re-prefill）。

Method: 提出了 Leyline 系统，包含一套声明式编辑 4 元组（4-tuple）接口，用于描述缓存编辑请求；并设计了与架构无关的内核接口，通过闭式 RoPE 旋转校正（closed-form RoPE-rotation correction）在修改后恢复注意力机制的数学正确性。

Result: 在 splice 操作模式下，缓存命中率提升了 11.2 个百分点，延迟降低高达 241 毫秒；在智能体调试任务中，通过 10 行代码的截断策略，任务解决率提升了 14.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: Leyline 引入了一种声明式的编辑原语，填补了现有 LLM 推理系统在处理智能体任务（Agentic tasks）时缺乏灵活 KV 缓存编辑能力的空白。它证明了通过将策略层与底层缓存操作解耦，可以显著提升智能体任务的效率和成功率。

Abstract: Modern KV cache management assumes the chatbot workload: prompts arrive once and the cache grows append-only, so prefix caching and forward-only eviction are correct by construction. Agentic LLMs break this assumption. Their conversations evolve through policy-driven editing: failed tool calls are retried, stale outputs dropped, trajectories pivoted. Two distinct cache problems result. First, identical content moves to new positions between turns, invalidating exact-prefix caches even though the underlying KV would still be valid; recent work on position-independent caching for MLA addresses this reuse problem. Second, and this paper's focus, a policy may need to direct the serving system to actively remove or replace a span of cached content and continue without re-prefilling everything that came after. No existing primitive offers this. Production agentic harnesses fall back to re-prefill on every edit, paying full prefix-recomputation cost; kernel-level eviction methods make their own decisions and cannot accept policy directives from outside the kernel. We introduce Leyline, a serving-side primitive that closes this gap. A declarative directive 4-tuple separates what to edit from how to preserve position correctness. The policy declares the edit and its mode (in-place splice or prefix-trimmed re-prefill for semantic forgetting); an architecture-agnostic interface routes to a per-architecture kernel that restores attention math via a closed-form RoPE-rotation correction. The splice kernel lifts replay cache-hit by +11.2 pp and cuts latency by up to 241 ms. A ten-line truncation rule routed through the same interface lifts agentic solve rate by +14.3 pp on debug-gym. The mechanism is open; the policy space it enables is the agenda.

</details>


### [148] [Observation, Not Prediction: Conversation-Level Disaggregated Scheduling for Agentic Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01839)
*Jianru Ding,Ryien Hosseini,Pouya Mahdi Gholami,Mingyuan Xiang,Henry Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ConServe 通过将调度逻辑从“按轮次预测”转变为“按对话生命周期管理”，成功规避了 LLM 代理任务中不可预测的负载波动，利用确定性的阶段特征实现了更高效的计算资源调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 代理系统以单轮为调度单位，由于难以准确预测每轮的解码长度、工具行为及 KV 缓存增长，导致调度决策（如是否拆分 Prefill 和 Decode）依赖于不准确的预测，限制了系统效率。

Method: 提出 ConServe 调度方案：1. 改变调度粒度：将调度单位从“轮次（Turn）”提升至“对话（Conversation）”；2. 分阶段处理：将任务划分为“计算密集型首轮 Prefill”和“内存密集型剩余交互尾部”；3. 路由优化：首轮任务路由至高吞吐 Prefiller，后续交互固定在一个解码器上，无需预测未来负载。

Result: 相比基于单轮预测的基线系统，ConServe 将 p95 首个有效 token 延迟降低了 51.08%，能源效率提升 7.51%（若结合异构 GPU 层级，能效进一步提升 22.75%），且在保持尾部延迟（TBT）和服务水平协议（SLO）的前提下实现了零模型预测。

Conclusion: 通过将调度单位从“轮次”提升到“对话”级别，ConServe 实现了高度可预测的负载结构，消除了对不可测量的未来负载的预测依赖，在降低延迟的同时显著提升了能源效率，证明了改变调度调度粒度是解决 LLM 代理负载调度问题的有效方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents resolve a user task through many turns of dependent inference and tool calls, producing a workload whose total cost is unknown when the task arrives. Existing multi-turn systems keep the turn as the scheduling unit and decide, turn by turn, whether to disaggregate prefill from decode. That decision rests on the turn's decode length, tool behavior, and KV growth, quantities that are not observable when the scheduler must act, forcing the system to predict them. We show this dependence on prediction is imposed by the scheduling unit, not the workload. Raising the scheduling unit from the turn to the conversation converts turn-level irregularity into a stable, two-phase structure: 1) a compute-bound turn-1 prefill followed by 2) a long, memory-bound tail. Thus, with the conversation as the scheduling unit, placement reduces to reading the first-turn input length and per-decoder KV occupancy, both directly observable. We instantiate this principle in ConServe, which routes the first-turn prefill to a high-throughput prefiller, transfers the KV cache exactly once, and pins the conversation to a single decoder for its entire tail, with no learned model of decode-side cost. Against a per-turn prediction baseline, ConServe reduces p95 time-to-first-effective-token (the latency of a conversation's first user-visible output) by 51.08% and improves energy efficiency by 7.51% while preserving last-turn TBT and SLOs; mapping the two phases onto heterogeneous GPU tiers adds a further 22.75% in energy efficiency.

</details>


### [149] [Magnum.np.distributed: Accelerating Finite Difference Micromagnetic Simulations with Multiple GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01114)
*Tsz Chung Cheng,Yuichiro Kurokawa,Hiromi Yuasa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个原生Python多GPU微磁模拟框架，通过扩展magnum.np并集成PyTorch Distributed，有效突破了单设备计算瓶颈，大幅提升了大规模微磁系统模拟的计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主流微磁模拟求解器（如Mumax3和magnum.np）主要局限于单设备计算，难以满足日益增长的大规模、复杂微磁系统模拟的需求，限制了科研效率。

Method: 通过扩展现有的Python原生求解器magnum.np，引入PyTorch Distributed库，实现了支持多GPU并行计算的微磁模拟框架。该框架利用NVLink进行高速GPU间通信，并支持通过MPI后端在NUMA架构的CPU上进行并行计算。

Result: 在处理计算密集型的退磁场有效场计算时，8个通过NVLink连接的GPU实现了7.0倍的加速比；同时，在支持NUMA绑定的CPU环境下通过MPI后端也实现了6.8倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该框架通过利用PyTorch Distributed实现了高效的多GPU磁矩模拟，显著提升了大规模复杂磁性系统的计算效率，为尖端自旋电子器件的研发提供了更快的迭代周期。

Abstract: Micromagnetic simulations are essential tools in nanomagnetism and spintronics research. Although widely adopted solvers like Mumax3 and the Python-native magnum.np use GPU acceleration to improve performance, these tools are limited to single-device computation. In this work, we present the first Python-native multi-GPU micromagnetic framework by extending magnum.np with PyTorch Distributed. This leverages high-speed communication and computation across multiple GPUs while retaining the benefits of ease of installation, platform-agnostic design, and compatibility with Python. For computationally intensive demagnetisation effective-field calculations, we achieve a 7.0x speedup across 8 GPUs connected via NVLink, whereas Halo exchange required for Heisenberg exchange shows limited scaling due to kernel dispatch latency. We also demonstrated the framework's versatility by achieving a 6.8x speedup in demagnetisation field computation on CPU with NUMA pinning via the MPI backend of PyTorch Distributed. Faster turnaround times will enable researchers to explore larger, more complex systems and accelerate the design cycle for novel spintronic devices.

</details>


### [150] [Schedule-Level Shared-Prefix Reuse for LLM RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01143)
*Pengbo Li,Feiyuan Zhang,Guangming Sheng,Guangxin He,Di Chai,Ziniu Li,Taiqiang Wu,Binhang Yuan,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对长上下文LLM强化学习的高效训练调度策略，通过复用共享前缀的计算过程和梯度缓存，在保证训练数值一致性的同时，大幅降低显存需求并提升训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRPO/PPO等长上下文RL训练中，多个采样轨迹共享相同的冗余前缀（如提示词、检索文档），标准训练方式重复计算这些前缀导致计算资源浪费严重，且显存开销巨大。

Method: 提出了一种调度级别的重用机制（Schedule-level reuse mechanism）。该方法仅计算一次前缀的前向传播；在处理后缀时，复用缓存的前缀 K/V 值；最后在累计的梯度缓存上仅进行一次前缀反向传播，从而避免了重复的冗余计算。

Result: 实现了最高4.395倍的加速，最高降低了59.1%的峰值显存占用（Phase-B），并将Llama3-8B的模型上下文容量从17,920提升至29,696个Token。

Conclusion: 通过解耦前缀与后缀的计算，该方法在保持数学等价性的前提下，显著降低了长上下文RL训练的显存占用，并大幅提升了吞吐量，同时具备良好的并行扩展性。

Abstract: GRPO- and PPO-style LLM post-training commonly sample multiple trajectories from the same prompt and then train on the resulting group. In long-context RL workloads, this shared prompt-side prefix can contain retrieved passages, visual tokens, tool schemas, system instructions, or task context, while the full rollout group is still too large to pack into one training microbatch. Standard dense trainers therefore recompute the same prefix forward and backward for every trajectory. We present a schedule-level reuse mechanism that decouples prefix and suffix computation. The schedule runs prefix forward once, executes suffixes as ordinary microbatches while reading prefix K/V and accumulating prefix-side gK/gV , and then runs prefix backward once on the accumulated gradient cache. This reordered schedule is equivalent to baseline training over real arithmetic and aligns numerically within finite-precision tolerance. Because only K/V and gK/gV are hot during suffix computation, the approach offloads dormant prefix activations, integrates with TP/EP/CP/PP and DP-style placement at the execution level, and preserves aux-loss-based MoE router semantics through logical prefix-token accounting. On dense Llama3-8B, Qwen3-8B, and MoE Qwen3-MoE-30B-A3B configurations, the schedule matches optimizer updates across TP/CP/PP/EP combinations, aligns on a 100-step real RL trace replay, reaches up to 4.395x speedup (2.930x under a conservative compile-on comparison) as prefix ratio and rollout group size grow, and reduces Phase-B peak HBM by up to 59.1%, extending the Llama3-8B capacity frontier from 17,920 to 29,696 total tokens.

</details>


### [151] [AcOrch: Accelerating Sampling-based GNN Training under CPU-NPU Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01161)
*Kefu Chen,Xin Ai,Qiange Wang,Yanfeng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AcOrch是一款针对CPU-NPU异构平台优化的GNN训练系统，通过多级流水线并行与精细化异构计算资源调度，显著提升了大图神经网络的训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于采样的GNN训练在分布式异构硬件（CPU-NPU）上存在资源利用不均衡与多阶段执行效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出AcOrch系统，采用两级流水线模型实现采样、聚合与模型训练的重叠执行；对NPU内部资源进行精细化映射（AIC处理矩阵运算，AIV处理向量运算），并结合CPU实现协同计算。

Result: 在Ascend 910B AI处理器上的实验表明，AcOrch相比目前先进的MindSporeGL系统，平均加速比达到2.31倍。

Conclusion: AcOrch通过细粒度的任务编排与两级流水线模型，有效解决了CPU-NPU异构平台上图神经网络训练的资源协同与利用率问题，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable success in various applications. Sampling-based GNN training, which conducts mini-batch training on sampled subgraphs, has become a promising solution for large-scale graphs. Given the resource-intensive nature of sampling-based GNN training, Neural Processing Units (NPUs), such as the Ascend AI processor, offer a promising alternative due to their high throughput and energy efficiency, making them well-suited for GNN workloads. However, the multi-stage nature of sampling-based training, which involves subgraph sampling, feature gathering, and model training, with different resource requirements and computation volume. This requires careful coordination to fully utilize the heterogeneous computation resources of CPUs and NPUs. In this work, we present AcOrch, a sampling-based GNN training system optimized for CPU-NPU heterogeneous platforms. AcOrch offers fine-grained task orchestration and adopts a two-level pipelined execution model to overlap sampling, gathering, and training. It analyzes the heterogeneous compute features of NPUs and maps tasks to AI Cube (AIC) units, AI Vector (AIV) units, and CPU cores accordingly. Moreover, the two-level pipeline enables overlapping execution not only between the CPU and NPU, but also among different types of compute units within the NPU (e.g., AIC and AIV units), thereby maximizing the utilization of available resources. Experiments on an Ascend 910B AI processor show that AcOrch achieves an average speedup of 2.31x over the state-of-the-art NPU-native graph learning system, MindSporeGL.

</details>


### [152] [Self-Conditioned Positional HNSW for Overlap-Aware Retrieval in Chunked-Document RAG Systems: Method and Industrial Evidence-Quality Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01542)
*Nataraj Agaram Sundar,Tejas Morabia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 RAG 系统中因重叠分块导致的信息冗余问题，本文提出了 SCP-HNSW 算法，通过加入位置感知先验来优化检索上下文，并结合大规模工业质量审计验证了该方法的必要性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 RAG 系统中，虽然对文档进行重叠分块可以改善边界覆盖，但会导致检索出大量相邻的冗余信息，浪费提示词（prompt）空间。研究旨在解决这一因分块重叠导致的冗余检索失效模式，并评估工业级生成证据的质量。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的 Self-Conditioned Positional HNSW (SCP-HNSW) 方法：在分块嵌入中添加低维位置编码，并采用两阶段查询程序来估计和应用与查询相关的位置先验；最后使用可审计的最小索引间距选择器来构建最终上下文。此外，通过对 770 份文本审查数据和 70 份 OCR 案例进行质量审计。

Result: SCP-HNSW 在保持原 HNSW 结构不变的前提下，实现了对检索内容冗余的有效控制；审计结果显示，大部分审查意见评分为 3/5，叙述性细节丰富；OCR 审计表明，证据质量因输入格式而异，手写/模糊图片的 OCR 通过率仅为 45%。

Conclusion: SCP-HNSW 能够有效解决 RAG 系统中因文本分块重叠导致的检索冗余问题，通过引入位置感知机制优化了上下文构建质量；同时，对工业级审核数据（文本与 OCR）的审计分析揭示了当前 RAG 应用中证据质量的实际分布状况。

Abstract: Chunked-document retrieval is a common component of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Documents are split into overlapping chunks, embedded, and indexed with approximate nearest-neighbor search such as hierarchical navigable small world graphs (HNSW). Overlap improves boundary coverage but induces a practical failure mode: top-k retrieval often returns near-adjacent chunks that repeat evidence and waste prompt budget. We propose Self-Conditioned Positional HNSW (SCP-HNSW), a lightweight modification that appends a low-dimensional positional code to chunk embeddings and uses a two-pass query procedure to estimate and apply a query-specific document-position prior. SCP-HNSW leaves HNSW graph construction and traversal unchanged while adding an auditable minimum-index-gap selector for final context construction. We also integrate industrial review artifacts for generated evidence quality: a 770-review text-evidence audit with 318 fully labeled reviews and a 70-case OCR audit with 350 ratings. The text audit shows that 574 of 770 projected reviews are rated 3/5, only 39 fall in the 1-2 range, and narrative reviewer detail appears much more often than structured issue flags. The OCR audit shows slice-level pass rates from 95% for clean chat screenshots to 45% for handwritten/blurry captures, with moderate to strong agreement. These results motivate overlap-aware, audit-friendly RAG retrieval and identify the remaining controlled retrieval ablations needed for causal performance claims.

</details>


### [153] [Fail-Closed Lowering of Resident KV Claims onto LLM Serving Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01387)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了LLM推理系统中KV缓存的未来重用语义一致性，提出了一个形式化框架“ResidentClaim”来评估现有运行时原语是否能可靠地履行缓存重用承诺，并实现了相应的检测工具，证明了当前主流推理框架在此语义层面尚存在缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务运行时提供了许多KV缓存控制原语（如优先级、卸载、TTL等），但这些原语缺乏对未来KV缓存重用义务（future-KV obligations）的严格语义保障。论文旨在解决如何将这些“弱”原语映射为可靠的“强”重用承诺的技术难题。

Method: 1. 定义了“ResidentClaim lowering”模型，将运行时原语与KV未来重用承诺绑定；2. 提出了包含Fail-closed降低关系、检测器、描述符格式及评估用例集的完整方法论；3. 对主流LLM服务框架进行了实证分析与分类映射。

Result: 1. 建立了一套针对运行时模式映射的分类体系（原生一致性、适配器证据、近似基底等）；2. 发现目前大多数主流LLM推理框架尚未达到ResidentClaim一致性；3. 通过对vLLM的改进，验证了该边界语义在故障处理和状态一致性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究提出了“ResidentClaim”框架和检测机制，界定了大模型推理服务中KV缓存未来重用语义的合规判定标准。目前的系统（如TensorRT-LLM, SGLang, vLLM等）大多仅提供底层支撑，尚未实现原生的ResidentClaim一致性。

Abstract: LLM serving runtimes increasingly expose KV-cache primitives that resemble future-reuse controls: retention priority, TTL-like duration, host or storage offload, block events, active no-evict scheduling, and KV-aware routing. This paper argues that such primitives are weaker than accepted future-KV obligations. A runtime can expose priority, offload, events, and routing without accepting responsibility for a future reuse claim. We study ResidentClaim lowering: when a runtime primitive, trusted adapter, or patch can be treated as satisfying an accepted claim about future KV reuse. A conformant lowering must bind behavior to accepted claim identity, a materialization predicate, ordered lifecycle events, and claim-scoped outcomes. We contribute a fail-closed lowering relation, checker, descriptor format, and bad-lowering suite that classify runtime/mode mappings as native conformance, adapter-observational evidence, adapter-policy evidence under controlled pressure, approximation substrate, rejected mapping, or unknown evidence. The checker validates manually curated, anchored runtime descriptors against obligation bundles; it does not prove that unaudited runtime behavior is complete. Public TensorRT-LLM, SGLang/HiCache, and Dynamo expose strong substrates and selected adapter positives, but not native ResidentClaim conformance. The positive systems witness is a local patched vLLM connector/scheduler-boundary mechanism: claim metadata flows through real in-process offload/load behavior, and controlled same-claim restoration failure reaches vLLM's invalid-KV-load path and becomes an ordered claim-scoped fail-closed outcome. The result is a calibrated semantics boundary, not a production performance claim or a compatibility survey.

</details>


### [154] [Hierarchical Online Prompt Mutation with Dual-Loop Feedback for Guardrailed Evidence Document Generation: A Production-Evaluation Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01472)
*Nataraj Agaram Sundar Tejas Morabia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOPM框架，通过分层的路由、突变和双重反馈机制，在真实争议解决的业务场景中实现了提示词的自动化进化，显著提升了文档生成的质量和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险生产文档生成系统需要构建具备自适应性、事实依据且可审计的提示词管理机制，以应对静态提示词难以维护及性能不稳定的挑战。

Method: 提出了HOPM（分层在线提示词突变）框架，采用“家族/版本路由（Router）”选择提示词，引入“确定性护栏（Guardrails）”进行故障归因，并结合“人类评估+自动判别器”的双重反馈机制来更新路由策略和突变优先级。

Result: 在600个真实争议案例的评估中，完整版HOPM相比静态控制组，胜率由34.7%提升至45.7%，金额加权胜率由22.3%提升至41.4%，Likert质量评分从3.18提升至4.40，且问题标记率从15.3%降至5.2%。

Conclusion: HOPM框架通过分层在线提示词突变和双重反馈机制，有效解决了高风险业务中提示词难以维护及性能不稳定的问题，证明了在线自动化优化在复杂生产环境下的显著增益。

Abstract: High-stakes production document-generation systems require language models to be adaptive, evidence-grounded, and auditable. We present HOPM, a hierarchical online prompt mutation framework evaluated on a real marketplace dispute-evidence workflow. HOPM treats prompts as online policies: a family/version router selects a prompt, deterministic guardrails attribute failures to mutable prompt-token categories, and dual feedback from human review and an automated judge updates both routing and mutation priorities. The primary evidence is an observed matched production-evaluation ablation: seven variants are evaluated on the same 600 cases each, enabling component comparisons against static prompting, manual iteration, bandit-only routing, mutation-only adaptation, human-only feedback, auto-judge-only feedback, and full dual-loop HOPM. Full HOPM improves count win rate over a static control from 34.7% to 45.7% (+11.0 pp; paired McNemar p = 1.31e-11) and amount-weighted win rate from 22.3% to 41.4% (+19.1 pp; 95% paired bootstrap CI [10.3, 28.9] pp). It also increases mean Likert quality from 3.18 to 4.40 and reduces issue-flag rate from 15.3% to 5.2%. Supporting review artifacts cover 770 generated-text reviews, 318 labeled reviewer exports, a 10-case/61-rating calibration slice, and a 70-case/350-rating OCR benchmark; these artifacts calibrate rubric, guardrail, title-risk, and OCR-risk interpretation rather than substituting for the production ablation. The paper includes control setup, sample sizes, confidence intervals, paired tests, prompt-token categories, pseudocode, schema, rubric, guardrail taxonomy, and a constructed example so the evaluation structure can be reproduced without exposing proprietary evidence.

</details>


### [155] [Move the Query, Not the Cache: Characterizing Cross-Instance Latent Attention Redistribution Across GPU Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01502)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对跨节点分布式推理，提出了一种基于MLA架构的“路由查询”优化策略，通过对比实验证明在特定网络条件下，路由查询比移动KV缓存更高效，并设计了通用的性能预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型（如DeepSeek系列）使用KV缓存分块和稀疏注意力，导致跨节点请求处理中，“拉取缓存”开销过大，需探索更优的跨节点注意力计算策略。

Method: 在多节点H100集群上利用RDMA技术进行实测，构建了一个拓扑感知成本模型（覆盖探测、传输、计算等环节），并提出了一个评估“路由查询”与“拉取缓存”优劣的闭式谓词（Predicate）。

Result: 实测显示，对于MLA架构，路由约1KB的压缩查询远快于移动大块的KV缓存；利用RDMA，路由开销降低至数十微秒量级，模型对批处理往返时间的预测精度达到~7%。

Conclusion: 在当前多代理、大上下文的LLM负载下，针对特定模型架构，通过路由查询（Query Routing）替代拉取缓存（Cache Fetching）在跨节点注意力计算中更具性能优势；本文提供的成本模型和路由判断标准可广泛应用于各类压缩或稀疏注意力机制。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs increasingly decide what a query attends to with a sparse-attention indexer that picks a few KV-cache blocks per query: attention's unit is now a small, reusable chunk. Agentic workloads hammer it: many sub-agents query one large codebase, reusing the same blocks. When that corpus outgrows one GPU it is partitioned across instances, so a query and the blocks it selects often sit on different GPUs: answering it means attention across instances.
