The reflex of prior cross-instance KV systems is to move the cache: pull the selected blocks to the requester. Multi-head Latent Attention inverts the arithmetic, compressing each token's key and value into one narrow vector, so a routed query row is only ~1 KB, smaller than the chunk it attends; routing the query is then often cheaper than moving the cache. Which primitive wins, over which fabric and request shape, is uncharted, least of all on device-initiated RDMA that makes per-request cross-node transfers cheap. We characterize cross-instance MLA attention on a real multi-node H100 cluster, distilling two reusable artifacts: a topology-aware cost model (probe / transfer / compute / return / merge) and a closed-form route/fetch/local predicate, whose constants we measure on real IBGDA, where the model tracks batched round-trips to within ~7%. At decode it routes the query, trading the cost of moving the cache (a ~3 ms re-adaptation splice for a contiguous chunk, or a scattered gather under selection) for a tens-of-microsecond round trip, and picks the fabric by probe latency, not peak bandwidth. We instantiate the cost model and predicate for MLA, but neither is MLA-specific: they apply wherever compression or sparse selection shrinks attention to small chunks (DeepSeek-V3.2, V4, and GLM-5.1 today). Extending them to a new architecture requires measuring just two coefficients: the routed payload and fetch's move-the-cache cost.

</details>


### [156] [Compliance-Scored Best-of-N Guardrail Orchestration for Multimodal Document Generation in Payments Dispute Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01513)
*Nataraj Agaram Sundar,Tejas Morabia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对企业级文档生成的高合规与低延迟需求，提出了一种集成化护栏编排框架，通过多候选生成与加权评分机制，在提升内容合规性的同时实现了生产环境的高效落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险企业文档生成（如金融纠纷、合规通知）对模式正确性、合规性和低延迟要求极高，传统碎片化的验证方案导致逻辑割裂、延迟增加及运营成本升高。

Method: 构建一个多候选生成（multi-candidate generation）的护栏编排层，并行运行配置化的生成头，并利用加权合规评分（包含PII识别、内容审核、模式约束和领域规则）进行早停（early exit）筛选。

Result: 通过实际运营数据验证，该方案实现了91%的合规率，并在支付纠纷辩护摘要任务中，胜率显著提升了11.0个百分点（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 引入统一的护栏编排层在处理高风险企业文档生成时，显著提升了合规性与生成质量，证明了集成化合规评分框架对于生产环境的有效性。

Abstract: High-stakes enterprise document generation, including financial dispute narratives, compliance notices, and audit summaries, demands schema correctness, policy compliance, and low-latency operation at scale. Prior to a unified guardrail layer, production systems often stitched together separate PII redaction, content moderation, and format validation steps, leading to fragmented logic, slower request paths, and higher operational cost.
  We present a guardrail orchestration layer for text and image inputs that couples multi-candidate generation with an explicit compliance score used for early exit. The framework runs configurable parallel generation heads, scores candidates against weighted guardrails including PII detection, content moderation, schema constraints, and domain rules, and returns the best-scoring output with selection metadata. The available operational readout reports 5 attempts within 20 seconds and 91 percent compliance.
  For payments dispute defense summaries, we analyze aggregate operational scenario readouts rather than a randomized A/B test. Variable cohorts show higher count win rates than controls overall, 301/659 versus 536/1548, corresponding to +11.0 percentage points with 95 percent confidence interval [6.6, 15.5] and p < 0.001, and for adjusted item-not-received cases, +7.5 percentage points with 95 percent confidence interval [0.2, 15.7] and p = 0.045. Fraud and local evidence-ranking deltas are directionally positive but not statistically significant from the aggregate count data. We also report reviewer-calibrated Responsible-AI evidence-quality signals from 770 generated-evidence reviews and a 70-case OCR slice, and document the reproducibility boundary through the request interface, scoring logic, pseudocode, and operational evidence boundary.

</details>


### [157] [TwinQuant: Learnable Subspace Decomposition for 4-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01556)
*Haodong Wang,Junjie Liu,Zicong Hong,Qianli Liu,Jian Lin,Song Guo,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TwinQuant 是一种新型 4-bit 量化框架，通过学习量化友好的矩阵分解与重塑，并结合片上融合算子优化内存访问，在保持高模型精度的同时大幅提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的两阶段量化分解方法（如 SVD）旨在最小化原始矩阵的能量，而非量化后的实际误差，导致在追求 4-bit 压缩时模型精度大幅下降。

Method: 1. 提出了 TwinQuant 框架，通过在 Stiefel 和一般线性流形上进行联合优化，学习量化友好的分解方式，重塑低秩分量与残差分量的分布，减少动态范围不平衡。 2. 设计了融合的双组件算子（fused dual-component kernel），在片上流水化处理两级低秩计算，并通过单一的 epilogue 合并结果，避免了全局内存的额外开销。

Result: TwinQuant 在 LLaMA3 和 Qwen3 模型上保持了接近 FP16 的高精度，并实现了最高 1.8 倍的端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: TwinQuant 成功打破了现有分解式量化方法（如 SVD）在量化误差优化上的局限，通过联合优化量化友好的子空间转换，在 LLaMA3 和 Qwen3 上实现了近乎 FP16 的精度与显著的推理加速，证明了联合设计量化策略与硬件算子的有效性。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization reduces the memory footprint and latency of large language model inference, but its aggressive precision reduction can severely degrade accuracy. Prior methods address this by decomposing each weight matrix into two components (e.g., via singular value decomposition) and quantizing them separately, assigning the bulk of values to a low-precision residual component while handling outliers with a high-precision low-rank component. However, such decompositions are designed to minimize the real-valued energy of the residual, rather than the post-quantization error of the residual and low-rank components. We propose TwinQuant, a 4-bit quantization framework that learns quantization-friendly decomposed subspaces and jointly reshapes both the low-rank and residual components. TwinQuant learns component-specific transformations via a joint optimization over the Stiefel and general linear manifolds, flattening their distributions and reducing dynamic-range imbalance. To enable efficient end-to-end execution, we further design a fused dual-component kernel that pipelines the two-stage low-rank computation on-chip and merges both components with a single epilogue, avoiding intermediate global-memory traffic. Across LLaMA3 and Qwen3 models, TwinQuant preserves near-FP16 accuracy and delivers up to $1.8\times$ end-to-end speedup over an FP16 baseline.

</details>


### [158] [Don't Let a Few Network Failures Slow the Entire AllReduce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01680)
*Peiqing Chen,Jiedong Jiang,Nengneng Yu,Yuefeng Wang,Sixian Xiong,Wei Wang,Zaoxing Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 GPU 集群中网络故障导致的带宽衰减问题，OptCC 通过一种四阶段流水线算法实现了接近最优的容错 AllReduce 通信，极大地降低了性能损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 GPU 集群中网络故障导致的带宽衰减会严重拖累 NCCL 等现有通信库的环形（ring）算法效率，导致任务训练性能严重下降。

Method: 1. 推导了非对称网络带宽下 AllReduce 完成时间的理论下界（证明开销为 O(1/p)）；2. 设计了 OptCC——一种四阶段流水线 AllReduce 算法，旨在逼近该理论下界。

Result: 在 SimAI 仿真实验中，面对高达 50% 的带宽损失，OptCC 的执行效率与无故障时的 NCCL 环形算法相比仅有 2-6% 的延迟增量，而现有先进方案的延迟增量高达 57%。

Conclusion: OptCC 能够有效解决异构带宽下的 AllReduce 通信延迟问题，在 50% 带宽衰减的故障场景下，性能损失仅为 2-6%，显著优于现有容错机制。

Abstract: Network failures are among the most frequent hardware faults in large-scale GPU clusters and a leading cause of training-job interruptions. Modern collective communication libraries such as NCCL mitigate network failures by rerouting traffic through surviving NICs on the same server, trading reduced inter-node bandwidth for uninterrupted training. However, the degraded server remains on the critical path of the standard ring algorithm, slowing the entire collective. We present the first information-theoretic lower bound on AllReduce completion time under asymmetric network bandwidth and show that when the straggler retains at least half of its original bandwidth, the unavoidable overhead relative to the fault-free optimum is only O(1/p) for p GPUs. We then design OptCC, a four-stage pipelined AllReduce algorithm that approaches this lower bound. Experiments on SimAI confirm that OptCC closes the gap left by existing fault-tolerant schemes: under practical network failures with up to 50% bandwidth loss, OptCC completes AllReduce within 2-6% of NCCL's fault-free ring performance, whereas the state-of-the-art incurs up to 57% overhead.

</details>


### [159] [Scalable Concurrent Queues for GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01693)
*Pratheek Prakash Shetty,Thomas R. W. Scogland,Wu-chun Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对超算系统中的GPU性能瓶颈，设计并实现了三种线性化的并发队列（G-WFQ-YMC, G-LFQ, G-WFQ），解决了在SIMT和海量并行环境下实现高效任务交换和避免锁争用的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HPC系统向千万级核心扩展，高效的并发队列对于任务分发和负载均衡至关重要。传统的CPU队列设计在GPU的SIMT执行模型、海量并行及原子竞争环境下不再适用，缺乏专门针对GPU设计的并发队列方案。

Method: 提出并实现三种GPU并发队列：(1) G-WFQ-YMC：基于预分配段的无等待队列；(2) G-LFQ：使用波次批处理（wave-batched）快速路径的有界无锁队列；(3) G-WFQ：通过将共享状态压缩进64位CAS操作来实现线性化和有界内存的无等待队列。

Result: 这些设计成功克服了GPU架构下的内存分配和原子竞争限制，在高并发场景下显著提升了任务分发效率和整体并行加速比。

Conclusion: 本文提出的三种线性化GPU并发队列（G-WFQ-YMC, G-LFQ, G-WFQ）成功解决了在超大规模并行环境下GPU并发队列的性能瓶颈问题，证明了通过适配SIMT架构和处理原子竞争，可以在GPU上实现高效的无锁和无等待队列。

Abstract: Concurrent queues can significantly impact supercomputing performance by being critical bottlenecks for task distribution, load balancing, and resource utilization. As HPC systems move beyond 10-million processor cores, the ability to rapidly move items between producer and consumer threads without excessive locking is essential for efficient queues, preventing idle cores, maximizing utilization, and achieving high parallel speedup. While concurrent queues are well studied on CPUs, they remain largely unexplored on modern GPUs, where SIMT execution, massive parallelism, and atomic contention reshape the design space. We present three linearizable GPU concurrent queues spanning from lock-free to wait-free guarantees: (1) G-WFQ-YMC, an adaptation of Yang and Mellor-Crummey's wait-free queue using preallocated segments; (2) G-LFQ, a bounded lock-free queue that uses wave-batched fast paths to maximize throughput; and (3) G-WFQ, a bounded wait-free queue that packs shared state into 64-bit compare-and-swap operations while preserving linearizability and bounded memory.

</details>


### [160] [Night-Window Batching versus Carbon-Aware Scheduling for Clinical AI GPU Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01766)
*Nishi Doshi,Shrey Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过仿真证明，在医疗计算任务中，简单的“夜间批处理”调度策略能在有效降低碳足迹的同时，比复杂调度算法更好地保证紧急医疗任务的截止期限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机构在GPU上运行机器学习负载日益增加，而电网碳排放强度随时间剧烈波动。如何在保障临床任务截止期限（Clinical Priority & Deadline）的前提下，实现医疗计算的低碳化调度，成为主要研究动机。

Method: 利用计算机仿真技术构建混合GPU环境模型；对比了13种不同的调度规则（包括基于紧急程度、时间、碳排放强度的不同权重组合）；设置“合成患者任务”模拟真实临床环境下的负载特性。

Result: 夜间批处理规则在平均碳排放上弥补了“仅考虑紧急程度”与“最优感知策略（CUCA_0.45）”之间约78%的差距，同时在紧急任务截止期限的达标率上优于复杂策略。对比显示，激进的碳优化算法会导致紧急任务大量超时，不适用于临床生产环境。

Conclusion: 在医院医疗场景中，简单的“夜间批处理”策略在降低碳足迹方面表现优异，不仅能接近复杂的“碳感知”调度算法效果，还能更好地保障临床任务的截止期限要求。纯碳优化策略虽能显著减排，但会严重影响任务优先级，仅适用于极端压力测试。

Abstract: Hospitals run more machine learning on GPUs while the carbon footprint of grid electricity rises and falls through the day. Using a computer simulation, we compare $13$ scheduling rules on mixed GPU hardware, with synthetic patient-style jobs, urgency tiers, and time-of-day carbon traces. We do not study patient outcomes; every percentage we report is a simulator queue number, not a clinical finding. We ask whether running non-urgent jobs overnight is almost as good as a richer rule that mixes urgency and carbon (CUCA at weight 0.45, written CUCA$_{0.45}$). The comparison keeps carbon reduction secondary to clinical priority and deadline compliance, so each policy is judged on both average kg CO$_2$e and missed-deadline behavior. CarbonGreedy and CarbonShift are carbon-first stress tests that demonstrate how poorly wrong vendor presets can disrupt clinical priorities, and are not meant for production. Numbers are averages over many test settings, with wide run-to-run spread and no statistical adjustment, so headline ratios are exploratory. On an eight-GPU baseline, the overnight rule closes about $78\%$ of the carbon gap between urgency-only and CUCA$_{0.45}$ while missing fewer urgent deadlines than either. CarbonShift lets about $46\%$ of the most urgent jobs miss their deadline; this is simulated queueing, not bedside harm. At $48$ jobs per hour, the carbon footprints almost tie, yet the overnight rule still misses fewer urgent deadlines. A geography test, where regions share one daily carbon shape with only timezone shifts, trims under one percentage point of average carbon; a twelve-hour routine window saves a little carbon for CUCA$_{0.45}$ but raises overall missed deadlines. Overnight batching stays competitive on average modelled carbon; carbon-only rules belong only in stress tests.

</details>


### [161] [Parallelizing Large-Scale Tensor Network Contraction on Multiple GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01852)
*Feng Pan,Hanfeng Gu,Paul Springer,Xipeng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于显式通信的分布式张量网络收缩框架，通过优化中间张量分配和通信策略，解决了传统切片方法在大规模并行计算中的冗余和扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的张量网络模拟主要依赖切片（slicing）技术，但该技术具有指数级扩展代价并存在大量冗余计算，限制了大规模量子电路和复杂多体系统的模拟效率。

Method: 提出了一种多GPU框架，摒弃了冗余计算的切片策略，转而将中间张量分布于多设备间。核心技术包括：基于GEMM导向的模式重排序（mode reordering），以及具备通信感知能力的模式分布规划（communication-aware distribution planning）。

Result: 在单节点8个H100 GPU上，实现了比传统切片法高出7-173倍的加速，计算利用率达到87-101%；在1024个H100 GPU集群上，性能提升幅度进一步扩大，最高达到67,869倍。

Conclusion: 在张量网络收缩任务中，通过显式通信进行张量分配的分布式策略显著优于传统的切片（slicing）方法。随着硬件规模的扩大（从单机到千卡集群），其性能优势呈指数级增长，克服了切片法在极端尺度下的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Exact tensor network contraction underpins quantum circuit simulation, quantum error correction, combinatorial optimization, and many-body dynamics. The dominant parallelization strategy, slicing, scales exponentially and incurs redundant computation. We present a multi-GPU framework that instead distributes intermediate tensors across devices with explicit communication, converting a fixed contraction path into a communication-efficient schedule via GEMM-oriented mode reordering and communication-aware mode distribution planning. Within a single DGX H100 node (8 GPUs, NVLink), distribution delivers $7$--$173\times$ extra speedup beyond embarrassingly parallel slicing, capturing nearly all of the available compute reduction (87--101%) because NVLink's high bandwidth keeps communication small relative to compute. Scaling the same four workloads to 1024 H100 GPUs over InfiniBand, the extra speedup beyond slicing ranges from $42\times$ to $67{,}869\times$, demonstrating that communication-aware distributed contraction far surpasses slicing-based scaling limits for frontier tensor networks.

</details>


### [162] [Boosting Multimodal Federated Learning via Chained Modality Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01856)
*Zixin Zhang,Fan Qi,Shuai Li,Xiaoshan Yang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FedMChain是一种新型多模态联邦学习框架，通过分阶段训练和符号驱动的聚合策略，解决了模态竞争导致的性能瓶颈，并降低了通信负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多模态学习视为联合优化问题，忽略了“模态竞争（Modality competition）”这一核心瓶颈，即强势模态会抑制弱势模态，导致全局模型性能欠佳。

Method: 1. 提出“分阶段训练（Phase-wise design）”，为每种模态提供专门的本地优化窗口以减少竞争；2. 引入“误差补偿正则化器（Error-compensated regularizer）”促进跨模态互补；3. 采用“稀疏符号引导聚合（Sparse sign-guided aggregation）”策略进行服务器端聚合，以实现鲁棒的模态内聚合并减少通信频率。

Result: 在多个多模态基准测试上表现出色，在提升预测性能的同时，实现了更低的通信频率。

Conclusion: FedMChain通过分阶段训练和稀疏符号引导聚合策略，有效缓解了多模态联邦学习中的模态竞争问题，在提升模型预测性能的同时降低了通信开销。

Abstract: Multimodal Federated Learning (MMFL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative learning across decentralized clients with heterogeneous data and modality availability. However, most existing MMFL methods cast multimodal training as a joint optimization problem, overlooking a key bottleneck: modality competition, where dominant modalities suppress weaker ones and lead to suboptimal global models. To address this, we propose FedMChain, a balanced MMFL framework that structures federated multimodal training as a chain of modality-wise phases. This phase-wise design gives each modality a dedicated local optimization window on multimodal clients to mitigate modality competition, and further promotes cross-modal complementarity via an error-compensated regularizer. On the server side, we employ a sparse sign-guided aggregation strategy that leverages directional sign agreement for robust intra-modality aggregation, avoids destructive averaging, and supports less frequent synchronization to reduce communication overhead. Extensive experiments on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that FedMChain consistently improves predictive performance while requiring less frequent communication than baselines.

</details>


### [163] [Scaling LLM Inference Beyond Amdahl`s Limits via Eliminating Non-Scalable Overheads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01927)
*Alan Zhao,Cyril Y. He,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Albireo 是一种新型推理系统，通过优化集群调度与计算的并行重叠，突破了张量并行度在大模型推理中的性能扩展瓶颈，显著提升了推理吞吐量与能源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线大模型推理需在固定 GPU 资源下最大化集群性能。张量并行（TP）虽然能解决显存限制，但受限于 Amdahl 定律，随 TP 度升高，通信开销会导致性能扩展性下降。研究旨在找到并突破最优 TP 度（t_e）的限制。

Method: 提出了 Albireo 推理系统，通过重叠机制（Overlap）缩减非可扩展部分（如调度、I/O），引入序列并行采样，在不改变模型架构的前提下提高了张量并行（TP）的最优度。

Result: 相比 vLLM，Albireo 在多模型和基准测试中实现了最高 1.9 倍的吞吐量提升，延迟降低 48%，GPU 利用率提升 28%，能耗降低 54%；生产环境吞吐量提升最高达 2 倍。

Conclusion: Albireo 系统有效提升了在线大模型推理的性能，证明了通过重叠调度、I/O 与计算及序列并行采样，能够优化分布式推理中的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Deployers of online LLM services usually seek to maximize cluster-wide performance given a fixed number of GPUs. Tensor parallelism (TP) is necessary to fit modern models but scales sub-linearly as the TP degree t grows, due to cross-GPU communication and non-scalable runtime work, as predicted by Amdahl's Law. Conversely, increasing t improves memory efficiency and alleviates KV-cache contention and swapping. We identify and validate an empirical optimal TP degree t_e that balances these effects. We present Albireo, a parallel inference system that raises the attainable t_e by shrinking the non-scalable portion via overlap of scheduling and I/O with compute and sequence-parallel sampling, without changing model architectures. Across models and benchmarks, Albireo achieves up to 1.9x higher throughput, 48% lower latency, 28% higher GPU utilization, and 54% lower energy than vLLM; in production it yields up to 2x higher throughput.

</details>


### [164] [TAPAAL SMC: Statistical Model Checking of Stochastic Timed-Arc Petri Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02007)
*Tanguy Dubois,Kim G. Larsen,Jiri Srba*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为Timed-Arc Petri net（TAPN）引入了首个随机语义，通过在TAPAAL模型检测器中实现统计模型检测（SMC）算法，解决了TAPN在处理随机行为和复杂系统验证时的建模与分析难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的TAPN模型虽然能够描述复杂的时间约束系统，但往往面临计算复杂度高或不可判定问题，且缺乏对随机行为的建模支持，难以满足现实应用的需求。

Method: 定义了TAPN的随机语义，并基于该语义设计并实现了统计模型检测（SMC）算法；在TAPAAL模型检测器中进行了实现，并通过多个案例研究验证了方法的有效性。

Result: 提出了完善的随机语义，证明了其数学特性良好（well-behaving），并在TAPAAL工具中实现了定量与定性的SMC功能，通过案例验证了其在复杂模型分析中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究为TAPN定义了首个随机语义，并成功将其集成至TAPAAL模型检测器中，通过SMC算法证明了该扩展在复杂系统验证中的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: Timed-Arc Petri net (TAPN) is a timed extension of the classical Petri net model where tokens have their age and input arcs are associated with time intervals restricting the ages of tokens available for transition firing. Additionally, a TAPN can also contain place invariants constraining the ages of tokens in places, inhibitor arcs preventing a transition from firing and transport arcs that preserve token ages upon firing. This set of features, as much as it allows us to model complex systems, also often makes verification problems computationally hard or even undecidable. Moreover, in order to model real-life examples, additional stochastic aspects are often necessary to capture the desired behaviour. We suggest the first stochastic semantics for TAPNs and design and implement the quantitative and qualitative Statistical Model Checking (SMC) algorithms in the model checker TAPAAL. We argue for the semantic choices we made in the stochastic semantics and prove that the semantics is well-behaving. On a number of case studies we demonstrate the practical applicability of our modelling formalism and its SMC implementation.

</details>


### [165] [EES-CND: Collaborative Neural Decision-Making for Drift-Aware Fault-Tolerant Edge-Cloud Service Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02259)
*Mohammadsadeq Garshasbi Herabad,Javid Taheri,Bestoun S. Ahmed,Calin Curescu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘云环境的故障处理问题，论文提出了一种基于协同神经网络与进化策略的自适应服务部署方法（EES-CND），有效提升了系统可靠性并大幅降低了运维成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘云计算环境具有异构性与高度复杂性，容易遭受硬件故障、软件缺陷及动态操作条件的影响，导致服务中断及服务级别目标（SLO）违规。因此，急需一种具备自适应能力的容错服务部署策略。

Method: 提出了一种名为 EES-CND（用于协作神经决策的增强进化策略）的容错服务部署方法。该方法利用多个轻量级神经网络进行协作推理，并采用增强的进化策略进行在线模型更新，以适应系统动态与性能漂移。

Result: 仿真实验表明，EES-CND 在服务恢复时间、响应时间及可靠性方面均优于现有方法，且容错成本较独立模型降低了 44.8%。

Conclusion: EES-CND 通过协同决策与在线模型演化，有效提升了边缘云环境下的容错服务部署能力，显著降低了故障恢复成本并提高了系统可靠性。

Abstract: The edge-cloud paradigm improves service delivery by orchestrating resources across edge nodes and cloud data centres. These environments consist of heterogeneous, interconnected computing nodes that cooperate to deliver continuous services. However, their scale and complexity increase vulnerability to failures from hardware malfunctions, software defects, and dynamic operating conditions. These failures can disrupt system configurations and service execution, leading to reduced reliability, performance degradation, and violations of service-level objectives. Ensuring service execution requires adaptive service placement strategies across edge-cloud resources. This study introduces a fault-tolerant service placement approach (Enhanced Evolution Strategy for Collaborative Neural Decision-making, EES-CND) for edge-cloud environments. The method employs collaborative decision-making, wherein multiple lightweight neural networks jointly infer redeployment strategies during failure events. To address the system dynamics and mitigate performance drift, adaptive models are updated online using an enhanced evolution strategy. Extensive simulations show that EES-CND effectively handles performance drift and significantly outperforms existing methods in service recovery time, response time, and reliability, achieving a 44.8\% reduction in fault-tolerance cost compared to standalone models.

</details>


### [166] [Strategies for Molecular Dynamics using Hybrid Systems: LAMMPS Use Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02319)
*Paulo Henrique Leme Ramalho,Dennis Alves Pedersen,Fábio Andrijauskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了 LAMMPS 在粗粒度生物分子模拟中的性能，指出混合式 MPI+OpenMP 模型在处理大规模多节点计算时，比纯 MPI 具有更好的扩展性和更高的效率，优于解决通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子模拟（如分子动力学和粗粒度建模）复杂度的提升对高性能计算（HPC）基础设施的需求大幅增加，亟需针对该类模拟在 HPC 环境下的性能优化策略。

Method: 在包含最多8个计算节点和1024个并发核心的HPC环境下，使用抗菌肽 Tritrpticin (PDB ID: 1D6X) 作为工作负载，对 LAMMPS 模拟器进行了系统化的性能与可扩展性分析。对比了纯 MPI 和混合 MPI+OpenMP 的执行情况，并分析了执行时间、加速比、并行效率、统计变异性及内部时间分解等指标。

Result: 1. 纯 MPI 在单节点环境下性能表现优异，但在多节点环境下因通信开销和进程间同步问题导致扩展性下降；2. 混合 MPI+OpenMP 配置在大规模下表现出更高的效率，有效降低了通信成本并更好地利用了 NUMA 内存层级；3. 内部时间分解显示，在大规模纯 MPI 运行中，通信和静电相互作用例程占用了大部分执行时间。

Conclusion: 在现代众核架构下，混合式 MPI+OpenMP 并行策略是实现粗粒度生物分子模拟高性能和可持续扩展性的更优选择。

Abstract: The complexity of biomolecular simulations has substantially increased the demand for High-Performance Computing (HPC) infrastructures, particularly in molecular dynamics and coarse-grained modeling. This work presents a systematic performance and scalability analysis of the LAMMPS simulator for coarse-grained biomolecular simulations, using the antimicrobial peptide Tritrpticin (PDB ID: 1D6X) as the experimental workload. Pure MPI and hybrid MPI+OpenMP executions were evaluated in HPC environments comprising up to 8 compute nodes and 1024 simultaneous cores. Metrics of execution time, speedup, parallel efficiency, statistical variability, and internal time decomposition were investigated. Results showed that pure MPI executions deliver excellent performance in single-node environments but suffer scalability degradation in multi-node executions due to communication overhead and inter-process synchronization. Hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations proved more efficient at large scale, reducing communication costs and better exploiting the NUMA memory hierarchy. The computational breakdown revealed that communication and electrostatic interaction routines accounted for the largest fraction of execution time at the largest pure-MPI scales. These results reinforce that performance of biomolecular HPC applications depends directly on the balance among parallelization granularity, spatial decomposition, and distributed communication costs. Hybrid MPI+OpenMP strategies represent a more sustainable alternative for coarse-grained biomolecular simulations on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [167] [Not All Errors Are Equal: A Systematic Study of Error Propagation in Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02430)
*Yafan Huang,Sheng Di,Guanpeng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了软错误对LLM推理的影响，提出了LLMFI故障注入框架，揭示了脆弱性模式，并为提升LLM在HPC环境下的可靠性提供了软件层面的改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）在高性能计算（HPC）中应用日益广泛，但目前关于软错误如何在该类环境下传播并影响LLM推理的研究依然处于空白。

Method: 开发了名为LLMFI的配置化、确定性故障注入框架，对三种开源LLM在13项涵盖推理、多语言、数学及编程任务中进行了系统性的故障注入测试，并结合细粒度案例研究揭示了脆弱性模式。

Result: 通过实验揭示了LLM推理中关键的脆弱性模式，确立了17条关于错误传播的深刻见解，并验证了四种可直接通过软件修改实现的低开销可靠性改进方向。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM推理中软错误的传播规律，总结了17条关键发现，并提出了四种低开销的软件级可靠性增强方案，为LLM的错误检测与缓解提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-performance computing (HPC) workflows, accelerating scientific discovery through diverse perspectives such as code generation and domain-specific decision-making. Yet, how soft errors propagate and affect LLM inference remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we present a comprehensive study on error propagation in LLM inference, enabled by our proposed LLMFI, a configurable and deterministic fault-injection framework. Using LLMFI, we systematically inject faults across three open-weighted LLMs and thirteen representative tasks, covering reasoning, multilingual, mathematical, and coding domains. In addition, we conduct fine-grained case studies that reveal critical vulnerability patterns. Overall, our study yields 17 takeaways that advance the understanding of error propagation in LLM inference and introduces four low-overhead directions to improve reliability through software-only modification, offering practical guidance for future error detection and mitigation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [SPARQLe: Sub-Precision Activation Representation for Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00365)
*Aradhana Mohan Parvathy,Soumendu Kumar Ghosh,Shamik Kundu,Arnab Raha,Souvik Kundu,Deepak A. Mathaikutty,Anand Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPARQLe 是一种通过利用激活值高位冗余和稀疏性来优化大模型推理的硬件-软件协同设计，能在保持 2k-bit 精度的情况下，通过亚精度计算和存储显著提升推理速度并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型规模的快速增长导致推理阶段算力和内存成本极高。现有的量化技术往往由于激活值需要高精度（如 8-bit）以维持准确率，导致硬件无法充分利用更低精度（如 4-bit）的数据通路进行高效运算。

Method: 提出了一种硬件-软件协同设计框架 SPARQLe：1. 采用混合存储格式，将 2k-bit 激活张量拆分为稠密的 k-bit LSB（低位）张量和稀疏的 k-bit MSB（高位）张量（配合精度位图）；2. 开发轻量级算法以增加 MSB 的稀疏性；3. 设计专用加速器直接处理该混合格式，减少内存流量和计算开销。

Result: 在 BitNet 3B、Llama2 7B 和 Llama3 8B 模型上，SPARQLe 将预填充（prefill）延迟降低了 16-24.3%，解码（decode）延迟降低了 13.5-23.4%；同时预填充能耗降低 17-26.7%，解码能耗降低 6.5-14.2%。

Conclusion: SPARQLe 通过硬件-软件协同设计，利用激活值在零附近的统计分布特性及高位冗余，实现了在不牺牲精度的前提下优化 LLM 推理效率。该研究证明了亚精度激活稀疏性是提升大模型推理效能的有效且互补的路径。

Abstract: The rapid growth in sizes of Large language models (LLMs) results in high compute and memory costs during inference. Quantization has been a significant pathway to addressing this challenge. In the quest to push the limits of quantization, weights, which are static, can often be quantized aggressively (e.g. 4 bits), while activations often require higher precision (e.g., 8 bits) to preserve accuracy, forcing hardware to operate with higher-precision datapaths. We leverage the statistical property that a significant fraction of activations are concentrated around zero, resulting in sparsity in the higher-order bits. Our proposal, SPARQLe, is a hardware-software co-design framework that exploits this sub-precision redundancy in any given quantized model. SPARQLe represents each 2k-bit activation tensor as a dense k-bit LSB tensor and a sparse k-bit MSB tensor compressed with a precision bitmap, and proposes a lightweight algorithm to increase MSB sparsity. SPARQLe reduces activation memory traffic and enables efficient computation on k-bit datapaths while preserving 2k-bit activation accuracy. SPARQLe includes an accelerator that operates directly on this hybrid format with minimal control overheads. Across the BitNet 3B, Llama2 7B, and Llama3 8B models, SPARQLe reduces prefill latency by 16-24.3% and decode latency by 13.5-23.4%, with 17-26.7% and 6.5-14.2% lower prefill and decode energy, respectively. SPARQLe demonstrates that sub-precision activation sparsity offers an effective and complementary pathway towards efficient LLM inference.

</details>


### [169] [Regular-Dead on Arrival: Characterizing and Protecting Against Dead-Entry TLB Misses in GPU Microarchitectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00486)
*Shafayat Mowla Anik,Yongchan Jung,Jeeho Ryoo,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对GPU中高昂的L2 TLB冗余遍历问题，本文通过分类诊断负载类型，提出了一种基于布隆过滤器的保护机制（DEPOT），在低开销下有效减少了性能损耗并提升了硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU工作负载中存在显著的冗余L2 TLB缺失问题，这些“死条目”缺失严重影响了性能，且不同负载因内存访问结构差异，其瓶颈（突发放大或容量溢出）不同，需要针对性的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种两类分类方法对死条目缺失进行分类，并据此设计了DEPOT（Dead-Entry PrOTection）机制。该机制采用1 KB的布隆过滤器（Bloom filter）来保护近期被驱逐的翻译条目，防止其在重新安装后被立即替换。

Result: 分析了24个GPU负载，发现死条目缺失占比可达99%。DEPOT机制在干扰驱动型负载上实现了高达72%的IPC提升，在其他负载上保持零开销，并能与现有的TLB预取和压缩机制协同工作，进一步提升2%-7%的收益。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DEPOT方案通过保护近期驱逐的翻译条目，有效解决了GPU负载中的“死条目”导致的冗余页表遍历问题，在干扰驱动的负载中显著提升了IPC，且具备零开销和优秀的可组合性。

Abstract: GPU workloads with large memory footprints frequently suffer from redundant L2 TLB misses in which a recently evicted translation is immediately re-walked at full page-walk cost. We characterize these dead-entry misses across 24 GPU workloads, finding they account for up to 99% of L2 TLB misses in the most TLB-sensitive applications, yet their performance impact varies widely depending on memory access structure. Workloads where warps share the same virtual page suffer from burst amplification, where a single eviction stalls many warps simultaneously waiting for one translation to return. In contrast, workloads where each warp accesses a distinct set of pages face a capacity-overflow problem that no replacement policy can resolve, a distinction validated by huge page experiments. Building on this two-class taxonomy, we design DEPOT (Dead-Entry PrOTection), a 1 KB Bloom filter mechanism that prevents recently evicted translations from being displaced immediately upon reinstallation, delivering up to 72% IPC improvement on interference-driven workloads with zero overhead on others, and composing with the state-of-the-art TLB prefetching and compaction mechanism, for 2 to 7% additional gain.

</details>


### [170] [Regular-Activation Concentration: Characterizing Column-Level Output Sparsity Across Diffusion Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00567)
*Dazhi Yang,Shafayat Mowla Anik,Byeong Kil Lee,Jeeho Ryoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了扩散模型加速器在“元素级”与“硬件列级”稀疏度之间的巨大差距，证明了仅靠元素级指标无法指导优化，并提出了基于模型维度和工作负载类型的针对性布局优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型加速器多基于“元素级稀疏性”进行优化，但主流硬件（如脉动阵列）是以“列”为单位处理数据的，这导致现有模型大幅高估了硬件可实际获得的加速效果。

Method: 通过对七种扩散模型工作负载（涵盖三种组别及四种模态）进行首次系统化的列级稀疏度表征，并结合GDDR6加速器上的周期级仿真，分析内存瓶颈及不同阈值下的准确性变化。

Result: 研究发现元素级稀疏度和实际硬件稀疏度之间存在高达78个百分点的巨大偏差；不同模型展现出三种稀疏分布模式（集中、分散、极值），周期级仿真显示内存停顿占总周期的84%-89%，且不同模型在准确性下降方面表现出显著的异质性（如部分模型直接出现性能断崖）。

Conclusion: 现有的“元素级稀疏度”指标无法准确评估扩散模型在脉动阵列硬件上的加速潜力。研究表明，硬件可利用的稀疏度取决于工作负载组、模型维度及内存布局的综合影响，必须采用列级（Column-level）分析方法才能实现有效的性能优化。

Abstract: Recent diffusion accelerators exploit activation sparsity by skipping near-zero GELU outputs, reporting 52--85% element-level sparsity. However, systolic-array hardware processes activations at column granularity, where a single non-zero element forces the entire column to be computed. We present the first systematic column-level sparsity characterization across seven diffusion workloads spanning three workload groups and four modalities. Our measurements reveal that element-level sparsity overstates hardware-exploitable sparsity by up to 78 percentage points and exposes a three-way taxonomy. UNet+transformer workloads exhibit activation concentration with workload-dependent cycle reductions up to 30.6%. Pure-transformer DiT shows dispersion, yielding 12.4%. Motion/dance transformer workloads range from modest reductions to 50.8% for MLD, driven by its extreme token dimension and expansion ratio. Cycle-level simulation on a GDDR6-based accelerator confirms that memory stalls account for up to 84--89% of total cycles and that layout sensitivity tracks the profiling-based taxonomy. A full accuracy sweep across five thresholds reveals that UNet+transformer workloads degrade gracefully, while motion models exhibit an accuracy cliff between the primary operating point and the next threshold. Our characterization shows that workload group and model dimensions jointly determine whether column-level memory layout optimization is beneficial, and element-level sparsity alone is insufficient for that prediction.

</details>


### [171] [LP5X-PIM Sim: A High-Fidelity HW/SW Integrated Simulator for LPDDR5X-PIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00636)
*SangHoon Cha,Jaewan Choi,Byeongho Kim,Yoonah Paik,Sukhan Lee,Kyomin Sohn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 三星电子开发了一款用于LPDDR5X-PIM的高保真软硬件集成仿真器，旨在精确评估存内计算架构的性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在LPDDR5X-PIM（Processing-in-Memory，存内计算）硬件部署前，通过高精度的建模与仿真，精确评估系统的性能、能效以及PIM资源的利用率，从而指导架构设计与优化。

Method: 采用集成软硬件的仿真方法，对LPDDR5X-PIM块的硬件数据路径和软件控制层进行高保真建模，并针对实际硬件实现对现有框架进行了优化。

Result: 成功研发出LPDDR5X-PIM仿真器，实现了与实际硬件行为的高一致性，能够有效模拟和分析PIM架构的运行效能。

Conclusion: 该仿真器能够通过软硬件协同仿真，实现对LPDDR5X-PIM系统性能、能效及资源利用率的精确评估，是支持PIM架构设计与验证的关键技术支撑。

Abstract: This tech note describes the architecture and execution results of the LPDDR5X-PIM simulator, developed by Samsung Electronics. Based on the latest research and internal specifications, the simulator provides a high-fidelity model of both the hardware data paths and the software control layers of the LPDDR5X-PIM block. This integrated hardware-software simulation approach enables precise evaluation of system performance and energy efficiency while maximizing PIM resource utilization. We have refined existing simulation frameworks to align with actual hardware implementation, ensuring consistent behavioral accuracy. Further technical details regarding the specific architecture and circuit design of the LPDDR5X-PIM will be disclosed in future publications

</details>


### [172] [Linear Complexity Fermionic Simulation on Quantum Devices with Hardware Connectivity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00982)
*Xiangyu Gao,Winston Li,Jiakang Li,Zirui Li,Yipeng Huang,Costin Iancu,Eddy Z. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Accordion 是一种新型费米子系统量子编译框架，通过协同优化映射、合成与路由，解决了现有方法电路扩展性差、开销大的问题，在保证理论最优复杂度的同时，大幅提升了硬件执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的费米子系统量子模拟编译方法多采用独立阶段编译与局部启发式算法，导致电路深度和门计数呈超四次幂（superquartic）增长，且大规模实例的编译时间过长。

Method: 提出名为 Accordion 的端到端编译框架，利用 Jordan-Wigner 映射的结构规律性，将费米子到量子比特映射、电路合成与硬件路由三个阶段进行协同优化（Co-design），而非独立处理。

Result: 在理论上实现了与信息论下界 O(N^4) 相匹配的复杂度和电路深度。在实际硬件场景（线性结构、IBM heavy-hex、方格结构）中，门计数降低了最多 79%，电路深度降低了最多 77%。

Conclusion: Accordion 通过协同设计费米子到量子比特的映射、电路合成与硬件路由，打破了以往分阶段编译的局限。该框架证明了在全秩全连通电子结构哈密顿量下，能够达到 O(N^4) 的电路深度和门数量上限，在多种硬件架构上实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Simulating fermionic systems on quantum hardware requires compiling fermionic Hamiltonians into executable quantum circuits. Existing approaches treat each compilation stage independently, applying heuristics with localized objectives that produce circuits with superquartic gate count and depth scaling and compilation times reaching several hours for large instances. We present Accordion, an end-to-end framework that co-designs the fermion-to-qubit mapping with circuit synthesis and hardware routing. Accordion fixes the Jordan Wigner mapping, which despite its higher Pauli weight produces Pauli operators with structural regularity that enables provably efficient circuit generation. For full-rank all-to-all electronic structure Hamiltonians, we prove O(N^4) gate count and circuit depth, matching the information-theoretic lower bound imposed by the Theta(N^4) second excitation terms. On linear, IBM heavy-hex, and square-grid architectures, Accordion reduces gate count by up to 79% and circuit depth by up to 77% relative to the best baseline.

</details>


### [173] [OpenEye: A Scalable Open-Source Hardware Accelerator for DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01450)
*Denis Lebold,Hendrik Wöhrle*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: OpenEye 是一种面向嵌入式平台的可扩展、稀疏感知 FPGA 加速器，通过模块化流式架构有效提升了深度学习计算的资源效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络推理的计算复杂度不断增加，导致嵌入式平台在资源消耗和可扩展性方面面临严峻挑战，因此需要一种高效、可配置且能利用数据稀疏性的硬件加速方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于 FPGA 的可扩展且具备稀疏性感知（sparsity-aware）的硬件加速器（OpenEye）。采用流式数据流（streaming-based dataflow）架构，由多个处理单元（PE）集群组成，支持权重和激活信息的稀疏计算，并通过高度参数化的硬件设计实现近线性的扩展性。

Result: 在 Xilinx ZU19EG FPGA 平台上对多种配置进行了验证，结果表明该架构能够高效处理卷积、稠密和池化操作，且随着 PE 数量增加，路由与互联开销保持近线性增长，验证了其在不同性能和资源约束下的有效性和扩展性。

Conclusion: OpenEye 架构在资源利用率、执行延迟和可扩展性之间取得了良好的平衡，通过其模块化和稀疏性感知设计，能够有效应对嵌入式平台对深度学习加速器高性能与低功耗的需求。

Abstract: The increasing computational complexity of deep neural network inference poses significant challenges for efficient hardware acceleration on embedded platforms, particularly with respect to resource consumption and scalability. This work presents OpenEye, a scalable and sparsity-aware FPGA-based hardware accelerator designed to efficiently execute common neural network operations such as convolutions, dense layers, and pooling.
  OpenEye is based on a highly parameterizable architecture composed of clusters of processing elements interconnected by a streaming-based dataflow. The paper provides a detailed explanation of the internal operation of the accelerator, including data movement, buffering strategies, control logic, and the coordination between clusters and PEs. The architecture natively supports sparse weights and activations, enabling the efficient processing of sparse data without unnecessary computations or memory accesses.
  A key design property of OpenEye is its scalability: the number of clusters and processing elements can be varied to adapt the accelerator to different performance and resource constraints. The design achieves a near-linear scaling of routing and interconnect overhead with increasing PE counts, which is essential for maintaining efficiency on large FPGA devices.
  To evaluate scalability across different design points, multiple OpenEye configurations with varying cluster and PE sizes were implemented on a Xilinx ZU19EG FPGA. Representative neural network operations, including convolutional, fully connected, and pooling layers, were used to analyze resource utilization, execution latency, and scalability behavior. The results show favorable trade-offs between performance and resource consumption across the explored configurations.

</details>


### [174] [O-POPE: High-Frequency Pipelined Outer Product based GEMM acceleration with minimal buffering overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02333)
*Danilo Cammarata,Angelo Garofalo,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: O-POPE 是一种新型浮点矩阵乘法（GEMM）加速器，通过复用 FPU 流水线寄存器作为缓存，在实现高频率和高利用率的同时，极大地降低了硬件开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习任务中 GEMM 占据了大量的计算和能耗；现有的浮点 GEMM 加速器难以在工作频率、算术利用率和缓冲开销之间取得良好的平衡。

Method: 提出 O-POPE（Scalable Outer-Product Engine）架构，利用输出平稳（Output-Stationary）外积执行模式的数据复用优势，并创新性地将 FPU 内部的流水线寄存器重用为缓冲区，从而降低了额外的缓冲开销。

Result: 在 12nm FINFET 工艺下实现了 1 GHz 工作频率；FPU 利用率高达 99.97%；缓冲区面积占比小于 2%（2048-MACs 配置）；相较于现有先进方案，性能提升 1.33 倍，性能密度提升 9%，能效提升 8%。

Conclusion: O-POPE 通过复用 FPU 流水线寄存器作为缓冲区，提出了一种高效、可扩展的外积引擎架构，在保证高精度的前提下，有效解决了高性能浮点矩阵乘法（GEMM）在硬件效率上的权衡难题。

Abstract: General matrix multiply (GEMM) dominates both execution time and energy consumption of modern machine learning (ML) workloads, placing increasing pressure on hardware efficiency. While quantization mitigates computational and data movement costs, accuracy-sensitive tasks such as training still require higher-precision floating-point formats. Existing floating-point GEMM accelerators face trade-offs between operating frequency, arithmetic utilization, and buffering overhead. This work presents O-POPE, a scalable outer-product engine that achieves concurrently high utilization, low overhead, and a fast operating frequency by repurposing floating-point unit (FPU) pipeline registers as buffers. This solution leverages the data-reuse advantages of output-stationary outer-product execution and enables 1 GHz (0.72 V) operation in 12 nm FINFET technology with less than 2% buffer area for a 2048-MACs configuration. Our evaluation shows that O-POPE achieves up to 99.97% FPU utilization and improves performance (1.33x), performance density by 9%, and energy efficiency by 8%, compared to state-of-the-art floating-point GEMM accelerators.

</details>


### [175] [CHIMERA: A Flexible and Scalable 3.1 TOPS/W AI-MCU with Transformer Accelerator and 563 Gb/s Shared-L2 Memory Subsystem with QoS Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02358)
*Lorenzo Leone,Philip Wiese,Gamze İslamoğlu,Michael Rogenmoser,Davide Rossi,Francesco Conti,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Chimera 是一款专为超低功耗边缘端设计的 Transformer 加速 MCU，通过创新的多核集群与 L2 内存岛技术，实现了极高的能效比与面积效率，有效支持实时 Transformer 模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对实时边缘设备（数百毫瓦功耗受限）在大规模、快速演变的 Transformer 模型推理上算力不足且能效受限的问题，亟需一种兼具灵活性与扩展性的低功耗架构解决方案。

Method: 设计了一款集成 transformer 加速器的计算集群（含 9 个 RV32IMA 内核），并引入了一种配备 L2 内存岛子系统的新型存储架构，该子系统支持跨集群数据共享并具备 QoS 延迟保证机制。

Result: 峰值能效达到 3.1 TOPS/W，面积效率为 281 GOPS/mm2；相比同类先进 SoC，能效提升 1.37 倍，面积效率提升高达 100 倍；同时具备极高的内存带宽（563 Gb/s）和显著的延迟削减效果（最高 16 倍）。

Conclusion: Chimera 是一款在 22nm 工艺下实现的高效边缘计算 MCU，证明了通过创新的内存子系统和架构设计，可在极低功耗限制下有效加速不断演进的 Transformer 模型，且在能效和面积效率上均显著优于现有最先进的（SoA）SoC。

Abstract: We present Chimera, a flexible and scalable Microcontroller Unit (MCU) designed to accelerate real-time inference of rapidly evolving transformer-based models at the ultra-low-power edge (hundred of mW). The chip, implemented in 22 nm FDX technology, integrates a transformer accelerator tightly coupled within a compute cluster featuring nine general-purpose RV32IMA cores. Scalability extends to the memory hierarchy through a novel L2 memory island subsystem, which enables data sharing across multiple clusters while delivering 563 Gb/s aggregate bandwidth. The L2 subsystem enforces quality-of-service guarantees for latency-critical traffic, achieving up to 16x latency reduction. Chimera achieves peak energy and area efficiencies of 3.1 TOPS/W and 281 GOPS/mm2, demonstrating 1.37x higher energy efficiency and up to 100x higher area efficiency compared to State of the Art (SoA) SoCs. Compared to SoA standalone accelerators, Chimera achieves comparable energy efficiency and up to 1.8x higher area efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Edge-Based QoS-Aware Adaptive Task Placement: A Closed-Loop Control in Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00552)
*Thien Tran,Jonathan Kua,Thuong Hoang,Minh Tran,Honghao Lyu,Jiong Jin*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种QoS感知的自适应任务放置（ATP）控制器，通过实时监测延迟与资源利用率动态调整多机器人系统在边缘端的任务部署，有效解决了静态卸载带来的长尾延迟和任务逾期问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统（MRS）通过向边缘节点卸载计算密集型任务来满足严格的服务质量（QoS）约束，但静态任务编排在面临网络延迟、抖动和资源竞争时会导致性能严重下降。现有方案缺乏对动态环境的实时适应性。

Method: 提出了一种QoS感知的自适应任务放置（ATP）控制器，通过基于多指标代价函数（归一化延迟、CPU利用率、切换开销）在两秒控制窗口内对放置方案进行评分，并根据监测到的延迟和资源利用率阈值动态切换任务位置。此方法在一个使用树莓派搭建的包含相机到机械臂管道的试点边缘计算测试平台上进行了评估，该平台集成了亚毫秒级时钟同步和网络仿真。

Result: 实验表明，静态边缘卸载虽然降低了机载CPU负载，但加剧了长尾延迟和任务期限逾期。相比之下，ATP控制器能动态切换任务放置，显著降低了任务期限违规频率和长尾延迟，在计算压力和网络故障场景下表现稳健。

Conclusion: ATP是一种实用的边缘侧控制原语，证明了在云边机器人部署中，引入考虑服务质量（QoS）的多目标工作负载编排对工业信息物理系统至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems (MRS) increasingly offload compute-intensive perception tasks to edge nodes to meet strict time-sensitive Quality-of-Service (QoS) constraints. However, static task orchestration on a shared edge node can severely degrade QoS due to network latency, jitter, and edge-resource contention. We present a pilot edge-centric MRS testbed using Raspberry Pi nodes to evaluate a camera-to-manipulator pipeline under three modes: local execution, static offloading, and a QoS-aware Adaptive Task Placement (ATP) controller. ATP scores candidate placements using a multi-metric cost (normalized latency, CPU utilization, and switching overhead) over two-second control windows. The closed-loop visual servoing testbed is instrumented with sub-millisecond clock synchronization, network emulation, and detailed monitoring of multiple metrics across nodes to capture realistic jitter. Experimental results under compute-stress and network-fault scenarios show that static edge offloading reduces on-board CPU load but amplifies tail latency and deadline misses. In contrast, the QoS-aware ATP controller, by switching task placement based on measured latency and utilization thresholds, consistently lowers deadline violations and tail latency. Overall, the results position ATP as a practical edge-side control primitive for MRS and concrete design guidelines for Cloud-Edge Robotics deployments within the broader cloud-fog automation, while motivating QoS-aware multi-objective workload orchestration for industrial cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [177] [Idleness is Relative: Exploiting Tool-Call Idle Windows for Offloading in Agentic Systems with MORI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00866)
*Tian Xia,Hanchen Li,Zhifei Li,Xiaokun Chen,Hao Kang,Yifan Qiao,Yi Xu,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: MORI 是一种面向代理工作负载的 LLM 推理卸载系统，通过将程序按“空闲程度”排序并动态分配内存，解决了传统缓存策略在跨层传输 KV Cache 时的高代价问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理工作负载（Agentic Workloads）具有频繁的工具调用和 KV Cache 重用特性，传统的 LRU 等缓存驱逐策略无法适应其复杂的“忙碌/空闲”周期转换，导致在 GPU 和 CPU 内存层级间进行频繁、低效的卸载和迁移。

Method: MORI（Memory-Optimized Reinforcement-based Inference 系统）：提出了一种基于“空闲程度”的连续分级模型。该方法对所有活跃程序按空闲程度进行动态排序，将最忙碌的程序置于 GPU HBM，最空闲的置于 CPU DRAM，并根据硬件容量动态调整分界线和强制执行各层准入控制。

Result: 在 Claude Code 的实际编程代理工作负载测试中，MORI 相比现有的最佳卸载基准系统，吞吐量提高了 20%–71%，首次令牌延迟（TTFT）降低了 18%–43%。

Conclusion: MORI 通过引入基于 idleness（空闲程度）的连续分级管理机制，有效地解决了代理工作负载（agentic workloads）中 KV Cache 卸载引起的性能瓶颈，显著提升了吞吐量并降低了首次令牌延迟（TTFT）。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving systems increasingly host agentic workloads, whose sessions issue tens of model invocations interleaved with tool calls, accumulating KV cache that can be reused across steps. As requests' total KV cache size easily exceeds GPU HBM capacity, researchers offload them to CPU DRAM. However, tool-call durations span orders of magnitude, and the cost of transferring KV cache between tiers makes it impractical to re-place entries on every call. We observe that agentic programs exhibit a two-phase structure: busy phases of rapid short tool calls and idle phases dominated by long-running calls. Current eviction policies such as LRU fail to capture this property. A binary busy/idle label also falls short because the ratio of busy to idle programs may not match the hardware's GPU-to-CPU capacity ratio. When it does not, one tier sits underutilized while the other is oversubscribed, wasting memory or forcing unnecessary evictions. We present MORI, an agent serving system that solves the above problem. Our key insight is that idleness is a continuous, relative spectrum. MORI ranks all active programs by idleness, assigns the busiest to GPU HBM and the most idle to CPU DRAM, dynamically shifts the partition boundary to match hardware capacity, and enforces admission control at each memory tier. Evaluated on real coding agent workloads collected from Claude Code across four GPU and model pairs, MORI delivers 20--71% higher throughput and 18--43% lower TTFT than the best baseline with offloading.

</details>


### [178] [Characterizing Metastable Faults and Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00942)
*Ali Farahbakhsh,Qingjie Lu,Lorenzo Alvisi,Andreas Haeberlen,Robbert Van Renesse*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文通过建立“亚稳态故障”的因果模型，重新定义了亚稳态失效，打破了其仅与过载相关的成见，并提出了一套通用的系统设计方法以构建亚稳态故障容错（MFT）系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工作主要将亚稳态失效视为过载现象，缺乏对潜在因果动力学的理解，且无法解释不表现为过载的亚稳态失效情况，难以有效预防和缓解此类故障。

Method: 1. 提出亚稳态故障（metastable faults）的因果特征分析模型；2. 识别系统组件间结构性不稳定交互循环；3. 开发了一套预测亚稳态失效并构建亚稳态故障容错（MFT）系统的通用方法论。

Result: 在三个案例研究中成功应用了该方法论，验证了其在识别、预测和构建亚稳态故障容错系统方面的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将亚稳态失效归因于“亚稳态故障”（即组件间的不稳定交互循环），研究提供了一种系统性的防御与预测框架，证明了亚稳态失效可以通过设计可容错系统来规避，而不单单是过载问题。

Abstract: Metastable failures are hard to detect, prevent, and mitigate. During a metastable failure, a system exhibits self-sustaining bad behavior even in the absence of adversarial conditions. Prior work focuses on symptoms and has portrayed metastable failures as instances of self-sustaining overload. This characterization leaves the underlying failure causes and dynamics unknown, and does not account for metastable failures that do not manifest as overload. We present the first causal characterization of metastable failures by identifying their origin in metastable faults, i.e., structural destabilizing cycles of interaction among systems components that, in isolation, are stabilizing. Metastable failures arise when scheduling decisions let these destabilizing interactions gain the upper hand over the individual components' stabilizing tendencies. We then derive a methodology to predict metastable failures, and to build metastable-fault-tolerant (MFT) systems. We apply our methodology to three case studies, showcasing the generality of our results.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [VLAMotor: Test-Guided Enhancement of Vision-Language-Action Models via Agent-BasedData Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00053)
*Zeqin Liao,Peifan Ren,Zixu Gao,Hongyu Gong,Lianyu Hu,Wenbing Tang,Yuhong Nan,Zibin Zheng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型在边缘情境下的失效问题，VLAMotor 提出了一个闭环分析框架：通过距离感知测试暴露失败模式，利用智能体生成修复数据进行模型微调，显著提升了机器人的任务成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型多依赖数据驱动，在处理边缘情境（edge-case）时表现不足，且目前缺乏将“失败检测”转化为“模型修复”的有效闭环机制。

Method: VLAMotor 包含两个核心模块：1. 距离感知测试：基于训练数据距离估计输入不确定性，结合冗余消除构建紧凑测试集以高效暴露失败；2. 智能体修复：将失败轨迹抽象为语义表示，规划参数化修复序列，通过逆运动学和运动执行生成成功轨迹，并以此对原始 VLA 模型进行自动标注与微调。

Result: 在四项机器人操作任务中，VLAMotor 生成的测试用例失败触发率为 92.33%，测试覆盖率比现有最佳工具提升了 18.93%。通过合成数据微调，模型成功率在模拟环境中提升了 49.25%，在真实硬件上提升了 57.50%。

Conclusion: VLAMotor 提供了一种高效、低成本的 VLA 模型增强方案，通过闭环的“测试-修复-微调”流程，显著提高了模型在模拟和真实机器人任务中的泛化性和成功率，是解决 VLA 模型长尾问题（edge-case）的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models follow a data-driven paradigm and are constrained by the coverage of training data, making them prone to failure on edge-case configurations after deployment. To mitigate such risks, it is essential to expose high-quality failure modes and convert the resulting failures into supervisory data for model enhancement. Existing studies largely stop at failure detection and lack a mechanism for leveraging discovered failures for model repair.
  We propose VLAMotor, the first analysis framework for VLA enhancement, which integrates distance-aware model testing for failure exposure and agent-based data synthesis for model finetunning. First, VLAMotor estimates input uncertainty based on the distance to training samples, and combines uncertainty ranking with redundancy elimination to build compact test sets that expose diverse failures. Then, VLAMotor abstracts failure trajectories into structured semantic representations, and plans parameterized repair-skill sequences, which are then realized as executable trajectories through inverse kinematics and motion execution. The resulting successful trajectories are automatically labeled and used to fine-tune the original VLA model, yielding an enhanced VLA model. Evaluation on four representative robotic manipulation tasks shows that 92.33% of the in-simulation test cases generated by VLAMotor trigger VLA failures, and VLAMotor improves test coverage over the state-of-the-art tool by 18.93%. By fine-tuning VLA models with synthetic data derived from failed test cases, VLAMotor further enhances the overall success rate of VLA models by 49.25%. When deployed on real hardware, the simulation-enhanced models improve the success rate over the original VLA models by 57.50%, demonstrating an effective and low-cost direction for VLA enhancement.

</details>


### [180] [From Human Videos to Robot Manipulation: A Survey on Scalable Vision-Language-Action Learning with Human-Centric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00054)
*Zhiyuan Feng,Qixiu Li,Huizhi Liang,Rushuai Yang,Yichao Shen,Zhiying Du,Zhaowei Zhang,Yu Deng,Li Zhao,Hao Zhao,Zongqing Lu,Oier Mees,Marc Pollefeys,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用海量人类视频辅助具身智能（VLA）模型训练的研究进展，将现有方法归纳为四种范式，并讨论了闭合动作标注缺失、具身差异及部署评估等关键开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型高度依赖昂贵的机器人演示数据，且与特定具身紧密耦合；而人类视频丰富且蕴含多样化的交互信息，但由于具身差异和缺乏动作标注，难以直接用于机器人训练。本研究旨在建立统一的视角，探讨如何将人类视频转化为VLA模型的高效知识。

Method: 该调查综述将现有的利用人类视频辅助VLA训练的方法分为四类：(i)编码帧间变化的潜在动作表示；(ii)预测未来帧的预测性世界模型；(iii)提取图像平面线索的显式2D监督；(iv)恢复几何或运动信息的显式3D重建。

Result: 通过系统分类梳理，本文明确了人类视频在VLA训练中的四种知识提取路径，并提出了三个核心挑战：视频结构化处理、跨具身动作映射的接地（grounding），以及更有效的部署性能评估协议。

Conclusion: 本文概括了利用人类视频提升VLA模型能力的现有技术路线，分析了核心挑战，并为未来的研究方向提供了指导。总结认为，从人类视频中提取知识是突破机器人数据瓶颈的关键，但需解决具身差异和动作对齐等核心矛盾。

Abstract: Recent progress in generalizable embodied control has been driven by large-scale pretraining of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, most existing approaches rely on large collections of robot demonstrations, which are costly to obtain and tightly coupled to specific embodiments. Human videos, by contrast, are abundant and capture rich interactions, providing diverse semantic and physical cues for real-world manipulation. Yet, embodiment differences and the frequent absence of task-aligned annotations make their direct use in VLA models challenging. This survey provides a unified view of how human videos are transformed into effective knowledge for VLA models. We categorize existing approaches into four classes based on the action-related information they derive: (i) latent action representations that encode inter-frame changes; (ii) predictive world models that forecast future frames; (iii) explicit 2D supervision that extracts image-plane cues; and (iv) explicit 3D reconstruction that recovers geometry or motion. Beyond this taxonomy, we highlight three key open challenges in this area: structuring unstructured videos into training-ready episodes, grounding video-derived supervision into robot-executable actions under embodiment and viewpoint heterogeneity, and designing evaluation protocols that better predict real-world deployment performance and transfer efficiency, thereby informing future research directions. A curated list of papers and resources is available at https://github.com/AaronFengZY/HumanCentricToVLA-Survey.

</details>


### [181] [Reinforcement Learning for Optimal Experiment Design in Parameter Identification of Mechatronic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00059)
*Julian Langschwert,Georg Schaefer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于奖励塑造的强化学习方法，用于机电系统激励信号设计，实现了在满足硬件安全约束下的高精度自动系统辨识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的系统辨识方法依赖专家知识设计激励信号，难以平衡辨识精度与硬件安全，且通用性较差。

Method: 提出了一种强化学习（RL）代理方法，通过奖励塑造（reward shaping）机制在训练过程中自动实施硬件安全约束，并在Quanser Aero 2测试台上进行训练与验证。

Result: 在10个独立训练种子实验中，该RL代理实现了具有竞争力的估计精度，模型参数辨识效果优于传统基准方法，且安全违规率控制在0.75%的低水平。

Conclusion: 强化学习代理能够在保证系统硬件安全的前提下，自动生成最优激励信号，实现机电系统的准确辨识，证明了其在无需人工调试信号情况下的优越性和自适应性。

Abstract: Informative excitation signals are critical for accurate system identification of mechatronic systems, yet classical system identification (SI) approaches require expert knowledge and hand-crafted signal design to respect hardware safety constraints, limiting their generalizability. We propose a reinforcement learning (RL) agent that learns optimal excitation signals for a Quanser Aero 2 testbed while autonomously enforcing safety constraints through reward shaping. Evaluated across 10 independent training seeds, our comprehensive agent achieves competitive estimation accuracy across all three identified parameters, outperforming classical baselines while incurring only 0.75% safety violations.

</details>


### [182] [Linear Motility Maps in Nonlinear Viscous Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00063)
*Yishun Zhou,Shai Revzen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了低雷诺数下微尺度运动的几何力学理论，证明了在特定的非牛顿流体（Carreau-Yasuda）中，经典“扇贝定理”失效，通过往复运动即可实现有效位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究低雷诺数下“扇贝定理”（reciprocal motion不能产生位移）在不同流体环境中的适用性，特别是是否存在能打破该定理限制的流体物理机制。

Method: 利用几何力学中的运动映射（motility map）框架，推导了其在幂律流体（Ostwald-de Waele）中的适用性，并利用“尺蠖模型”（两个不等质量、不同阻力系数的物体进行往复运动）在 Carreau-Yasuda 流体中进行建模分析。

Result: 证明了线性速度运动映射适用于幂律流体；发现在 Carreau-Yasuda 流体中，通过非线性阻力关系，可以通过往复运动产生净位移，且运动方向可通过改变运动速度进行切换。

Conclusion: 即使在“扇贝定理”限制的往复运动下，非牛顿流体（如 Carreau-Yasuda 流体）中的非线性阻力关系也可以实现净位移，这为微纳尺度运动系统的设计提供了新的机制。

Abstract: Systems moving in low Reynolds number fluid regimes are known to be governed by a ``motility map'' which linearly relates their shape change rates to they body frame velocity moving through the fluid. A consequence of this is ``Purcell's Scallop Theorem'' -- a locomotion system that undergoes shape changes that follow the same path forward and backward in time (reciprocal body deformations) cannot achieve net displacement, regardless of pacing of those changes.We show that linear-in-velocity motility maps extend to any power law viscosity (a.k.a. Ostwald--de Waele fluid), and therefore to many biological fluids in intermediate shear ranges. We also show that the linear-in-velocity property can be violated in Carreau-Yasuda fluids to produce net motion using an ``inchworm'' model consisting of two unequal masses with unequal drag coefficients performing reciprocal motions. Interestingly, the direction of motion can be switched by changing speeds. Our results show that the linear motility map of geometric mechaincs can be used to analyze and design locomotion in power-law fluids, and that some nonlinear drag relationships such as Carreau-Yasuda can be exploited to generate net locomotion in seeming violation of the ``scallop theorem''.

</details>


### [183] [Invascal: Inverse-Vacuity Self-Calibration for Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Range-View Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00069)
*Kerim Turacan,Hannes Reichert,Andrei Bolandut,Konrad Doll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Invascal的轻量级不确定性估计框架，旨在解决LiDAR语义分割中实时性与不确定性估计准确性难以兼顾的问题，通过分解预测头与新型优化目标，实现了高效、鲁棒的不确定性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR语义分割中现有的置信度评估方法（如Softmax）往往校准不佳且过度自信，而准确的方法（如MC Dropout、集成学习）计算开销过大，难以满足自动驾驶的实时性需求，因此需要一种既高效又可靠的不确定性估计方案。

Method: 1. 提出“不确定性感知自适应头”（Uncertainty-aware Adapter Head），将预测分解为偏好头（Preference Head，用于类别排序）和强度头（Strength Head，用于评估不确定性）；2. 构建基于证据狄利克雷分布（Evidential Dirichlet representation）的表示方法；3. 提出一种逆空自我校准目标函数（Invascal），用于监督强度信号，防止证据过度增长。

Result: 在多个LiDAR数据集和骨干网络上进行了验证。结果显示，该方法在显著提升不确定性校准效果的同时，计算开销极小，且在语义分割精度上优于现有的证据深度学习方法，保持了与确定性模型相当的竞争实力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种架构无关的自适应头（Adapter Head）和Invascal训练目标，在保持高性能LiDAR语义分割精度的同时，实现了实时、可靠且校准良好的不确定性估计，克服了传统方法无法兼顾精度与计算效率的局限性。

Abstract: LiDAR semantic segmentation is a core perception capability for autonomous vehicles and mobile robots. However, safe operation also depends on knowing when predictions are unreliable. Existing approaches typically rely on softmax confidence, which is often miscalibrated and overconfident, while stronger uncertainty estimates from Monte Carlo dropout or ensembles are often computationally expensive for real-time use. To this end, we introduce a novel, architecture-agnostic uncertainty-aware Adapter Head. It decomposes the prediction into a Preference Head for class ranking and a Strength Head that refines uncertainty assessment, thereby enabling a principled construction of evidential Dirichlet representations. Building on this design, we propose our inverse-vacuity self-calibration objective (Invascal), which directly supervises the strength signal to produce reliable and well-calibrated uncertainty estimates while preventing runaway evidence growth. We evaluate our framework across multiple LiDAR datasets and backbone architectures. We compare against deterministic training, Monte Carlo dropout and ensembles, and prior evidential methods. Our approach consistently improves uncertainty calibration over traditional deterministic methods with minimal computational overhead. At the same time, it preserves competitive segmentation accuracy, where prior evidential methods often suffer performance degradation.

</details>


### [184] [Balancing Accuracy and Efficiency: Adaptive Dynamics Orchestration for Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00085)
*Francesco Cancelliere,Aniket Datar,Giovanni Muscato,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决自动驾驶MPC中模型精度与实时性之间的 trade-off，本文提出了ADO框架，通过在线评估和动态选择模型，实现了高效且可靠的复杂地形自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主导航的MPC中，高保真模型计算成本高导致延迟，轻量级模型虽然快但易出现预测偏差，导致安全风险，需要一种机制来动态平衡两者。

Method: 提出自适应动态编排（Adaptive Dynamics Orchestration, ADO）框架：1. 维护一个包含不同精度-效率配置的模型库；2. 利用在线反事实回放（online counterfactual rollouts）评估模型的残差误差；3. 根据地形条件动态选择最合适的模型进行控制。

Result: 实验表明，ADO相比固定的低延迟基准显著降低了建模误差，同时在不增加显著计算开销的前提下，达到了接近最高保真度模型的精度。

Conclusion: ADO框架成功平衡了MPC中模型精度与计算实时性之间的矛盾，在复杂地形下的离线机器人实验中实现了更高的预测准确度与控制性能。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) for autonomous navigation faces a fundamental trade-off between model accuracy and real-time efficiency. High-fidelity dynamics models can accurately predict complex vehicle-terrain interactions during trajectory rollouts, but incur significant computational cost, increasing inference latency and reducing control frequency. Conversely, lightweight models enable fast updates and dense sampling, yet may produce erroneous predictions under safety-critical conditions, potentially leading to catastrophic failures such as vehicle rollover. To address this trade-off, we propose Adaptive Dynamics Orchestration (ADO), a framework that dynamically selects the most appropriate dynamics model for the current navigation context. ADO maintains a library of models spanning diverse accuracy-efficiency profiles and continuously refines terrain-conditioned performance estimates using residual errors from online counterfactual rollouts, where executed control actions are replayed across the model library to assess predictive discrepancy. These estimates guide model selection in real time, balancing computational efficiency and predictive accuracy. Real-world experiments on an off-road ground robot demonstrate that ADO significantly reduces modeling error compared to a fixed low-latency baseline, while approaching the accuracy of the highest-fidelity model without incurring its computational cost, resulting in more reliable and effective navigation in challenging terrain.

</details>


### [185] [Whole-Body Inverse Kinematics with Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00086)
*Helong Huang,Kai Tan,Feng Wen,Guowei Huang,Xingyue Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraphDiff-IK 是一种基于图扩散模型的通用逆运动学求解框架，通过将机器人表示为运动学图并结合层次化消息传递机制，有效解决了不同构型机器人的高精度逆运动学求解及多解生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆运动学方法难以泛化至不同机器人构型，且难以有效捕捉冗余、多分支系统的多模态解分布。

Method: 提出 GraphDiff-IK 框架：1. 将机器人建模为基于 URDF 的运动学图结构；2. 将逆运动学建模为条件图扩散过程；3. 设计层次化阶段性消息传递机制与躯干感知调节（Torso-aware conditioning）以捕捉复杂分支结构的依赖关系；4. 引入带噪正运动学反馈与任务空间监督以增强几何一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，GraphDiff-IK 在多类机器人平台上实现了高精度、稳定的逆运动学求解，并能有效生成冗余系统的多种可行解。

Conclusion: GraphDiff-IK 通过结合图神经网络与扩散模型，为解决具有不同构型（单臂、多臂、多分支）的机器人逆运动学问题提供了一种通用、准确且具备多解生成能力的建模新范式。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) is a fundamental problem in robotics, requiring the generation of joint configurations that satisfy target end-effector poses. Existing approaches often struggle to generalize across diverse robot morphologies and to effectively model the multi-modal nature of IK, particularly in articulated systems with multiple kinematic branches. In this work, we propose GraphDiff-IK, a structure-aware graph diffusion framework for inverse kinematics. Specifically, we represent the robot as a kinematic graph constructed from the robot URDF, where nodes correspond to actuated joints and edges encode kinematic dependencies. Building upon this representation, we formulate IK as a conditional graph diffusion process that directly generates joint configurations on the robot graph. To better capture structural dependencies in articulated systems, we further introduce a structure-aware graph reasoning framework with hierarchical stage-wise message passing and torso-aware conditioning for multi-branch robots. In addition, we incorporate noisy forward kinematics feedback and task-space supervision to improve geometric consistency during denoising. The proposed framework provides a unified formulation that naturally supports single-arm robots, dual-arm systems, and articulated robots with torso or waist structures. Extensive experiments on diverse robotic platforms demonstrate that the proposed method achieves accurate and stable IK performance while preserving the ability to generate multiple feasible solutions for redundant robotic systems.

</details>


### [186] [Can Predicted Dynamics Exist in the Physical World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00089)
*Barak Or*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对预测性物理AI难以保证动作可物理执行的问题，本文提出了一种物理可容许性门控评估框架，在执行前通过多维度物理约束识别并拦截非法动作，在保持任务进度的同时显著提升了系统的安全性可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测性物理AI系统（如输出动作块或潜在计划的模型）仅通过低RMSE评估精度，但这并不能保证预测结果在物理上是可执行的。因此，需要一套机制来在执行前评估策略的物理可行性。

Method: 提出了一种将物理可容许性视为“预测-控制接口”的方法，在执行前对解码后的动作候选进行评估。该评估涵盖了运动学、动力学及直接到组合（direct-to-composed）的水平条件，并通过组件级的归因提供拒绝原因。

Result: 在Hugging Face LeRobot PushT基准测试中，该全物理门控机制的AUC达到0.957。在干预实验中，该机制成功预防了87-89%的无效建议，同时保持了近0.998的平均任务进度。

Conclusion: 验证了所提出的“物理可容许性门控机制”能够有效识别不可执行的策略建议。实验表明，该机制在保持高任务成功率的同时，能显著过滤掉大部分无效的预测动作，为物理人工智能系统的安全部署提供了可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Predictive Physical AI systems output state rollouts, action chunks, and latent plans, yet a low root-mean-square error (RMSE) does not imply that a particular proposal is physically executable. We formulate physical admissibility as a prediction-control interface: before execution, a decoded proposal is treated as candidate dynamics and evaluated using kinematic, dynamic, and direct-to-composed horizon conditions. Passing is not a certificate of task success; rejection identifies violation of the specified physical envelope and gives a component-level reason. On Hugging Face LeRobot PushT, controlled falsification shows that one-step prediction-RMSE and standardized dynamics residuals reach area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) 0.982 and 0.972, kinematic-only conditions reach AUC 0.592, and the full gate reaches AUC 0.957 with condition-level attribution. In replay-based intervention experiments, residual-based filters and the full physical-admissibility gate prevent 87-$89% of invalid proposals while preserving mean progress near 0.998.

</details>


### [187] [Silent Failures in Physical AI: A Literature Review of Runtime Action Authorization for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00090)
*Barak Or*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了“物理AI”系统在将AI策略转化为实体动作时的安全空白，通过建立运行时护栏的分类学与评估标准，旨在解决黑盒模型决策失效导致的物理损害问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “物理AI”系统（如机器人、自动驾驶）虽然依赖多模态基础模型进行决策，但传统的AI内容审核和经典机器人安全机制无法应对其特有的故障模式：即模型可能在“逻辑表现良好”的情况下输出错误的物理命令，导致潜在的静默失效。现有安全机制普遍与模型研发处于割裂状态，缺乏统一的运行时安全边界。

Method: 本文采用综述分析法，整合了嵌入式基础模型、世界模型、机器人仿真、安全控制、运行时保证、不确定性估计及验证等多个技术领域的文献。通过综合分析，提出了一个有界的问题定义、静默物理动作失效的定义、运行时护栏（guardrail）功能的分类法，以及评估护栏的标准化需求。

Result: 确定了物理AI安全领域的一个核心认知缺口，并为该领域提供了系统的概念框架（分类法与评估规范），推动了黑盒物理决策模型向更安全、可保证的执行形式转型。

Conclusion: 论文指出，目前的“物理AI”（Physical AI）系统缺乏一种能连接黑盒模型与物理执行的运行时授权机制，亟需建立统一的保障框架来应对“静默物理动作失效”（silent physical-action failure）这一新型安全挑战。

Abstract: Physical AI systems increasingly map multimodal observations, language instructions, and learned world representations into physically consequential actions. Robotics foundation models, vision-language-action models, and world-model-based autonomous systems can condition decisions that move vehicles, robots, drones, and industrial machines. This transition exposes a safety problem that is not fully captured by conventional AI content moderation or by classical robot safety alone: a black-box model may issue a physically consequential action while appearing confident, plausible, and semantically aligned. The resulting failure can be silent, arising from sensor drift, occlusion, state-estimation error, distribution shift, hallucinated affordances, or invalid physical assumptions before downstream hardware controllers detect a violation.
  Across embodied foundation models, world models, robotics simulation, embodied safety benchmarks, safe control, runtime assurance, uncertainty estimation, verification, and guardrail evaluation, model capability and safety mechanisms have advanced along largely separate technical tracks. A recurring gap synthesized here is that no single stream surveyed in this review supplies a complete runtime authorization boundary between black-box Physical AI models and physical execution. The resulting analysis develops a bounded problem formulation, a definition of silent physical-action failure, a taxonomy of runtime guardrail functions, and evaluation requirements for comparing guardrails as Physical AI assurance mechanisms.

</details>


### [188] [RocketSmith: An Agentic System for High-Powered Rocket Design and Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00097)
*Peter Pak,Jesse Barkley,Rumi Loghmani,Derek Baich,Ananya Pamal,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RocketSmith 是一个用于高功率火箭开发的全流程智能体系统，通过自动化设计、增材制造与迭代优化，实现了火箭从仿真预测到物理飞行的成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率火箭的开发过程复杂，需要设计、验证与制造的高度协同；现有流程中自动化程度不足，旨在开发一套能够实现从设计到制造自动化流程的智能体系统。

Method: 构建了一个由多个子智能体和技能组成的自动化系统，利用软件工具进行飞行稳定性验证与参数化组件设计，支持零样本（zero-shot）及人机协同（human-in-the-loop）的优化工作流，并结合增材制造（FDM）技术完成硬件制造。

Result: 成功制造并试飞了四枚不同配置的火箭，全部实现平稳发射；其中两枚成功回收并可重复飞行；飞行实测远地点与仿真计算结果对比表现出84%的准确率。

Conclusion: RocketSmith 展示了智能体系统在复杂工程（如高功率火箭开发）中实现设计、制造与优化闭环的可行性，有效提升了开发效率与仿真预测的准确性。

Abstract: This work presents RocketSmith, an agentic system capable of the design, manufacturing, and optimization processes in high powered rocket development. The system enables the intelligent automation of software tools as to not only validate factors such as flight stability but also generate the parametric design components for the rocket assembly. A collection of subagents and skills enable optimization workflows of flight parameters via iteration in both zero-shot and human-in-the-loop workflows. With this system, four distinct high power rockets with various motor and assembly configurations were developed utilizing the unique design capabilities of additive manufacturing. These assembly components were fabricated using various FDM printers, manually evaluated for flight readiness, and flight tested at a launch event. From these tests, all rockets achieved a stable launched and two of the four rockets were successfully recovered in reflyable condition. Within the collected flight data, an 84% accuracy was achieved when comparing measured apogee to that calculated in flight simulations.

</details>


### [189] [PEACE: A Planner-Executor Agent with Constraint Enforcement for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00104)
*Erdem Uysal,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一项为PX4无人机设计的“规划器-执行器”代理架构，通过将高层LLM规划与底层安全控制相分离，实现了更安全、可解释且鲁棒的自主任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基础模型（Foundation Models）的自主系统存在两大弊端：一是将模型直接置于紧耦合控制回路中，导致高延迟和幻觉风险；二是将自然语言编译为端到端的黑盒策略，导致可解释性差、难以施加约束且依赖特定领域微调。

Method: 提出了一种基于“规划器-执行器”（planner-executor）的架构：1. 使用大语言模型进行单次任务规划；2. 通过结构化的ROS 2工具调用接口（连接MAVLink）驱动PX4无人机；3. 利用视觉模型和深度投影构建3D世界模型；4. 引入专门的约束强制层（如高度限制和地理围栏）及有界重规划机制。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真环境中的验证表明，相较于紧耦合的LLM控制方案，该系统提升了可解释性和约束执行能力，并减少了LLM的调用次数，证明了架构的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦高层规划与底层控制，并集成专门的约束强制层，该方案有效解决了大模型直接控制自主系统时存在的延迟高、幻觉风险大及可解释性差的问题，是实现可靠、安全无人机自主作业的有效架构。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used to drive autonomous systems, yet existing approaches either keep the model in a tight control loop, raising latency and hallucination risk, or compile natural language into opaque end-to-end policies that are hard to explain, constraint and require domain-specific datasets and fine-tuning. We propose a planner-executor agent for PX4-based drones that decouples high-level mission planning from low-level control. A large language model performs single-pass task planning, while execution is handled through a structured ROS 2 tool-calling interface bridged to MAVLink. The system constructs a world model by combining modular 2D detectors (e.g., YOLO or vision-language models) with a pinhole depth projection module for 3D object localization. A constraint enforcement layer enforces altitude limits and horizontal geofencing, and bounded replanning enables recovery from execution-time action failures. We position our approach within three common design patterns for foundation-model-based robotics systems and demonstrate its feasibility in PX4 software-in-the-loop simulations in Gazebo. Results highlight improved explainability, constraint enforcement, and reduced LLM calls compared to tightly coupled LLM control. The code, dataset, videos, and other material can be found at the following link: https://github.com/erdemuysalx/PEACE

</details>


### [190] [World Models for Robotic Manipulation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00113)
*Fangyuan Wang,Ziyuan Wang,Guorui Pei,Mengshi Zhang,Canxi Liang,Jun Hu,Zhongxuan Li,Jinsong Wu,Ning Han,Zeqing Zhang,Jiaming Qi,Hongmin Wu,Shiyao Zhang,Pai Zheng,Jia Pan,David Navarro-Alarcon,Sichao Liu,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对机器人操控中的世界模型进行了全面综述，通过定义操作性标准、建立功能分类法和分析基础设施角色，理清了该领域碎片化的现状，并指出了未来在真实世界部署和评测中的关键技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于“世界模型”的术语定义复杂且碎片化（涵盖潜空间动力学、视频生成、场景预测等），导致研究领域模糊，难以明确操纵任务中的设计关键。因此，需要系统性地梳理和分类现有研究，厘清其在机器人操控中的角色和价值。

Method: 本文通过三个核心问题（预测什么、如何连接动作、何时使用）对现有的机器人操控世界模型展开调查；通过定义操作性标准区分不同模块，将现有研究划分为五个表示家族，并建立功能分类法，分析了其在预训练、微调及推理阶段的架构角色。

Result: 提出了一种功能性分类框架，明确了世界模型在合成经验生成、候选动作过滤、搜索评估、模拟环境构建及结果验证中的基础设施作用；整理了34个操控数据集，并总结了预测保真度、任务性能及模拟器可靠性的评估协议。

Conclusion: 世界模型正从任务特定的动力学预测器演变为机器人学习的预测基础设施；未来重点在于解决接触建模、幻觉控制、动作对齐及闭环基准测试等挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation depends on the ability to anticipate how actions reshape objects, contacts, and scene geometry before execution. Learned world models provide this capability by predicting task-relevant future evolution under robot intervention, yet the term now spans latent dynamics models, action-conditioned video generators, three- and four-dimensional scene predictors, physics-informed simulators, and predictive modules inside vision-language-action systems. This breadth has fragmented the literature and obscured the design choices that matter for manipulation. We survey world models for robotic manipulation through three questions: what future representation is predicted, how prediction is connected to action, and when prediction is used in the robot-learning pipeline. We operationally define a world model as an action-conditioned predictive system and distinguish it from perception modules, inverse models, policies, rewards, and value functions. We then organize existing work into five representation families, develop a functional taxonomy that separates integrated prediction-action models from explicit predictive planners, and characterize infrastructure roles including synthetic experience generation, candidate filtering, search-based evaluation, learned environments, and outcome verification. We further map these roles across pretraining, post-training, and inference adaptation, review 34 manipulation datasets, and synthesize evaluation protocols for predictive fidelity, task performance, and simulator reliability. This survey shows that world models are evolving from task-specific dynamics predictors into predictive infrastructure for robot learning, while exposing open challenges in contact modeling, hallucination control, action alignment, and benchmarking under closed-loop use.

</details>


### [191] [Ontology-Guided Reasoning for Affordance-Based Explanations of Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00117)
*Amar Halilovic,Vahidin Hasic,Senka Krivic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体论的推理方法，使机器人能够通过分析周围物体的“能力（Affordances）”来解释导航受阻的原因，生成的解释不仅具备语义深度，而且能够转化为具体的行动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂人机环境中，机器人仅仅识别出路障是不够的；它需要理解环境中物体的“潜在功能（Affordance）”以及如何通过状态改变来消除障碍，从而为导航决策提供可解释的理由。

Method: 提出了“局部能力本体（Local Affordance Ontology）”，通过建模周围实体、能力、能力状态及定性空间关系，并评估假设性的对象-能力状态变化，作为解释导航受阻原因的因素。

Result: 在图书馆机器人基准测试中，该方法识别相关解释因素的准确度优于仅基于语义的基准方法，并且在环境语义噪声增加的情况下仍表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本体论引导的推理能够为机器人导航提供具备语义基础且可执行的解释，证明了能力本体不仅是描述性模型，更是实现机器人可解释性和可靠自主性的核心推理基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes ontology-guided reasoning for affordance-based explanations of robot navigation. In human environments, it is not sufficient for a robot to detect that its route is blocked. It must also reason about what nearby objects afford, which state changes are possible, and which of these changes would allow it to continue safely. We address this problem by representing nearby entities, their affordances, affordance states, and qualitative spatial relations in a local affordance ontology and by evaluating hypothetical object--affordance state changes as candidate explanation factors. This yields explanations that are not only semantically grounded but also actionable. We instantiate the approach in a lightweight benchmark centered on a robot librarian scenario and evaluate it on procedurally generated navigation cases. The results show that ontology-guided reasoning identifies relevant explanation factors more accurately than a semantic-only baseline and remains robust as semantic clutter increases. Overall, the paper argues that affordance ontologies can serve not merely as semantic descriptions of the environment, but as reasoning foundations for explainability and reliable robot autonomy.

</details>


### [192] [V2I Work Zone Geometry Reconstruction with Pose-Conditioned UWB Range Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00119)
*Jiaxi Liu,Hangyu Li,Yang Cheng,Rui Gana,Junwei You,Weizhe Tang,Peng Zhang,Steven T. Parker,Xiaopeng Li,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型UWB去噪模型，通过整合车辆位姿信息与置换等变网络结构，有效提升了自动驾驶汽车在施工区域通过V2I进行几何映射的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决自动驾驶车辆在施工区导航时，受脉冲噪声、非视距误差、锚点乱序及位姿不确定性影响而导致的UWB测距精度下降问题。

Method: 提出了一种位姿条件下的置换等变预测去噪器（Pose-conditioned, permutation-equivariant predictive denoiser），包含共享锚点时间预测、对称集合聚合及位姿条件残差解码，并配合两阶段训练策略（预测学习+NLOS加权微调）。

Result: 在真实V2I UWB实地数据及仿真基准测试中，验证了模型对锚点重排序和丢失的鲁棒性，并大幅降低了测距误差。

Conclusion: 所提模型在非视距（NLOS）场景下显著提高了测距精度、锥桶定位及施工区几何重构能力，并具有良好的鲁棒性，测量加权均方误差（MSE）较原始输入降低了66.9%。

Abstract: Reliable work zone mapping is important for connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) to navigate safely and smoothly through work zone areas. Cone-mounted ultra-wideband (UWB) roadside units (RSU) offer a cost-effective way for work zone layout inference, as roadside anchors and vehicle tags provide direct vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) range constraints for work zone geometry reconstruction. However, UWB range estimation is degraded by bursty outliers, non-line-of-sight (NLOS) errors, arbitrary anchor-ordering issues, and vehicle pose uncertainties in practical field deployments.
