To address these challenges, this study proposes a pose-conditioned, permutation-equivariant predictive denoiser for multi-anchor UWB ranging. The model employs shared anchor-wise temporal prediction to capture range dynamics, symmetric set aggregation to handle unordered and missing anchors, and pose-conditioned residual decoding to incorporate vehicle motion as a geometric prior. A two-stage training strategy first learns prediction from observed ranges, and then fine-tunes the denoiser with NLOS-weighted supervision. The method is evaluated on rare real-world V2I UWB field data collected with a CAV, as well as on controlled large-scale simulation benchmarks for ablative insights. Results show that the proposed method substantially improves range accuracy, cone localization, and work zone geometry reconstruction in challenging NLOS-dominated regimes, remains robust to anchor re-indexing and moderate anchor dropout, and reduces measurement-weighted field MSE by 66.9% relative to the raw input.

</details>


### [193] [Completion at the Boundary (CaB): Deployable Switching with Completion-Aware Control under Limited Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00145)
*Yusuke Sano,Takeshi Itoga*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA智能体在复合任务执行中因无法准确判定指令完成点而导致任务失败的问题，本文提出了CaB方法。通过引入边界阶段标记来捕捉局部完成证据，实现了更稳定、可审计的任务切换判断与动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）智能体缺乏有效的边界判断能力，在执行复合指令（如“做A，然后做B”）时，判断指令何时完成对于后续动作的无缝切换至关重要，错误的判断会导致级联失效。

Method: 提出“边界完成”（CaB）框架；使用边界阶段标记（Boundary-Phase Tokens: Before/Hit/After）作为中间表征；包含CaB-When（处理任务切换决策）与CaB-How（利用该表征稳定边界处的动作生成）两个模块；遵循低校准（low-calibration）约束，即仅在开发集上全局校准，测试时不进行重训练。

Result: 在Minecraft第一人称VLA基准测试中，相较于基准模型，CaB在相同计算约束和部署限制下，展现出了更好的复合任务执行效果和更高的切换质量。

Conclusion: CaB通过引入边界阶段标记（Before/Hit/After）有效解决了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）代理在执行复合指令时难以判断何时完成的问题，能显著提升任务流程的切换质量与执行稳定性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) agents can execute natural-language instructions, yet deployed systems still lack an operational interface: deciding when the instruction is complete. This gap is acute in short composites ("do A, then B"), where mistimed handoffs cascade into downstream failures. Completion is inherently closed-loop because switching is an intervention that changes the instruction context and thus future actions and observations. We study completion under a deployable low-calibration regime motivated by open-ended instruction spaces, enforcing no test-time relearning and a single globally calibrated switching rule selected once on development set and reused unchanged on test set. Under this constraint, collapsing asymmetric boundary evidence into a single scalar can be brittle under polarity shifts across tasks. We propose Completion at the Boundary (CaB), which predicts an event-local completion object in the form of Boundary-Phase Tokens (Before/Hit/After), retaining two-sided boundary evidence under this discipline. CaB-When converts this completion object into a minimal, auditable switching decision (when), while CaB-How reuses the same completion object to condition action generation for boundary-stable control through handoffs (how). Using an intervention-aware E1/E2 protocol, we show that CaB improves composite execution and handoff quality on a first-person Minecraft VLA benchmark under matched capacity and deployability constraints.

</details>


### [194] [Modeling Robotics Dataset Construction as an Artifact-Based Build Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00162)
*Leon Pohl,Lukas Beer,George Sebastian,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bagzel通过将ROS数据集构建过程建模为基于Bazel的依赖图构建任务，实现了高效、确定性的增量数据集生成，显著降低了机器人机器学习工作流的开发迭代延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将ROS bag转换为机器学习数据集的方法多依赖临时的、顺序执行的脚本，存在工程开销大、迭代周期慢等问题，缺乏高效的重现性和增量更新能力。

Method: 提出并实现了Bagzel，这是一种基于Bazel的开源扩展。它将数据集构建过程抽象为基于依赖图的工件构建过程，并引入了“Bagzel-xattr”来进行服务端摘要管理（digest management）以优化增量处理。

Result: Bagzel在所有评估模式下均优于传统的顺序处理流程。在20.4 GB数据集的测试中，热构建（warm builds）速度提升高达386.26倍，增量构建提升7.21倍。Bagzel-xattr进一步优化了性能，相比Bagzel平均运行时减少了5.9%。相较于基准方法，Bagzel在多种数据集规模下表现出更好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 将机器人数据集构建建模为基于工件的构建过程，能够显著降低数据集更新的延迟，同时通过确定性的构建设计确保数据处理的可重复性。

Abstract: Robotic systems generate large volumes of multimodal sensor data, but converting ROS bag recordings into machine learning datasets is often handled by ad hoc sequential scripts, creating engineering overhead and slow iteration cycles. We model dataset construction as an artifact-based build process over a dependency graph and implement this approach in Bagzel, an open-source Bazel extension for reproducible, incremental dataset generation (including nuScenes-format export). We compare Bagzel and Bagzel-xattr (server-side digest management) against a sequential rosbag2nuscenes baseline. Bagzel reduces runtime in all evaluated execution modes, with the largest gains in iterative workflows (up to 386.26x in warm builds and 7.21x in incremental builds on a 20.4 GB dataset). Across dataset sizes from 5.1 to 20.4 GB, Bagzel variants show markedly better scaling behavior than the baseline, especially in warm and incremental modes. Bagzel-xattr provides additional gains, with a mean runtime reduction of 5.9% compared to Bagzel in the input granularity study. Overall, modeling robotics dataset construction as an artifact-based build process substantially reduces dataset update latency while maintaining a deterministic build design that supports reproducibility. Bagzel is publicly available at https://github.com/UniBwTAS/bagzel.

</details>


### [195] [Safe2Drive: Evaluating Safe Driving Behaviors of E2E Autonomous Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00191)
*Nishad Sahu,Kalpana Panda,Congyuan Yu,Changzhong Qian,Shounak Sural,Ragunathan Rajkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Safe2Drive的自动驾驶安全评估基准，揭示了当前主流端到端自动驾驶模型在应对现实复杂危险场景时存在严重的安全性缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端自动驾驶策略在闭环模拟中的高评分掩盖了其处理关键安全场景能力的不足。

Method: 构建了Safe2Drive (S2D) 测试集，包含100种针对施工区、行人横穿、遮挡行人等高频危险场景；提出了安全核心指标 SafeDriving Score (SDS)，涵盖预防性刹车、施工区碰撞、车道保持及平稳度检查。

Result: 主流模型（LEAD和SimLingo）在 S2D 上的驾驶评分（DS）相比 Bench2Drive 基准大幅下降，且计算出的 SDS 分数极低，表现出严重的施工区理解不足、违反交通信号灯及刹车迟钝等问题。

Conclusion: 端到端自动驾驶模型在处理关键安全场景时表现脆弱，缺乏真正的安全行为推理能力，即使是在训练集中的场景下也表现不佳。

Abstract: Recent end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving policies achieve high driving scores in closed-loop simulations. Yet it remains unclear whether these policies handle common safety-critical scenarios. We present Safe2Drive (S2D), a set of Bench2Drive-aligned scenario extensions focused on three frequent families of road hazards: work zones, pedestrian jaywalking, and occluded vulnerable road users (VRUs). Safe2Drive adds 100 common but challenging scenarios and introduces SafeDriving Score (SDS), a safety-centric metric that augments prior evaluators with pre-crash braking, work zone-object contact, lane centering, and smoothness checks. Evaluating two state-of-the-art policies (LEAD and SimLingo) on S2D, we find that their driving scores drop sharply relative to their reported Bench2Drive baselines (LEAD: from 94.70 DS on Bench2Drive to 39.95 DS on S2D; SimLingo: from 85.07 DS on Bench2Drive to 41.00 DS on S2D) and that SDS on S2D is low (11.85 for LEAD and 15.27 for Sim-Lingo). These results are consistent with brittle safe-driving behaviors such as poor work-zone understanding, red-light violations, and late or absent braking for pedestrians. This study highlights a lack of safe behavioral reasoning in E2E models even when tested on CARLA towns that are part of the training set. We plan to release the code and videos for all 100 S2D scenarios.

</details>


### [196] [Cuttlebot: a platform demonstration for complex, autonomous, bio-inspired swimmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00197)
*Alexander Nicholas White,Ang Leo Li,Alexander Yin,Derrick Roseman,Valeria Saro-Cortes,Hannah Wiswell,Aimy Wissa,Mihai Duduta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CORE的软体机器人控制平台，并利用该平台开发出能够进行三维仿生游动及精细抓取的“乌贼机器人”Cuttlebot，验证了介电弹性体人工肌肉在深海软体机器人中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深海作业对具有环境耐受性且生态友好的软体机器人需求日益增长，而介电弹性体致动器（DEA）虽具备良好特性但难以在机器人系统中集成，缺乏成熟的控制平台。

Method: 1. 设计了CORE自主控制平台，能够驱动多达六个电致动器并处理视觉/空间传感数据；2. 构建了受乌贼启发的Cuttlebot原型，集成四个人工肌肉鳍片及一个软体抓手；3. 通过系留与非系留实验，对机器人的机动性、运动能力及六轴力/力矩控制输出进行了评估。

Result: Cuttlebot表现出良好的三维游动性能，最高平移速度达2.5 cm/s，旋转速度达10°/s；CORE系统成功实现了对人工肌肉的复杂控制，能够精确输出六轴向力与力矩。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用介电弹性体驱动器（DEA）开发自主、软体水下机器人的可行性，为海洋探索和监测领域提供了新的仿生机器人范式。

Abstract: Increasing interest in deep-sea operations and resources motivates the development of ecologically sensitive but environmentally durable robots. Dielectric elastomer actuator artificial muscles are good candidates for powering such systems due to their pressure and temperature tolerance and soft makeup, but they are difficult to integrate with robotic systems. This work presents an autonomous robotic platform: the CORE, capable of driving six artificial muscles while sensing visual and spatial information. To validate the platform, we developed the Cuttlebot - a cuttlefish-inspired robot that swims in three dimensions using undulatory fin locomotion. The Cuttlebot has four primary artificial muscles in its fins in addition to a tentacle-inspired soft gripper. The robot was evaluated in a series of tethered and untethered swimming tests, demonstrating a top speed of 2.5 centimeters per second translation and 10 degrees per second rotation. Furthermore, the CORE system was capable of driving specialized control signals into the artificial muscles to controllably output force and torque in six axes. This work provides a platform for developing complex, bio-inspired swimming robots for ocean exploration and monitoring, laying the foundation with our leading example: the Cuttlebot.

</details>


### [197] [Series-Parallel Integrated Nonlinear Elastic Actuator applied to the lean motion of a bicycle simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00201)
*Christina Kohler,Michiel Plooij,Nuria Peña-Perez,Arend L. Schwab,Heike Vallery*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对高转矩、高保真力反馈需求，本文提出 SPINEA 执行器，通过独特的几何设计让单个弹性元件兼具串联（SEA）和并联（PEA）功效，实现了更紧凑、高性能的执行器方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEA 和 PEA 各有优劣，现有结合方案通常需要复杂的结构（如独立元件或离合器），无法兼顾紧凑性、高转矩输出和高力控精度，为此需要一种更集成化的驱动设计。

Method: 提出 SPINEA 设计，通过非线性传动机构实现电机与负载轴轴错位，并利用单一弹性元件同时承担串联（实现力反馈控制）和并联（辅助输出峰值转矩）功能。将其应用于触觉自行车模拟器的倾斜驱动系统进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，SPINEA 在自行车模拟器中实现了低阻抗及精确的转矩跟踪能力，固定框架下跟踪带宽达 4.25 Hz，实际骑行下达 4 Hz。

Conclusion: SPINEA 成功实现了在单弹性元件下同时具备 SEA 和 PEA 的优势，为需要高转矩密度和高保真力反馈的紧凑型执行器设计提供了一种创新方案。

Abstract: Designing robots for high-torque, high-fidelity haptic interaction is challenging. Parallel Elastic Actuators (PEAs) use elastic elements in parallel to smaller motors to complement torques, and Series Elastic Actuators (SEAs) use elastic elements in series to decouple motor impedance and improve force control. Recent work combines SEAs and PEAs to obtain both benefits but requires separate elastic elements or clutching. This paper presents the Series Parallel Integrated Nonlinear Elastic Actuator (SPINEA), which merges SEA and PEA such that a single elastic element takes on dual roles simultaneously, parallel and series. This is achieved by a nonlinear transmission in which the motor and load have misaligned rotation axes and are elastically connected. This geometry enables both high peak torque and precise torque tracking. We apply SPINEA to actuate lean of a haptic bicycle simulator, which requires high moments and precise rendering for safe and realistic rider interactions. We realized a prototype and performed experiments, both with an external excitation setup and with riders cycling. Our results confirm SPINEA's low impedance and precise torque tracking, up to 4.25 Hz with the bicycle frame fixed and up to 4 Hz with riders. The benefits may transfer to other applications requiring compact, high-performance actuation.

</details>


### [198] [Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00229)
*Yueh-Hua Wu,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为“连续推理”的 VLA 模型框架，通过使用共享的 Gaussian 隐变量作为内部语言，而非传统的文本 token，实现了更细粒度、可验证的机器人动作控制，并在多项任务中显著优于现有水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本推理在时间粒度上与细粒度的连续控制存在错位；单纯的语言模型推理无法很好地耦合细微的机器人动作，且易形成模型私有的“快捷方式”，缺乏泛化性。

Method: 提出“连续推理（Continuous Reasoning）”架构：首先预测结构化的连续思维（continuous thoughts）作为共享上下文，随后通过带有“自我验证目标（self-verification objective）”的机制进行训练，即利用 EMA 教师模型来验证学生模型输出的推理对动作序列预测的有效性。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 和真实机器人实验中表现出色，在 TX-G2 和 HSR 机器人上的平均子任务成功率分别较 π0.5 提升了 40.4% 和 26.3%。

Conclusion: VLA 模型中的推理不应仅局限于文本 token，更有效的途径是建立一种“可共享、可验证”的连续内部语言（Continuous Reasoning），这能显著提升复杂任务中的泛化能力与控制精度。

Abstract: Natural language is a powerful reasoning medium for language and vision-language models, but it is mismatched to the granularity of continuous control. Text and explicit subgoals operate at task-level granularity, whereas vision-language-action (VLA) policies must choose actions at a much finer temporal scale; a single reasoning step can therefore span many action chunks while remaining only weakly coupled to the action needed now. This suggests a different question for VLA: what should play the role of language? We argue that a useful VLA reasoning medium must be shareable across model instances, verifiable through downstream action improvement, and aligned with temporally extended control structure.
  Based on this view, we propose Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action. Our model first predicts continuous reasoning in the form of a structured set of continuous thoughts, then reuses them as shared context for chunk-structured action generation. Better action prediction alone does not certify good reasoning: if the same internal medium cannot be shared across model instances and independently verified through improved downstream control, the added latent may simply become a model-private shortcut that helps on seen behaviors without supporting generalizable control. We therefore instantiate continuous reasoning as a shared Gaussian latent interface and train it with a self-verification objective in which an exponential-moving-average teacher must successfully consume the student's reasoning when predicting target actions.
  Empirically, Continuous Reasoning improves LIBERO-PRO robustness and performs strongly on real robots, raising mean subtask success over π0.5 by 40.4% on TX-G2, an AgiBot G2-compatible variant, and 26.3% on HSR. This suggests that reasoning in VLA is less about extra tokens than about a shareable, verifiable internal language for action.

</details>


### [199] [HOIST: Humanoid Optimization with Imitation and Sample-efficient Tuning for Manipulating Suspended Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00252)
*Songyang Liu,Shunyu Yao,Dingyuan Huang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOIST 是一种结合了 VLA 策略微调与迭代强化学习的新型框架，有效解决了人形机器人操纵欠驱动吊装载荷时的精度与稳定性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人操纵吊装负载面临欠驱动和振荡的挑战；传统的模仿学习难以优化精度，而直接进行从零开始的强化学习则存在安全性差和样本效率低的问题。

Method: 提出 HOIST 框架：1. 使用 VR 示教数据微调视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略；2. 结合全身控制器（WBC）执行动作；3. 通过迭代批量强化学习（Iterative Batched RL）优化放置精度与停止行为。

Result: 在仿真和实机实验中，相比仅使用 VLA 策略，HOIST 将平移放置误差降低了 19.9 cm，角度误差降低了 3.56 度，表现出更优的控制稳定性与任务完成度。

Conclusion: HOIST 框架通过结合模仿学习与迭代强化学习，成功实现了人形机器人对欠驱动吊装负载的精确操纵，显著优于仅使用模仿学习的基准方法，证明了人形机器人在工业物料搬运任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Manipulating suspended payloads with humanoid robots is challenging because the robot can only influence an underactuated, oscillatory load through whole-body motion and intermittent contact. Imitation learning provides safe initial behavior but does not directly optimize final placement, while reinforcement learning from scratch is unsafe and sample-inefficient on real humanoids. We present HOIST-Humanoid Optimized with Imitation and Sample-efficient Tuning for manipulating suspended loads. HOIST first finetunes a high-level vision-language-action (VLA) policy from virtual-reality (VR) teleoperation demonstrations and executes its commands through a whole-body controller. It then uses VLA rollouts and iterative batched RL to improve placement accuracy and stopping behavior. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid show that HOIST improves over imitation-only and additional-demonstration baselines; compared with pure VLA rollouts, HOIST reduces translational placement error by 19.9 cm and raw angular error by 3.56 degrees, demonstrating the potential of humanoids for underactuated material-handling tasks.

</details>


### [200] [Per-Group Error, Not Total MSE: Fine-Tuning Vision-Language-Action Models for 11-DoF Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00253)
*Pau Montagut Bofi,Mario García Blasco,Tessa Pulli,Markus Vincze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VLA模型在异构动作机器人上微调时，全局MSE作为评估指标存在误导性。通过将动作空间拆分为不同组别（如底座、手臂）进行误差分析，能更准确地选择在真实机器人表现最佳的检查点，避免简单关节掩盖复杂任务的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对移动操纵（mobile manipulation）任务，发现微调VLA模型时，总MSE最低的检查点往往在实际机器人上的表现并非最优，这是由于异构关节空间中简单关节的低误差掩盖了关键关节的高失败率造成的。

Method: 研究对比了SmolVLA（450M）和π0.5（3.3B）模型在11自由度Toyota HSR机器人上的微调表现；提出了按关节组（手臂、夹爪、头部、移动底座）拆解MSE误差的分析方法，而非依赖全局汇总MSE，并在60次真机实验中进行验证。

Result: 实验显示，尽管“仅专家头微调”策略的全局MSE最低，但在实际任务执行中表现最差。相反，使用按组别分析（特别是手臂组误差）能更有效地选择表现最优的检查点。实验证明较大的预训练基线（π0.5）优于特定微调模型。

Conclusion: 在具有异构动作空间的机器人上，使用“按组（per-group）”计算的误差指标作为检查点选择的依据，比传统的全局汇总MSE更为可靠且具备更好的性能预测能力。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for mobile manipulators with heterogeneous joint spaces can produce a counterintuitive result: the checkpoint with the lowest aggregate MSE is not the one that performs best on the real robot. We argue this is a predictable consequence of collapsing heterogeneous joint groups (arm, gripper, head, wheeled base) into a single metric, where easy-to-predict joints can mask joints that still fail.
  We fine-tune SmolVLA (450M, action-expert only) on the 11-DoF Toyota HSR and compare it against $π_{0.5}$ (3.3B), a stronger pretrained baseline. Per-group analysis exposes two patterns: in SmolVLA, the mobile base converges slowest and limits overall performance. In expert-only fine-tuning of $π_{0.5}$ (training only the action head, backbone frozen), total MSE drops below the baseline but arm accuracy degrades. On 60 real-robot trials (20 per model), $π_{0.5}$ 80k (4.0/4) significantly outperforms both fine-tuned variants (expert-only 3k: 3.75/4; HSR-SmolVLA: 3.5/4; Mann-Whitney $p \leq 0.010$), despite expert-only 3k having the lowest total MSE. This separation is most consistent with the offline arm-group error, not total MSE or base-group error. We conclude that per-group error is a more reliable signal than total MSE for checkpoint selection on robots with heterogeneous action spaces. Code: https://github.com/paumontagut/per-group-mse-vla

</details>


### [201] [ScaRF-SLAM: Scale-Consistent Reconstruction with Feed-Forward Models and Classical Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00307)
*Yuhao Zhang,Yifu Tao,Frank Dellaert,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种解耦SLAM框架，利用经典特征法处理位姿跟踪（保证稳健性），利用几何基础模型（GFMs）进行高质量建图，有效规避了模型预测误差对位姿的影响，提升了多尺度环境下的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将SLAM与几何基础模型（GFMs）融合的方法通常直接使用GFM预测进行位姿跟踪，但GFM输出的几何不确定性和精度偏差会直接恶化位姿估计，导致后端重建不一致。

Method: 提出了一种解耦SLAM系统：1. 使用经典特征点法SLAM负责鲁棒的低延迟跟踪；2. 仅将几何基础模型（GFMs）用于建图；3. 通过将建图锚定在SLAM位姿上并进行跨尺度深度优化，避免GFM预测误差传递；4. 通过关键帧子图构建、尺度一致性优化及基于投影的点云融合技术，实现一致性高的稠密重建。

Result: 该方法在自建的室内数据集及大型室外数据集上进行了验证，轨迹精度优于现有方法，重建精度提升了10%-20%；在室内场景达到每10米2厘米误差，室外大规模场景达到每30米10厘米误差。

Conclusion: 提出了一种解耦框架，通过结合经典特征点法SLAM（用于稳健跟踪）与几何基础模型（用于高质量建图），成功解决了直接利用GFM进行位姿估计带来的不确定性和准确性问题，在室内外大规模场景下实现了高精度的轨迹估计与稠密重建。

Abstract: Recent works have explored unifying SLAM with geometric foundation models (GFMs). However, directly using GFM predictions for tracking is highly sensitive to model capability and uncertainty, as geometric inaccuracies in the predictions can adversely affect pose estimation. To address this limitation, we present a decoupled framework that integrates classical feature-based SLAM with GFMs, which achieves higher quality and more consistent dense reconstruction. In brief, we use classical visual SLAM for robust low-latency tracking and use GFMs exclusively for mapping. By anchoring mapping to poses produced by the SLAM module and optimizing across depth scales, the proposed design avoids propagating inaccuracies from GFM predictions into pose estimation while imposing geometric constraints on the reconstruction. The system builds submaps from multiple posed keyframes and enforces scale consistency via lightweight frame and submap scale optimization. It also performs projection-based point cloud fusion within each submap, and updates submaps online to reflect trajectory updates from the feature-based SLAM. To evaluate tracking and reconstruction of our method, we introduce a loop-rich, building-scale indoor dataset with accurate sensor trajectories and LiDAR ground-truth. Experiments show that our approach achieves superior trajectory accuracy while improving reconstruction precision by 10%-20% over existing methods, with about 2 cm reconstruction error per 10 m chunk on building-scale dataset. On large-scale outdoor datasets, it attains 10 cm error per 30 m chunk (w.r.t LiDAR ground-truth models).

</details>


### [202] [DRL-Based Pose Control for Double-Ackermann Robots Under Actuation Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00313)
*Oussama Zaim,Mélodie Daniel,Aly Magassouba,Miguel Aranda,Olivier Ly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种 sim-to-sim-to-real 的训练方案，通过在训练中建模真实的执行器偏差，显著提升了双阿克曼转向机器人在不同环境及真实硬件上的策略迁移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双阿克曼（double-Ackermann）转向机器人在从仿真部署到现实世界时，由于其非完整约束特性及执行器动力学差异，导致训练好的深度强化学习（DRL）策略性能严重下降。

Method: 1. 基于 ManeuverNet 框架，将任务从位置控制扩展至全姿态控制； 
2. 引入 sim-to-sim-to-real 方法，通过在训练环境（PyBullet）中显式引入更复杂的执行器不确定性（源自 Gazebo 观测）；
3. 使用多环境 DRL 配置（结合 SAC 和 CrossQ 算法）进行稳健策略训练。

Result: 在 Gazebo 仿真中的成功率从 25% 提升至 92%（严格门槛下保持在 69%），并且模型成功部署在真实机器人上，无需进一步调优。

Conclusion: 通过采用 sim-to-sim-to-real 的训练策略（将高保真模拟器的动力学特性反馈至训练环境），可以有效解决双阿克曼转向机器人在仿真与现实不匹配（sim-to-real gap）下的鲁棒性问题，并在无需额外调优的情况下成功迁移至现实环境。

Abstract: Robust deployment of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) policies on real robots remains challenging due to discrepancies between simulation and real-world dynamics. We address this issue in the context of maneuvering with double-Ackermann-steering mobile robots, which introduce additional constraints due to their non-holonomic nature. Building upon the DRL framework ManeuverNet, we extend its objective from position control to full pose control, resulting in a more challenging task. We further investigate the impact of actuation-related uncertainties on policy transfer. The use of simplified actuation models during training of the extended policy can lead to poor generalization, shown by a success rate drop from 100% in PyBullet to 25% in Gazebo under stricter evaluation conditions. To address this limitation, we adopt a sim-to-sim-to-real approach, where actuation effects observed in Gazebo are incorporated into the PyBullet training environment. Using multi-environment DRL with SAC and CrossQ, we learn policies that remain robust despite modeling inaccuracies. This approach can significantly reduce the performance gap across simulators, achieving up to 92% success rate in Gazebo and maintaining 69% under stricter thresholds, with successful transfer to a real robot without additional tuning.

</details>


### [203] [Belief Consistency Between Foundation-Model Evidence and Geometric Perception in Persistent Robotic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00318)
*Christoffer Heckman,Harel Biggie,Brendan Crowe,Nicholas Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人映射中几何感知与基础模型语义冲突的问题，论文提出了一种校准提交与冲突过滤机制，大幅提升了语义地图的精度与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人持久映射系统在融合几何感知（高可靠性）与基础模型语义（低可靠性，缺乏校准）时，缺乏处理两者矛盾及评估模型可靠性的机制，导致映射结果不准确。

Method: 提出了一个包含两个核心协同机制的更新算子：1. 基于类别的校准提交门（per-class calibrated commit gate），用于评估语义声明的可靠性；2. 基于事件的冲突丢弃窗口（per-event conflict-drop window），用于实时过滤与几何感知相矛盾的语义声明。

Result: 在KITTI-360和ScanNet数据集上的实验表明，该算子显著提升了地图提交的准确性（如汽车类别精度从43.9%提升至99.7%，平均IoU从0.180提升至0.522），并在保持高精度的同时保留了比单体模型提示更丰富的正确语义信息，且具备跨模型和语义器的通用性。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的更新算子通过校准机制和冲突检测机制，成功解决了自主移动机器人持久地图中几何感知与基础模型语义声明之间的不一致问题，验证了在保留高准确率的同时实现稳健语义映射的可行性。

Abstract: Persistent maps used by autonomous robots increasingly fuse a geometric perception stack whose assertions are well-characterized with a foundation-model channel that produces semantic claims without calibrated reliability about the same scene. Contemporary mapping systems integrate the two channels by treating the foundation-model channel as an additional voter into a per-element posterior, uncalibrated for its own per-class reliability and without machinery to flag when the two channels contradict each other at a given moment. We propose an update operator with two cooperating mechanisms: a per-class calibrated commit gate, and a per-event conflict-drop window that refuses to commit foundation-model claims contradicted by the geometric channel at the moment of the claim. We evaluate on KITTI-360 and ScanNet, with an oracle geometric channel (panoptic ground truth) and an off-the-shelf online semantic segmenter (Mask2Former) to demonstrate real-world performance. The operator produces substantially more accurate committed maps (KITTI is car commit precision 99.7% vs. 43.9% for the calibration-only operator; mean per-class IoU 0.522 vs. 0.180), retains more compositional true positives at higher precision than a monolithic compositional VLM prompt. The framework operates at deployment quality across both oracle and off-the-shelf-segmenter geometric channels, and is invariant under foundation-model substitution.

</details>


### [204] [FAIR^2 Drones: An AI-Ready Standard for Cross-Domain Wildlife Drone Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00355)
*Jenna Kline,Kilian Meier,Vandita Shukla,Edouard G. A. Rolland,Elena Iannino,Lucie Laporte-Devylder,Constanza Andrea Molina Catricheo,Blair Costelloe,Elizabeth Campolongo,Henrik S. Midtiby,Devis Tuia,Benjamin Risse,Ulrik P. S. Lundquist,Anders Lyhne Christensen,Fabio Remondino,Thomas Richardson,Tanya Berger-Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAIR^2 Drones 是一个连接生态学、机器人学和计算机视觉领域的新型无人机数据集标准，通过标准化元数据与标注，打破学科壁垒，提升了昂贵科研数据的复用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机生态数据采集成本高昂且数据孤岛现象严重，现有数据集往往仅服务单一领域，缺乏跨学科再利用的标准与规范。

Method: 提出 FAIR^2 Drones 数据集标准，结合现有的 FAIR 及 AI-ready 数据框架，引入特定的平台元数据与标注规范，并提供配套的开源验证工具、参考实现及多模态数据扩展方案（整合相机捕捉、GPS 和声学传感器）。

Result: 创建了一个能够同时支持生态分析、机器人算法开发和计算机视觉基准测试的统一数据标准，提升了环境监测领域科研投入的产出效率。

Conclusion: FAIR^2 Drones 标准化框架通过统一跨学科数据规范，最大化了无人机生态监测数据的科学价值与利用率，促进了生态学、机器人技术与计算机视觉领域的协作。

Abstract: Animal ecology data collection using drones represents a substantial investment of time, expertise, and financial resources. Yet most existing datasets serve only a single research community, limiting interdisciplinary reuse. We propose a unified drone dataset standard, FAIR^2 Drones, that bridges ecology, robotics, and computer vision by building on existing FAIR and AI-ready data frameworks while adding essential platform metadata and annotation specifications. Our standard enables datasets to simultaneously support ecological analysis, robotics algorithm development, and computer vision benchmarking. We provide open-source validation tools, reference implementations, and multimodal extensions linking drone imagery with complementary sensors such as camera traps, GPS, and acoustics. By standardizing metadata across disciplines, this framework maximizes the scientific return on investment for costly field deployments and accelerates cross-domain collaboration in environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [205] [Constrained Whole-Body Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00374)
*Daniel Morton,Pranit Mohnot,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConstrainedMimic 是一种新型控制框架，通过引入控制障碍函数（CBF）与操作空间控制，使强化学习驱动的仿人机器人能在不牺牲敏捷性的前提下，实现训练后实时约束的满足与安全避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习（RL）在仿人机器人敏捷运动方面取得了进展，但如何确保系统安全性并满足（特别是训练后指定的）动态约束仍是一个巨大挑战。

Method: 提出了 ConstrainedMimic 框架，结合了全身运动学与动力学，并集成了操作空间控制（Operational Space Control）与控制障碍函数（CBFs）。该方法通过在 RL 跟踪策略中添加约束层，实现对运动参考和动力学的实时修正。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 仿真实验中，该方法成功实现了碰撞避免（包括自身与环境）、关节限制以及质心稳定性维护。该方法在保证约束的同时，最大限度地保留了 RL 策略的原始性能，且框架在 CPU/GPU/TPU 上均可运行并达到 300-500 Hz 的刷新频率。

Conclusion: ConstrainedMimic 实现了在实时跟踪策略中对约束的有效执行，不仅保证了安全性及约束满足，同时保持了仿人机器人的运动敏捷性和任务执行能力。其高效性（300-500 Hz）和跨硬件兼容性使其非常适合实际部署。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning (RL) have demonstrated impressive whole-body agility for humanoid robots, yet ensuring safety and satisfying constraints -- particularly those specified after training -- remains a challenge. Towards this goal, we present ConstrainedMimic, a control framework that leverages whole-body kinematics and dynamics for real-time constraint enforcement within RL tracking policies. By integrating principles from operational space control and control barrier functions (CBFs), we enable the satisfaction of arbitrary runtime constraints on both the kinematic reference motion and the underlying dynamics. In whole-body motion-tracking and teleoperation experiments on a (simulated) Unitree G1 with a learned policy, we demonstrate collision avoidance (both with the robot body and external obstacles), joint limits, and center of mass stability constraints. By remaining consistent with the current contact mode and tracking objectives, we minimally restrict the capabilities of the policy when constraints are active. Our method is fully differentiable, runs on CPU, GPU, and TPU, and can be deployed at up to 300-500 Hz. All software will be freely available upon publication.

</details>


### [206] [Behavior Cloning of MPC for 3-DOF Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00383)
*Theo Guegan,Dexter Wen Jie Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过行为克隆近似MPC策略以用于3-DOF机器人操纵器的实时控制，在维持较高任务成功率的同时显著降低了计算延迟，但指出在满足高精度控制方面仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型预测控制（MPC）虽然稳定性与鲁棒性强，但计算开销巨大，限制了在实时控制系统中的应用。因此，需要研究如何利用行为克隆技术通过近似MPC策略来降低计算负担。

Method: 将行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）应用于近似MPC策略；构建了融合逆运动学（IK）与MPC的基准控制器；对比评估了经典回归算法、深度多层感知机（MLP）及循环神经网络（RNN）等多种架构，并通过在线和离线实验进行评估。

Result: 推理延迟降低了3倍，在宽松公差条件下任务成功率达到84.98%；实验显示静态架构性能优于时序模型架构。

Conclusion: 行为克隆可作为MPC的有效计算替代方案，显著降低推理延迟；静态架构优于时序架构，证明了瞬时状态观测即足够；通过该方法获得的策略在严格精度要求下仍存在误差，需进一步研究以改善稳态精度。

Abstract: While Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides strong stability and robustness, it imposes a significant computational burden on real-time systems. This paper investigates the application of Behavior Cloning to approximate MPC policies for the real-time control of a 3-degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator. We present a baseline controller combining Inverse Kinematics with MPC and evaluate neural network architectures, ranging from classical regression algorithms to deep learning models including Deep MLPs and RNNs, to derive computationally efficient surrogate policies. We analyze generalization capabilities, stability considerations, and the trade-offs inherent in different architectural choices. Our empirical study employs both online and offline evaluations to assess performance regarding accuracy, computational efficiency, and fidelity to the original MPC policy. Our results demonstrate that Behavior Cloning can effectively reduce the computational burden of MPC policies for 3-DOF robotic manipulators, achieving a 3x reduction in inference latency with a 84.98% success rate under relaxed tolerances. Notably, we find that static architectures outperform temporal variants, confirming the sufficiency of instantaneous state observations for this task. However, we observe a precision gap under strict tolerances, which suggest that while Behavior Cloning captures the global optimal trajectory, further research is needed to minimize terminal steady-state error.

</details>


### [207] [SoFiE: Soft Finger Exoskeleton for Intelligent Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00397)
*Magnus Malthe Sigsgaard Nielsen,Nicklas Nikolaj Grønvall,Xiaofeng Xiong,Saravana Prashanth Murali Babu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SoFiE：一种模块化、软体指部外骨骼系统，集成了本体感知（StretchSense）和触觉传感（MagSense），实现了轻量化、无线化的手部辅助抓握功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对手部功能障碍人群，现有辅助设备成本高、结构笨重，需要一种轻量化、模块化、具备良好感知能力的软体可穿戴机器人来改善抓握任务的辅助效果。

Method: 1. 构建模块化软体手指外骨骼（SoFiE），采用 3D 打印柔性材料；2. 驱动机制：采用线传动（tendon-driven）配合紧凑型直流电机；3. 传感系统：集成 StretchSense（导电弹簧作为本体感知传感器）和 MagSense（磁铁+磁力计组合作为触觉传感器）；4. 控制系统：基于嵌入式微控制器的无线控制架构。

Result: 实验验证了该系统能够实现精准的姿态估计，通过传感反馈有效区分不同硬度的物体，并为不同的抓取任务生成独特的传感器特征签名，证明了系统的安全性和自适应控制基础。

Conclusion: SoFiE 为模块化、轻量化的软体辅助手套提供了一种可行设计，其集成传感方案（StretchSense 和 MagSense）在实现人机协作、感知交互对象力学属性方面表现出潜力，证明了该系统在辅助机器人领域的应用前景。

Abstract: Soft wearable robotic systems have emerged as a promising solution for assisting individuals with reduced hand function. This paper presents SoFiE, a modular soft finger exoskeleton designed to assist index-finger flexion during grasping tasks. The proposed system is primarily fabricated using 3D-printed flexible materials, enabling a lightweight, low-profile, and modular design. Actuation is achieved through a tendon-driven mechanism powered by a compact DC motor, while passive extension is provided by a compliant conductive spring. This element, termed StretchSense, also functions as a proprioceptive sensor by exhibiting resistance changes under deformation. Furthermore, a novel tactile sensing approach, MagSense, is introduced, using a magnet and magnetometer pair embedded in a soft fingertip structure to estimate contact force and object compliance. The system is fully untethered and controlled by an embedded microcontroller. In addition, actuator-level sensing through motor encoder feedback enables estimation of the system state, providing a foundation for safe and adaptive control strategies. Experimental validation demonstrates the capability of the system to provide reliable pose estimation, distinguish between materials with different stiffness, and generate distinct sensor signatures across different grasping tasks. This paper details the design, fabrication, and sensing concepts of the proposed exoskeleton as a proof of concept toward modular, soft, and assistive wearable robotics.

</details>


### [208] [Literary Emotions in Motion: A Soft Robotics Installation for Tactile Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00418)
*Carolina Silva-Plata,Abraham Villavicencio-Carmona,Miguel Silva Plata,Stefan Escaida,Ruben Fernandez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计了一种通过语义情感分析控制软体致动器刚度的交互装置，验证了触觉与视觉的多感官耦合能有效提升艺术装置的情感交互深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索软体机器人在艺术装置中的应用，以提供超越传统视觉和听觉信号的具身触觉交互，增强观众的情感参与度。

Method: 开发并表征了一种具有薄膜层的硅胶“软模块”，利用自然语言处理模型分析叙事文本中的情感，将识别出的两种主导情感映射为七个六边形排列软体致动器的充气状态（刚度变化）。

Result: 研究证明该结构能有效扩展刚度范围，且制造成本低廉；用户研究显示，刚度调制与颜色变化的协同作用可显著影响观众对情感的感知。

Conclusion: 通过刚度调节（结合LED光源）进行多感官耦合，能够为软体机器人装置提供具有情感意义且引人入胜的触觉交互体验。

Abstract: Soft robotics is increasingly explored in artistic contexts, where tactile interaction provides audiences with embodied engagement beyond visual or auditory signals. This work presents an interactive installation that maps semantic emotion analysis of narrative text into variable stiffness of soft pneumatic modules. A natural language model identifies two dominant emotions from a predefined set of six, driving the inflation of seven hexagonally arranged soft actuators. The central actuator represents the primary emotion, while the surrounding ones express the secondary. We develop and mechanically characterize silicone actuators, called soft modules, featuring a thin membrane layer, demonstrating how this morphological control expands the achievable stiffness range while preserving simplicity and low-cost fabrication. A user study with ten participants further evaluates how multisensory coupling of stiffness and LEDs intensity influences emotional perception. The results suggest that stiffness modulation accompanied by color change can support emotionally meaningful and engaging tactile interaction in soft robotic installations.

</details>


### [209] [ROG-Grasp: Root-Oriented Geometry for Robotic Grasping and Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00449)
*Zijian An,Augustus Sroka,Ran Yang,Bill Cai,Satoru Eto,Brian Poon,Kelvin Cai,Shijie Geng,Feng Liu,Yiming Feng,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ROG-Grasp框架，通过基于RGB-D的几何感知与定向算法，实现了农产品的高效、精确定向抓取与放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农产品收获后处理需要对物体进行特定的定向抓取与放置，而现有的通用方法在处理这一类任务时精度和效率不足。

Method: 提出了ROG-Grasp框架：1. 使用基于YOLO的根茎检测器；2. 利用点云平面拟合法推断农产品法线，从而确定物体朝向；3. 实现受限笛卡尔运动规划以完成定向抓取与放置。

Result: 在番茄和洋葱的实验中，无论是在独立还是杂乱场景下，该方法均实现了高成功率及稳定的执行时间，表现优于VLA策略。

Conclusion: 基于几何特征的感知方法在处理农产品定向抓取任务中，展现出优于视觉语言动作（VLA）模型的可靠性、准确性和实时性，验证了几何驱动感知在实际农业自动化中的有效性。

Abstract: Orientation-aware manipulation is essential in post-harvest agricultural processing, where produce must be grasped and placed in consistent configurations. This paper presents ROG-Grasp, a geometry-based robotic grasping and placement framework that estimates the produce orientation from root surface geometry using RGB-D perception. A YOLO-based root detector and point cloud plane fitting are used to infer the root normal, enabling stable grasp pose generation and orientation-constrained Cartesian motion planning. Experiments on tomatoes and onions demonstrate high success rates and stable execution time in both isolated and cluttered scenarios. Compared with vision-language-action (VLA) policies, the proposed method achieves more reliable and accurate grasp completion with faster execution. These results highlight the effectiveness of geometry-driven perception for practical orientation-controlled manipulation tasks. A video of our paper is available online https://youtu.be/Ir2UtGODdMo.

</details>


### [210] [Adaptive PD Gains for Energy-Conscious Control in Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00459)
*Danyal Saqib,Francisco Andrade Chavez,Marie Charbonneau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有机器人安全交互控制对传感器要求高或实现复杂的问题，本文提出了一种基于能量限制的自适应PD控制器，该控制器无需力/转矩传感器，可灵活控制机器人的机械能阈值并保证系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的力控和转矩控制方法依赖于昂贵的外部传感器或关节转矩传感，而现有的基于能量的方法通常实现复杂且缺乏充分的稳定性验证。研究旨在提供一种既能确保人机交互安全、又无需特定传感器、且具有严格稳定性保证的轻量化控制器。

Method: 提出了一种自适应比例-微分（PD）控制器。该方法能够限制机器人的动能与势能，通过参数调节控制器的增益以定义能量阈值和响应锐度；同时通过李雅普诺夫等理论构建了控制器的稳定性证明并定义了稳定条件。

Result: 在TALOS仿人机器人平台（仿真与硬件）上的测试结果表明，该控制器能够实现预期的能量限制行为，表现出良好的顺应性，并成功通过了稳定性验证。

Conclusion: 该研究通过实验验证了自适应PD控制器在限制机器人机械能以实现安全pHRI方面的有效性与稳定性。该方法不仅消除了对力/转矩传感器的依赖，还通过参数化设计提供了灵活的顺应性调节能力。

Abstract: Compliant force or torque control are approaches often investigated to achieve safe physical human-robot interaction (pHRI). However, these approaches have limitations. Force control requires a robot to be equipped with external force sensors to track the amplitude and direction of applied forces. Torque control requires torque sensing or estimation in each joint. As this is not available on every robot, energy-based approaches offer a promising alternative. Such approaches aim to achieve safe pHRI by limiting the mechanical energy of the robot. Current schemes leveraging an energy-based approach tend to have a complex implementation, and some may require further stability verification. We hence propose an adaptive proportional-derivative (PD) controller that can limit a robot's energy under any given limit to achieve safe pHRI. The proposed controller can limit both the kinetic and potential energy of a robot, and the behaviour of the controller gains can be shaped using various parameters, defining precisely the cutoff limit and sharpness. We construct a stability proof for the controller and define a condition to ensure the controller's stability. The proposed controller's behaviour and compliance are tested on the TALOS robot from PAL Robotics both in simulation and on hardware, verifying the expected compliant and energy-limiting behaviour of the controller.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [BitsMoE: Efficient Spectral Energy-Guided Bit Allocation for MoE LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00079)
*Jiayu Zhao,Zihan Teng,Minhao Fan,Tianrui Ma,Wentao Ren,Song Chen,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BitsMoE 是一种创新的 MoE 模型压缩框架，通过 SVD 分解保留公共结构并利用谱能量引导的混合精度量化，解决了传统 MoE 量化在超低比特下精度大幅下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型在部署时由于需将所有专家权重常驻内存，导致内存开销巨大。现有的剪枝方法会造成永久性的容量损失，而粗粒度的量化方法无法针对不同专家及权重方向的重要性进行灵活的位宽分配，难以在超低比特（ultra-low-bit）环境下保持模型性能。

Method: 提出了一种名为 BitsMoE 的谱能量引导位分配框架。首先，利用奇异值分解 (SVD) 将 MoE 层分解为共享基向量和专家特定的谱因子，仅对谱因子进行量化，保留共享基以维护跨专家结构；其次，引入谱能量感知机制，通过整数线性规划 (ILP) 求解最优的混合精度位宽分配，以最小化重建误差，满足特定的比特预算约束。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base 模型上进行 2-bit 量化实验，BitsMoE 相比 GPTQ 将量化速度提升了 12.3 倍，平均准确率提升了 27.83 个百分点，解码速度提升了 1.76 倍。

Conclusion: BitsMoE 通过一种基于谱能量感知和混合精度量化的策略，有效解决了 MoE 模型在超低位量化下的性能退化问题，在显著降低模型内存占用的同时保持了极高的模型精度和推理速度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models reduce per-token computation through sparse expert activation, but their deployment remains memory-intensive because all expert weights must be kept resident in memory. Existing MoE compression methods struggle in the ultra-low-bit regime: pruning irreversibly removes model capacity, while coarse-grained quantization fails to allocate bits according to heterogeneous expert and weight-direction importance. We propose BitsMoE, a spectral-energy-guided bit-allocation framework for MoE LLM quantization. BitsMoE decomposes each MoE layer by SVD into a shared basis and expert-specific spectral factors, retaining the shared basis without quantization to preserve common cross-expert structure and using the expert-specific factors as fine-grained quantization units. To determine the bit-width of each unit, BitsMoE formulates spectrum-wise mixed-precision quantization as an activation-aware reconstruction surrogate and solves an integer linear program that minimizes estimated reconstruction loss under a fixed bit budget. Experiments across multiple MoE LLMs show that BitsMoE substantially reduces downstream task accuracy degradation in ultra-low-bit regimes. Under 2-bit quantization on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, BitsMoE accelerates quantization by 12.3$\times$, improves average accuracy by 27.83 percentage points, and increases decoding speed by 1.76$\times$ over GPTQ. Our model and code are publicly available at https://github.com/zjiayu064/BitsMoE.

</details>


### [212] [DAStatFormer: A Hybrid Multibranch Transformer with Statistical Feature Integration for DAS-Based Pattern Recognitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00081)
*Michel Dione,Jerry Lonlac,Hélène Louis,Anthony Fleury,Stephane Lecoeuche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DAS数据高维处理成本高的问题，DAStatFormer通过特征工程提取轻量化统计特征，结合多分支门控Transformer，实现了高精度、低开销的实时事件分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式声学传感（DAS）数据维度极高且具有复杂的时空模式，现有的深度学习方案（如CNN、循环模型、Transformer）在捕捉长程依赖时效率低下，且直接处理原始数据的计算成本过高。

Method: 提出了DAStatFormer架构，摒弃处理原始高维DAS矩阵的方法。首先从时域、波形域和频域提取24个经ANOVA筛选的统计学特征（大幅压缩数据量），随后采用多分支Gated Transformer网络分别处理各域特征，最后通过自适应门控机制进行融合。

Result: 在Φ-OTDR基准数据集和真实场景DAS数据集上，DAStatFormer达到了高达99.4%的分类精度，且在参数量和推理成本上均显著优于DASFormer和DeepViT等基准模型。

Conclusion: DAStatFormer通过结合多域统计特征与门控Transformer架构，在保证高识别精度的同时，显著降低了计算资源需求，证明了该方法在实现大规模、实时DAS监测任务中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Distributed Acoustic Sensing (DAS) enables large-scale monitoring through optical fibers, but its high dimensionality and complex spatio-temporal patterns make event classification demanding. Existing deep learning approaches-CNNs, recurrent models, and Transformer variants-either fail to capture long-range dependencies or require processing raw DAS matrices at prohibitive cost. We propose DAStatFormer, a hybrid multibranch Transformer that combines compact multidomain statistical features with Gated Transformer Networks. Instead of raw signals, we extract 24 ANOVA-selected attributes per channel from the temporal, waveform, and spectral domains, reducing data size by orders of magnitude while preserving discriminative information. Each domain is processed via dedicated step-wise and channel-wise attention branches, fused by an adaptive gating mechanism. Experiments on the open $Φ$-OTDR benchmark and a real-scenario DAS dataset show that DAS-tatFormer achieves up to 99.4% accuracy and near-perfect real-world performance, while using significantly fewer parameters and lower inference cost than models such as DASFormer and DeepViT. These results demonstrate its suitability for scalable, real-time DAS-based monitoring. We release our code at https://github.com/MichelD-git/DAStatFormer

</details>


### [213] [Hoeffding Concept Bottleneck Models with Applications to Overhead Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00082)
*Clément Bénard,Manon Arfib,Christophe Labreuche,Victor Quétu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有线性概念瓶颈模型在可解释性和非线性建模上的不足，提出了一种基于 Hoeffding 函数分解的新型模型 HCBM，通过稀疏且非线性的聚合方式提升了预测性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于线性聚合的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在处理大量概念时会削弱可解释性，且易受信息泄露影响，无法有效捕捉概念与输出间的非线性关系。

Method: 引入 Hoeffding Concept Bottleneck Models (HCBM)，基于梯度提升树（GBTs）的 Hoeffding 函数分解，实现概念评分的非线性及稀疏聚合，并利用素蕴含式（prime implicants）生成紧凑的预测路径。

Result: 实验表明 HCBM 在分类任务中优于线性 CBM，具有更强的鲁棒性，并且成功应用于高空俯瞰图像的目标检测任务。

Conclusion: HCBM 优于标准线性 CBM，证明了非线性、稀疏聚合在提高可解释性、抵御信息泄露及保持高性能方面的有效性，并成功扩展至目标检测任务。

Abstract: Explainability of deep learning algorithms is critical for computer-vision applications with high-stake decisions. Concept bottleneck models (CBM) have recently shown promising performance to provide explainable and accurate predictions for classification problems, based on a bottleneck of high-level concepts. Existing CBM methods rely on a linear aggregation of the concept scores to compute predictions. However, a large number of concepts is often used in this linear approach, which undermines explainability and favors information leakage. In general, the underlying relation between concepts and output logits is not linear. Therefore, we introduce Hoeffding Concept Bottleneck Models (HCBM), which build on the Hoeffding functional decomposition of gradient-boosted trees to provide non-linear and sparse aggregations of concept scores, and generate compact predictions using prime implicants. HCBM are proved to be robust to interconcept leakage, and outperform standard linear CBM in practice, as shown in extensive experiments. Beyond classification, HCBM can be adapted to object detection, and we focus on a challenging case with overhead images to show the high performance of HCBM in these settings.

</details>


### [214] [From Demonstrations to Rewards: Test-Time Prompt Optimization for VLM Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00083)
*Christian Gumbsch,Leonardo Barcellona,Lennard Schünemann,Platon Karageorgis,Andrii Zadaianchuk,Zehao Wang,Sergey Zakharov,Fabien Despinoy,Rahaf Aljundi,Efstratios Gavves*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Demo2Reward，通过少量示教数据优化 VLM 的语言指令，将其转化为更精准的奖励模型，解决了强化学习中奖励函数难以获取且 VLM 零样本效果不佳的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的奖励函数设计困难且往往不可用；现有利用 VLM 作为零样本奖励模型的方法在缺乏仔细提示工程时效果欠佳，容易产生误报并严重损害策略学习。

Method: 提出 Demo2Reward 技术，通过 3-10 条轨迹的少量示教对预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）的语言指令进行测试时（test-time）适配优化，旨在减少奖励模型的误报（false positives）同时保留真阳性。该方法无需额外的模型训练或策略学习期间的计算资源。

Result: 在多项模拟机器人任务和不同策略骨干网络中表现出色，且成功迁移至真实世界的机器人学习场景，无需手动设计奖励函数。

Conclusion: Demo2Reward 证明了利用少量示教数据进行测试时提示词适配，能有效提升 VLM 作为奖励模型在机器人任务中的性能，在模拟和真实场景中均优于现有的零样本/少样本方法，实现了无需手动设计奖励函数的策略学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning relies on accurate reward functions, which are often hand-crafted or even unavailable in real-world applications, such as robotics. Recent work has explored the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models. However, without careful prompt engineering, these approaches tend to produce suboptimal rewards, where false positive predictions can severely degrade downstream policy learning. In robotics, limited datasets comprising expert demonstrations are often collected to bootstrap policy learning. This scenario provides an opportunity to optimize a reward model prior policy training. We propose Demo2Reward a test-time adaptation technique to optimize the language instruction of a reward model based on a few demonstrations (3-10 trajectories) to reduce false positives while preserving true positives. Crucially, this requires no additional model training or computation resources during policy learning. We show that Demo2Reward consistently outperforms existing zero- and few-shot VLM reward models across a range of simulated robotic tasks and policy backbones. Finally, we demonstrate that Demo2Reward effectively transfers to a real-world robotic learning scenario, enabling policy learning without manually engineering a reward function.

</details>


### [215] [A Shared Valence Axis Across Modern LLMs and Human EEG: The Saturation Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00129)
*Yousef A. Radwan,Xuhui Liu,Kilichbek Haydarov,Yuqian Fu,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现LLM与人类大脑在情感效价表征上存在收敛，但传统的强力监督对齐策略会因“饱和正则性”损害性能；作者通过利用监督未覆盖的残差空间进行集成学习，实现了脑电情感解码精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型（LLM）的内部表征是否可作为理解人类脑电（EEG）情感表征的“透镜”，并试图通过这种跨模态对齐来改进脑电情感解码的准确性。

Method: 1. 利用少量情感句子从LLM中构建“效价轴”（V-axis）；2. 在EEG数据集上通过线性投影验证该轴的有效性；3. 对比并评估25种脑-机对齐策略（如蒸馏、对比损失等）；4. 提出并实施基于残差多样性的集成学习方法。

Result: 1. 验证了LLM内部的效价结构与人类EEG表征高度一致；2. 发现直接的监督对齐策略会导致由于过拟合导致的解码精度下降；3. 提出利用残差空间进行集成，在FACED和SEED-V数据集上将平衡准确率提升了10.5%。

Conclusion: LLM 与人类大脑在处理情感效价（Valence）时表现出收敛的表征结构；尽管存在这种对应关系，强制性的脑-机监督/对齐策略往往会因“饱和正则性”（saturation regularity）导致性能下降；通过利用监督无法覆盖的残差子空间进行集成，可显著提升脑解码性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful representation learners whose internal features increasingly align with human cognition. We study whether modern LLMs can serve as a lens for understanding neural representations in the human brain, focusing on emotional valence in EEG.
  We first build a one-dimensional valence direction, the V-axis, from modern LLMs using only nine emotion-evocative sentences. We validate it through zero-shot transfer to sentiment benchmarks and cross-model consistency across fourteen LLMs. We then show that this LLM-derived direction maps onto human neural activity. On a public EEG cohort of 123 subjects watching affective videos, a single linear projection on EEG features tracks the V-axis position of each stimulus. Moreover, 36 EEG emotion classifiers trained without exposure to the V-axis spontaneously rediscover the same direction in their internal representations, suggesting that the same valence structure emerges in both language models and human electrophysiology.
  Yet this convergence does not provide an effective training signal. We test twenty-five alignment strategies, including knowledge distillation, representational similarity, contrastive, and topographic losses; none improve decoding, and sixteen significantly reduce accuracy. We formalize this result as the saturation regularity: once task labels alone drive a brain-decoding network onto the target direction, additional supervision mainly distorts an already-saturated basin, while the load-bearing within-class residual receives little useful gradient.
  This regularity also indicates where improvement should come from: the residual subspace unreachable by supervision. Motivated by this insight, we ensemble across residual diversity rather than supervising the basin, improving balanced accuracy by 10.5% over the prior best on FACED, with the same effect replicated on SEED-V.

</details>


### [216] [Automatically Differentiable Nonlinear Tensor Networks (ADNTNs) for Exponential Compression of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00130)
*Andrzej Cichocki,Michal Wietczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出 ADNTNs，通过利用自动微分端到端训练结构化的张量核心，实现了对神经网络模型极高压缩比（高达 77000x）且保持高性能的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有神经网络参数冗余问题，旨在扩展低秩适应和张量分解技术，提供一种结构化、硬件感知且能实现高压缩比的模型压缩方案。

Method: 提出自动可微非线性张量网络 (ADNTNs)，利用反向模式自动微分 (AD) 端到端训练核心张量。涵盖树张量网络 (TTN)、增强型 TTN (aTTN) 及多尺度纠缠重整化拟设 (MERA) 三种架构，并支持非线性激活、任务感知目标及硬件感知执行调度。

Result: 在 AlexNet 和 VGG-16 上实现约 2000 倍至 77000 倍的压缩比，且在多数情况下准确率与稠密基准相当，在部分 VGG-16 任务中甚至优于基准。

Conclusion: ADNTNs 展示了通过层级化、结构化的低秩张量分解实现极端模型压缩的潜力，这种方法在保持甚至提升精度的同时，能显著减小模型规模。结论强调了优化算法、收缩调度及硬件部署协同设计的重要性。

Abstract: We study Automatically Differentiable Nonlinear Tensor Networks (ADNTNs), a family of structured weight generators whose compact core tensors are trained end-to-end by reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD). The approach can be viewed as a natural extension of low-rank adaptation and tensor factorisation: instead of using one low-rank matrix update, an ADNTN builds a large weight tensor through a hierarchy of small cores, nonlinear activations, and optional lateral mixing tensors. The paper focuses on three architectures: Tree Tensor Networks (TTNs), augmented TTNs (aTTNs) with boundary disentanglers, and Multi-scale Entanglement Renormalisation Ansatze (MERA).
  The formulation supports nonlinear activations, task-aware objectives, batching, and hardware-aware execution schedules. At the same time, the paper keeps a clear distinction between \emph{differentiating} a contraction program and making contraction free: AD does not remove the cost of large intermediates, poor contraction orders, or exact contraction of general loopy tensor networks.
  Extensive simulations on AlexNet and VGG-16 layers show per-layer compression ratios from roughly $2000\times$ to $77000\times$ in the studied settings, with accuracy often matching the dense baseline and, in several VGG-16 cases, improving it. These results are encouraging rather than final: they suggest that ADNTNs are a promising, mathematically structured, and hardware-aware route toward much smaller neural networks, provided that optimisation, contraction schedules, and deployment kernels are designed together.

</details>


### [217] [Foundation-Preserving Adaptation via Generalized Rayleigh-Quotient Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00132)
*Dongjun Kim,Adrian de Wynter,Huancheng Chen,Heasung Kim,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoLoRA优化框架通过方向性门控更新和动态遗忘惩罚，在微调模型时有效解决了性能提升与通用能力遗忘之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大模型会导致基础模型预训练阶段习得的非目标能力（即通用能力）退化。现有的遗忘感知方法大多采用固定限制，缺乏在训练过程中动态调节适配与保存权衡的机制。

Method: 提出了FoLoRA框架，核心包括：1. 基于一阶保存条件，定义预训练代理激活的遗忘惩罚与目标任务激活的任务效用；2. 利用广义瑞利商对更新方向进行评分以优化权衡；3. 构建基于预训练模型采样的代理校准数据集；4. 采用方向性门控Adam更新来抑制低效用、高遗忘的更新方向。

Result: 在数学、代码和指令遵循任务的实验中，FoLoRA不仅提升了下游目标任务的表现，还在保留原始模型通用能力方面达到了最佳的整体水平。

Conclusion: FoLoRA通过在微调过程中动态平衡任务性能与遗忘惩罚，实现了比现有方法更优的下游任务适配与预训练知识保存的平衡，证明了方向性遗忘控制的有效性。

Abstract: While finetuning effectively adapts foundation models to specialized downstream tasks, it can degrade nontarget capabilities acquired during pretraining. Existing forgetting aware methods typically seek safer updates through specialized initialization or fixed constraints, but do not regulate the adaptation preservation trade-off during training. We propose Foundation Preserving LoRA (FoLoRA), a forgetting aware optimization framework. Guided by a first order preservation condition, FoLoRA defines a forgetting penalty over pretraining-proxy activations and a task utility over downstream task activations. It then scores update directions by task utility per unit forgetting penalty via a generalized Rayleigh quotient. The resulting spectral coordinate system enables direction wise gated Adam updates, attenuating low utility to penalty directions during training. To estimate the forgetting penalty, FoLoRA constructs pretraining proxy calibration data by sampling from the pretrained model rather than relying on a single proxy dataset. Experiments on math, code, and instruction following adaptation show that FoLoRA achieves the strongest preservation adaptation balance over baselines, improving target task performance with best aggregate preservation of non target capabilities.

</details>


### [218] [PE-means: Improved Differentially Private $k$-means Clustering through Private Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00342)
*Thomas Humphries,Zinan Lin,Sergey Yekhanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 PE-means 的差分隐私 $k$-means 聚类算法，通过改进私有演化（PE）技术，实现了比传统方法更优的聚类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的差分隐私 $k$-means 聚类方法依赖于直接对私有数据求和，其灵敏度与数据域（domain）成正比，导致隐私开销大且精度受限。亟需一种灵敏度更低、性能更好的算法。

Method: 提出了 PE-means，将私有演化（Private Evolution）框架适配至 $k$-means 聚类问题。主要通过计算具有恒定灵敏度的私有直方图来引导群体演化，从而避免了传统方法中直接求和导致的高灵敏度问题。

Result: 在聚类损失（clustering loss）指标上，相较于当前最先进的基线方法，平均提升了 20%。

Conclusion: 通过引入新颖的演化算子和改进算法，PE-means 证明了基于私有演化（PE）的方法在差分隐私 $k$-means 聚类中优于传统直接求和方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of differentially private (DP) $k$-means clustering in Euclidean space. Previous solutions rely on summing the private data directly, which induces a sensitivity proportional to the domain. We introduce PE-means, an extension of the private evolution (PE) algorithm (an increasingly popular method for synthetic data generation), to the problem of $k$-means clustering. The key advantage of PE is that it only computes a private histogram with constant sensitivity to guide the evolution. Our adaptation of PE includes new evolutionary operators for clustering, as well as other algorithmic improvements of independent interest. Overall, PE-means achieves an average improvement of 20% in clustering loss over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [219] [World Models: A Comprehensive Survey of Architectures, Methodologies, Reasoning Paradigms, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00133)
*Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Hanqi Jiang,Ruiyu Yan,Wei Ruan,Zihao Wu,Lifeng Chen,Weihang You,Xinliang Li,Bowen Chen,Huawen Hu,Peilong Wang,Sizhuang Liu,Jing Zhang,Siyuan Li,Zhengliang Liu,Yu Bao,Lin Zhao,Lichao Sun,Dajiang Zhu,Xiang Li,Jinglei Lv,Quanzheng Li,Wei Liu,Tianming Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了世界模型的发展脉络，从架构、方法论、推理策略及应用维度构建了多轴分类法，总结了现代世界的范式演进，并指出了构建通用多模态世界模型在安全与泛化能力上面临的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在人工智能领域虽取得快速进展，但在架构选择、训练方法及评估体系上缺乏统一的科学框架，导致领域发展碎片化，亟需系统梳理与整合。

Method: 通过多轴分类法（Multi-axis taxonomy）对世界模型进行系统性综述，涵盖架构（输入/表示/学习范式）、方法论族系（Transformer/扩散模型/物理约束模型等）、推理策略（规划/反事实推理）及应用领域。梳理了从经典系统到现代大模型的演进脉络，并分析了评估协议与关键挑战。

Result: 本综述梳理了世界模型的发展历程，明确了其在多领域的应用现状，识别了领域内的核心技术难点（如预测误差传播、评估不统一），并归纳了思维链（CoT）推理与世界模型想象力融合的最新趋势。

Conclusion: 世界模型正处于向大规模多模态、通用交互式模拟器演进的阶段。未来研究需聚焦于解决预测误差积累、提升虚实迁移能力以及构建统一的评估基准，以推动其在安全关键领域的应用。

Abstract: World models, internal simulators that learn the structure and dynamics of an environment, have emerged as a central paradigm in the pursuit of artificial general intelligence, enabling agents to predict, plan, and reason within learned representations. Despite rapid progress across reinforcement learning, robotics, autonomous driving, and video generation, the field lacks a unified framework integrating its diverse architectural choices, training methods, reasoning mechanisms, and application settings. This survey addresses that gap with a multi-axis taxonomy organized along four dimensions: (i) architecture, encompassing representation format, dynamics formulation, input modality, learning paradigm, and downstream application; (ii) methodological family, including state-space and recurrent approaches, transformer-based models, diffusion-based generators, physics-informed networks, and language-augmented multimodal systems; (iii) reasoning strategy, covering imagination-based planning, latent policy learning, counterfactual reasoning, and planning under uncertainty; and (iv) application domain, spanning robotics, autonomous driving, video prediction, multimodal agents, reinforcement learning, scientific modeling, medical imaging, educational measurement, and business and finance. Tracing the field from early cognitive-science foundations to milestone systems such as PlaNet, the Dreamer family, MuZero, Sora, Cosmos, and Genie, we examine how these dimensions interact and highlight the recent convergence of chain-of-thought reasoning with world-model imagination. We review evaluation protocols and benchmarks, identify persistent challenges such as compounding prediction errors, sim-to-real transfer, and fragmented evaluation, and outline future directions toward unified multimodal world models, foundation-scale interactive simulators, and safe deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [220] [On Effectiveness and Efficiency of Agentic Tool-calling and RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00135)
*Tong Liu,Cheng Qian,Matej Cief,Yuan He,Daniele Dan,Nikolaos Aletras,Gabriella Kazai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM工具调用评估标准化的缺失问题，并针对RL训练中的计算冗余提出了优化技术，实现了训练效率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型代理的工具调用能力评估存在不稳定性（缺乏标准），且基于强化学习（RL）的工具调用训练过程存在计算资源浪费严重的问题。

Method: 1. 评估分析：系统分析了影响工具调用评估的变量（如随机种子、系统提示词、多轮模板构建等）；2. RL优化：针对采样中的无效学习信号和策略更新中的高计算消耗，引入了两种加速RL训练的技术。

Result: 研究发现评估结果极易受实现细节干扰，导致排行榜不可靠；通过提出的优化技术，实现了在不降低性能的前提下大幅缩短工具调用训练的时间（墙上时间）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了工具调用评估对实现细节的高度敏感性，强调了标准化评估的重要性，并证实了通过优化RL流程中的无效样本和计算冗余可以显著提高模型训练的能效。

Abstract: Tool-calling is a central component of modern large language model (LLM) agents, equipping them with skills beyond their parametric knowledge. This paper studies tool-calling along two complementary axes: effectiveness, i.e., how this capability is measured, and efficiency, i.e., how it is learned. On effectiveness, we systematically analyze tool-calling evaluation pipelines and show that results can be highly sensitive to seemingly minor, often undocumented implementation choices including the random seed, system prompt, multi-turn template construction, and how prior interaction/reasoning history is carried forward. These choices can lead to substantial differences in reported performance, especially in multi-turn settings where without rigorous standardization, leaderboard rankings are unreliable. On efficiency, we examine standard reinforcement learning (RL) for tool-calling and identify two sources of computational waste: (i) during rollouts, many prompts produce no learning signal, and (ii) during policy updates, optimization incurs high computational cost. Guided by these findings, we introduce two techniques that accelerate RL-based tool-calling training, achieving substantial wall-clock speedup without degrading performance.

</details>


### [221] [Generative AI and Digital Ecosystem Resilience: A Proactive Lifecycle-Based Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00136)
*Jonghyun Chung,Rishabh Chaddha,Sanket Badhe,Debanshu Das,Nathan Huang,Amanpreet Kaur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向生成式AI时代的主动防御机制，提出利用C5生命周期框架整合社会科学与计算科学方法，以应对不断演变的不真实叙事及合成威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（GenAI）加速了对抗性合成内容的传播，导致传统的被动检测方法失效，亟需从被动防御转向对新兴不真实叙事的主动检测。

Method: 1. 采用基于生命周期的C5交互模型（情境、起因、内容、放大周期、后果）整合社会科学与机器学习研究；2. 利用协调性不真实行为（CIB）分析、流行病学建模及Hawkes过程进行传播模式分析；3. 梳理高维嵌入空间异常检测、多层图无监督协作检测及智能体代理系统等主动防御技术。

Result: 综述了从内容创建到传播的全生命周期检测技术，系统化梳理了识别合成内容放大行为的计算方法，并分析了GenAI带来的多级分布漂移等技术挑战。

Conclusion: 面对生成式AI带来的挑战，未来的研究应重点关注异常聚类分析以及构建具有前瞻性和弹性的防御系统，以维护信息生态的安全。

Abstract: The proliferation of adversarial synthetic content, accelerated by Generative AI (GenAI) is rendering traditional reactive detection methods ineffective. This survey synthesizes emerging research to demonstrate a paradigm shift toward the proactive detection of emerging inauthentic narratives. In this survey, we adopt a unified, lifecycle-based taxonomy to combine socio-technical lifecycle models of adversarial campaigns with advanced computational methodologies for emerging inauthentic narrative detection. By structuring the analysis around the C5 Interaction Model (Context, Causes, Content, Cycle of Amplification, Consequences), we integrate different research streams from machine learning and social science. To differentiate spread patterns of synthetic amplification from authentic baseline traffic, this paper surveys state-of-the-art techniques for modeling the creation, seeding, and propagation of fresh narratives, including the analysis of Coordinated Inauthentic Behavior (CIB), epidemiological modeling, and Hawkes process. This survey also provides a systematic review of proactive detection methods for adversarial threats at different stages in the C5 interaction model, specifically, anomaly detection in high-dimensional embedding spaces, unsupervised coordination detection on multi-layer graphs, and agentic AI systems. Finally, this survey addresses challenges posed by GenAI, including the difficulty of tracking rapidly changing threats and multi-level distributional drift, and it outlines a future research agenda focused on detecting anomalous clusters and building anticipatory and resilient systems. This survey provides a comprehensive, lifecycle-based review of methods for the proactive detection of emerging synthetic threats for more resilient information ecosystems.

</details>


### [222] [TimeBlocks: Foundational and Continual Time-Series Blockbase -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02142)
*David Campos,Bin Yang,Tung Kieu,Lei Chen,Chenjuan Guo,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeBlocks 是一种轻量级时间序列处理框架，通过模块化组件与持续数据流校准技术，实现了在资源受限场景下对多任务的高效实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型时间序列基础模型虽然功能强大，但在硬件资源受限和实时数据流处理场景下因规模过大、无法持续校准而表现欠佳，难以部署。

Method: 1. 基于模块化设计：维护一个可互换的模块池，根据数据特征动态组装轻量级模型；2. 路由策略：选择最优模块组合以适应特定数据；3. StreamCore 方法：通过构建具有代表性的小型数据子集，实现模型的持续校准（Continual Calibration）。

Result: 实验表明，TimeBlocks 在多个数据集和任务上均优于现有的基准模型，实现了性能与轻量化部署的平衡。

Conclusion: TimeBlocks 提出了一种轻量化、模块化的时间序列处理框架，通过模块拼接与流式校准（StreamCore）有效解决了传统大型基础模型在实时流数据场景下计算开销大、无法持续校准的缺陷。

Abstract: The ongoing digitization has led to a proliferation of time-series data streams that monitor a variety of processes, from which valuable insights may be obtained. Further, the emergence of successful foundational language models begs the question of whether it is possible to achieve time-series models with the foundational properties of handling multiple tasks, while being sufficiently lightweight to allow real-time data stream processing. Existing foundational time-series models are often large and only effective in offline settings without stringent time and computational constraints, and where repeated model calibration is not needed. However, when applied to data streams, these models are ineffective due to their size and lack of support for continual calibration, which compromise their ability to deliver accurate real-time responses, their durability, and their deployability in hardware-limited settings. We propose TimeBlocks to enable versatile time-series processing by facilitating the efficient building of lightweight models suitable for multiple tasks under variable conditions. In particular, the method maintains a pool of interchangeable and modular model blocks that can be used to construct new time-series models. When presented with specific time-series data, a routing strategy iteratively selects the most suitable blocks to construct a lightweight and accurate model for the data. We equip TimeBlocks with a method called StreamCore to build a representative small subset of the data stream, which preserves a guaranteed approximation of the stream over time, enabling continual model calibration. An experimental study on multiple data sets and covering multiple tasks shows that TimeBlocks enables to build models capable of outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [223] [Geometric Erasure by Contrastive Velocity Matching in Rectified Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00140)
*Jonas Henry Grebe,Tobias Braun,Anna Rohrbach,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Rectified Flow 模型架构，本文提出了 GEM 框架，通过融合轨迹遗忘与教师引导机制，实现了高效、精准的模型概念擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型从 U-Net 过渡到 Rectified Flow Transformer，现有的概念擦除技术难以适配，导致在面对多模态生成模型带来的有害内容、深度伪造及版权隐患时缺乏有效的安全方案。

Method: 提出了 GEM（Geometric Erasure Method）框架。该方法将基于轨迹的遗忘逻辑（Generative Flow Networks）与教师引导的正则化相结合，构建了一个统一的流匹配（flow-matching）机制，利用教师模型提供的吸引和排斥信号进行联合优化。

Result: 在 Rectified Flow 模型上实现了对特定有害或敏感概念的针对性抑制，且在擦除过程中有效保护了模型原本的良性生成能力。

Conclusion: GEM 框架通过将轨迹引导的遗忘转化为教师引导的流匹配任务，在 Rectified Flow Transformer 模型上实现了高效、精准的概念擦除，同时保持了模型原有的生成能力。

Abstract: While the rapid adoption of multimodal generative models offers immense potential, it has also increased the risks of harmful content synthesis, deepfakes, and copyright infringements. To address these challenges, concept erasure has emerged as a prospective safeguard. However, as the field gradually transitions from U-Net-based diffusion models to Rectified Flow Transformers, erasure research has struggled to keep pace. In this work, we introduce GEM, a simple but highly effective erasure framework for Rectified Flow models. As part of our contribution, we establish a principled bridge between trajectory-based unlearning grounded in Generative Flow Networks and classic teacher-guided erasure: we translate trajectory-based signals into a teacher-guided flow-matching setup that unifies the strengths of both paradigms. Concretely, a teacher provides complementary attraction and repulsion signals that we combine into a single geometric guidance objective, yielding targeted suppression of unwanted concepts while preserving benign generation.

</details>


### [224] [Adaptive data selection improves wearable prediction under low baseline performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00141)
*Ali Kargarandehkordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了可穿戴设备中的自适应数据采样策略，指出其收益与用户基线性能呈负相关。策略虽能大幅提升表现欠佳者的预测效果，但并非通用，应根据个体基线进行定制化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自适应感知策略被广泛用于可穿戴设备以提升有限数据预算下的预测性能，但其在不同个体间的具体表现和收益分布尚不明确。

Method: 利用长周期可穿戴设备数据集（心率、活动和EMA模态），在固定测量预算下对比自适应选择时间窗口策略与随机采样策略，并通过AUROC和F1分数进行评估。

Result: 自适应策略显著提升了基线性能较差者的AUROC（最高提升0.7），但对基线性能强的人群效果有限甚至有负影响；自适应收益与基线性能呈显著负相关；多数个体（60-80%）AUROC有所获益，但F1分数的提升较小且不稳定。

Conclusion: 自适应感知策略并非对所有用户均有效，其价值在于改善基线性能较差的个体的预测效果；研究支持根据个体基线性能定制自适应感知策略，以优化可穿戴健康监测的效率。

Abstract: Adaptive sensing strategies that selectively sample data are increasingly used in wearable health systems to improve prediction performance under limited data budgets, yet their benefits across individuals remain poorly understood. Here, we evaluate adaptive selection of time windows for model training under fixed measurement budgets across multiple sensing modalities, including heart rate, activity, and ecological momentary assessment (EMA), in a longitudinal wearable dataset. We quantify performance gains relative to random sampling using both area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) and F1 score. Adaptive strategies yield substantial improvements in AUROC for participants with low baseline performance (with gains up to 0.7), while offering limited or negative gains for participants with strong baselines. Across modalities, adaptive gain is strongly inversely correlated with baseline performance (Pearson r = -0.67; Spearman p = -0.62). At the participant level, most individuals benefit in AUROC (60-80% across modalities), although improvements in F1 are smaller and less consistent. These findings show that adaptive sensing is not uniformly beneficial, but instead provides the greatest value in underperforming settings. Our results support selective deployment strategies that tailor adaptive sensing based on baseline performance to improve efficiency in wearable health monitoring.

</details>


### [225] [BudgetDraft: Acceptance-Aware Multi-View Training for Sparse-KV Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00144)
*Liang He,Jingbo Wen,Qishi Zhan,Yixiong Chen,Kangning Cui,Qizhen Lan,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BudgetDraft 是一种面向中长文本推理的推测解码训练方案，通过多视角稀疏训练解决草稿模型与校验模型在稀疏度上的不匹配问题，在节省内存的同时显著提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限场景下，推测解码常采用“稀疏草稿模型+全量校验模型”的架构。然而，随着上下文长度增加（4K-16K），草稿模型的稀疏 KV 缓存与校验模型的全量 KV 缓存之间会出现严重的失配，导致预测准确率（接受率）急剧下降，限制了推理加速效果。

Method: 提出了一种多视角稀疏训练方法（Multi-view sparse training）。该方法通过在训练过程中让草稿模型接触多个采样到的 KV 预算（KV budget），并利用针对全缓存分支的“接受感知损失（acceptance-aware loss）”和针对稀疏缓存分支的“多视角损失（multi-view loss）”进行联合优化，从而训练出一个具备鲁棒性的草稿模型。

Result: 在 PG-19、LongBench 和 LWM 数据集上，相比于常规自回归（AR）解码，BudgetDraft 在 4K、8K 和 16K 上下文长度下分别实现了最高 6.55 倍、4.46 倍和 2.10 倍的端到端加速，同时保持了高效的内存节省。

Conclusion: BudgetDraft 成功解决了中长文本推理中稀疏草稿模型与全量校验模型之间的“稀疏/全量不匹配”问题，在无需额外推理组件的情况下，实现了在不同稀疏度下的高效解码，显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up autoregressive decoding by using a drafter to propose multiple tokens that a verifier validates in parallel. In resource-constrained deployments, the drafter uses a sparse KV cache to limit peak GPU memory and end-to-end latency under a fixed KV budget, while the verifier keeps a full KV cache. Mid-to-long context inference (4K--16K context length) is common in real applications. However, naive sparse/full speculative decoding suffers from the sparse/full mismatch as context length grows, causing the acceptance rate to drop quickly. We propose BudgetDraft, a multi-view sparse training method for sparse drafting in mid-to-long inference. The drafter is exposed to multiple sampled KV budgets during training and learns to align each sparse view with one shared full-cache teacher target. BudgetDraft combines an acceptance-aware loss on a full-cache branch with a multi-view loss on a sparse-cache branch, producing a single budget-robust drafter that recovers acceptance across sparsity levels without extra inference-time components. Experimental results on PG-19, LongBench, and LWM show that BudgetDraft achieves up to 6.55x, 4.46x, and 2.10x end-to-end speedup vs AR at 4K, 8K, and 16K context lengths, while keeping the inference pipeline memory-friendly.

</details>


### [226] [RAFT: Data Refinement and Adaptive Distillation for Domain Fine-Tuning with Alleviated Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00147)
*Yuduo Li,Xiaofeng Shi,Qian Kou,Longbin Yu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAFT 是一种通过数据精炼与轨迹级在线策略蒸馏来缓解大模型领域微调中通用能力遗忘的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的领域特定监督微调（SFT）常导致通用能力下降，主要源于两个缺口：监督兼容性缺口（领域目标与模型自然响应风格不符）以及轨迹保留缺口（仅优化固定目标，忽略了模型生成轨迹的连贯性）。

Method: 提出 RAFT 框架，包含两个阶段：1. 数据精炼：通过自条件重写、语义过滤和答案融合构建模型兼容的监督数据；2. 适应性蒸馏：在学生模型生成的轨迹上进行基于答案条件的在线策略蒸馏（ACOP），并采用 Top-K 温度蒸馏和 EMA 自适应损失平衡技术。

Result: 在三个指令微调基座和五个领域上的实验表明，相比传统 SFT，RAFT 在领域准确率上平均提升 23.2%，同时在 MS-Bench 和 IFEval 任务上的通用能力分别恢复了 18.2% 和 10.2%。

Conclusion: 通过结合数据精炼与轨迹层面的约束（Answer-Conditioned On-Policy Distillation），RAFT 能够有效解决领域微调中的“灾难性遗忘”问题，在显著提升领域性能的同时，最大程度保留了模型的通用能力。

Abstract: Domain-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often improves in-domain performance at the cost of degrading a model's general capabilities. We view this degradation through two practical gaps in domain SFT: a supervision-compatibility gap, where domain targets differ in style and reasoning format from the original model's natural responses, and a trajectory-preservation gap, where teacher-forced SFT optimizes fixed target tokens without constraining the model's behavior on its own generated prefixes. This process fails to preserve the model's original behavior. We propose RAFT (Data Refinement and Adaptive Distillation for Domain Fine-Tuning with Alleviated Forgetting), a two-stage framework that addresses both factors. First, RAFT constructs model-compatible supervision through self-conditioned rewriting, semantic filtering, and answer fusion. Second, RAFT performs Answer-Conditioned On-Policy Distillation, where the original instruction-tuned model provides soft targets on student-generated trajectories while being conditioned on the fused answer as helpful context. We further introduce top-K temperature distillation and EMA-based adaptive loss balancing to stabilize the domain-general trade-off. Across three instruction-tuned backbones and five domains, RAFT improves average domain accuracy by 23.2% over standard SFT, while recovering part of the SFT-induced degradation on MS-Bench and IFEval, with relative improvements of 18.2% and 10.2%, respectively. These results show that coupling data refinement with trajectory-level preservation provides an effective recipe for domain fine-tuning with alleviated forgetting.

</details>


### [227] [Emergence of Exploration in Policy Gradient Reinforcement Learning via Retrying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00151)
*Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas,Sotetsu Koyamada,Tadashi Kozuno,Toshinori Kitamura,Shin Ishii,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReMax目标函数，将探索内化为策略优化的自然属性，并通过RePPO算法实现对探索程度的连续可控，在不使用额外探索奖励的情况下提升了RL算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的探索行为本质上源于代理在相似状态下的多次尝试。现有的方法多依赖显式的奖励项（exploration bonus）来激励探索，而本文旨在通过刻画“重试以改良收益”这一直觉，使随机探索成为策略优化过程中的一种涌现属性（emergent property）。

Method: 1. 提出了ReMax目标函数：通过计算M次采样的期望最大收益（expected maximum return）来评估策略，并将收益的不确定性纳入考量；
2. 导出了ReMax的策略梯度公式，实现对目标的有效优化；
3. 开发了RePPO算法：将离散重试次数M推广为连续参数m，实现了对探索程度的精细化调节。

Result: 在MinAtar和Craftax基准测试中，RePPO无需任何显式探索奖励，成功促进了策略的有效探索，并表现出良好的鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: ReMax将探索视为一种通过多次重试以获得更高期望收益的自然属性，证实了在不依赖显式探索奖励（exploration bonus）的情况下，通过优化最大期望收益目标亦能实现有效的随机探索策略。RePPO作为一种实用的实现方案，在复杂基准测试中展现了优异的性能。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning (RL), agents benefit from exploration only because they repeatedly encounter similar states: trying different actions can improve performance or reduce uncertainty; without such retries, a greedy policy is optimal. We formalize this intuition with ReMax, an objective that evaluates a policy by the expected maximum return over $M$ samples, where $M$ is a positive integer, while accounting for return uncertainty. Optimizing this objective induces stochastic exploration as an emergent property, without explicit bonus terms. For efficient policy optimization, we derive a new policy-gradient formulation for ReMax and introduce ReMax PPO (RePPO), a PPO variant that optimizes ReMax while generalizing the discrete retry count $M$ to a continuous parameter $m > 0$, enabling fine-grained control of exploration. Empirically, RePPO promotes exploration, without any explicit exploration bonuses, on the MinAtar and Craftax benchmarks.

</details>


### [228] [Inner Product Aware Quantization: Provably Fast, Accurate, and Adaptive Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00289)
*Nathan White,Krish Singal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了能够保内积的自适应量化框架，通过与自适应随机量化（ASQ）结合，实现了在保持量化精度的同时，显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法主要关注最小化均方误差（MSE），但在许多下游任务中，直接保留内积至关重要。因此需要开发能够适应性地保留内积的量化方案。

Method: 构建了旨在保留对任意输入内积的量化目标函数；开发了针对最差情况和平均情况输入的精确及近似求解算法，并以此推导出了高效的实用量化策略。

Result: 理论上证明了新目标函数与ASQ的深层联系；实验表明，所提算法在多种工作负载分布下表现优异，且在标准ASQ任务中表现出比现有SOTA方法高出2-10倍的计算效率。

Conclusion: 本文建立了内积感知量化与自适应随机量化（ASQ）之间的紧密联系，并提出的高效算法在保证量化质量的前提下，显著提升了现有ASQ方法的计算速度（2-10倍）。

Abstract: Quantization is a fundamental tool used to compress datasets, neural network weights, and memory usage in a range of computational tasks. Many downstream applications of vector quantization perform inner products with arbitrary inputs. This motivates the study of inner product aware quantization schemes that approximately preserve inner products with unseen vectors -- in contrast to simply minimizing the mean-squared error.
  In this work, we formulate objectives that capture natural desiderata and develop adaptive and unbiased quantization methods that approximately preserve inner products with worst-case and average-case inputs. An analysis of these objectives shows a tight connection with the well-studied notion of Adaptive Stochastic Quantization (ASQ).
  We develop provably fast exact and approximate algorithms for our objectives. Our theoretical results inspire efficient practical algorithms that perform well across a variety of workload distributions. They also lead to practical algorithms for standard ASQ which are 2-10$\times$ faster than prior state-of-the-art methods while maintaining quality. These theoretical and empirical results contribute towards making adaptive quantization techniques more efficient and tractable in practical settings.

</details>
